oṃṃ tatsadbrahmaṇe namaḥ
vṛndapraṇīto vṛndamādhavāparanāmā siddhayogaḥ
śrīkaṇṭhadattaviracitavyākhyākusumāvalyākhyaṭīkāsametaḥ
Adhikāra 1
dhyātvā śivaṃ paramatattvavicāravedyaṃ caṇḍīmabhīṣṭaphaladāṃ sagaṇaṃ gaṇeśam
dhanvantariṃ munivaraṃ munisuśrutādīnātreyamugratapasaṃ manasā praṇamya || 1
nānāmataprathitadṛṣṭaphalaprayogaiḥ prastāvavākyasahitairiha siddhayogaḥ
vṛndena mandamatinā''tmahitārthinā'yaṃ saṃlikhyate gadaviniścayajakrameṇa || 2
rogamādau parīkṣeta tato'nantaramauṣadham
tataḥ karma bhiṣakpaścājjñānapūrvaṃ samācaret || 3
yastu rogamavijñāya karmāṇyārabhate bhiṣak
apyauṣadhavidhānajñastasya siddhiryadṛcchayā || 4
yastu rogaviśeṣajñaḥ sarvabhaiṣajyakovidaḥ
deśakālaviśeṣajñastasya siddhirasaṃśayam || 5
ādāvante rujāṃ jñāne prayateta cikitsakaḥ
sādhyāsādhyavibhāgajñastataḥ kuryāccikitsitam || 6
nāsti rogo vinā doṣairyasmāttasmādvicakṣaṇaḥ
anuktamapi doṣāṇāṃ liṅgairvyādhimupācaret || 7
vikāranāmākuśalo na jihvīyātkathaṃcana
na hi sarvavikārāṇāṃ nāmato'sti dhruvā sthitiḥ || 8
sa eva kupito doṣaḥ samutthānaviśeṣataḥ
sthānāntaragataścāpi vikārānkurute bahūn || 9
darśanasparśanapraśnairvyādhijñānaṃ tridhā matam
āyurādi dṛśā sparśācchītādi praśnato'param || 10
kṛcchropāyaḥ sukhopāyo dvividhaḥ sādhya ucyate
asādhyo dvividho jñeyo yāpyo yaścāpratikriyaḥ || 11
yāpyāḥ kecitprakṛtyaiva sādhyā yāpyā upekṣayā
svabhāvādvyādhayo'sādhyāḥ kecidyāpyā upekṣitāḥ || 12
sādhyā yāpyatvamāyānti yāpyāścāsādhyatāṃ tathā
ghnanti prāṇānasādhyāstu narāṇāmakriyāvatām || 13
jātamātraścikitsyastu nopekṣyo'lpatayā gadaḥ
vahniśatruviṣaistulyaḥ svalpo'pi vikarotyasau || 14
nivṛtto'pi punarvyādhiḥ svalpenā''yāti hetunā
kṣīṇe mārgīkṛte doṣe śeṣaḥ sūkṣma ivānalaḥ || 15
tasmāttamanubadhnīyātprayogeṇānapāyinā
yāvanna prakṛtisthaḥ syāddoṣataḥ prāṇatastathā || 16
vyādhestattvaparijñānaṃ vedanāyāśca nigrahaḥ
etadvaidyasya vaidyatvaṃ na vaidyaḥ prabhurāyuṣaḥ || 17
karmajā vyādhayaḥ keciddoṣajāḥ santi cāpare
karmadoṣodbhavāścānye karmajāsteṣvahetukāḥ || 18
yathāśāstraṃ sunirṇīto yathāvyādhi cikitsitaḥ
na śamaṃ yāti yo vyādhiḥ sa jñeyaḥ karmajo budhaiḥ
svalpadoṣo garīyānyaḥ sa jñeyaḥ karmadoṣajaḥ || 19
karmakṣayātkarmakṛto doṣajaḥ svaśamauṣadhaiḥ
karmadoṣodbhavo yāti karmadoṣakṣayātkṣayam || 20
yathā viṣaṃ yathā śastraṃ yathā'gniraśaniryathā
tathauṣadhamavijñātaṃ vijñātamamṛtaṃ yathā || 21
yābhiḥ kriyābhirjāyante śarīre dhātavaḥ samāḥ
sā cikitsā vikārāṇāṃ karma tadbhiṣajāṃ matam || 22
utpadyate hi sā'vasthā deśakālabalaṃ prati
yasyāṃ kāryamakāryaṃ syātkarma kāryaṃ vivarjitam || 23
svedāvarodhaḥ saṃtāpaḥ sarvāṃgagrahaṇaṃ tathā
yugapadyatra roge tu sa jvaro vyapadiśyate || 24
āmāśayastho hatvā'gniṃ sāmo mārgānpidhāya ca
vidadhāti jvaraṃ doṣastasmāllaṅghanamācaret || 25
No verses of the Siddhayoga on this page.
The commentary Kusumāvali was not digitized.
laṅghanena kṣayaṃ nīte doṣe saṃdhukṣite'nale
vijvaratvaṃ laghutvaṃ ca kṣuccaivāsyopajāyate || 26
sadyo bhuktasya saṃjāte jvare sāme viśeṣataḥ
vamanaṃ vamanārhasya śastamityāha vāgbhaṭaḥ || 27
anutkliṣṭeṣu doṣeṣu mohādyastaruṇe vamet
prāpnuyātso'ṅgabhaṅgādīnhṛdrogaviṣamajvarān || 28
anavasthitadoṣāgnerlaṅghanaṃ doṣapācanam
jvaraghnaṃ dīpanaṃ kāṅkṣārucilāghavakārakam || 29
vamitaṃ laṅghayetprājño laṅghitaṃ na tu vāmayet
vamanaṃ kleśabāhulyāddhanyāllaṅghanakarśitam || 30
avekṣya doṣaṃ prāṇaṃ ca kāryaṃ syādapatarpaṇam || 31
taddhi mārutakṣuttṛṣṇāmukhaśoṣabhramānvite
kāryaṃ na bāle vṛddhe vā na garbhiṇyāṃ na durbale
na tathā'dhvaśramakrodhaśokakāmakṣayajvare || 32
balāvirodhinā cainaṃ laṅghanenopapādayet
balādhiṣṭhānamārogyaṃ yadartho'yaṃ kriyākramaḥ || 33
vātamūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ visarge gātralāghave
hṛdayodgārakaṇṭhāsyaśuddhau tandrāklame gate || 34
svede jāte rucau vā'pi kṣutpipāsāsahodaye
kṛtaṃ laṅghanamādeśyaṃ nirvyathe cāntarātmani || 35
parvabhedo'ṅgamardaśca kāsaḥ śoṣo mukhasya ca
kṣutpraṇāśo'rucistṛṣṇā daurbalyaṃ śrotranetrayoḥ || 36
manasaḥ saṃbhramo'bhīkṣṇamūrdhvavātastamo hṛdi
dehāgnibalahāniśca laṅghane'tikṛte bhavet || 37
vinā'pi bheṣajairvyādhiḥ pathyādeva nivartate
na tu pathyavihīnasya bheṣajānāṃ śatairapi || 38
raktaśālyādayaḥ śastāḥ purāṇāḥ ṣaṣṭikaiḥ saha
yavāgvodanalājāśca jvaritānāṃ jvarāpahāḥ || 39
dīpanī pācanī laghvī jvarārtānāṃ jvarāpahā
annakāle hitā peyā yathāsvaṃ pācanaiḥ kṛtā || 40
dāhacchardyarditaṃ kṣāmaṃ nirannaṃ tṛṣṇayā'nvitam
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ pāyayellājatarpaṇam || 41
śramopavāsānilaje hito nityaṃ rasaudanaḥ || 42
mudgayūṣaudanaścāpi deyaḥ kaphasamutthite
sa eva sitayā yuktaḥ śītaḥ pittajvare hitaḥ || 43
mudgāmalakayūṣastu vātapittātmake hitaḥ
hrasvamūlakayūṣastu kaphavātātmake hitaḥ || 44
nimbakūlakayūṣastu hitaḥ pittakaphātmake
yathāsvaṃ pācanaiḥ siddhaṃ vātapittakaphātmake || 45
laghunā pañcamūlena pippalyā saha dhānyayā
mahatyā pañcamūlyā tu vyāghrīduḥsparśagokṣuraiḥ || 46
siddhāni bhiṣagannāni prayuñjīta yathākramam
vātapitte śleṣmapitte kaphavāte tridoṣaje || 47
kvāthyadravyāñjaliṃ kṣuṇṇaṃ śrapayitvā jalāḍhake
ardhaśṛtena tenātha yavāgvādyupakalpayet || 48
vṛddhavaidyāḥ palaṃ dravyaṃ grāhayantyāḍhake'mbhasi
bheṣajasyātibāhulyātkadācidarucirbhavet || 49
yadapsu śṛtaśītāsu ṣaḍaṅgādi prayujyate
karṣamātraṃ tato dravyaṃ sādhayetprāsthike'mbhasi
ardhaśṛtaṃ prayoktavyaṃ pāne peyādisaṃvidhau || 50
karṣārdhaṃ vā kaṇāśuṇṭhyoḥ kalkadravyasya vā palam || 51
vinīya pācayedyuktyā vāri prasthena cāparān
yūṣānnarasakāṃścaiva kalpenānena sādhayet || 52
kaphavātajvare deyaṃ jalamuṣṇaṃ pipāsave
pittamadyaviṣottheṣu tiktakaiḥ śṛtaśītalam || 53
mustāparpaṭakośīracandanodīcyanāgaraiḥ
śṛtaśītaṃ jalaṃ dadyātpipāsājvaraśāntaye || 54
pādaśiṣṭaḥ kaṣāyaḥ syādyaḥ ṣoḍaśaguṇāmbhasā
kvathito'taḥ ṣaḍaṅgādirna niṣiddho navajvare || 55
arucau mātuluṅgasya kesaraṃ sājyasaindhavam
dhātrīdrākṣāsitānāṃ vā kalkamāsyena dhārayet || 56
jvarito hitamaśnīyādyadyapyasyārucirbhavet
annakāle hyabhuñjānaḥ kṣīyate mriyate'pi vā || 57
gurvabhiṣyandyakāle ca jvarī nādyātkathaṃcana
na tu tasyāhitaṃ bhuktamāyuṣe vā sukhāya vā || 58
jvaritaṃ jvaramuktaṃ vā dinānte bhojayellaghu
śleṣmakṣayavivṛddhoṣmā balavānanalastadā || 59
sātatyātsvādvabhāvācca pathyaṃ dveṣyatvamāgatam
kalpanāvidhibhistaistaiḥ priyatvaṃ gamayetpunaḥ || 60
pariṣekānpradehāṃśca snehānsaṃśodhanāni ca
divāsvapnaṃ vyavāyaṃ ca vyāyāmaṃ śiśiraṃ jalam
krodhapravātabhojyāni varjayettaruṇajvarī || 61
śoṣacchardirmado mūrchā bhramastṛṣṇā hyarocakaḥ
prāpnotyupadravānetānpariṣekādisevanāt || 62
āsaptarātrāttaruṇaṃ jvaramāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ
madhyaṃ caturdaśāhaṃ tu purāṇamata uttaram || 63
mṛdau jvare laghau dehe pracaleṣu maleṣu ca
pakvaṃ doṣaṃ vijānīyājjvare deyaṃ tadauṣadham || 64
doṣaprakṛtivaikṛtyādekeṣāṃ pakvalakṣaṇam || 65
vātikaḥ saptarātreṇa daśarātreṇa paittikaḥ
ślaiṣmiko dvādaśāhena jvaraḥ pākaṃ niyacchati || 66
pakvo hyanirhṛto doṣo dehe tiṣṭhanmahātyayam
viṣamaṃ vā jvaraṃ kuryādbalavyāpadameva vā || 67
saptarātrātparaṃ kecinmanyante deyamauṣadham
daśarātrātparaṃ keciddātavyamiti niścitāḥ || 68
No verses of the Siddhayoga on this page.
The commentary Kusumāvali was not digitized.
na kaṣāyaṃ praśaṃsanti narāṇāṃ taruṇajvare
kaṣāyeṇā''kulībhūtā doṣā jetuṃ sudustarāḥ || 69
na tu kalpanamuddiśya kaṣāyaḥ pratiṣidhyate
yaḥ kaṣāyaḥ kaṣāyaḥ syātsa varjyastaruṇajvare || 70
doṣā baddhāḥ kaṣāyeṇa stambhitvāttaruṇajvare
stabhyante na vipacyante kurvanti viṣamajvaram || 71
pāyayeddoṣaharaṇaṃ mohādāmajvare tu yaḥ
sa suptaṃ kṛṣṇasarpaṃ tu karāgreṇa parāmṛśet || 72
pītāmburlaṅghitaḥ kṣīṇo'jīrṇī bhuktaḥ pipāsitaḥ
na pibedauṣadhaṃ jantuḥ saṃśodhanamathetarat || 73
roge śodhanasādhye tu yaṃ vidyāddoṣadurbalam
taṃ samīkṣya bhiṣakkuryāddoṣapracyāvanaṃ mṛdu || 74
cale doṣe mṛdau koṣṭhe nekṣetātra balaṃ nṛṇām
avyāpaddurbalasyāpi śodhanaṃ hi tadā bhavet || 75
dvicatvāriṃśatā māṣairaṣṭādaśanibaddhakaiḥ
palaṃ dvādaśabaddhaṃ syādguñjāṣaṭkasamanvitaiḥ || 76
uttamasya palaṃ mātrā tribhiścākṣaiśca madhyame
jaghanyasya palārdhena snehakvāthyauṣadheṣu ca || 77
kvāthyadravyapale vāri dviraṣṭaguṇabhiṣyate
caturbhāgāvaśiṣṭaṃ tu peyaṃ palacatuṣṭayam || 78
dīptānalaṃ mahākāyaṃ pāyayantyañjaliṃ jalam
anye tvardhaṃ parityajya prasṛtiṃ tu cikitsakāḥ || 79
kvāthatyāgamanicchantastvaṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣitam
pāraṃparyopadeśena vṛddhavaidyāḥ paladvayam || 80
pāyayedāturaṃ sāmaṃ pācanaṃ saptame dine
śamanenāthavā dṛṣṭvā nirāmaṃ tamupācaret || 81
nāgaraṃ devakāṣṭhaṃ ca dhānyakaṃ bṛhatīdvayam
dadyātpācanakaṃ pūrvaṃ sarvajvareṣu pācanam || 82
vīryādhikaṃ bhavati bheṣajamannahīnaṃ hanyāttadāmayamasaṃśayamāśu caiva
tadbālavṛddhavanitāmṛdavo'tha pītvā glāniṃ parāṃ samupayānti balakṣayaṃ ca || 83
anulomānilaḥ svāsthyaṃ kṣuttṛṣṇā sumanaskatā
laghutvamindriyodgāraśuddhirjīrṇauṣadhākṛtiḥ || 84
klamo dāho'ṅgasadanaṃ bhramo mūrchā śirorujā
aratirbalahāniśca sāvaśeṣauṣadhākṛtiḥ || 85
auṣadhaśeṣe bhuktaṃ pītaṃ ca tathauṣadhaṃ hi śeṣe'nne
na karoti gadopaśamaṃ prakopayatyanyarogāṃśca || 86
śīghraṃ vipākamupayāti balaṃ na hiṃsyādannāvṛtaṃ na ca muhurvadanānnireti
prāgbhaktasevitamathauṣadhametadeva dadyācca vṛddhaśiśubhīruvarāṅganābhyaḥ || 87
bilvādipañcamūlāmbu pācanaṃ vātike jvare
na śodhayati yaddoṣānsamānnodīrayatyapi
samī karoti yatkruddhāṃstatsaṃśamanamucyate || 88
kirātābdāmṛtodīcyabṛhatīdvayagokṣuraiḥ
sasthirākalaśīviśvaiḥ kvātho vātajvarāpahaḥ || 89
pippalīsārivādrākṣāśatapuṣpāhareṇubhiḥ
kṛtaḥ kaṣāyaḥ saguḍo hanyācchvasanajaṃ jvaram || 90
guḍūcīsārivādrākṣāśatapuṣpāpunarnavāḥ
saguḍo'yaṃ kaṣāyaḥ syādvātajvaravināśanaḥ || 91
drākṣāguḍūcīkāśmaryatrāyamāṇāḥ sasārivāḥ
niṣkvāthya saguḍaṃ kvāthaṃ pibedvātajvarāpaham || 92
sakṣaudraṃ pācanaṃ paitte tiktābdendrayavaiḥ kṛtam || 93
lodhrotpalāmṛtāpadmasārivāṇāṃ saśarkaraḥ
kvāthaḥ pittajvaraṃ hanyādathavā parpaṭodbhavaḥ || 94
durālabhāparpaṭakapriyaṅgūbhūnimbavāsākaṭurohiṇīnām
jalaṃ pibeccharkarayā'vagāḍhaṃ tṛṣṇāsrapittajvaradāhayuktaḥ || 95
paṭolayavaniṣkvātho madhunā madhurīkṛtaḥ
tīvrapittajvarāmardī pānāttṛḍdāhanāśanaḥ || 96
ekaḥ parpaṭakaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ pittajvaranivarhaṇaḥ
kiṃ punaryadi yujyeta candanodīcyanāgaraiḥ || 97
viśvāmbuparpaṭośīraghanacandanasādhitam
dadyātsuśītalaṃ vāri tṛṭchardijvaradāhanut || 98
parpaṭāmṛtadhātrīṇāṃ kvāthaḥ pittajvaraṃ jayet
drākṣāragvadhayoścāpi kāśmaryasyāthavā punaḥ || 99
drākṣābhayāparpaṭakābdatiktākvāthaṃ saśamyākaphalaṃ vidadhyāt
pralāpamūrchābhramadāhaśoṣatṛṣṇānvite pittabhave jvare ca || 100
paryuṣitaṃ dhānyajalaṃ prātaḥ pītaṃ saśarkaraṃ puṃsām
antardāhaṃ śamayetpravṛddhamapi tatkṣaṇādeva || 101
amlapiṣṭaiḥ suśītairvā palāśatarujairdihet
badarīpallavotthena phenenāriṣṭakasya ca || 102
vidārī dāḍimaṃ lodhraṃ dadhitthaṃ bījapūrakam
ebhiḥ pradihyānmūrdhānaṃ tṛḍdāhārtasya dehinaḥ || 103
uttānasuptasya gabhīratāmrakāṃsyādipātraṃ praṇidhāya nābhau
tatrāmbudhārā bahalā patantī nihanti dāhaṃ tvaritaṃ suśītā || 104
vāpyaḥ kamalahāsinyo dalayantragṛhāḥ śubhāḥ
nāryaścandanadigdhāṅgyo dāhadainyaharā matāḥ || 105
harmye śubhrābhrasaṃkāśe śaśāṅkakaraśītale
malayodakasikte vā svapyātpittajvarī naraḥ || 106
jihvātālugalaklomaśoṣe mūrdhni tu dāpayet
kesaraṃ mātuluṅgasya madhusaindhavasaṃyutam || 107
pippalyādikaṣāyaṃ tu pācanaṃ kaphaje jvare
kuṣṭhamindrayavaṃ mūrvā paṭolaṃ cāpi sādhitam
pibenmaricasaṃyuktaṃ sakṣaudraṃ ślaiṣmike jvare || 108
triphalā paṭolavāsā chinnaruhā tiktarohiṇī ca ṣaḍgranthā
madhunā śleṣmasamutthe daśamūlīvāsakasya vā kvāthaḥ || 109
mātrākṣaudraghṛtādīnāṃ kvāthasneheṣu cūrṇavat || 110
āmalakyabhayā kṛṣṇā citrakaścetyayaṃ gaṇaḥ
sarvajvarakaphātaṅkabhedī dīpanapācanaḥ || 111
kaṭphalaṃ pauṣkaraṃ śṛṅgī kṛṣṇā ca madhunā saha
śvāsakāsajvaraharaḥ śreṣṭho lehaḥ kaphāntakṛt || 112
kṣaudropakulyāsaṃyogaḥ kāsaśvāsajvarāpahaḥ
plīhānaṃ hanti hikkāṃ ca bālānāṃ tu praśasyate || 113
karṣaścūrṇasya kalkasya guṭikānāṃ tu sarvaśaḥ
dravaśuktyā'valeḍhavyaḥ pātavyaśca caturdravaḥ || 114
mātrāyā nāstyavasthānaṃ doṣamagniṃ balaṃ vayaḥ
vyādhiṃ dravyaṃ ca koṣṭhaṃ ca vīkṣya mātrāṃ prayojayet || 115
saṃsṛṣṭadoṣeṣu hitaṃ saṃsṛṣṭamatha pācanam
triphalāśālmalīrāsnārājavṛkṣāṭarūṣakaiḥ
śṛtamambu harettūrṇaṃ vātapittodbhavaṃ jvaram || 116
viśvāmṛtābdabhūnimbaiḥ pañcamūlīsāmanvitaiḥ
kṛtaḥ kaṣāyo hantyāśu vātapittodbhavaṃ jvaram || 17
kirātatiktamamṛtāṃ drākṣāmāmalakīṃ śaṭhīm
niṣkvāthya pittānilaje tatkvāthaṃ saguḍaṃ pibet || 118
nidigdhikābalārāsnātrāyamāṇāmṛtāyutaiḥ
masūravidalaiḥ kvātho vātapittajvaraṃ jayet || 119
guḍūcī parpaṭaṃ mustaṃ kirātaṃ viśvabheṣajam
vātapittajvare deyaṃ pañcabhadramidaṃ śubham || 120
kirātaṃ nāgaraṃ mustaṃ guḍūcīṃ ca kaphādhike
pāṭhodīcyamṛṇālaiśca saha pittādhike pibet || 121
kaṇṭakāryamṛtābhārṅgīnāgarendrayavāsakam
bhūnimbaṃ candanaṃ mustaṃ paṭolaṃ kaṭurohiṇī || 122
kaṣāyaṃ pāyayedenaṃ pittaśleṣmajvarāpaham
dāhatṛṣṇājvaracchardikāsahṛtpārśvaśūlanut || 123
guḍūcīnimbadhānyākaṃ candanaṃ padmakāni ca || 124
eṣa sarvajvarānhanti guḍūcyādistu dīpanaḥ
hṛllāsārocakacchardiḥpipāsādāhanāśanaḥ || 125
paṭolaṃ candanaṃ mūrvāpāṭhātiktāmṛtāgaṇaḥ
pittaśleṣmajvaracchardidāhakaṇḍūviṣāpahaḥ || 126
sapatrapuṣpavāsāyā rasaḥ kṣaudrasitāyutaḥ
kaphapittajvaraṃ hanti sāsṛkpittaṃ sakāmalam || 127
saśarkarāmakṣamātrāṃ kaṭukāmuṣṇavāriṇā
pītvā jvaraṃ jayejjantuḥ kaphapittasamudbhavam || 128
paṭolaṃ picumandaṃ ca triphalāṃ madhukaṃ balām
sādhito'yaṃ kaṣāyaḥ syātpittaśleṣmodbhave jvare || 129
dīpanaṃ kaphavicchedi pittavātānulomanam
jvaraghnaṃ pācanaṃ bhedi śṛtaṃ dhānyapaṭolayoḥ || 130
kharparabhṛṣṭapaṭasthitakāñjikasikto hi vālukāsvedaḥ
śamayati vātakaphāmayamastakaśūlāṅgabhaṅgādīn || 131
srotasāṃ mārdavaṃ kṛtvā nītvā pāvakamāśayam
hatvā vātakaphastambhaṃ svedo jvaramapohati || 132
mātuluṅgaphalakesaro dhṛtaḥ sindhujanmamaricānvito mukhe
hanti vātakapharogamāsyagaṃ śoṣamāśujaḍatāmarocakam || 133
pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
dīpanīyaḥ smṛto vargaḥ kaphānilagadāpahaḥ || 134
āragvadhagranthikamustatiktāharītakībhiḥ kvathitaḥ kaṣāyaḥ
sāme saśūle kaphavātayukte jvare hito dīpanapācanaśca || 135
pippalībhiḥ śṛtaṃ toyamanabhiṣyandi dīpanam
vātaśleṣmavikāraghnaṃ plīhaghnaṃ jvaranāśanam || 136
mustaparpaṭadusparśaguḍūcīviśvabheṣajam
kaphavātārucicchardidāhaśoṣajvarāpaham || 137
kṣudrāmṛtānāgarapuṣkarāhvayaiḥ kṛtaḥ kaṣāyaḥ kaphamārutottare
saśvāsakāsārucipārśvarukkare jvare tridoṣaprabhave'pi śasyate || 138
daśamūlīrasaḥ peyaḥ kaṇāyuktaḥ kaphānile
avipāke'tinidrāyāṃ pārśvarukśvāsakāsake || 139
śleṣmanigrahamevā''dau kuryādvyādhau tridoṣaje
niraste śleṣmaṇi hyasya srotaḥsūddhāṭiteṣu ca
lāghavaṃ jāyate sadyastandrā caivopaśāmyati || 140
durge'mbhasi yathā majjadbhājanaṃ tvarayā budhaḥ
gṛhṇīyāttalamaprāptaṃ tathā'bhinyāsapīḍitam || 141
nidropetamabhinyāsaṃ kṣīṇaṃ vidyāddhataujasam || 142
laṅghanaṃ vālukāsvedo nasyaṃ niṣṭhīvanaṃ tathā
avaleho'ñjanaṃ caiva prākprayojyaṃ tridoṣaje || 143
trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā daśarātramathāpi vā
laṅghanaṃ saṃnipāteṣu kuryādārogyadarśanāt || 144
doṣāṇāmeva sā śaktirlaṅghane yā sahiṣṇutā
na tu doṣakṣaye kaścitsahate laṅghanādikam || 145
kaphapitte drave dhātū sahete laṅghanaṃ mahat
āmakṣayādūrdhvamapi vāyurna sahate kṣaṇam || 146
ārdrakasvarasopetaṃ saindhavaṃ kaṭukatrayam
ākaṇṭhaṃ dhārayedāsye niṣṭhīvecca punaḥ punaḥ || 147
tenāsya hṛdgataḥ śleṣmā manyāpārśvaśirogalāt
līno'pyākṛṣyate śuṣko lāghavaṃ cāsya jāyate || 148
parvabhedo jvaro mūrchā nidrākāsagalāmayāḥ
mukhākṣigauravaṃ jāḍyamutkleśaścopaśāmyati || 149
sakṛddvitricatuḥ kuryāddṛṣṭvā doṣabalābalam
etaddhi paramaṃ prāhurbheṣajaṃ saṃnipātinām || 150
mātuluṅgārdrakarasaṃ koṣṇaṃ trilavaṇānvitam
anyadvā siddhivihitaṃ nasyaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ prayojayet || 151
tena prabhidyate śleṣmā prabhinnaśca prasicyate
śirotdṛdayakaṇṭhāsyapārśvarukcopaśāmyati || 152
madhūkasārasindhūtthavacoṣaṇakaṇāḥ samāḥ
ślakṣṇaṃ piṣṭvā'mbhasā nasyaṃ kuryātsaṃjñāprabodhanam || 153
śirīṣabījagomūtrakṛṣṇāmaricasaindhavaiḥ
añjanaṃ syātprabodhāya sarasonaśilālakaiḥ || 154
kaṭphalaṃ pauṣkaraṃ śṛṅgī vyoṣaṃ yāsaśca kāravī
ślakṣṇaṃ cūrṇīkṛtaṃ caitanmadhunā saha lehayet || 155
eṣā'valehikā hanti saṃnipātaṃ sudāruṇam
hikkāṃ śvāsaṃ ca kāsaṃ ca kaṇṭharodhaṃ niyacchati
etadyojyaṃ kaphodreke cūrṇamārdrakajai rasaiḥ || 156
sarveṣu saṃnipāteṣu na kṣaudramavacārayet
śītopacāri kṣaudraṃ syācchītaṃ cātra virudhyate || 157
kriyābhistulyarūpābhiḥ kriyāsāṃkaryamiṣyate
bhinnarūpatayā tāstu tanna kurvanti dūṣaṇam || 158
śastaṃ sulaṅghitasyā''dau vidhāya kavalagraham
lājasaktukapathyaṃ syātsaindhavenāvacūrṇitam || 159
raktapittahitatvena dāhajvarahṛtestathā
saktavaḥ śītavīryāḥ syurlājapūrvā hitā na te || 160
pācano dīpano lājamaṇḍastenoṣṇa iṣyate || 161
ato'yaṃ daśamūlādisādhito bhiṣajāṃ mataḥ
taccejjīryatyavighnena jvarī jīvettadā dhruvam || 162
pañcamuṣṭikayūṣeṇa trikaṇṭakakṛtena vā
ā doṣaśamanātpathyaṃ trikaṇṭenaiva sādhayet || 163
yavakolakulatthānāṃ mudgamūlakaśuṇṭhayoḥ
ekaikaṃ muṣṭimāhṛtya pacedaṣṭaguṇe jale || 164
pañcamuṣṭika ityeṣa vātapittakaphāṣahaḥ
śasyate gulmaśūle ca śvāse kāse kṣaye jvare || 165
pañcamūlī kirātādirgaṇo yojyastridoṣaje
pittotkaṭe ca madhunā kaṇayā vā kaphotkaṭe || 166
bilvasyonākagambhārīpāṭalīgaṇakārikā
dīpanaṃ śleṣmavātaghnaṃ pañcamūlamidaṃ mahat || 167
śāliparṇī pṛthakparṇī bṛhatīdvayagokṣuram
vātapittaharaṃ vṛṣyaṃ kanīyaḥ pañcamūlakam || 168
ubhayaṃ daśamūlaṃ tu saṃnipātajvarāpaham
kāse śvāse ca tandrāyāṃ pārśvaśūle ca śasyate
pippalīcūrṇasaṃyuktaṃ kaṇṭhahṛdgrahanāśanam || 169
cirajvare vātakapholbaṇe vā tridoṣaje vā daśamūlamiśraḥ
kirātatiktādigaṇaḥ prayojyaḥ śuddhyarthine vā trivṛtā vimiśraḥ || 170
daśamūlī śaṭhī śṛṅgī pauṣkaraṃ sadurālabham
bhārgī kuṭajabījaṃ ca paṭolaṃ kaṭurohiṇī || 171
aṣṭādaśāṅga ityeṣa saṃnipātajvarāpahaḥ
kāsahṛdgrahapārśvārtiśvāsahikkāvamīharaḥ || 172
bhūnimbadārudaśamūlamahauṣadhābda-
tiktendrabījadhanikebhakaṇākaṣāyaḥ
tandrāpralāpakasanārucidāhamoha-
śvāsādiyuktamakhilaṃ jvaramāśu hanti || 173
drākṣā'mṛtā śaṭhī śṛṅgī mustakaṃ raktacandanam || 174
nāgaraṃ kaṭukaṃ pāṭhā bhūnimbaṃ sadurālabham
uśīraṃ padmakaṃ dhānyaṃ vālakaṃ kaṇṭakārikā || 175
puṣkaraṃ picumandaṃ cāṣṭādaśāṅgamidaṃ śubham
jīrṇajvarāruciśvāsakāsaśvayathunāśanam || 176
kaṭphalaṃ pauṣkaraṃ śṛṅgī mustakaṃ kaṭukaṃ śaṭhī
samāṃśānyekaśo vā'pi ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet || 177
ārdrakasvarasakṣaudraṃ lihyātkaphavināśanam
śūlānilārucicchardikāsaśvāsakṣayāpaham || 178
mustāparpaṭakośīradevadārumahauṣadham
triphalā dhanvayāsaśca nīlī kampillakaṃ trivṛt || 179
kirātatiktakaṃ pāṭhā balā kaṭukarohiṇī
madhukaṃ pippalīmūlaṃ mustādyo gaṇa ucyate || 180
aṣṭādaśāṅgamuditametadvā saṃnipātanut
pittottare saṃnipāte hitaṃ coktaṃ manīṣibhiḥ
manyāstambha uroghāta uraḥpārśvaśirograhe || 181
bṛhatyau pauṣkaraṃ bhārgī śaṭhī śṛṅgī durālabhā
vatsakasya ca bījāni paṭolaṃ kaṭurohiṇī || 182
bṛhatyādirgaṇaḥ proktaḥ saṃnipātajvarāpahaḥ
kāsādiṣu ca sarveṣu hitaḥ sopadraveṣu ca || 183
śaṭhī puṣkaramūlaṃ ca vyāghrī śṛṅgī durālabhā
guḍūcī nāgaraṃ pāṭhā kirātaṃ kaṭurohiṇī || 184
eṣa śaṭhyādiko vargaḥ saṃnipātajvarāpahaḥ
kāsahṛdgrahapārśvārtiśvāse tandryāṃ ca śasyate || 185
svedodgame bhṛṣṭakulatthacūrṇanipātanaṃ śastamiti bruvanti || 186
mṛtyuśca tasminbahupicchilatvācchītasya jantoḥ paritaḥ saratvāt
svedo lalāṭe himavānnarasya śītārditasyaiti supicchilaśca
kaṇṭhasthito yasya na yāti vakṣo nūnaṃ yamasyaiti gṛhaṃ sa martyaḥ || 187
trivṛdviśālākaṭukātriphalāragvadhaiḥ kṛtaḥ
sakṣāro bhedanaḥ kvāthaḥ peyaḥ sarvajvarāpahaḥ || 188
pittakaphānilavṛddhyā daśadivasadvādaśāhasaptāhāt
hanti vimuñcati puruṣaṃ tridoṣajo dhātumalapākāt || 189
saptamī dviguṇā yā tu navamyekādaśī tathā
eṣā tridoṣamaryādā mokṣāya ca vadhāya ca || 190
saṃnipāte prakampantaṃ vilapantaṃ ca yo naram
bhojayetpiśitaiḥ sārdhaṃ sa vaidyākhyāṃ vrajetkatham || 191
saṃnipāte prakampantaṃ vilapantaṃ ca yo ghṛtam
bhojayetpāyayeccāpi tau syātāṃ ca kathaṃ śubhau || 192
saṃnipāteṣu dāhārtaṃ yaḥ siñcecchītavāriṇā
āturaḥ sa kathaṃ jīvedbhiṣagvā sa kathaṃ bhavet || 193
saṃnipātārṇave magnaṃ yo'bhyuddharati mānavam
kastena na kṛto dharmaḥ kāṃ tu pūjā na so'rhati || 194
mṛtyunā saha yoddhavyaṃ saṃnipātaṃ cikitsatā
yaśca tatra bhavejjetā sa jetā''mayasaṃkare || 195
saṃnipātāntakarṇasya śophe mahati dāruṇe || 196
raktāvasecanaiḥ pūrvaṃ sarpiṣpānaiśca taṃ jayet
pradehaiḥ kaphapittaghnairvamanaiḥ kavalagrahaiḥ || 197
bījapūrakamūlāni agnimanthastathaiva ca
sanāgaraṃ devadāru rāsnācitrakapeṣitam || 198
pralepanamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ galaśothavināśanam
kulatthaṃ kaṭphalaṃ śuṇṭhī kāravī ca samāṃśakaiḥ
sukhoṣṇairlepanaṃ kāryaṃ karṇamūle muhurmuhuḥ || 199
abhicārābhiśāpotthau jvarau homādinā jayet
dānasvastyayanātithyairutpātagrahapīḍajau || 200
oṣadhīgandhaviṣajau viṣapittaprabādhanaiḥ
jayetkaṣāyairmatimānsarvagandhakṛtairbhiṣak || 201
krodhaje pittajitkāmyā arthāḥ sadvākyameva ca || 202
aśvāsaneṣṭalābhena vāyoḥ praśamanena ca
harṣaṇaiśca śamaṃ yānti kāmaśokabhayajvarāḥ || 203
bhūtavidyāsamuddiṣṭairbandhāveśanapūjanaiḥ
jayedbhūtābhiṣaṅgotthaṃ manaḥsāntvaiśca mānasam || 204
nidigdhikānāgarakāmṛtānāṃ kvāthaṃ pibenmiśritapippalīkam
jīrṇajvarārocakakāsaśūlaśvāsāgnimāndyārditapīnaseṣu || 205
hantyūrdhvajānayaṃ prāyaḥ sāyaṃ tenopayujyate
kāsājīrṇāruciśvāsahṛtpāṇḍukṛmiroganut
jīrṇajvare'gnimāndye ca śasyate guḍapippalī || 206
pippalīcūrṇasaṃyuktaḥ kvāthaśchinnodbhavodbhavaḥ
jīrṇajvarakaphadhvaṃsī pañcamūlīkṛto'thavā || 207
śirogauravaśūlaghnamindriyapratibodhanam
jīrṇajvare rucikaraṃ dadyācchīrṣavirecanam || 208
saṃtataṃ viṣamaṃ vā'pi kṣīṇasya sucirotthitam
jvaraṃ saṃbhojanaiḥ pathyairjvaraghnaiḥ samupācaret || 209
taṇḍulīyakavāstūkabālamūlakaparpaṭān || 210
paṭolamamṛtāpatraṃ śākārthaṃ dārvipatrikām
māṃsārthameṇalāvādīndadyādyuktyā vicakṣaṇaḥ || 211
jvarakṣīṇasya na hitaṃ vamanaṃ na virecanam
kāmaṃ tu payasā tasya nirūhairvā harenmalān || 212
arocake gātrasāde vaivarṇye'ṅgamalādiṣu
śāntajvaro'pi śodhyaḥ syādanubandhabhayānnaraḥ || 213
jīrṇajvare kaphe kṣīṇe kṣīraṃ syādamṛtopamam
tadeva taruṇe pītaṃ viṣavaddhanti mānavam || 214
kāsācchvāsācchiraḥśūlātpārśvaśūlātsapīnasāt
mucyate jvaritaḥ pītvā pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ payaḥ || 215
trikaṇṭakavacāvyāghrīguḍanāgarasādhitam
varcomūtravibandhaghnaṃ śoṣajvaraharaṃ payaḥ || 216
dravyādaṣṭaguṇaṃ kṣīraṃ kṣīrāttoyaṃ caturguṇam
kṣīrāvaśeṣaḥ kartavyaḥ kṣīrapāke tvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 217
pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ catugurṇajalena vā
śiṃśapāgaṇḍikābhirvā dhāroṣṇaṃ vā jvarāpaham || 218
kṛśānāṃ jvaramuktānāṃ mithyāhāravihāriṇām
doṣaḥ svalpo'pi saṃvṛddho dehināmanileritaḥ || 219
satatānyedyuṣkatryākhyacaturthānsapralepakān
kaphasthānavibhāgena yathāsaṃkhyaṃ karoti hi || 220
ahorātrādahorātrātsthānātsthānaṃ prapadyate
tataścā''māśayaṃ prāpya doṣaḥ kuryājjvaraṃ nṛṇām || 221
samau vātakaphau yasya hīnapittasya dehinaḥ
prāyo rātrau jvarastasya divā hīnakaphasya ca || 222
pralepakastvaviṣamaḥ prāyaḥ kleśāya śoṣiṇām
kaliṅgakāḥ paṭolasya patraṃ ca kaṭurohiṇī || 223
paṭolaṃ sārivā mustaṃ pāṭhā kaṭukarohiṇī
nimbaṃ paṭolaṃ triphalā mṛdvīkāmustavatsakaiḥ || 224
kirātatiktamamṛtā candanaṃ viśvabheṣajam
guḍūcyāmalakaṃ mustamardhaślokasamāpanāḥ
kaṣāyāḥ śamayantyāśu pañca pañcavidhaṃ jvaram || 225
kaṣāyāḥ saṃtatādīnāṃ pañcaite carakādinā
pralepakaṃ vihāyaiva tathā vātabalāsakam || 226
gadyam--kaṣāyāḥ krameṇa vyatikrameṇa vā kriyante || 227
ajājī guḍasaṃyuktā viṣamajvaranāśinī
agnimāndyaṃ jayetsamyagvātarogāṃśca nāśayet || 228
guḍapragāḍhāṃ triphalāṃ pibedvā viṣamārditaḥ
guḍūcīnimbadhātrīṇāṃ kaṣāyaṃ vā samākṣikam || 229
mustāmalakaguḍūcīviśvauṣadhakaṇṭakārikākvāthaḥ
pītaḥ sakaṇācūrṇaḥ samadhurviṣamajvaraṃ hanti || 230
prātaḥ prātaḥ sasarpirvā rasonamupakalpayet
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā pibetkṣīrarasāśanaḥ || 231
dinamekaṃ tu viśramya pratyeti sa tṛtīyakaḥ
dinadvayaṃ tu viśramya pratyeti sa caturthakaḥ || 232
karma sādhāraṇaṃ jahyāttṛtīyakacaturthakau
atastau pratikartavyau viśeṣoditabheṣajaiḥ || 233
mahauṣadhāmṛtāmustacandanośīradhānyakaiḥ
kvāthastṛtīyakaṃ hanti śarkarāmadhuyojitaḥ || 234
apāmārgajaṭā kaṭyāṃ lohitaiḥ saptatantubhiḥ
baddhā vāre ravestūrṇaṃ jvaraṃ hanti tṛtīyakam || 235
gaṅgāyā uttare kūle aputrastāpaso mṛtaḥ
tasmai tilodakaṃ dadyānmuñcatyaikāhiko jvaraḥ || 236
vāsādhātrīsthirādārupathyānāgarasādhitaḥ
sitāmadhuyutaḥ kvāthaścāturthikanivāraṇaḥ || 237
agastipatrasvarasena nasyaṃ nihanti cāturthikamugrarūpam
kaphasthāneṣu vā tiṣṭhandoṣo dvitricaturṣu vā
viparyayākhyānkurute viṣamānkaṣṭasādhanān || 238
rasonakalkaṃ tilatailayuktaṃ yo'śnāti nityaṃ viṣamajvarārtaḥ
vimucyate so'pyacirājjvareṇa vātāmayaiścāpi sughorarūpaiḥ || 239
raktāśvamārapuṣpaṃ ca dhātrī dhānyaṃ vacāmayam
piṣṭvā koṣṇo jvare kāryo lepo mūrdharujāpahaḥ || 240
palaṃkaṣā nimbapatraṃ vacā kuṣṭhaṃ harītakī
sarṣapāḥ sayavāḥ sarpirdhūpanaṃ jvaranāśanam || 241
baiḍālaṃ vā śakṛdyojyaṃ vepamānasya dhūpane || 242
somaṃ sānucaraṃ devaṃ samātṛgaṇamīśvaram
pūjayanprayataḥ śīghraṃ mucyate viṣamajvarāt || 243
viṣṇuṃ sahasramūrdhānaṃ carācarapatiṃ vibhum
stuvannāmasahasreṇa jvarānsarvānvyapohati || 244
mūlaṃ bhūmijayantyāśca sahadevodbhavaṃ tathā
taṇḍulīyakamūlaṃ vā baddhaṃ mūrdhni jvarāpaham || 245
viṣamajvareṣu kartavyamūrdhvaṃ cādhaśca śodhanam
śāntiṃ nayettrivṛdvā'pi sakṣaudrā viṣamajvaram || 246
madhunā vā'bhayā līḍhā hantyāśu viṣamajvaram
jvaravegaṃ ca kālaṃ ca cintayañjvaryate tu yaḥ
tasyeṣṭairadbhutairvā'pi viṣayairnāśayetsmṛtim || 247
ghṛtaṃ dvādaśarātrāttu deyaṃ kṣīṇakaphe jvare || 248
pippalyaścandanaṃ mustamuśīraṃ kaṭurohiṇī
kaliṅgakāstāmalakī sārivā'tiviṣā sthirā || 249
drākṣāmalakabilvāni trāyamāṇā nidigdhikā
siddhamebhirghṛtaṃ sadyo jvaraṃ jīrṇaṃ vyapohati || 250
kṣayaṃ kāsaṃ śiraḥśūlaṃ pārśvaśūlamarocakam
aṃsābhitāpamagniṃ ca viṣamaṃ saṃniyacchati || 251
kalkāccaturguṇaḥ snehaḥ snehātkvāthaścaturguṇaḥ
kvāthyāccaturguṇaṃ vāri kvāthaḥ kvāthyasamo mataḥ || 252
daśamūlīrasaiḥ sarpiḥ sakṣāraiḥ pañcakolakaiḥ
sakṣīraṃ hanti tatsiddhaṃ jvarakāsāgnimandatām
vātapittakaphavyādhīnplīhānaṃ vā'pi pāṇḍutām || 253
pañcakolaiḥ sasindhūtthaiḥ palikaiḥ payasā samam
sarpiḥ prasthaṃ śṛtaṃ plīhaviṣamajvaragulmanut || 254
viśālā triphalā kauntī devadārvelabālukam || 255
sthirā nataṃ haridre dve sārive dve priyaṅgukā
nīlotpalailāmañjiṣṭhādantīdāḍimakesaram || 256
viḍaṅgaṃ pṛśniparṇī ca kuṣṭhacandanapadmakaiḥ
tālīsapatraṃ bṛhatī mālatyāḥ kusumaṃ navam || 257
aṣṭāviṃśatibhiḥ kalkairetairakṣasamanvitaiḥ
caturguṇaṃ jalaṃ dattvā ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 258
apasmāre jvare kāse śoṣe mande'nale kṣaye
vātarakte pratiśyāye tṛtīyakacaturthake || 259
chardyarśomūtrakṛcchre ca visarpopadraveṣu ca
kaṇḍūpāṇḍvāmayonmādaviṣamehagareṣu ca || 260
bhūtopahatacittānāṃ gadgadānāmaretasām
śastaṃ strīṇāṃ ca vandhyānāmāyurvarṇabalapradam || 261
alakṣmīpāparakṣoghnaṃ sarvagrahanivāraṇam
kalyāṇakamidaṃ sarpiḥ śreṣṭhaṃ puṃsavaneṣu ca || 262
suvarcikānāgarakuṣṭhamūrvālākṣāniśālohitayaṣṭikābhiḥ
tailaṃ jvare ṣaḍguṇatakrasiddhamabhyañjanācchītavidāhanutsyāt || 263
dadhnaḥ sasārakasyātra takraṃ kaṭvaramiṣyate
ghṛtatailaguḍādīṃśca ekāhānnaiva sādhayet
uṣitāstu prakurvanti viśeṣeṇa guṇānyataḥ || 264
snehakalko yadā'ṅgulyā vartito vartivadbhavet
vahnau kṣipte ca no śabdastadā siddhiṃ vinirdiśet || 265
śabdavyuparame prāpte phenasyoparame tathā
gandhavarṇarasādīnāṃ saṃpattau siddhimādiśet || 266
ghṛtasyaivaṃ vipakvasya jānīyātkuśalo bhiṣak
pheno'timātraṃ tailasya śeṣaṃ ghṛtavadādiśet || 267
mukunda govinda hare murāre vaikuṇṭha śaure kuru me'nukampām
lokatrayatrāṇavidhānadakṣa kṣamasva doṣaṃ druhiṇādya vaidya || 268
candanādyaṃ hitaṃ tailaṃ śoṣādhikārakīrtitam
tathā nārāyaṇaṃ tailaṃ jīrṇajvaraharaṃ param || 269
mūrvālākṣāharidre dve mañjiṣṭhā sendravāruṇī
bṛhatī saindhavaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ rāsnā māṃsī śatāvarī || 270
āranālāḍhakenaivaṃ tailaṃ prasthaṃ vipācayet
tailamaṅgārakaṃ nāma sarvajvaravimokṣaṇam || 271
abdādūrdhvaṃ ghṛtaṃ pakvaṃ hīnavīryaṃ ca tadbhavet
taile viparyayaṃ vidyātpakve cāpakva eva ca || 272
deho laghurvyapagataklamamohatāpaḥ pāko mukhe karaṇasauṣṭhavamavyathatvam
svedaḥ kṣavaḥ prakṛtiyogimano'nnalipsā kaṇḍūśca mūrdhni vigatajvaralakṣaṇāni || 273
vyāyāmaṃ ca vyavāyaṃ ca snānaṃ caṃkramaṇāni ca
jvaramukto na seveta yāvanna balavānbhavet || 274
Adhikāra 2
pṛthaguktanidānena jvarātīsāranirṇayaḥ
kartavyo bhiṣajā cātra tatkrameṇa vidhiryataḥ || 1
jvarātisārayoruktaṃ bheṣajaṃ yatpṛthakpṛthak
na tanmilitayoḥ kāryamanyonyaṃ vardhayedyataḥ || 2
atastau pratikurvīta viśeṣoditabheṣajaiḥ
laṅghanamubhayoruktaṃ milite kāryaṃ viśeṣatastadanu
utpalaṣaṣṭhakasiddhaṃ lājāmaṇḍādikaṃ sakalam || 3
pṛśniparṇībalābilvanāgarotpaladhānyakaiḥ
jvarātisārī peyāṃ vā pibetsāmlāṃ śṛtāṃ naraḥ || 4
nāgarātiviṣāmustābhūnimbāmṛtavatsakaiḥ
sarvajvaraharaḥ kvāthaḥ sarvātīsāranāśanaḥ || 5
pāṭhendrayavabhūnimbamustāparpaṭakāmṛtāḥ
jayantyāmamatīsāraṃ jvaraṃ ca samahauṣadhāḥ || 6
hrīverātiviṣāmustābilvanāgaradhānyakam || 7
pibetpicchāvibandhaghnaṃ śūladoṣāmapācanam
saraktaṃ hantyatīsāraṃ sajvaraṃ vā'tha vijvaram || 8
guḍūcyativiṣādhānyaśuṇṭhībilvābdavālakaiḥ
pāṭhābhūnimbakuṭajaiścandanośīrapadmakaiḥ || 9
kaṣāyaḥ śītalaḥ peyo jvarātīsāraśāntaye
hṛllāsārocakacchardiḥpipāsādāhanāśanaḥ || 10
kaliṅgātiviṣāśuṇṭhīkirātāmbuyavāsakam
jvarātīsārasaṃtāpaṃ nāśayedavikalpataḥ || 11
vatsakasya phalaṃ dāru rohiṇī gajapippalī
śvadaṃṣṭrā pippalī dhānyaṃ bilvaṃ pāṭhā yavānikā || 12
dvāvapyetau siddhayogau ślokārdhenābhibhāṣitau
jvarātisāraśamanau viśeṣāddāhanāśanau || 13
pañcamūlībalāvilbaguḍūcīmustanāgaraiḥ
pāṭhābhūnimbahrīverakuṭajatvakpalaiḥ śṛtam || 14
hanti sarvānatīsārāñjvaradoṣaṃ vamiṃ tathā
saśūlopadravaṃ śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ hanyātsudustaram || 15
pañcamūlīti sāmānyādyojyā pitte kanīyasī
mahatī pañcamūlī tu vātaśleṣmādhike hitā || 16
utpalaṃ dāḍimatvakca padmakesarameva ca
pibettaṇḍulatoyena jvarātīsāranāśanam || 17
uśīraṃ vālakaṃ mustaṃ dhānyakaṃ bilvameva ca
samaṅgā dhātakī rodhraṃ viśvaṃ pācanadīpanam || 18
hantyarocakapicchāmaṃ vibandhaṃ sātivedanam
saśoṇitamatīsāraṃ sajvaraṃ vā'tha vijvaram || 19
daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa viśvamakṣasamaṃ pibet
jvare caivātisāre ca saśophe grahaṇīgade || 20
viḍaṅgā'tiviṣā mustaṃ dāru pāṭhā kaliṅgakam
maricena samāyuktaṃ śophātīsāranāśanam || 21
guḍūcīcitrabilvābdaraktacandanavālakaiḥ
kaliṅgaikasamāyuktairjvarātīsāraśophanut || 22
kirātābdāmṛtānimbacandanodīcyavatsakaiḥ
śoṣātīsāraśamanaṃ viśeṣājjvaranāśanam || 23
sitāñjanakaṇālājamadhukaṃ nīlamutpalam
pittajvarātisāraghnaṃ pralīḍhaṃ madhunā saha || 24
Adhikāra 3
laṅghanamekaṃ muktvā na cānyadastīha bheṣajaṃ balinaḥ
samudīrṇaṃ doṣanicayaṃ śamayati tatpācayatyapi ca || 1
dhānyodīcyaśṛtaṃ toyaṃ tṛṣṇādāhātisāriṇe
hrīveraśṛṅgaverābhyāṃ mustaparpaṭakena vā
mustodīcyaśṛtaṃ toyaṃ deyaṃ cātipipāsave || 2
nāgarātiviṣāmustaiḥ śṛtaṃ vā dhānyanāgaraiḥ
tṛṣṇāśūlātisāraghnaṃ rocanaṃ dīpanaṃ param || 3
hrīveraśṛṅgaverābhyāṃ mustaparpaṭakena vā
ābhyāmeva sapāṭhābhyāṃ siddhamāhāramācaret || 4
śāliparṇyādikādyaistu sādhitena sasindhujam
masūrajena yūṣeṇa pathyaṃ syāllājasaktubhiḥ || 5
gurvī piṇḍī kharā'tyarthaṃ laghvī saiva viparyayāt
saktūnāmāśu jīryeta mṛdutvādavalehikā || 6
lājamaṇḍakṛtairyogaistaiḥ kṛtā hastamaṇḍikā || 7
vastrasrutā yavāgūrvā prasrutaṃ kṣudrabhaktakam
suniṣaṇṇakavārtākakañcaṭaṃ hitamucyate || 8
śāliparṇī pṛśniparṇī bṛhatī kaṇṭakārikā
balā'śvadaṃṣṭrā bilvāni pāṭhā nāgaradhānyakam
etadāhārasaṃyoge hitaṃ sarvātisāriṇām || 9
śākamāṃsaphalairyuktā vilepyo'mlāśca durjarāḥ || 10
śāliparṇībalābilvaiḥ pṛthakparṇyā ca sādhitā
dāḍimā'mlā hitā peyā pittaśleṣmātisāriṇām || 11
dhānyapañcakasiddho vā dhānyaviśvakṛto'pi vā
āhāro bhiṣajā yojyo vātaśleṣmātisāriṇām || 12
laghunā pañcamūlena vātapitte praśasyate
āmapakvakramaṃ hitvā nātisāre kriyā yataḥ
ataḥ sarve'tisārāstu jñeyāḥ pakvāmalakṣaṇaiḥ || 13
majjatyāmā gurutvādviṭ pakvā tūtplavate jale
vinā'tidravasaṃghātaśaityaśleṣmapradūṣaṇāt || 14
śakṛddurgandhi sāṭopaviṣṭambhārtiprasekinaḥ
viparitaṃ nirāmaṃ tu kaphātpakvaṃ ca majjati || 15
nā''me saṃgrahaṇaṃ dadyādatīsāre kathaṃcana
akāle saṃgṛhīto hi vikārānkurute bahūn || 16
śothapāṇḍvāmayaplīhakuṣṭhagulmodarajvarān
daṇḍakālasakādhmānagrahaṇyarśogadāṃstathā || 17
kṣīṇadhātubalārtasya bahudoṣo'tiniḥsrutaḥ
āmo'pi stambhanīyaḥ syātpācanānmaraṇaṃ vrajet || 18
stokaṃ stokaṃ vibaddhaṃ vā saśūlaṃ yo'tisāryate
abhayāpippalīkalkaiḥ sukhoṣṇaistaṃ virecayet || 19
haridrādiṃ vacādiṃ vā pibedāmeṣu buddhimān
khaḍayūṣayavāgūṣu pippalyādiṃ ca yojayet || 20
dhānyanāgaramustaṃ ca vālakaṃ bilvameva ca
āmaśūlavibandhaghnaṃ pācanaṃ vahnidīpanam || 21
paitte dhānyacatuṣkaṃ tu śuṇṭhītyāgādvadanti hi || 22
pakvo'sakṛdatīsāro grahaṇīmārdavādyadā
pravartate tadā kāryaḥ kṣipraṃ sāṃgrāhiko vidhiḥ || 23
salodhradhātakī bilvaṃ mustāmrāsthikaliṅgakam
pibenmāhiṣatakreṇa pakvātīsāranāśanam || 1
pañcamūlībalāviśvadhānyakotpalabilvajāḥ
vātātisāriṇe deyāstakreṇānyatamena vā || 24
kaṃcaṭajambūdāḍimaśṛṅgāṭakapatrabilvahrīveram
jaladharanāgarasahitaṃ gaṅgāmapi veginīṃ rundhyāt || 25
kirātatiktakaṃ mustaṃ vatsakaṃ sārasāñjanam
pibetpittātisāraghnaṃ sakṣaudraṃ vedanāpaham || 26
palaṃ vatsakabījasya śrapayitvā jalaṃ pibet
yo rasāśī jayecchīghraṃ sa paittaṃ jaṭharāmayam || 27
kuṭajātiviṣāmustaṃ haridrāparṇinīdvayam
sakṣaudraśarkaraṃ śastaṃ pittaśleṣmātisāriṇām || 28
kuṭajatvakphalaṃ mustaṃ kvāthayitvā pibejjalam
atīsāraṃ jayedāśu śarkarāmadhuyojitam || 29
bilvacūtāsthiniryūhaḥ patiḥ sakṣaudraśarkaraḥ
nihanyācchardyatīsāraṃ vaiśvānara ivā''hutim || 30
priyaṅgvañjanamustākhyaṃ pāyayettu yathābalam
tṛṣṇātīsāracchardighnaṃ sakṣaudraṃ taṇḍulāmbunā || 31
paṭolayavadhānyākakvāthaḥ peyaḥ suśītalaḥ
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaśchardyatīsāranāśanaḥ || 32
pṛśniparṇībalābilvanāgarotpaladhānyakaiḥ
viḍaṅgātiviṣāmustadārupāṭhākaliṅgakaiḥ
maricena samāyuktaṃ śothātīsāranāśanam || 1
śleṣmātisāre prathamaṃ kāryaṃ laṅghanapācanam
yojyaścāmātisāraghno yathokto dīpano gaṇaḥ || 33
tryūṣaṇātiviṣāhiṅguvacāsauvarcalābhayāḥ
pītvoṣṇenāmbunā jahyādāmātīsāramuddhatam || 34
dalotthaḥ svarasaḥ pīto vijjalasya samākṣikaḥ
jayatyāmamatīsāraṃ kvātho vā kuṭajatvacaḥ || 35
kuṭajatvakkṛtaḥ kvātho ghanībhūtaḥ suśobhanaḥ
lehito'tiviṣāyuktaḥ sarvātīsāranudbhavet || 36
lehe yatrāsti no bhāgo nirdiṣṭo dravakalkayoḥ
prāyeṇa pādikaḥ kalko dravātkāryo vijānatā
icchantyatrāṣṭamāṃśena kvāthādativiṣārajaḥ || 37
tadvatkārpāsaparkaṭyoḥ svarasaḥ samadhurmataḥ || 38
kaṣāyo madhunā pītastvaco dāḍimavatsakāt
sadyo jayedatīsāraṃ raktajaṃ durnivārakam || 39
guḍena khādayedbilvaṃ raktātīsāranāśanam
āmaśūlavibandhaghnaṃ kukṣirogavināśanam || 40
śallakībadarījambupriyālāmrārjunatvacaḥ
pītāḥ kṣīreṇa madhvāḍhyāḥ pṛthakśoṇitavāraṇāḥ || 41
pītvā śatāvarīkalkaṃ payasā kṣīrabhugjayet
raktātisāraṃ pītvā vā tayā siddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ naraḥ || 42
savatsakaḥ sātiviṣaḥ sabilvaḥ sodīcyamustaśca kṛtaḥ kaṣāyaḥ
sāme saśūle sahaśoṇite ca cirapravṛtte'pi hito'tisāre || 43
bhayaśokasamudbhūte kriyā cobhayaśarmadā
sarvaje bahuvarṇatvātpuṭapākakriyā matā || 44
avedanaṃ susaṃpakvaṃ dīptāgneḥ sucirotthitam
nānāvarṇamatīsāraṃ puṭapākairupācaret || 45
snigdhaṃ ghanaṃ kuṭajavalkamajantujagdhamādāya tatkṣaṇamatīva ca pothayitvā
jambūpalāśapuṭataṇḍulatoyasiktaṃ baddhaṃ kuśena ca bahirghanapaṅkaliptam || 46
susvinnametadavapīḍya rasaṃ gṛhītvā kṣaudreṇa yuktamatisāravate pradadyāt
kṛṣṇātriputramatapūjita eṣa yogaḥ sarvātisāraharaṇeṣvayameva rājā || 47
bheṣajatvātpalaṃ peyaṃ puṭapākarasasya ca || 48
tvakpiṇḍaṃ dīrghavṛntasya kāśmarīparṇaveṣṭitam
mṛdā'valiptaṃ sukṛtamaṅgāreṣvavakūlayet || 49
svinnamuddhṛtya niṣpīḍya rasamādāya yatnataḥ
śītīkṛtaṃ madhuyutaṃ pāyayedudarāmaye || 50
puṭapākasya pāko'yaṃ bahirāraktavarṇatā
bheṣajatvātpalaṃ mānaṃ saṃmataṃ svarasādivat || 51
śataṃ kuṭajamūlasya kṣuṇṇaṃ toyārmaṇe pacet
kvāthe pādāvaśeṣe'smiṃṃllehe pūte punaḥ pacet
sauvarcalayavakṣāraviḍasaindhavapippalīḥ || 52
dhātakīndrayavājājīcūrṇaṃ dattvā paladvayam
lihyādbadaramātraṃ tu śītaṃ kṣaudreṇa saṃplutam || 53
pakvāpakvamatīsāraṃ nānāvarṇaṃ savedanam
durvāraṃ grahaṇīrogaṃ jayeccaiva pravāhikām || 54
tulāmathā''rdrāṃ girimallikāyāḥ saṃkṣudya paktvā rasamādadīta
tasminsupūte palasaṃmitāni ślakṣṇāni piṣṭvā saha śālmalena || 55
pāṭhāsamaṅgātiviṣāḥ samustā bilvaṃ ca puṣpāṇi ca dhātakīnām
prakṣipya bhūyo vipacecca tāvaddarvīpralepaḥ svarasastu yāvat || 56
pītastvasau kālavidā jalena maṇḍena vā'jāpayasā'thavā'pi
nihanti sarvaṃ tvatisāramugraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ sitaṃ lohitapītakaṃ vā || 57
doṣaṃ grahaṇyā vividhaṃ ca raktaṃ pittaṃ tathā'rśāṃsi sa śoṇitāni
asṛgdaraṃ caivamasādhyarūpaṃ nihantyavaśyaṃ kuṭajāṣṭako'yam || 58
tulādravye jaladroṇo droṇe dravyatulā matā || 59
kuṭajasya palaṃ grāhyamaṣṭabhāge jale śṛtam
tathaiva vipacedbhayo dāḍimodakasaṃyutam
yāvacca lasikābhāsaṃ śṛtaṃ tamupakalpayet || 60
tasyārdhakarṣaṃ takreṇa pibedraktātisāravān
avaśyamaraṇīyo'pi na mṛtyoryāti gocaram
kuṭajakvāthatulyo'tra dāḍimasya raso mataḥ || 61
vatsakasya ca bījāni dārvyāśca tvaca uttamāḥ
pippalīśṛṅgaveraṃ ca lākṣā kaṭukarohiṇī || 62
ṣaḍbhiretairghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ peyāmaṇḍāvacāritam
atīsāraṃ jayecchīghraṃ tridoṣamapi dāruṇam || 63
yathā'mṛtaṃ tathā kṣīramatīsāreṣu pūjitam
cirotthiteṣu tatpeyamapāṃ bhāgaistribhiḥ śṛtam || 64
srute rakte purīṣe ca vāyunā viḍvivarjitam
pravāhiketi tatkhyātaṃ yatphenābhaṃ pravartate || 65
bālabilvaṃ guḍaṃ tailaṃ pippalī viśvabheṣajam
lihyādvāte pratihate saśūle sapravāhike || 66
payasā pippalīkalkaḥ pīto vā maricodbhavaḥ
tryahānnirvāhikāṃ hanyāccirakālasamutthitām || 67
bilvapeśīṃ guḍaṃ rodhraṃ tailaṃ maricayojitam
līḍhvā pravāhikāṃ hanti kṣipraṃ sukhamavāpnuyāt || 68
dadhnā sasāreṇa samākṣikeṇa bhuñjīta niścārakapīḍitastu
sutaptakupyakvathitena vā'pi kṣīreṇa śītena madhuplutena || 69
gudadāhe prapāke vā paṭolamadhukāmbunā
sekādikaṃ praśaṃsanti cchāgena payasā'thavā || 70
svedo vā mūṣikāmāṃsairgovasāmrakṣaṇaṃ tathā || 71
gudaniḥsaraṇe śastaṃ cāṅgerīghṛtamuttamam
śothādīnavirodhācca sādhayetsvaiścikitsitaiḥ || 72
śothaṃ śūlaṃ jvaraṃ tṛṣṇāṃ śvāsaṃ kāsamarocakam
chardi mūrchāṃ ca hikkāṃ ca dṛṣṭvā'tīsāriṇaṃ tyajet || 73
snānābhyaṅgāvagāhaṃ ca gurusnigdhādibhojanam
vyāyāmamagnisaṃtāpamatīsārī vivarjayet || 74
Adhikāra 4
grahaṇīmāśritaṃ doṣamajīrṇavadupācaret
atīsāroktavidhinā tasyā''maṃ ca vipācayet || 1
kapitthabilvacāṅgerītakradāḍimasādhitā
yavāgūḥ pācayatyāmaṃ śakṛtsaṃvartayatyapi || 2
laghunā pañcamūlena pañcakolena pāṭhayā
annāni kalpayedvidvānbilvavṛkṣāmladāḍimaiḥ || 3
grahaṇīdoṣiṇāṃ takraṃ dīpanaṃ grāhi lāghavāt
pathyaṃ madhurapākitvānna ca pittaprakopaṇam || 4
kaṣāyoṣṇavikāṣitvādrūkṣatvācca kaphe hitam
vāte svādvamlasāndratvātsadyaskamavidāhi tat || 5
śuṭhīṃ samustāṃ saviṣāṃ guḍūcīṃ pibejjalena kvathitāṃ samāṃśām
mandānalatve satatāmatāyāmāme'nubandhe grahaṇīgade ca || 6
dhānyakātiviṣodīcyayavānīmustanāgaram
balādviparṇībilvaṃ ca dadyāddīpanapācanam || 7
citrakaṃ pippalīmūlaṃ dvau kṣārau lavaṇāni ca
vyoṣaṃ hiṅgvajamodāṃ ca cavyaṃ caikatra cūrṇayet || 8
guṭikā mātuluṅgasya dāḍimasya rasena vā
kṛtā vipācayatyāmaṃ dīpayatyāśu cānalam || 9
śrīphalaśalāṭukalko nāgaracūrṇena miśritaḥ saguḍaḥ
grahaṇīgadamatyugraṃ takrabhujā śīlito jayati || 10
jambūdāḍimaśṛṅgāṭapāṭhākaṃcaṭapallavaiḥ
pakvaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bālabilvaṃ saguḍanāgaram
hanti sarvānatīsārāngrahaṇīmatidustarām || 11
nāgarātiviṣāmustākvāthaḥ syādāmapācanaḥ
cūrṇaṃ hiṅgvaṣṭakaṃ vā'pi vātike'ṣṭapalaṃ ghṛtam || 12
nāgarātiviṣāmustaṃ dhātakī sarasāñjanam
vatsakatvakphalaṃ bilvaṃ pāṭhāṃ kaṭukarohiṇīm || 13
pibetsamāṃśaṃ taccūrṇaṃ sakṣaudraṃ taṇḍulāmbunā
paittike grahaṇīdoṣe raktaṃ yaccopaveśyate || 14
arśāṃsyatha gude śūlaṃ jayeccaiva pravāhikām
nāgarādyamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṛṣṇātreyeṇa pūjitam || 15
jalamaṣṭaguṇaṃ dattvā palaṃ kaṇḍitataṇḍulāt
bhāvayitvā tato deyaṃ taṇḍulodakakarmaṇi || 16
bhūnimbakaṭukāvyoṣamustakendrayavānsamān
dvau citrakādvatsakatvagbhāgānṣoḍaśa cūrṇayet || 17
guḍaśītāmbunā pītaṃ grahaṇīdoṣagulmanut
kāmalājvarapāṇḍutvamehārucyatisārajit || 18
grahaṇyāṃ śleṣmaduṣṭāyāṃ vamitasya yathāvidhi
kaṭvamlalavaṇakṣāraistiktaiścāgniṃ vivardhayet || 19
sarvajāyāṃ grahaṇyāṃ tu sāmānyo vidhiriṣyate || 20
masūrasya kaṣāyeṇa bilvagarbhaṃ paceddhṛtam
hanti kukṣyāmayānsarvāngrahaṇīpāṇḍukāmalāḥ
vrīhiprāṇyaṅgayoḥ kvāthaṃ vyuṣitaṃ doṣalaṃ viduḥ || 21
masūrasya tulākvāthe bilvagarbhaṃ pacedghṛtam
bhinnaviṭke praśaṃsanti masūraghṛtamuttamam || 22
tryūṣaṇatriphalākalke bilvamātre guḍātpale
sarpiraṣṭapalaṃ paktvā mātrāṃ mandānalaḥ pibet || 23
bilvāgnicavyārdrakaśṛṅgaverakvāthena kalkena ca siddhamājyam
sacchāgadugdhaṃ grahaṇīgudotthe śothāgnisādārucinudvariṣṭham || 24
mocasindhūtpalavyoṣalajjābilvāmbudhātaki || 25
siddhamājyamajākṣīre cāṅgerīrasavacchiśoḥ
hanyādgrahaṇyatīsārau pathyāpathyāśino drutam || 26
pāṭhāntare kapittho'pi viśatyatra ghṛte punaḥ
nāgaraṃ pippalīmūlaṃ citrako hastipippalī || 27
śvadaṃṣṭrā pippalī dhānyaṃ bilvaṃ pāṭhā yavānikā
cāṅgerīsvarase sarpiḥ kalkairebhirvipācitam || 28
caturguṇena dadhnā ca tadghṛtaṃ kaphavātanut
arśāṃsi grahaṇīdoṣaṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ pravāhikām
gudabhraṃśārtimānāhaṃ ghṛtametadvyapohati || 29
marīcaṃ pippalīmūlaṃ nāgaraṃ pippalī tathā || 30
bhallātakaṃ yavānī ca viḍaṅgaṃ hastipippalī
hiṅgu sauvarcalaṃ caiva biḍasaindhavacavyakam || 31
sāmudraṃ sayavakṣāraṃ citrako vacayā saha
etairardhapalairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 32
daśamūlīrase siddhaṃ payasā dviguṇena ca
mandāgnīnāṃ ghṛtaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ grahaṇīdoṣanāśanam || 33
viṣṭambhamāmadaurbalyaplīhānamapakarṣati
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ kṣayaṃ cāpi durnāma sabhagaṃdaram || 34
kaphajānhanti rogāṃśca vātajānkṛmisaṃbhavān
tānsarvānnāśayatyāśu śuṣkaṃ dārvanalo yathā || 35
viśvauṣadhasya garbheṇa daśamūlajale śṛtam
ghṛtaṃ nihanyācchvayathuṃ grahaṇīmāmatāmayam || 36
ghṛtaṃ nāgarakalkena siddhaṃ vātānulomanam
grahaṇīpāṇḍurogaghnaṃ plīhakāsajvarāpaham || 37
prasthatrayeṇā''malakīrasasya śuddhasya dattvā'rdhatulāṃ guḍasya
cūrṇīkṛtairgranthikajīracavyavyoṣebhakṛṣṇāhapuṣājamodaiḥ || 38
viḍaṅgasindhutriphalāyavānīpāṭhāgnidhānyaiśca palapramāṇaiḥ
dattvā trivṛccūrṇapalāni cāṣṭāvaṣṭau ca tailasya pacedyathāvat || 39
taṃ bhakṣayedakṣaphalapramāṇaṃ yatheṣṭaceṣṭaṃ trisugandhiyuktam
anena sarve grahaṇīvikārāḥ saśvāsakāsasvarabhedaśophāḥ || 40
śāmyanti cāyaṃ ciramantaragnerhatasya puṃstvasya ca vṛddhihetuḥ
strīṇāṃ ca vandhyāmayamāśu hanyātkalyāṇako nāma guḍaḥ prasiddhaḥ || 41
taile trivṛnmanāgbhṛṣṭvā trisugandhi palaṃ palam
siddhe nidheyamatraiva guḍe kalyāṇapūrvake || 42
liṅgairasādhyo grahaṇīvikāro yaistairatīsāragado na sidhyet
vṛddhasya nūnaṃ grahaṇīvikāro hatvā tanuṃ no na nivartate ca || 43
Adhikāra 5
trayo vikārāḥ prāyeṇa ye parasparahetavaḥ
arśāṃsi cātisāraśca grahaṇīdoṣa eva ca || 1
teṣāmagnibale hīne vṛddhirvṛddhe parikṣayaḥ
agnimūlaṃ balaṃ puṃsāṃ balamūlaṃ hi jīvitam
tasmādagniṃ sadā rakṣedeṣu triṣu viśeṣataḥ || 2
durnāmnāṃ sādhanopāyaścaturdhā parikīrtitaḥ
bheṣajakṣāraśastrāgnisādhyatvādādya ucyate || 3
yadvāyorānulomyāya yadagnibalavṛddhaye
annapānauṣadhaṃ sarvaṃ tatsevyaṃ nityamarśasaiḥ || 4
vātātisāravadbhinnavarcāṃsyarśāṃsyupācaret || 5
udāvartavidhānena gāḍhaviṭkāni cāsakṛt
pravṛttabahulāsrāṇi pittaśoṇitanāśanaiḥ || 6
viḍvibandhe hitaṃ takraṃ yavānībiḍasaṃyutam
na virohanti gudajāḥ punastakrasamāhatāḥ || 7
srotaḥsu takraśuddheṣu rasaḥ samyagupaiti yaḥ
tena puṣṭirbalaṃ varṇaḥ praharṣaścopajāyate
vātaśleṣmavikārāṇāṃ śataṃ ca vinivartate || 8
saguḍāṃ pippalīyuktāmabhayāṃ ghṛtabharjitām
trivṛddantīyutāṃ vā'pi bhakṣayedānulomikām || 9
tilāruṣkarasaṃyogaṃ bhakṣayedagnivardhanam
kuṣṭharogaharaṃ śroṣṭhamarśasāṃ nāśanaṃ param || 10
tilā bhallātakaṃ pathyā guḍaśceti samāṃśakam
durnāmaśvāsakāsaghnaṃ plīhapāṇḍujvarāpaham || 11
mṛlliptaṃ sauraṇaṃ kandaṃ paktvā'gnau puṭapākavat
adyātsatailalavaṇaṃ durnāmavinivṛttaye || 12
asitānāṃ tilānāṃ prāk prakuñcaṃ śītavāryanu
khādato'rśāṃsi śāmyanti dvijadārḍhyāṅgapuṣṭidam || 13
svinnaṃ vārtākaphalaṃ ghoṣāyāḥ kṣārajena salilena
tadghṛtabhṛṣṭaṃ yuktaṃ guḍena vā tṛptito yo'tti || 14
pibati ca takraṃ nūnaṃ tasyā''śvevātivṛddhagudajāni
yānti vināśaṃ puṃsaḥ sahajānyapi saptarātreṇa || 15
pittaśleṣmapraśamanī kacchūkaṇḍūrujāpahā
gudajānnāśayatyāśu yojitā saguḍā'bhayā || 16
citrakaṃ hapuṣā hiṅgu dadyādvā takrasaṃyutam || 17
pañcakolakayuktaṃ vā takramasmai pradāpayet
tvacaṃ citrakamūlasya piṣṭvā kumbhaṃ pralepayet
takraṃ vā dadhi vā tatra jātamarśoharaṃ pibet || 18
triphalādaśamūlāgninikumbhānāṃ palaṃ palam || 19
vāri droṇe śṛtaṃ pādaśeṣe guḍatulāyutaḥ
ājyabhāṇḍasthito māsaṃ dantyariṣṭo niṣevitaḥ || 20
gudajakṛmyudāvartagrahaṇīpāṇḍurogahā
ariṣṭādestu saṃdhānaṃ dhātakīliptabhājane || 21
harītakīnāṃ prasthārdhaṃ prasthamāmalakasya ca
kapitthānāṃ daśapalaṃ tato'rdhenendravāruṇī || 22
viḍaṅgapippalīlodhramaricaṃ sailavālukam
dvipalāṃśaṃ jalasyaitaccaturdroṇe vipācayet || 23
droṇaśeṣarase tasminpūte śīte pradāpayet
guḍasya dviśataṃ tiṣṭhenmāsārdhaṃ ghṛtabhājane || 24
pakṣādūrdhvaṃ bhavatyevaṃ tato mātrāṃ yathābalam
asyābhyāsādariṣṭasya gudajā yānti saṃkṣayam || 25
grahaṇīpāṇḍutdṛdrogaplīhagulmodarāpahaḥ
aṣṭaśophāruciharo balavarṇāgnivardhanaḥ || 26
siddho'yamabhayāriṣṭaḥ kāmalāśvitranāśanaḥ
kṛmigranthyarbudavyaṅgarājayakṣmajvarāpahaḥ || 27
pānapānamariṣṭādeḥ kvāthapānasamaṃ jaguḥ
bheṣajatvātpalaṃ kecitprathamaṃ mandalakṣaṇam || 28
bhallātakāni triphalā dantī citrakameva ca
samabhāgāni sarvāṇi saindhavaṃ dviguṇaṃ bhavet || 29
kapālasaṃdhau tatpakvaṃ mṛdunā gomayāgninā
etatkalyāṇalavaṇaṃ śreṣṭhamarśovikāriṇām || 30
tripalaṃ śṛṅgaverasya caturthaṃ maricasya ca
pippalyāḥ kuḍavārdhaṃ ca cavyāyāḥ palameva ca || 31
tālīsapatrasya palaṃ palārdhaṃ kesarasya ca
dvipale pippalīmūlādardhakarṣaṃ ca patrakāt || 32
sūkṣmailākarṣamekaṃ tu karṣaṃ ca tvaṅmṛṇālayoḥ
guḍātpalāni triṃśacca cūrṇamekatra kārayet || 33
akṣapramāṇā guṭikāḥ prāṇadā iti viśrutāḥ
pūrvaṃ bhakṣyāstu paścācca bhojanasya yathābalam || 34
madyaṃ māṃsarasaṃ yūṣaṃ kṣīraṃ toyaṃ pibedanu
hanyādarśāṃsi sarvāṇi sahajānyasrajāni ca || 35
vātapittakaphotthāni saṃnipātodbhavāni ca
pānātyaye mūtrakṛcchre vātaroge galagrahe || 36
viṣamajvare ca mande'gnau pāṇḍuroge tathaiva ca
kṛmihadrogiṇāṃ caiva gulmaśūlārtināṃ tathā || 37
śvāsakāsaparītānāmetāḥ syuramṛtopamāḥ
śuṇṭhyāḥ sthāne'bhayā deyā viḍgrahe pittapāyuje || 38
prāṇado'yaṃ sitāṃ dattvā cūrṇamānāccaturguṇām
amlapittāgnimāndyādau prayojyo gudajāture || 39
anupānaṃ prayoktavyaṃ vyādhau śleṣmabhave palam
paladvayaṃ cānilaje pittaje tu palatrayam || 40
pathyāpañcapalānyekamajājyā maricasya ca
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ cavyacitrakanāgarāḥ || 41
palābhivṛddhāḥ kramaśo yavakṣārapaladvayam
bhallātakapalānyaṣṭau kandastu dviguṇo mataḥ || 42
dviguṇena guḍenaiṣāṃ vaṭakānakṣasaṃmitān
kṛtvaikaṃ bhakṣayetprātastakramambho'nu vā pibet || 43
mandāgniṃ dīpayatyeṣa grahaṇīpāṇḍuroganut
kāṅkāyanena śiṣyebhyaḥ śastrakṣārāgnibhirvinā
bhiṣagjitamidaṃ proktaṃ śreṣṭhamarśovikāriṇām || 44
lavaṇottamavahnikaliṅgayavāṃścirabilvamahāpicumandayutān
piba saptadinaṃ mathitālulitānyadi marditumicchasi pāyuruhān || 45
śuṇṭhīkaṇāmaricanāgadalatvagelaṃ cūrṇīkṛtaṃ kramavivardhitamūrdhvamantyāt
khādedidaṃ samasitaṃ gudajāgnimāndyagulmāruciśvasanakaṇṭhahṛdāmayeṣu || 46
sanāgarāruṣkaravṛddhadārakaṃ guḍena yo modakamatyudārakam
aśeṣadurnāmakarogadārakaṃ karoti vṛddhaṃ sahasaiva dārakam || 47
vyoṣāgnyaruṣkaraviḍaṅgatilābhayānāṃ
cūrṇaṃ guḍena sahitaṃ tu sadopayojyam
durnāmaśophagarakuṣṭhaśakṛdvibandhā-
nagnerjayatyabalatāṃ kṛmipāṇḍutāṃ ca || 48
cūrṇe cūrṇasamo jñeyo modake dviguṇo guḍaḥ || 49
cūrṇīkṛtāḥ ṣoḍaśa sūraṇasya bhāgāstato'rdhena ca citrakasya
mahauṣadhāddvau maricasya caiko guḍena durnāmajayāya piṇḍī || 50
maricamahauṣadhacitrakasūraṇabhāgā yathottaraṃ dviguṇāḥ
sarvasamo guḍabhāgaḥ sevyo'yaṃ modakaḥ prasiddhaphalaḥ || 51
jvalanaṃ jvalayati jāṭharamunmūlayati śūlagulmagadān
niḥśeṣayati śvayathuṃ ślīpadamarśāṃsi nāśayatyāśu || 52
ṣoḍaśa sūraṇabhāgā vahneraṣṭau mahauṣadhasyāpi
ardhena bhāgayuktirmaricasya tato'pi cārdhena || 53
triphalākaṇāsamūlātālīsāruṣkarakṛmighnānām
bhāgā mahauṣadhasamā dahanāṃśā tālamūlī ca || 54
bhāgaḥ sūraṇatulyo dātavyo vṛddhadārakasyāpi
bhṛṅgaile maricāṃśe sarvāṇyekatra kārayeccūrṇam || 55
dviguṇena guḍena yutaḥ sevyo'yaṃ modakaḥ prakāmadhanaiḥ
guruvṛṣyabhojyarahiteṣvitareṣūpadravaṃ kurute || 56
bhasmakamanena janitaṃ pūrvamagastyasya yogarājena
bhīmasya māruterapi mahāśanau yena tau jātau || 57
agnibalamatra heturna kevalaṃ sūraṇo mahāvīryaḥ
prabhavati śastrakṣārāgnibhirvinā'pyarśasāmeṣaḥ || 58
śvayathuślīpadajidayaṃ harati grahaṇīṃ kaphānilajām
nāśayati valīpalitaṃ medhāṃ kurute vṛṣatvaṃ ca || 59
hikkāṃ śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ sarājayakṣmapramehāṃśca
plīhānaṃ ca tathograṃ hantyāśu rasāyanaṃ puṃsām || 60
cavyaṃ trikaṭukaṃ pāṭhāṃ kṣāraṃ kustumburūṇi ca
yavānī pippalīmūlamubhe ca biḍasaindhave || 61
citrakaṃ bilvamabhayāṃ piṣṭvā sarpirvipācayet
śakṛdvātānulomārthaṃ jāte dadhni caturguṇe || 62
pravāhikāṃ gudabhraṃśaṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ parisravam
gudavaṅkṣaṇaśūlaṃ ca ghṛtametadvyapohati || 63
pippalīṃ madhukaṃ bilvaṃ śatāhvāṃ madanaṃ vacām
puṣkarākhyaṃ śaṭhīṃ kuṣṭhaṃ citrakaṃ devadāru ca || 64
piṣṭvā tailaṃ vipaktavyaṃ dviguṇakṣīrasaṃyutam
arśasāṃ mūḍhavātānāṃ tacchreṣṭhamanuvāsanam || 65
gudaniḥsaraṇaṃ śūlaṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ pravāhikām
kaṭyūrupṛṣṭhadaurbalyamānāhaṃ vaṅkṣaṇāśrayam || 66
picchāsrāvaṃ gude śothaṃ vātavarcovinigraham
utthānaṃ bahuśo yacca jayeccaivānuvāsanāt || 67
trivṛttejovatī dantī śvadaṃṣṭrā citrakaḥ śaṭhī
gavākṣīviśvamustāhvaviḍaṅgāni harītakī || 68
palonmitāni caitāni palānyaṣṭāvaruṣkarāt
ṣaṭpalaṃ vṛddhadārasya sūraṇasya tu ṣoḍaśa || 69
jaladroṇadvaye kvāthyaṃ caturbhāgāvaśeṣitam
pūtaṃ tu taṃ rasaṃ bhūyaḥ kvāthyebhyastriguṇo guḍaḥ || 70
lehaṃ pacettu taṃ tāvadyāvaddarvīpralepanam
abatārya tataḥ paścāccūrṇānīmāni dāpayet || 71
trivṛttejovatīkandacitrakāndvipalāṃśikān
elātvaṅmaricaṃ cāpi nāgāhvaṃ cāpi ṣaṭpalam || 72
dvātriṃśacca palānyeva cūrṇaṃ dattvā nidhāpayet
tato mātrāṃ prayuñjīta jīrṇe kṣīrarasāśanaḥ || 73
pañca gulmānpramehāṃśca pāṇḍurogaṃ halīmakam
jayedarśāṃsi sarvāṇi tathā sarvodarāṇi ca || 74
dīpayedgrahaṇīṃ mandāṃ yakṣmāṇaṃ cāpi karṣati
pīnase ca pratiśyāya āḍhyavāte tathaiva ca || 75
ayaṃ sarvagadeṣveva kalyāṇo leha uttamaḥ
durnāmārirayaṃ cā''śu dṛṣṭo vārasahasraśaḥ || 76
jīvantyenaṃ prayuñjānāḥ śatavarṣāṇyanāmayāḥ
āyuṣo dairghyajanano valīpalitanāśanaḥ || 77
rasāyanavaraścaiva medhājanana uttamaḥ
guḍaḥ śrībāhuśālo'yaṃ durnāmāriḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 78
toyapūrṇe yadā pātre kṣipto na plavate guḍaḥ
kṣiptastu niścalastiṣṭhetpatitastu na śīryate || 79
yadā darvīpralepaḥ syādyadā vā tantulī bhavet
eṣa pāko guḍādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ parikīrtitaḥ || 80
sukhamardaḥ sukhasparśo gandhavarṇarasānvitaḥ
pīḍito bhajate mudrāṃ guḍaḥ pākamupāgataḥ || 81
bhallātakasahasre dve jaladroṇe vipācayet
pādaśeṣe rase tasminpacedguḍatulāṃ bhiṣak || 82
bhallātakasahasrārdhaṃ chittvā tatraiva dāpayet
siddhe'smiṃstriphalāvyoṣayavānīmustasaindhavaiḥ || 83
karṣāṃśaiścūrṇitairdadyāttvagelāpatrakesaram
khādedagnibalāpekṣī prātarutthāya mānavaḥ || 84
kuṣṭhārśaḥkāmalāmehagrahaṇīgulmapāṇḍutāḥ
hanyātplīhodaraṃ kāsaṃ kṛmirogabhagaṃdarān
bhallātakaguḍo hyeṣa śreṣṭhaścārśovikāriṇām || 85
daśamūlyamṛtā bhārṅgī śvadaṃṣṭrā citrakaṃ śaṭhī || 86
bhallātakasahasraṃ tu palāṃśaṃ kvāthayedbudhaḥ
dattvā guḍatulāmekāṃ lehībhūtaṃ samuddharet || 87
mākṣikaṃ pippalīṃ tailamaurubūkaṃ ca dāpayet
kuḍavaṃ kuḍavaṃ cātra tvagelāmaricaṃ tathā || 88
arśaḥkāsamudāvartaṃ pāṇḍutvaṃ śophameva ca
nāśayedvahnisādaṃ ca guḍo bhallātakaḥ smṛtaḥ || 89
citrakaṃ triphalā mustaṃ granthikaṃ cavikā'mṛtā
hastipippalyapāmārgadaṇḍotpalakuṭherakāḥ || 90
eṣāṃ catuṣpalānbhāgāñjaladroṇe vipācayet
bhallātakasahasre dve chittvā tatraiva dāpayet || 91
tena pādāvaśeṣeṇa lohapātre pacedbhiṣak
tulārdhaṃ tīkṣṇalohasya ghṛtasya kuḍavadvayam || 92
tryūṣaṇaṃ triphalāṃ vahniṃ saindhavaṃ biḍamaudbhidam
sauvarcalaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ ca palikāṃśaṃ prakalpayet || 93
kuḍavaṃ vṛddhadārasya tālamūlyāstathaiva ca
sūraṇasya palānyaṣṭau cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā vinikṣipet || 94
siddhe śīte pradātavyaṃ madhunaḥ kuḍavadvayam
prātarbhojanakāle vā tataḥ khādedyathābalam || 95
arśāṃsi grahaṇīdoṣaṃ pāṇḍurogamarocakam
kṛmigulmāśmarīmehāñśūlaṃ cā''śu vyapohati || 96
karoti śukropacayaṃ valīpalitanāśanam
rasāyanamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sarvarogaharaṃ param || 97
raktārśasāmupekṣeta raktamādau sravadbhiṣak
duṣṭāsre nigṛhīte tu śūlānāhāvasṛggadāḥ || 98
kuṭajatvaṅniryūhaḥ sanāgaraḥ snigdharaktasaṃgrahaṇaḥ
tvagdāḍimasya tadvatsanāgaraścandanarasaśca || 99
candanakirātatiktakadhanvayavāsāḥ sanāgarāḥ kvathitāḥ
raktārśasāṃ praśamanā dārvītvaguśīranimbāśca || 100
navanītatilābhyāsātkesaranavanītaśarkarābhyāsāt
dadhisaramathitābhyāsādgudajāḥ śāmyanti raktavahāḥ || 101
samaṅgotpalamocāhvatirīṭatilacandanaiḥ
chāgīkṣīraṃ prayoktavyaṃ gudaje śoṇitāpaham || 102
kuṭajaphalavalkalakesaranīlotpalalodhradhātakīkalkaiḥ
siddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ vidheyaṃ śūle raktārśasāṃ bhiṣajā || 103
kuṭajatvakpalaśataṃ jaladroṇe vipācayet
aṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ tu kaṣāyamavatārayet || 104
vastrapūtaṃ punaḥ kvāthaṃ pacellehatvamāgatam
bhallātakaṃ viḍaṅgāni trikaṭu triphalāṃ tathā || 105
rasāñjanaṃ citrakaṃ ca kuṭajasya phalāni ca
vacāmativiṣāṃ bilvaṃ pratyekaṃ tu palaṃ palam || 106
triṃśatpalāni guḍasya cūrṇīkṛtya nidhāpayet
madhunaḥ kuḍavaṃ dadyāddhṛtasya kuḍavaṃ tathā || 107
eṣa lehastu śamayedarśoraktasamudbhavam
vātikaṃ paittikaṃ caiva ślaiṣmikaṃ sāṃnipātikam || 108
ye ca durnāmajā rogāstānsarvānnāśayatyapi
amlapittamatīsāraṃ pāṇḍurogamarocakam || 109
grahaṇīmārdavaṃ kārśyaṃ śvayathuṃ kāmalāmapi
anupānaṃ ghṛtaṃ dadyānmadhu takraṃ jalaṃ payaḥ
rogānīkavadhārthāya kauṭajo leha ucyate || 110
toye kālakamuṣkakasya vipacedbhasmāḍhakaṃ ṣaḍguṇe
pātre lohamaye dṛḍhe vipuladhīrdarvyā śanairghaṭṭayet
dagdhvā'gnau bahuśaṅkhanābhiśakalānpūtāvaśeṣe kṣipe-
dyadyeraṇḍajanālameṣa dahati kṣāro varo vākśatāt || 111
pānāya bhāvanāyātha parisrāvyaṃ caturguṇam || 112
dvaiguṇyaṃ nā''ḍhake'pyatra bhāgamātropalakṣaṇāt
acchapaicchilyaraktatve kṣārāmbho grāhyamiṣyate || 113
pānīyaḥ pratisāraṇīya iti ca kṣāro dvidhā
śasyate tatrā''dyo garagulmakādiśamane durnāmakādau paraḥ
turyeṇāṣṭamakena ṣoḍaśabhavenāṃśena saṃvyūhimo
madhyaḥ śreṣṭha iti krameṇa vihitaḥ kṣārodakācchaṅkhakaḥ || 114
bhāvitaṃ rajanīcūrṇaiḥ snuhīkṣīre punaḥ punaḥ
bandhanātsudṛḍhaṃ sūtraṃ chinattyarśobhagaṃdaram || 115
prāgdakṣiṇaṃ tato vāmaṃ pṛṣṭhajaṃ vā'grajaṃ kramāt
pañcatiktena saṃsnehya dahetkṣāreṇa vahninā
vātajaṃ śleṣmajaṃ cārśaḥ kṣāreṇāsrajapittaje || 116
gojīśephālikāpatrairarśaḥ saṃlikhya lepayet || 117
kṣāreṇa vākśataṃ tiṣṭhedyantradvāraṃ pidhāya ca
taṃ cāpanīya vīkṣeta pakvajambuphalopamam || 118
yadi tatsyāttadā bhadraṃ no cellimpettathā punaḥ
tattuṣāmbuplutaṃ sājyayaṣṭīkalkena lepayet || 119
na nimnaṃ tālakarṇābhaṃ vahnidagdhe sthitāsrakam
nirvāpya madhusarpirbhyāṃ vahnisaṃjātavedanam || 120
samyagdagdhe tugākṣīrīplakṣacandanagairikaiḥ
sāmṛtaiḥ sarpiṣā yuktairālepaṃ kārayedbhiṣak || 121
muddūrtamupaveśyo'sau toyasaṃpūrṇabhājane
kṣāramuṣṇāmbunā(no) pāsyo(syaṃ) vibandhe mūtravarcasoḥ || 122
dāhe vastyādije lepaḥ śatadhautena sarpiṣā
navānnamāṣatakrādi sevyaṃ pākāya mātrayā || 123
pibedvraṇaviśuddhyarthaṃ varākvāthaṃ saguggulum
jīrṇaśālyannamudgādi pathyaṃ tiktājyasaindhavam || 124
rūḍhasarvavraṇaṃ vaidyaḥ kṣāraṃ dattvā'nuvāsayet
pippalyādyena tailena seveddīpanabheṣajam || 125
mahāṃti gurumūlāni cchittvaivaṃ balino dahet
carmakīlaṃ tathā chittvā dahedanyatareṇa vā || 126
pakvajambūsamo varṇaḥ kṣāradagdhe praśasyate
vegāvarodhaṃ strīpṛṣṭhayānamutkaṭakāsanam
yathāsvaṃ doṣalaṃ cānnamarśasāṃ parivarjayet || 127
Adhikāra 6
samasya rakṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ viṣame vātanigrahaḥ
tīkṣṇe pittapratīkāro mande śleṣmaviśodhanam || 1
trikaṭukamajamodā saindhavaṃ jīrake dve samadharaṇadhṛtānāmaṣṭamo hiṅgubhāgaḥ
prathamakavalabhuktaṃ sarpiṣā cūrṇametatprabalayati hutāśaṃ vātarogānnihanti || 2
ādau maṇḍaṃ pibeduṣṇaṃ hiṅgusauvarcalānvitam
viṣamo'pi samastena mando dīpyeta pāvakaḥ || 3
kṣudbodhano bastiviśodhanaśca prāṇapradaḥ śoṇitavardhanaśca
jvarāpahārī kaphapittahantā vāyuṃ jayedaṣṭaguṇo hi maṇḍaḥ || 4
sutaṇḍulānāṃ prasṛtidvayaṃ ca tadardhamudgaṃ kaṭukaṃ satakram
kustumbarīsaindhavahiṅgutailamebhiśca sarvaiḥ kriyate hi maṇḍaḥ || 5
vardhamāno bhavettīkṣṇo bhasmakākhyo mahānalaḥ
tasmāttaṃ māhiṣaiḥ sarpirdadhikṣīrairjayedbhiṣak || 6
yatkiṃcidguru medhyaṃ ca śleṣmakāri ca bheṣajam
sarvaṃ tadatyagnihitaṃ bhuktvā prasvapanaṃ divā || 7
harītakī bhakṣyamāṇā nāgareṇa guḍena ca
saindhavopahitā vā'pi sātatyenāgnidīpanī || 8
bhojanāgre sadā pathyaṃ jihvākaṇṭhaviśodhanam
agnisaṃdīpanaṃ hṛdyaṃ lavaṇārdrakabhakṣaṇam || 9
sindhūtthapathyamagadhodbhavavahnicūrṇamuṣṇāmbunā pibati yaḥ khalu naṣṭavahniḥ
tasyā''miṣeṇa saghṛtena varaṃ navānnaṃ bhasmī bhavatyaśitamātramapi kṣaṇena || 10
viśvābhayāguḍūcīnāṃ kaṣāyeṇa ṣaḍūṣaṇam
pibecchleṣmaṇi mande'gnau tvakpatrasurabhīkṛtam || 11
sindhūtthahiṅgutriphalāyavānīvyoṣairguḍāṃśairguṭikāṃ prakuryāt
tāṃ bhakṣayettṛptimavāpnuvannā bhuñjīta mandāgnirapi prabhūtam || 12
guḍena śuṇṭhīmathavopakulyāṃ pathyātṛtīyāmatha dāḍimaṃ ca
āmeṣvajīrṇeṣu gudāmayeṣu varcovibandheṣu ca nityamadyāt || 13
viḍaṅgabhallātakacitrakāmṛtāḥ sanāgarāstulyaguḍena sarpiṣā
lihanti ye mandahutāśanā narā bhavanti te vāḍavatulyavahnayaḥ || 14
harītakī dhānyatuṣodasiddhā sapippalī saindhavahiṅguyuktā
sodgāradhūmaṃ bhṛśamapyajīrṇaṃ vijitya sadyo janayeddhutāśam || 15
glānigauravaviṣṭambhabhramamārutamūḍhatāḥ
vibandho'tipravṛttirvā sāmānyājīrṇalakṣaṇam || 16
prāyeṇā''hāravaiṣamyādajīrṇaṃ jāyate nṛṇām
tanmūlo rogasaṃghātastadvināśādvinaśyati
vacālavaṇatoyena vāntirāme praśasyate || 17
annaṃ vidagdhaṃ hi narasya śīghraṃ śītāmbunā vai paripākameti
taddhyasya śaityena nihanti pittamākledibhāvānnipatatyadhastāt || 18
viṣṭabdhe svedanaṃ kāryaṃ peyaṃ ca lavaṇodakam
rasaśeṣe divāsvapno laṅghanaṃ vātavarjanam || 19
vyāyāmapramadādhvavāhanarataklāntānatīsāriṇaḥ
śūlaśvāsavatastṛṣāparigatānhikkāmarutpīḍitān
kṣīṇānkṣīṇakaphāñśiśūnmadahatānvṛddhānrasājīrṇino
rātrau jāgaritānnarānniraśanānkāmaṃ divā svāpayet || 20
dhānyanāgarasaṃsiddhaṃ toyaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ
āmājīrṇapraśamanaṃ śūlaghnaṃ bastiśodhanam || 21
pathyāpippalisaṃyuktaṃ cūrṇaṃ sauvarcalaṃ pibet
mastunoṣṇodakenāpi buddhvā doṣagatiṃ bhiṣak || 22
caturvidhamajīrṇaṃ ca mandānalamathārucim
ādhmānaṃ vātagulmaṃ ca śūlaṃ cā''śu niyacchati || 23
bhavedajīrṇaṃ prati yasya śaṅkā snigdhasya jantorbalino'nnakāle
pūrvaṃ saśuṇṭhīmabhayāmaśaṅko bhuñjīta saṃprāśya hitaṃ hitāśī || 24
vidahyate yasya tu bhuktamātraṃ dahyeta hṛtkoṣṭhagalaṃ ca yasya
drākṣāsitāmākṣikasaṃprayuktāṃ līḍhvā'bhayāṃ vai sa sukhaṃ labheta || 25
prātarāśe tvajīrṇe tu sāyamāśo na duṣyati
sāyamāśe tvajīrṇe tu prātarāśo hi duṣyati || 26
hiṅgubhāgo bhavedeko vacā ca dviguṇā bhavet || 27
pippalī triguṇā deyā śṛṅgaveraṃ caturguṇam
yavānikā pañcaguṇā ṣaḍguṇā ca harītakī || 28
citrakaṃ saptaguṇitaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ cāṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet
etadvātaharaṃ cūrṇaṃ pītamātraṃ prasannayā || 29
piveddadhnā mastunā vā surayā koṣṇavāriṇā
sodāvartamajīrṇaṃ ca plīhānamudaraṃ tathā || 30
aṅgāni yasya śīryante viṣaṃ vā yena bhakṣitam
arśoghnaṃ dīpanaṃ caiva śūlaghnaṃ gulmanāśanam || 31
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ nihantyāśu tathaiva kṣayanāśanam
cūrṇamagnimukhaṃ nāma na kvacitpratihanyate || 32
dvau kṣārau citrakaṃ pāṭhā karañjaṃ lavaṇāni ca
sūkṣmailā patrakaṃ bhārṅgī kṛmighnaṃ hiṅgu puṣkaram || 33
śaṭhī dārvī trivṛnmustaṃ vacā cendrayavāstathā
dhātrī jīrakavṛkṣāmlaśreyasī copakuñcikā || 34
amlavetasamamlīkā dāḍimaṃ sakaṭutrikam
bhallātakājamodaṃ ca yavānī suradāru ca || 35
abhayā'tiviṣā śyāmā hapuṣā''ragvadhaṃ samam
tilamuṣkakaśigrūṇāṃ kokilākṣapalāśayoḥ || 36
kṣārāṇi lohakiṭṭaṃ ca taptaṃ gomūtrasecitam
samabhāgāni sarvāṇi sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet || 37
mātuluṅgarasenaiva bhāvayecca dinatrayam
dinatrayaṃ tu śuktena ārdrakasvarasena ca || 38
atyagnikārakaṃ cūrṇaṃ pradīptāgnisamaprabham
upayuktaṃ vidhānena nāśayatyacirādgadān || 39
ajīrṇakamatho gulmānplīhānaṃ gudajāni ca
udarāṇyantravṛddhiṃ ca aṣṭhīlāṃ vātaśoṇitam || 40
praṇudatyulbaṇānrogānnaṣṭamagniṃ pradīpayet
samastavyañjanopetaṃ bhaktaṃ dattvā tu bhojane || 41
dāpayedasya cūrṇasya biḍālapadamātrakam
godohamātrāttatsarvaṃ dravī bhavati soṣmakam || 42
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ citrakaṃ hastipippalī
hiṅgu cavyājamode ca pañcaiva lavaṇāni ca || 43
dvau kṣārau hapuṣā caiva dadyādardhapalonmitān
dadhikāñjikaśuktāni ghṛtamātrāsamāni ca || 44
ārdrakasya rasaprasthe ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
etadagnighṛtaṃ nāma mandāgnīnāṃ praśasyate || 45
arśasāṃ nāśanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tathā gulmodarāpaham
granthyarbudāpacīkāsakaphamedonilānapi || 46
nāśayedgrahaṇīdoṣaṃ śvayathuṃ sabhagaṃdaram
ye ca bastigatā rogā ye ca kukṣisamāśritāḥ
sarvāṃstānnāśayatyāśu sūryastama ivoditaḥ || 47
vahnerdvipañcamūlasya kvāthe palaśatadvaye || 48
amṛtāyā rasasyaike pūte'sminnabhayāḍhakam
pacedguḍatulāṃ dattvā yāvadāpākalakṣaṇam || 49
anyedyustatra madhunaḥ suśītakuḍavadvayam
prakṣipettrisugandhasya trikaṭośca paladvayam || 50
pratyekaṃ syādyavakṣārādyu(cchu) ktistasmin rasāyane
uttamaṃ kathitaṃ puṃsāmaśvibhyāmagnivṛddhaye || 51
jīryatyapi ca kāṣṭhāni kāsaśvāsakṣayakṛmīn
gulmodarārśaḥśūlāni sāntravṛddhīni hanti ca || 52
yogaśatairapyajitāṃstryahājjayati pīnasān
pāṭhāntare cātra praviśati dhātrīrasapalaśatam || 53
prasthaṃ taṇḍulatoyatastuṣajalātprasthatrayaṃ cāmlataḥ
prasthārdhaṃ dadhito'pi mūlakapalānyaṣṭau guḍānmānike
mānyau śodhitaśṛṅgaveraśakalāddve sindhvajājyoḥ
pale dve kṛṣṇoṣaṇayorniśāpalayugaṃ nikṣipya bhāṇḍe dṛḍhe || 54
snigdhe dhānyayavādirāśinihitaṃ trīnvāsarānvāsaye-
dgrīṣme toyadharātyaye ca caturo varṣāsu puṣpāgame
ṣaṭśīte'ṣṭa dinānyataḥ paramidaṃ visrāvya saṃcūrṇaye-
ccāturjātapalena saṃhitamidaṃ śuktaṃ ca cukraṃ ca tat
hanyādvātakaphāmadoṣajanitānnānāvidhānāmayā-
ndurnāmānilaśūlagulmajaṭharānhatvā'nalaṃ dīpayet || 55
yanmastvādi śucau bhāṇḍe saguḍakṣaudrakāñjikam
dhānyarāśau trirātrasthaṃ śuktaṃ cukraṃ taducyate || 56
guḍakṣaudrāranālāni samastūni yathottaram
śaṃsanti dviguṇānbhāgānsamyakcukrasya siddhaye || 57
kuṣṭhasaindhavayoḥ kalkaṃ cukratailasamanvitam
viṣūcyāṃ mardanaṃ koṣṇaṃ khallīśūlanivāraṇam || 58
karañjanimbaśikharīguḍūcyarjakavatsakaiḥ
pītaḥ kaṣāyo vamanāddhorāṃ hanti viṣūcikām || 59
vyoṣaṃ karañjasya phalaṃ haridrāṃ mūlaṃ samāvāpya ca mātuluṅgyāḥ
chāyāviśuṣkā guṭikāḥ kṛtāstā hanyurviṣūcīṃ nayanāñjanena || 60
tvakpatrarāsnāguruśigrukuṣṭhairamlaprapiṣṭaiḥ savacāśatāhvaiḥ
udvartanaṃ khalliviṣūcikāghnaṃ tailaṃ vipakvaṃ ca tadarthakāri || 61
pipāsāyāmanutkleśe lavaṅgasyāmbu śasyate
jātīphalasya vā śītaṃ śṛtaṃ bhadraghanasya vā || 62
viṣūcyāmativṛddhāyāṃ pārṣṇyordāhaḥ praśasyate || 63
sarukcā''naddhamudaramamlapiṣṭaiśca lepayet
dāruhaimavatīkuṣṭhaśatāhvāhiṅgusaindhavaiḥ || 64
takreṇa yuktaṃ yavacūrṇamuṣṇaṃ sakṣāramarti jaṭhare nihanyāt
svedo ghaṭairvā bahubāṣpapūrṇairuṣṇaistathā'nyairapi pāṇitāpaiḥ || 65
tīvrārtirapi nājīrṇī pibecchūlaghnamauṣadham
doṣacchanno'nalo nālaṃ paktuṃ doṣauṣadhāśanam || 66
suvāmitaṃ sādhu virecitaṃ ca sulaṅghitaṃ vā manujaṃ viditvā
peyādibhirdīpanapācanīyaiḥ samyakkṣudhārtaṃ samupakrameta || 67
Adhikāra 7
pārasīyayavānī pītā paryuṣitavāriṇā prātaḥ
guḍapūrvā kṛmijālaṃ koṣṭhagataṃ pātayatyāśu || 1
pāribhadrakapatrotthaṃ rasaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ pibet
kambukasya rasaṃ vā'pi pattūrasyāthavā rasam || 2
dīpanīyamannamaṇḍaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ vyoṣasaṃyutam
pāyayetkṛmināśāya agniṃ ca kurute bhṛśam || 3
lihyādvā'śvaśakṛccūrṇaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ vā samākṣikam
viḍaṅgapippalīmūlaśigrubhirmaricena ca
takrasiddhā yavāgūḥ syātkṛmighnī sasuvarcikā || 4
mustākhuparṇīphaladāruśigrukvāthaḥ sakṛṣṇākṛmiśatrukalkaḥ
mārgadvayenāpi cirapravṛttānkṛmīnnihanyātkṛmijāṃśca rogān || 5
ākhuparṇīdalaiḥ piṣṭaiḥ piṣṭakena ca pūlikām
jagdhvā sauvīrakaṃ cānu pibetkṛmiharaṃ param || 6
palāśabījasvarasaṃ pibedvā kṣaudrasaṃyutam
pibettadbījakalkaṃ vā takreṇa kṛmināśanam || 7
surasādigaṇaṃ vā'pi sarvathaivopayojayet || 8
triphalā trivṛtā dantī vacā kampillakaṃ tathā
siddhamebhirgavāṃ mūtre sarpiḥ kṛmivināśanam || 9
triphalāyāstrayaḥ prasthā viḍaṅgaprastha eva ca
dīpanaṃ daśamūlaṃ ca lābhatastūpapādayet || 10
pādaśeṣajaladroṇe śṛte sarpirvipācayet
prasthonmitaṃ sindhuyutaṃ tatpeyaṃ kṛmināśanam || 11
viḍaṅgaghṛtamityetallehyaṃ śarkarayā saha
sarvānkṛmīnpraṇudati muktaṃ vajramivāsurān || 12
rasendreṇa samāyukto raso dhattūrapatrajaḥ
tāmbūlapatrajo vā'tha lepanaṃ yaukanāśanam || 13
kṣīrāṇi māṃsāni ghṛtāni caiva dadhīni śākāni ca parṇavanti
samāsato'mlānmadhurānhimāṃśca kṛmīñjighāṃsuḥ parivarjayecca || 14
Adhikāra 8
sādhyaṃ tu pāṇḍvāmayinaṃ samīkṣya snigdhaṃ ghṛtenordhvamadhaśca śuddham
saṃpādayetkṣaudraghṛtapragāḍhairharītakīcūrṇayutaiḥ prayogaiḥ || 1
pibeddhṛtaṃ vā rajanīvipakvaṃ syāttraiphalaṃ tailvakameva vā'pi
virecanadravyakṛtaṃ pibedvā yogāṃśca vairecanikānghṛtena || 2
vidhiḥ snigdho'tha vātotthe tiktaśītastu paittike
ślaiṣmike kaṭurūkṣoṣṇaḥ kāryo miśrastu miśrake
dviśarkaraṃ trivṛccūrṇaṃ palārdhaṃ paittike pibet || 3
dvipañcamūlīkvathitaṃ saviśvaṃ kaphātmake pāṇḍugade pibedvā
jvare'tisāre śvayathau grahaṇyāṃ śvāse'rucau kaṇṭhahṛdāmaye ca || 4
phalatrikāmṛtāvāsātiktābhūnimbanimbajaḥ
kvāthaḥ kṣaudrayuto hanyātpāṇḍurogaṃ sakāmalam || 5
ayastilatryūṣaṇakolabhāgaiḥ sarvaiḥ samaṃ mākṣikadhātucūrṇam
tairmodakaḥ kṣaudrayuto'nutakraṃ pāṇḍvāmaye dūragate'pi śastaḥ || 6
ayomalaṃ tu saṃtaptaṃ bhūyo gomūtrasecitam || 7
madhusarpiryutaṃ cūrṇaṃ saha bhaktena yojayet
dīpanaṃ cāgnijananaṃ śothapāṇḍvāmayāpaham || 8
tryūṣaṇatriphalāmustaviḍaṅgacitrakāḥ samāḥ
navāyorajaso bhāgāstaccūrṇaṃ madhusarpiṣā
bhakṣayetpāṇḍuhṛdrogakuṣṭhārśaḥkāmalāpaham || 9
recanaṃ kāmalārtasya snigdhasyā''dau prayojayet
tataḥ praśamanī kāryā kriyā vaidyena jānatā || 10
triphalāyā guḍūcyā vā dārvyā nimbasya vā rasaḥ
prātarmākṣikasaṃyuktaḥ śīlitaḥ kāmalāpahaḥ || 11
añjanaṃ kāmalārtānāṃ droṇapuṣpīrasaḥ śubhaḥ
niśāgairikadhātrīṇāṃ cūrṇaṃ vā saṃprakalpayet || 12
nasyaṃ karkoṭamūlaṃ syādghreyaṃ vā jālinīphalam || 13
ayorajovyoṣaviḍaṅgacūrṇaṃ liheddharidrāṃ triphalānvitāṃ vā
saśarkarā kāmalināṃ tribhaṇḍī hitā gavākṣī saguḍā ca śuṇṭhī || 14
dhātrīloharajovyoṣaniśākṣaudrājyaśarkarāḥ
leho nivārayatyāśu kāmalāmuddhatāmapi || 15
dagdhvā'kṣakāṣṭhairmalamāyasaṃ tu gomūtranirvāpitamaṣṭavārān
vicūrṇya līḍhaṃ madhunā'cireṇa kumbhāhvayaṃ pāṇḍugadaṃ nihanti || 16
pāṇḍurogakriyāṃ sarvāṃ yojayecca halīmake
kāmalāyāṃ tu yā dṛṣṭā sā'pi kāryā bhiṣagvaraiḥ || 17
haridrātriphalānimbabalāmadhukasādhitam
sakṣīraṃ māhiṣaṃ sarpiḥ kāmalāharamuttamam || 18
mūrvātiktāniśāyāsakṛṣṇācandanaparpaṭaiḥ
trāyantīvatsabhūnimbapaṭolāmbudadārubhiḥ || 19
akṣamātrairghṛtaṃ prasthaṃ siddhaṃ kṣīracaturguṇam
pāṇḍutājvaravisphoṭaśothārśoraktapittajit || 20
vyoṣaṃ bilvaṃ dve rajanyau triphalā dvipunarnavam
mustamāyorajaḥ pāṭhā viḍaṅgaṃ devadāru ca || 21
vṛścikālī ca bhārṅgī ca sakṣīraistaiḥ śṛtaṃ ghṛtam
sarvānpraśamayatyetadvikārānmṛttikākṛtān || 22
tryūṣaṇaṃ triphalā mustaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ cavyacitrakau
dārvītvaṅmākṣiko dhāturgranthikaṃ devadāru ca || 23
eṣāṃ dvipalikānbhāgāṃścūrṇaṃ kṛtvā pṛthakpṛthak
maṇḍūraṃ dviguṇaṃ cūrṇācchuddhamañjanasaṃnibham || 24
mūtre cāṣṭaguṇe paktvā tasmiṃstu prakṣipettataḥ
udumbarasamānkuryādvaṭakāṃstānyathāgni ca || 25
upayuñjīta takreṇa sātmyaṃ jīrṇe ca bhojayet
maṇḍūravaṭakā hyete prāṇadāḥ pāṇḍurogiṇām || 26
kuṣṭhānyajarakaṃ śophamūrustambhakaphāmayān
arśāṃsi kāmalāmehaṃ plīhānaṃ nāśayanti ca || 27
punarnavā trivṛcchuṇṭhī pippalī maricāni ca
viḍaṅgaṃ devakāṣṭhaṃ ca citrakaṃ puṣkarāhvayam || 28
haridre dve ca triphalā dantī ca cavikā tathā
kuṭajasya phalaṃ tiktā pippalīmūlamustakam || 29
etāni samabhāgāni maṇḍūraṃ dviguṇaṃ tataḥ
mūtre cāṣṭaguṇe paktvā dhārayetsnigdhabhājane
pāṇḍuśothodarānāhaśūlārśaḥkṛmigulmanut || 30
pañcakolaṃ samaricaṃ devadāru phalatrikam || 31
viḍaṅgamustayuktāśca bhāgāstripalasaṃmitāḥ
yāvantyetāni cūrṇāni maṇḍūraṃ dviguṇaṃ tataḥ || 32
paktvā'ṣṭaguṇite mūtre ghanībhūte taduddharet
tato'kṣamātrānvaṭakānpibettakreṇa naktabhuk || 33
pāṇḍurogaṃ jayatyeṣa mandāgnitvamarocakam
arśāṃsi grahaṇīdoṣamūrustambhaṃ halīmakam || 34
kṛmiplīhānamudaraṃ galarogaṃ ca nāśayet
maṇḍūravajranāmā'yaṃ rogānīkapraṇāśanaḥ || 35
yavagodhūmaśālyannaṃ rasairjāṅgalajaiḥ śubhaiḥ
mudgāḍhakīmasūrādyaistayorbhojanamiṣyate || 36
Adhikāra 9
nodriktamādau saṃgrāhyamaśnato balinastu yat
hṛtpāṇḍugrahaṇīrogaplīhagulmajvarādikṛt || 1
ūrdhvaṃ pravṛttadoṣasya pūrvaṃ lohitapittinaḥ
akṣīṇabalamāṃsāgneḥ kartavyamapatarpaṇam || 2
pathyaṃ satīnayūṣeṇa sasitairlājasaktubhiḥ
ūrdhvage recanaṃ pūrvamadhoge vamanaṃ hitam || 3
trivṛtātriphalāśyāmāpippalīśarkarāmadhu
modakaḥ saṃnipātottharaktapittajvarāpahaḥ || 4
mustendrayavayaṣṭyāhvamadanāḍhyaṃ payomadhu
śiśiraṃ vamanaṃ yojyaṃ raktapittaharaṃ param || 5
śāliparṇyādinā siddhā peyā pūrvamadhogate
raktātīsārahantā ca yojyo vidhiraśeṣataḥ || 6
kṣīṇamāṃsabalaṃ bālaṃ vṛddhaṃ śoṣasamanvitam
avamyamavirecyaṃ ca stambhanaiḥ samupācaret || 7
vṛṣapatrāṇi niṣpīḍya rasaṃ samadhuśarkaram
pibettena śamaṃ yāti raktapittaṃ sudāruṇam || 8
āṭarūṣakaniryūhe priyaṃguṃ mṛttikāñjane
vinīya lodhraṃ sakṣaudraṃ raktapittaharaṃ pibet || 9
vāsākaṣāyotpalamṛtpriyaṃgulodhrāñjanāmbhoruhakesarāṇi
pītvā sitākṣaudrayutāni jahyātpittāsṛjo vegamudīrṇamāśu || 10
vāsāyāṃ vidyamānāyāmāśāyāṃ jīvitasya ca
raktapittī kṣayī kāsī kimarthamavasīdati || 11
tālīsacūrṇayuktaḥ peyaḥ kṣaudreṇa vāsakasvarasaḥ
kaphapittakāsatamakaśvāsasvarabhedaraktapittaharaḥ || 12
āṭarūṣakamṛdvīkāpathyākvāthaḥ saśarkaraḥ
kṣaudrāḍhyaḥ kasanaśvāsaraktapittanibarhaṇaḥ || 13
samākṣikaḥ phalguphalodbhavo vā pīto rasaḥ śoṇitamāśu hanti || 14
candanendrayavāḥ pāṭhā kaṭukā sadurālabhā
guḍucī vāsakaṃ lodhraṃ pippalīkṣaudrasaṃyutam
kaphānvitaṃ jayedraktaṃ kuṣṭhakāsajvarāpaham || 15
kaṣāyairvividhairyogairdīpte'gnau vijite kaphe
raktapittaṃ na cecchāmyettatra vātolbaṇe payaḥ || 16
chāgaṃ payaḥ syātparamaṃ prayoge gavāṃ śṛtaṃ pañcaguṇe jale vā
saśarkaraṃ mākṣikasaṃprayuktaṃ vidārigandhādigaṇe śṛtaṃ vā || 17
drākṣayā parṇinībhirvā balayā madhukena vā
śvadaṃṣṭrayā śatāvaryā raktajitsādhitaṃ payaḥ || 18
pakvodumbarakāśmaryapathyākharjūragostanāḥ
madhunā ghnanti saṃlīḍhā raktapittaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 19
khadirasya priyaṃgūṇāṃ kovidārasya śālmaleḥ
puṣpacūrṇaṃ tu madhunā lihannārogyamaśnute || 20
lihettathā vāstukabījacūrṇaṃ kṣaudrānvitaṃ taṇḍulajāhvayaṃ vā
lihyācca lājāñjanacūrṇamekamevaṃ sitākṣaudrayutāṃ tugākhyām || 21
vāsakasvarase pathyā saptadhā paribhāvitā
kṛṣṇā vā madhunā līḍhā raktapittaṃ drutaṃ jayet || 22
draveṇa yāvatā cūrṇamekībhūyā''rdratāṃ vrajet
tāvatpramāṇaṃ nirdiṣṭaṃ bhiṣagbhirbhāvanāvidhau || 23
abhayā madhunā yuktā pācanī dīpanī matā
śleṣmāṇaṃ raktapittaṃ ca hanti śūlātisāranut || 24
lohagandhini niśvāsa udgāre dhūmagandhini
pṛthvīkāṃ śāṇamātrāṃ ca khādeddviguṇaśarkarām || 25
elāpatratvaco'rdhākṣāḥ pippalyardhapalaṃ tathā
sitāmadhukakharjūramṛdvīkāśca palonmitāḥ || 26
saṃcūrṇya madhusaṃyuktā guṭikāḥ saṃprakalpayet
akṣamātrāṃ tataścaikāṃ bhakṣayettāṃ dine dine || 27
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ jvaraṃ hikkāṃ chardiṃ mūrchāṃ madaṃ bhramam
raktaniṣṭhīvanaṃ tṛṣṇāṃ pārśvaśūlamarocakam || 28
śoṣaṃ plīhāḍhyavātāṃśca svarabhedaṃ kṣatakṣayam
guṭikā tarpaṇī vṛṣyā raktapittaṃ ca nāśayet || 29
nāsāpravṛttarudhiraṃ ghṛtabhṛṣṭaṃ ślakṣṇapiṣṭamāmalakam
seturiva toyavegaṃ ruṇaddhi mūrdhni pralepena || 30
ghrāṇapravṛtte jalamāśu deyaṃ saśarkaraṃ nāsikayā payo vā
drākṣārasaṃ kṣīraghṛtaṃ pibedvā saśarkaraṃ cekṣurasaṃ himaṃ vā || 31
nasyaṃ dāḍimapuṣpottho raso dūrvābhavo'thavā
āmrāsthijaḥ palāṇḍorvā nāsikāsrutaraktajit
meḍhrage'tipravṛtte tu bastiruttarasaṃjñitaḥ || 32
sacchāgadugdhaśca saśarkaraśca raso hito dāḍimapuṣpajaśca || 33
śṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ pibedvā'pi pañcamūlyā tṛṇāhvayā
dūrvāsotpalakiñjalkamañjiṣṭhā sailavālukā || 34
sitāsitamuśīraṃ ca mustacandanapadmakaiḥ
vipacetkārṣikairetaiḥ sarpirājaṃ sukhāgninā || 35
taṇḍulāmbu tvajākṣīraṃ dattvā caiva caturguṇam
tatpānaṃ vamato raktaṃ nāvanaṃ nāsikāgate || 36
karṇābhyāṃ yasya gacchettu tasya karṇau prapūrayet
cakṣuḥsrāviṇi rakte ca pūrayettena cakṣuṣī || 37
meḍhrapāyupravṛtte ca vastikarmasu taddhitam
romakūpapravṛtte ca tadabhyaṅge prayojayet || 38
vāsāṃ saśākhāṃ sapalāśamūlāṃ kṛtvā kaṣāyaṃ kusumāni cāsyāḥ
pradāya kalkaṃ vipacedghṛtaṃ tu sakṣaudramāśveva nihanti raktam || 39
śaṇasya kovidārasya vṛṣasya kakubhasya ca
kalkāḍhyatvātpraśaṃsanti puṣpakalkaṃ catuṣpalam || 40
śatāvarīdāḍimatittiḍīkakākolimede madhukaṃ vidārīm
piṣṭvā ca mūlaṃ phalapūrakasya ghṛtaṃ pacetkṣīracaturguṇaṃ ca
kāsajvarānāhavibandhaśūlaṃ tadraktapittaṃ ca ghṛtaṃ nihanti || 41
śatāvaryāstu mūlānāṃ rasaprasthadvayaṃ matam || 42
tatsamaṃ ca bhavetkṣīraṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
jīvakarṣabhakau medā mahāmedā tathaiva ca || 43
kākolī kṣīrakākolī mṛdvīkā madhukaṃ tathā
mudgaparṇī māṣaparṇī vidārī raktacandanam || 44
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ siddhaṃ visrāvayedbhiṣak
raktapittavikāreṣu vātaraktagadeṣu ca || 45
kṣīṇaśukre ca dātavyaṃ vājīkaraṇamuttamam
aṃsadāhaṃ śirodāhaṃ jvaraṃ pittasamudbhavam || 46
yoniśūlaṃ ca dāhaṃ ca mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca paittikam
etānrogānnihantyāśu cchannābhramiva mārutaḥ || 47
śatāvarīsarpiridaṃ balavarṇāgnivardhanam
śatāvarīghṛte cāsmiñśarkarāmadhu pādikam
madhveva pādikaṃ deyaṃ vāsāsarpiṣi saṃmatam || 48
kuṣmāṇḍakātpalaśataṃ susvinnaṃ niṣkulīkṛtam || 49
pacettadā ghṛtaprasthe śanaistāmramaye dṛḍhe
yadā madhunibhaḥ pākastadā khaṇḍaśataṃ nyaset || 50
pippalīśṛṅgaverābhyāṃ dve pale jīrakasya ca
tvagelāpatramaricadhānyakānāṃ palārdhakam || 51
nyaseccūrṇīkṛtaṃ tatra darvyā saṃghaṭṭayettadā
lehībhūte suśīte ca dadyātkṣaudraṃ ghṛtārdhakam || 52
tadyathāgnibalaṃ khādedraktapittī kṣatakṣayī
kāsaśvāsatamaścharditṛṣṇājvaranipīḍitaḥ || 53
vṛṣyaṃ punarnavakaraṃ balavarṇaprasādanam
uraḥsaṃdhānakaraṇaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ svarabodhanam || 54
aśvibhyāṃ nirmitaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kūṣmāṇḍakarasāyanam
khaṇḍakāmalakīnyāyādrasaprasthadvayoktitaḥ || 55
khaṇḍakūṣmāṇḍake pātraṃ svinnakūṣmāṇḍakadravāt
anyatra khaṇḍakūṣmāṇḍātsaṃmataḥ sakalo rasaḥ
yuktasarpiṣi kūṣmāṇḍe pāko gandhena mudrayā || 56
pañcāśacca palaṃ svinnakūṣmāṇḍātprasthamājyataḥ || 57
pakvaṃ palaśataṃ khaṇḍaṃ vāsākvāthāḍhake pacet
śubhādhātrīghanābhārṅgītrisugandhaiśca kārṣikaiḥ || 58
aileyaviśvadhānyākamaricaiśca palāṃśakaiḥ
pippalīkuḍavaṃ caiva madhumānīṃ pradāpayet || 59
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ kṣayaṃ hikkāṃ raktapittaṃ halīmakam
hṛdrogamamlapittaṃ ca pīnasaṃ ca vyapohati || 60
tulāmādāya vāsāyāḥ pacedaṣṭaguṇe jale
tena pādāvaśeṣeṇa pācayedāḍhakaṃ bhiṣak || 61
cūrṇānāmabhayānāṃ tu khaṇḍācchuddhācchataṃ tathā
dve pale pippalīcūrṇātsiddhaśīte ca mākṣikāt || 62
kuḍavaṃ palamānaṃ ca cāturjātaṃ sucūrṇitam
kṣiptvā viloḍitaṃ khādedraktapittī yathābalam || 63
kāsaśvāsagṛhītaśca yakṣmaṇā ca nipīḍitaḥ || 64
śatāvarī chinnaruhā vṛṣo muṇḍitikā balā || 65
tālamūlī ca gāyatrī triphalāyāstvacastathā
bhārṅgī puṣkaramūlaṃ ca pṛthakpañca palāni ca || 66
jaladroṇe vipaktavyamaṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣitam
divyauṣadhihatasyāpi mākṣikeṇa hatasya ca || 67
paladvādaśakaṃ deyaṃ rukmalohasya cūrṇitam
khaṇḍatulyaṃ ghṛtaṃ deyaṃ palaṣoḍaśakaṃ budhaiḥ || 68
pacettāmramaye pātre guḍapāko mato yathā
prasthārdhaṃ madhuno deyaṃ śubhāśmajatukaṃ tvacam || 69
śṛṅgī viḍaṅgaṃ kṛṣṇā ca śuṇṭhyajājī palaṃ matam
triphalā dhānyakaṃ patraṃ dvyakṣaṃ maricakesaram || 70
cūrṇaṃ dattvā sumathitaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍe nivāsayet
yathākālaṃ prayuñjīta biḍālapadakaṃ tataḥ || 71
gavyakṣīrānupānaṃ ca sevyaṃ māṃsarasaṃ payaḥ
guruvṛṣyānnapānāni snigdhamāṃsādi bṛṃhaṇam || 72
raktapittaṃ kṣayaṃ kāsaṃ paktiśūlaṃ viśeṣataḥ
vātaraktaṃ pramehaṃ ca śītapittaṃ vamiṃ klamam || 73
śvayathuṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca kuṣṭhaṃ plīhodaraṃ tathā
ānāhaṃ mūtrasaṃsrāvamamlapittaṃ nihanti ca || 74
cakṣuṣyaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ vṛṣyaṃ māṅgalyaṃ prītivardhanam
ārogyaputradaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kāyāgnibalavardhanam || 75
śrīkaraṃ lāghavakaraṃ khaṇḍakhādyaṃ prakīrtitam
chāgaṃ pārāvataṃ māṃsaṃ tittiriḥ krakaraḥ śaśaḥ || 76
kuliṅgāḥ kṛṣṇasārāśca teṣāṃ māṃsāni yojayet
nārikelapayaḥpānaṃ suniṣaṇṇakavāstukam || 77
śuṣkamūlakajīvākhyaṃ paṭolaṃ bṛhatīphalam
phalaṃ vārtākapakvāmraṃ kharjūraṃ svādudāḍimam || 78
kakārapūrvakaṃ yacca māṃsaṃ cānūpasaṃbhavam
varjanīyaṃ viśeṣeṇa khaṇḍakhādyaṃ prakurvatā || 79
yacca pittajvare proktaṃ bahirantaśca bheṣajam
raktapitte hitaṃ tacca kṣatakṣīṇahitaṃ ca yat || 80
śāliṣaṣṭikagodhūmacaṇamudgamasūrikāḥ
śyāmākāśca priyaṃgvaśca bhojanaṃ raktapittinaḥ || 81
paṭolanimbavetrāgrataṇḍulīyādayo hitāḥ
pārāvatakapotādyāḥ śaśaiṇahariṇāstathā || 82
vṛṣasya svarasaṃ kṛtvā dravyairebhiḥ prayojayet
priyaṃgumṛttikālodhramañjanaṃ ceti cūrṇayet || 83
etaccūrṇaṃ pāyayettadrasaiḥ kṣaudrasamanvitaiḥ
nāsikāmukhapāyubhyo yāni meḍhrācca vegitam || 84
raktapittaṃ sravaddhanti siddha eṣa prayogarāṭ
yacca śastrakṣatenaiva raktaṃ tiṣṭhati vegitam
tadapyanena cūrṇena tiṣṭhatyevāvacūrṇitam || 85
sāmānyoditayogeṣu dravyaśaktiṃ samīkṣya hi
prayojyo raktapittādau yogo vātādije gade || 86
Adhikāra 10
śāliṣaṣṭikagodhūmayavamudgādayaḥ śubhāḥ
madyāni jāṅgalāḥ pakṣimṛgāḥ śastā viśuṣyataḥ || 1
sapippalīkaṃ sayavaṃ sakulatthaṃ sanāgaram
sadāḍimaṃ sāmalakaṃ snigdhamājaṃ rasaṃ pibet || 2
tena ṣaḍvinivartante vikārāḥ pīnasādayaḥ
dravyato dviguṇaṃ māṃsaṃ sarvato'ṣṭaguṇaṃ jalam
pādasthaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ cā''jye ṣaḍaṅgo yūṣa ucyate || 3
dhānyākapippalīviśvadaśamūlījalaṃ pibet
pārśvaśūlajvaraśvāsapīnasādinivṛttaye || 4
aśvagandhāmṛtābhīrudaśamūlībalāvṛṣāḥ
puṣkarātiviṣe ghnanti kṣayaṃ kṣīrarasāśinaḥ || 5
daśamūlībalārāsnāpuṣkaraiḥ suradārubhiḥ
nāgaraiḥ kvathitaṃ peyaṃ kṣayakāsādiśāntaye || 6
kakubhatvaṅnāgabalāvānaribījāni cūrṇitaṃ payasi
pakvaṃ madhughṛtayuktaṃ sasitaṃ yakṣmādikāsaharam || 7
madhutāpyaviḍaṅgāśmajatulohaghṛtāmayāḥ
ghnanti yakṣmāṇamatyugraṃ sevyamānā hitāśinā || 8
kṛṣṇādrākṣāsitālehaḥ kṣayahā kṣaudratailavān
madhusarpiryuto vā'śvagandhākṛṣṇāsitodbhavaḥ || 9
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ navanītaṃ lihankṣayī
kṣīrāśī labhate puṣṭimatulye vā''jyamākṣike || 10
sitopalātugākṣīrīpippalībahulātvacaḥ || 11
antyādūrdhvaṃ dviguṇitaṃ lehayenmadhusarpiṣā
cūrṇitaṃ prāśayedvā'pi śvāsakāsakaphāturam || 12
pārśvaśūlinamalpāgniṃ suptajihvamarocinam
hastapādāṃsadāheṣu jvare rakte tathordhvage || 13
daśamūlīśṛtātkṣīrātsarpiryadudiyānnavam
tatkaṇāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ ghṛtaṃ svaraviśodhanam || 14
śiroṃsapārśvaśūlaghnaṃ śvāsakāsajvarāpaham
siddhaṃ jagati vikhyātaṃ śoṣiṇāṃ paramauṣadham || 15
śatapuṣpāṃ nataṃ kuṣṭhaṃ madhukaṃ devadāru ca
piṣṭvā lepaḥ sasarpiṣkaḥ pṛṣṭhapārśvāṃsarukṣu ca || 16
cūrṇaṃ kākubhamiṣṭaṃ vāsakarasaparibhāvitaṃ bahūnvārān
ghṛtamadhusitopalābhirlehyaṃ kṣayakāsaraktapittaharam || 17
elājamodāmalakābhayākṣagāyatrinimbāsanaśālasārān
viḍaṅgabhallātakacitrakāṃśca kaṭutrikāmbhodasurāṣṭrajāṃśca || 18
paktvā jale tena pacettu sarpistasminsusiddhe tvavatārite ca
triṃśatpalānyatra sitopalāyā dadyāttugākṣīripalāni ṣaṭ ca || 19
prasthe ghṛtasya dviguṇaṃ ca dadyātkṣaudraṃ tato manthahataṃ vidadhyāt
palaṃ palaṃ prātarato lihecca paścātpibetkṣīramatandritaśca || 20
etaddhi medhyaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ cakṣuṣyamāyuṣyatamaṃ tathaiva
yakṣmāṇamāśu vyapahanti nūnaṃ pāṇḍvāmayaṃ cāpi bhagaṃdaraṃ ca || 21
śvāsaṃ ca hanti svarabhedakāsahṛtplīhagulmagrahaṇīgadāṃśca
na cātra kiṃcitparivarjanīyaṃ rasāyanaṃ caitadupāsyamānam || 22
balā vidārī hrasvā ca pañcamūlī punarnavā
pañcānāṃ kṣīrivṛkṣāṇāṃ śṛṅgā muṣṭyaṃśakā api || 23
eṣāṃ kaṣāye dvikṣīre vidāryājarasāṃśake
jīvanīyaiḥ pacetkalkairakṣamātrairghṛtāḍhakam || 24
sitāpalāni pūte ca śīte dvātriṃśataṃ kṣipet
godhūmapippalīvāṃśīcūrṇaṃ śṛṅgāṭakasya ca || 25
samākṣikaṃ kauḍavikaṃ tatsarvaṃ khajamūrchitam
styānaṃ sarpirguḍānkṛtvā bhūrjapatreṇa veṣṭayet || 26
tāñjagdhvā pālikānkṣīraṃ madyaṃ cānu pibetkaphe
śoṣe kāse kṣatakṣīṇe śramastrībhārakarṣite || 27
raktaniṣṭhīvane tāpe pīnase corasaḥ kṣate
hitāḥ pārśvaśiraḥśūle bhede ca svaravarṇayoḥ
droṇe vāriguḍe cāsminnatha ṣoḍaśikaṃ jalam || 28
bilvāgnimanthaśyonākakāśmaryaḥ pāṭalī balā || 29
parṇyaścatasraḥ pippalyaḥ śvadaṃṣṭrā bṛhatīdvayam
śṛṅgī tāmalakī drākṣā jīvantī puṣkarāguruḥ || 30
abhayā cāmṛtā ṛddhirjīvakarṣabhakau śaṭhī
mustā punarnavā medā sūkṣmailotpalacandanam || 31
vidārī vṛṣamūlāni kākolī kākanāsikā
eṣāṃ palonmitānbhāgāñśatānyāmalakasya ca || 32
pañca dadyāttadaikadhyaṃ jaladroṇe vipācayet
jñātvā gatarasaṃ dravyaṃ rasaṃ gṛhṇīta gālitam || 33
taccā''malakamuddhṛtya niṣkulaṃ tailasarpiṣoḥ
paladvādaśake bhṛṣṭvā dattvā cārdhatulāṃ bhiṣak || 34
matsyaṇḍikāyāḥ pūtāyā lehavatsādhu sādhayet
ṣaṭpalaṃ madhunaścātra siddhaśīte pradāpayet || 35
catuṣpalaṃ tugākṣīryāḥ pippalyā dvipalaṃ tathā
palamekaṃ nidadhyācca tvagelāpatrakesarāt || 36
ityayaṃ cyavanaprāśaḥ paramukto rasāyane
kāsaśvāsaharaścaiva viśeṣeṇopadiśyate || 37
kṣīṇakṣatānāṃ vṛddhānāṃ bālānāṃ cāṅgavardhanaḥ
svarakṣayamurorogaṃ hṛdrogaṃ vātaśoṇitam || 38
pipāsāṃ mūtraśukrasthāndoṣāṃścaivāpakarṣati
asya mātrāṃ prayuñjīta yoparundhyānna bhojanam
cyavano'sya prayogācca suvṛddho'bhūtpunaryuvā || 39
medhāṃ smṛtiṃ kāntimanāmayatvamāyuṣprakarṣaṃ balamindriyāṇām
strīṣu praharṣaṃ paramāgnidīptiṃ varṇaprasādaṃ pavanānulomyam || 40
rasāyanasyāsya naraḥ prayogāllabheta jīrṇo'pi kuṭīpraveśāt
jarākṛtaṃ rūpamapāsya pūrvaṃ bibharti rūpaṃ navayauvanānām || 41
kecidicchanti matsyaṇḍyāḥ sthāne tu sitaśarkarām
mṛdukalkasamaḥ pāko bhṛṣṭadhātryāḥ praśasyate || 42
daśamūlīsvayaṃguptāśaṅkhapuṣpīśaṭhībalāḥ || 43
hastipippalyapāmārgapippalīmūlacitrakān
bhārṅgī puṣkaramūlaṃ ca dvipalāṃśaṃ yavāḍhakam || 44
harītakīśataṃ caiva jale pañcāḍhake pacet
yavaiḥ svinnaiḥ kaṣāyaṃ taṃ pūtaṃ tatrābhayāśatam || 45
pacedguḍatulāṃ dattvā kuḍavaṃ ca pṛthagghṛtāt
tailātsapippalīcūrṇātsiddhe śīte ca mākṣikāt || 46
kuḍavaṃ palamānaṃ ca cāturjātaṃ sucūrṇitam
lihyāddve cābhaye nityamataḥ khādedrasāyanāt || 47
tadvalīpalitaṃ hanyādvarṇāyurbalavardhanam
pañca kāsānkṣayaṃ śvāsaṃ hikkāṃ ca viṣamajvaram || 48
gulmamehagrahaṇyarśohṛdrogārucipīnasān
agastyabhihitaṃ dhanyamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rasāyanam || 49
yathoddiṣṭaguṇaṃ kurvatpittaṃ ca kurute yadi
tadā sāyaṃ guḍo yojya eṣa evālpamātrayā || 50
pādaśeṣe kaṣāye ca svinnāstāstā harītakīḥ
bharjitā ghṛtatailena pacedālohitodayāt || 51
jīvantīṃ kuṭajaṃ drākṣāṃ phalāni kuṭajasya ca
śaṭhīṃ puṣkaramūlaṃ ca vyāghrīṃ gokṣurakaṃ balām || 52
nīlotpalaṃ tāmalakīṃ trāyamāṇāṃ durālabhām
pippalīṃ ca samaṃ piṣṭvā ghṛtaṃ vaidyo vipācayet || 53
etadvyādhisamūhasya rogeśasya samutthitam
rūpamekādaśavidhaṃ sarpirugraṃ vyapohati || 54
pippalīguḍasaṃsiddhaṃ chāgakṣīrayutaṃ ghṛtam
etadagnivivṛddhyarthaṃ sarpiśca kṣayakāsinām || 55
pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
sayāvaśūkaiḥ sakṣīraiḥ srotasāṃ śodhanaṃ ghṛtam
[kalko'tra pādikaḥ kāryaḥ kṣīraṃ cāpi caturguṇam] || 56
yaṣṭībalāguḍūcyalpapañcamūlītulāṃ pacet || 57
śūrpe'pāmaṣṭabhāgasthe tatra pātraṃ pacedghṛtam
dhātrīvidārīkṣurasatripātre payaso'rmaṇe || 58
supiṣṭairjīvanīyaiśca pārāśaramidaṃ ghṛtam
