sasainyaṃ rājayakṣmāṇamunmūlayati śīlitam || 59
chāgamāṃsatulāṃ gṛhya sādhayennalvaṇe'mbhasi
pādaśeṣeṇa tenaiva sarpiḥ prasthaṃ vipācayet || 60
ṛddhirvṛddhiśca mede dve jīvakarṣabhakau tathā
kākolī kṣīrakākolī kalkaiḥ pṛthakpalonmitaiḥ || 61
samyaksiddhaṃ ca vijñāya śīte tasminpradāpayet
śarkarāyāḥ palānyaṣṭau madhunaḥ kuḍavaṃ kṣipet || 62
palaṃ palaṃ pibetprātaryakṣmāṇaṃ hanti dustaram
kṣatakṣayaṃ pañca kāsānpārśvaśūlamarocakam || 63
svarakṣayamurorogaṃ śvāsaṃ hanyātsudāruṇam
balyaṃ māṃsakaraṃ vṛṣyamagnisaṃdīpanaṃ param || 64
toyadroṇadvitaye māṃsaṃ chāgasya palaśataṃ paktvā
jalamaṣṭāṃśaṃ sukṛtaṃ tasminvipacedghṛtaprastham || 65
kalkena jīvanīyānāṃ kuḍavena tu māṃsasarpiridam
pittānilaṃ nihanyāttajjānapi rasakayojitaṃ pītam || 66
kāsaśvāsāvugrau yakṣmāṇaṃ pārśvahṛdrujaṃ ghorām
adhvavyavāyaśoṣaṃ śamayati nātaḥparaṃ kiṃcit || 67
dvipañcamūlasya pacetkaṣāye prasthadvaye māṃsarasasya caike
kalkaṃ balāyāḥ suniyojya garbhaṃ siddhaṃ payaḥprasthayutaṃ ghṛtaṃ ca
sarvābhighātotthitayakṣmaśūlakṣatakṣayaṃ kāsaharaṃ pradiṣṭam || 68
balāṃ śvadaṃṣṭrāṃ bṛhatīṃ kalaśīṃ dhāvanīṃ sthirām
nimbaṃ parpaṭakaṃ mustaṃ trāyamāṇāṃ durālabhām || 69
kṛtvā kaṣāyaṃ peṣyārthaṃ dadyāttāmalakīṃ śaṭhīm
drākṣāṃ puṣkaramūlaṃ ca medāmāmalakāni ca || 70
ghṛtaṃ payasi tatsiddhaṃ sarpirjvaraharaṃ param
kṣayakāsapraśamanaṃ śiraḥpārśvarujāpaham || 71
candanāmbunakhaṃ vāpyaṃ yaṣṭīśaileyapadmakam || 72
mañjiṣṭhā saralaṃ dāru śaṭhyelāpūtikesaram
patraṃ cailaṃ murā māṃsī kaṅkolaṃ vanitā'mbudam || 73
haridre sārive tiktālavaṅgāgurukuṅkumam
tvagreṇunalikā caibhistailaṃ mastucaturguṇam || 74
lākṣārasasamaṃ siddhaṃ grahaghnaṃ balavardhanam
apasmārajvaronmādapāpālakṣmīvināśanam
āyuṣpuṣṭikaraṃ caiva vaśīkaraṇamuttamam || 75
jvarasaṃtāpadaurbalye lākṣātailaṃ prayojayet
bālarogādhikāroktaṃ pāraṃparyopadeśataḥ || 76
balā'śvagandhā śrīparṇī bahupatrī punarnavā
payasā nityamabhyastāḥ śamayanti kṣatakṣayam || 77
ikṣvālikāviśvagandhāpadmakesaracandanaiḥ
ghṛtaṃ payomadhuyutaṃ saṃdhānārthaṃ pibetkṣayī || 78
ghṛtaṃ balānāgabalārjunāmbusiddhaṃ sayaṣṭīmadhupādakalkam
hṛdrogaśūlakṣataraktapittaśoṣānilāsṛkśamayatyudīrṇam || 79
hitamatra viśeṣeṇa khaṇḍakūṣmāṇḍasaṃjñitam || 80
śukrāyattaṃ balaṃ puṃsāṃ malāyattaṃ hi jīvitam
tasmādyatnena saṃrakṣedyakṣmiṇo malaretasī || 81
nityaṃ svadehapūjī bhakto bhaiṣajyadevatāguruṣu
chāgaṃ māṃsapayo'śnañjīvati yakṣmī ciraṃ dhṛtimān || 82
upadravāṃśca svaravaikṛtādīñjayedyathāsvaṃ prasamīkṣya śāstram
śokaṃ striyaḥ krodhamasūyanaṃ ca tyaje-
dudārānviṣayānbhajecca
gurūndvijātīṃstridaśāṃśca pūjaye-
tkathāśca puṇyāḥ śṛṇuyāddvijebhyaḥ || 83
kāsātisārapārśvārtisvarabhedārucijvaraiḥ
tribhirvā pīḍitaṃ liṅgaiḥ śvāsakāsāsṛgāmayaiḥ || 84
bhaktadveṣo jvaraḥ śvāsaḥ kāsaḥ śoṇitadarśanam
svarabhedaśca jāyeta ṣaḍrūpe rājayakṣmaṇi || 85
paraṃ dinasahasraṃ tu yadi jīvati mānavaḥ
subhiṣagbhirupakrāntastaruṇaḥ śoṣapīḍitaḥ || 86
Adhikāra 11
pañcamūlīkṛtaḥ kvāthaḥ pippalīcūrṇasaṃyutaḥ
rasānnamaśnato nityaṃ vātakāsamudasyati || 1
bhārṅgīdrākṣāśaṭhīśṛṅgīpippalīviśvabheṣajaiḥ
guḍatailayuto leho hito mārutakāsinām || 2
cūrṇitā viśvaduḥsparśāśṛṅgīdrākṣāśaṭhīsitāḥ
līḍhvā tailena vātotthaṃ kāsaṃ jayati dustaram || 3
balādvibṛhatīvāsādrākṣābhiḥ kvathitaṃ jalam
pittakāsaharaṃ peyaṃ śarkarāmadhuyojitam || 4
śarādipañcamūlasya pippalīdrākṣayostathā
kaṣāyeṇa śṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ pibetsamadhuśarkaram || 5
drākṣāmadhukakharjūrapippalīmaricānvitam
pittakāsaharaṃ hyetallihyānmākṣikasarpiṣā || 6
kharjūrapippalīdrākṣāsitālājāḥ samāṃśakāḥ
madhusarpiryuto lehaḥ pittakāsaharaḥ paraḥ || 7
mudgāmalābhyāṃ yavadāḍimābhyāṃ karkandhunā mūlakaśuṇṭhakena
śuṇṭhīkaṇābhyāṃ ca kulatthakena yūṣo navāṅgaḥ kaphakāsahantā || 8
pauṣkaraṃ kaṭphalaṃ bhārṅgīpippalīviśvasādhitam
pibetkvāthaṃ kaphodreke kāse śvāse ca hṛdgrahe || 9
svarasaṃ śṛṅgaverasya mākṣikeṇa samanvitam
pāyayecchvāsakāsaghnaṃ pratiśyāyakaphāpaham || 10
pārśvaśūle jvare śvāse kāse śleṣmasamudbhave
pippalīcūrṇasaṃyuktaṃ daśamūlījalaṃ pibet || 11
kaṭphalaṃ kattṛṇaṃ bhārṅgī mustaṃ dhānyavacābhayāḥ
śuṇṭhī parpaṭakaṃ śṛṅgī surāhvaṃ ca jale śṛtam || 12
madhuhiṅguyutaṃ peyaṃ kāse vātakaphātmake
kaṇṭharogeṣu mukhyeṣu śvāsahikkājvareṣu ca || 13
tālīsapatraṃ maricaṃ nāgaraṃ pippalī śubhā
yathottaraṃ bhāgavṛddhyā tvagele cārdhabhāgike || 14
pippalyaṣṭaguṇā cātra pradeyā sitaśarkarā
śvāsakāsārucicchardiplīhatdṛtpārśvaśūlanut || 15
pāṇḍujvarātisāraghnaṃ mūḍhavātānulomanam
kalpayedguṭikāṃ caiva cūrṇaṃ paktvā sitopalām || 16
guṭikā hyagnisaṃyogāccūrṇāllaghutarāḥ smṛtāḥ
vātaśleṣmakṛte kāse tālīsādyaṃ prayojayet
pittayukte bhavecchreṣṭhaṃ vaṃśarocanayā'nvitam || 17
vāsakasvarasaḥ peyo madhuyukto hitāśinā
pittaśleṣmakṛte kāse raktapitte viśeṣataḥ || 18
kāse ca kṣataje balyairbṛṃhaṇairjīvanairapi
śamanaṃ pittakāsoktairanyaiśca madhurauṣadhaiḥ || 19
madhukaṃ pippalī drākṣā lākṣā śṛṅgī śatāvarī || 20
dviguṇā ca tugākṣīrī sitā sarvaiścaturguṇā
lehayenmadhusarpirbhyāṃ kṣatakāsanivṛttaye || 21
pippalī padmakaṃ lākṣā saṃpakvaṃ bṛhatīphalam
ghṛtakṣaudrayuto lehaḥ kṣatakāsanibarhaṇaḥ || 22
mukhe dhṛtā'bhayā śuṇṭhī kaṇayā vā vibhītakam
vibhītakamathaikaṃ vā kāsaśvāsau vyapohati || 23
pathyāśuṇṭhīghanaguḍairguṭikāṃ dhārayenmukhe
sarveṣu śvāsakāseṣu kevalaṃ vā vibhītakam || 24
manaḥśilāle maricamāṃsīmusteṅgudaiḥ pibet
dhūmaṃ tasyānu ca payaḥ sukhoṣṇaṃ saguḍaṃ pibet || 25
eṣa kāsānpṛthagdvaṃdvasarvadoṣasamutthitān
śatairapi prayogāṇāmasādhyānsādhayeddrutam || 26
manaḥśilāliptadalaṃ badaryātapaśoṣitam
sakṣīraṃ dhūmapānaṃ ca mahākāsanivāraṇam || 27
karṣaḥ karṣāṃśapalaṃ paladvayaṃ syāttato'rdhakarṣaśca
maricasya pippalīnāṃ dāḍimaguḍayāvaśūkānām || 28
sarvauṣadhairasādhyā ye kāsāḥ sarvavaidyavinirmuktāḥ
api pūyaṃ chardayatāṃ teṣāmidamauṣadhaṃ paramam || 29
arkacchallīśile tulye tato'rdhena kaṭutrikam
cūrṇitaṃ vahninikṣiptaṃ pibeddhūmaṃ ca yogavit || 30
bhakṣayedatha tāmbūlaṃ pibeddugdhamathāmbu vā
kāsāḥ pañcavidhā yānti śāntimāśu na saṃśayaḥ
kaṇṭakārīkṛtaḥ kvāthaḥ sakṛṣṇaḥ sarvakāsahā || 31
lavaṅgajātīphalapippalīnāṃ bhāgānprakalpyākṣasamānamīṣām
palārdhamekaṃ maricasya dadyātpalāni catvāri mahauṣadhasya || 32
sitāsamaṃ cūrṇamidaṃ prasahya rogāṃstvimānāśu balānnihanti
kāsajvarārocakamehagulmaśvāsāgnimāndyagrahaṇīpradoṣān || 33
daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa bhārṅgīkalkaṃ pacedghṛtam
dakṣatittiriniryūhe tatparaṃ vātakāsajit || 34
daśamūlāḍhake prasthaṃ ghṛtasyākṣasamaiḥ pacet
puṣkarāhvaśaṭhībilvasurasavyoṣahiṅgubhiḥ || 35
payonupānaṃ tatpeyaṃ kāse vātakaphātmake
śvāsarogeṣu sarveṣu kaphavātātmakeṣu ca || 36
samūlapatraśākhāyāḥ kaṇṭakāryā rasāḍhake || 37
ghṛtaprasthe balāvyoṣaviḍaṅgaśaṭhicitrakaiḥ
sauvarcalayavakṣārabilvāmalakapauṣkaraiḥ || 38
vṛścīva(ra)bṛhatīpathyāyavānīdāḍimarddhibhiḥ
drākṣāpunarnavācavyadurālambhāmlavetasaiḥ || 39
śṛṅgītāmalakībhārṅgīrāsnāgokṣurakaiḥ pacet
kalkaistatsarvakāseṣu hikkāśvāseṣu śasyate
kaṇṭakārīghṛtaṃ sarvakaphavyādhivināśanam || 40
kaṇṭakārīguḍūcībhyāṃ pṛthak triṃśatpale rase
prasthaḥ siddho ghṛtādvātakāsanudvahnidīpanaḥ || 41
droṇe'pāṃ sādhayedrāsnādaśamūlīśatāvarīḥ || 42
palikānmānikāṃśāṃstrīnkulatthaṃ badaraṃ yavam
tulārdhaṃ chāgamāṃsasya pādaśeṣeṇa tena ca || 43
ghṛtāḍhakaṃ samakṣīraṃ jīvanīyaiḥ palonmitaiḥ
siddhaṃ taddaśabhiḥ kalkairnasyapānānuvāsanaiḥ || 44
sarveṣu vātarogeṣu yathāvasthaṃ prayojayet
pañca kāsāñchiraḥkampaṃ yonivaṅkṣaṇavedanām || 45
sarvāṅgaikāṅgarogāṃśca saplīhordhvānilāñjayet
tathā śvāsaṃ kṣayaṃ caiva pārśvaśūlamarocakam || 46
jīvakarṣabhakau mede kākolyau madhukaṃ sahe
jīvantī jīvanīyo'yaṃ madhuro jīvano gaṇaḥ || 47
ghṛtaṃ rāsnābalāvyoṣaśvadaṃṣṭrākalkapācitam
kaṇṭakārīrase pānātpañcakāsaniṣūdanam || 48
daśamūlīcatuṣprasthe rase prasthonmitaṃ haviḥ
sakṣāraiḥ pañcakolaistu kalkitaṃ sādhu sādhitam
kāsahṛtpārśvaśūlaghnaṃ hikkāśvāsanibarhaṇam || 49
tālīsavahnidīpyakacavikāmlavetasavyoṣaiḥ
tulyaistrisugandhiyutairguḍena guṭikā prakartavyā || 50
kāsaśvāsārocakapīnasahṛtkaṇṭhavāgvirodheṣu
grahaṇīgudodbhaveṣu ca guṭikā vyoṣāntikā nāma || 51
Adhikāra 12
kolamajjāñjanaṃ lājātiktākāñcanagairikam
kṛṣṇā dhātrī sitā śuṇṭhī kāsīsaṃ dadhināma ca || 1
pāṭalyāḥ saphalaṃ puṣpaṃ kṛṣṇā kharjūramustakam
ṣaḍete pādikā lehā hikkāghnā madhusaṃyutāḥ || 2
madhukaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ pippalī śarkarānvitā
nāgaraṃ guḍasaṃyuktaṃ hikkāghnaṃ nāvanatrayam || 3
stanyena makṣikāviṣṭhā nasyaṃ vā'laktakāmbunā
yojyaṃ hikkābhibhūtāya stanyaṃ vā candanānvitam || 4
madhusauvarcalopetaṃ mātuluṅgarasaṃ pibet
hikkārto madhunā lihyācchuṇṭhīdhātrīkaṇānvitam || 5
kṛṣṇāmalakaśuṇṭhīnāṃ cūrṇaṃ madhusitāyutam
muhurmuhuḥ prayoktavyaṃ hikkāśvāsanivāraṇam || 6
prāṇāvarodhatarjanavismāpanaśītavāripariṣekaiḥ
citraiḥ kathāprayogaiḥ śamayeddhikkāṃ manobhighātaiśca || 7
hikkāśvāsāture pūrvaṃ tailākte sveda iṣyate
ūrdhvādhaḥ śodhanaṃ śakte durbale śamanaṃ matam || 8
hikkāśvāsī pibedbhārṅgīṃ saviśvāmuṣṇavāriṇā
nāgaraṃ vā sitābhārṅgīsauvarcalasamanvitam || 9
abhayānāgarakalkaṃ puṣkarayavaśūkamaricakalkaṃ vā
toyenoṣṇena pibeddhikkī śvāsī ca tacchāntyai || 10
tṛṣito daśamūlasya kvāthaṃ vā devadāruṇaḥ
madirāṃ vā pibedyuktyā hikkāśvāsaprapīḍitaḥ || 11
śṛṅgīkaṭutrikaphalatrikakaṇṭakārībhārṅgīsapuṣkarajaṭā lavaṇāni pañca
cūrṇaṃ pibedaśiśireṇa jalena hikkāśvāsordhvavātakasanārucipīnaseṣu || 12
śaṭhīcorakajīvantītvaṅmustaṃ puṣkarāhvayam
surasastāmalakyelāpippalyagurunāgaram || 13
vālakaṃ ca samaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā'ṣṭaguṇaśarkaram
sarvadā tamake śvāse hikkāyāṃ ca prayojayet || 14
kāsamardakapatrāṇāṃ yūṣaḥ saubhāñjanasya ca
śuṣkamūlakayūṣaśca hikkāśvāsanivāraṇaḥ || 15
yatkiṃcitkaphavātaghnamuṣṇaṃ vātānulomanam
bheṣajaṃ pānamannaṃ vā hikkāśvāseṣu taddhitam || 16
amṛtānāgaraphañjīvyāghrīparṇāsasādhitaḥ kvāthaḥ
pītaḥ sakaṇācūrṇaḥ kāsaśvāsau jayatyāśu || 17
sanāgarābhayātulyā kāsaśvāsau vyapohati
daśamūlīkaṣāyaśca puṣkareṇāvacūrṇitaḥ || 18
kāsaśvāsapraśamanaḥ pārśvahṛcchūlanāśanaḥ
kulatthanāgaravyāghrīvāsābhiḥ kkathitaṃ jalam
pītaṃ puṣkarasaṃyuktaṃ śvāsakāsanivāraṇam || 19
guḍaṃ kaṭukatailena miśrayitvā samaṃ lihet
trisaptāhaprayogeṇa śvāsaṃ nirmūlato jayet || 20
drākṣāharītakīkṛṣṇākarkaṭākhyādurālabhāḥ
vilihanmadhusarpirbhyāṃ śvāsānhanti sudāruṇān || 21
hiṃsrāviḍaṅgapūtīkatriphalāvyoṣacitrakaiḥ || 22
dvikṣīraṃ sarpiṣaḥ prasthaṃ caturguṇajalānvitam
kolamātraiḥ pacettaddhi śvāsakāsau vyapohati || 23
arśāṃsyarocakaṃ gulmaṃ śakṛdbhedaṃ kṣayaṃ tathā
kolasthāne'tra karṣaḥ syādanyatodṛṣṭahetunā || 24
śataṃ gṛhītvā bhārṅgyāstu daśamūlyāstathā param
śataṃ harītakīnāṃ ca pacettoye caturguṇe || 25
pādāvaśeṣe tasmiṃstu rase vastraparisrute
āloḍya ca tulāṃ pūtāṃ guḍasya tvabhayāṃ tataḥ || 26
punaḥ pacecca mṛdvagnau yāvallehatvamāgatam
śīte ca madhunaścātra ṣaṭpalāni pradāpayet || 27
trikaṭu trisugandhaṃ ca pālikaṃ ca pṛthakpṛthak
karṣadvayaṃ yavakṣāraṃ saṃcūrṇya prakṣipettataḥ || 28
bhakṣayedabhayāmekāṃ lehasyārdhapalaṃ lihet
śvāsaṃ sudāruṇaṃ hanti kāsaṃ pañcavidhaṃ tathā || 29
svaravarṇaprado leho jaṭharāgnipradīpanaḥ
harītakīśataikasya vāriprasthamihādhikam || 30
kulattho daśamūlaṃ ca tathaiva dvijayaṣṭikā
śataṃ śataṃ ca saṃgṛhya jaladroṇe vipācayet || 31
pādāvaśeṣe tasmiṃstu guḍasyārdhatulāṃ kṣipet
śītībhūte ca pakve ca madhuno'ṣṭau palāni ca || 32
ṣaṭpalāni tugākṣīryāḥ pippalyāśca paladvayam
trisugandhisugandhaṃ taṃ khādedagnibalaṃ prati || 33
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ jvaraṃ hikkāṃ nāśayettamakaṃ tathā
yogasaṃdarśanādatra vṛddhavaidyopadeśataḥ || 34
jalaṃ caturguṇaṃ deyamalpatvāddroṇavāriṇaḥ
mānasāṃnidhyasaṃvādāddvipalaṃ trisugandhinaḥ || 35
Adhikāra 13
ādyo koṣṇajalaṃ peyaṃ jagdhvā ghṛtaguḍaudanam
kṣīrānupānaṃ pittotthe pibetsarpiratandritaḥ || 1
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ maricaṃ viśvabheṣajam
pibenmūtreṇa matimānkaphaje svarasaṃkṣaye
svaropaghāte medotthe kaphavadvidhiriṣyate || 2
kṣayaje sarvaje vā'pi pratyākhyāyā''caretkriyām || 3
cavyāmlavetasakaṭutrikatittiḍīkatālīsajīrakatugādahanaiḥ samāṃśaiḥ
cūrṇaṃ guḍapramṛditaṃ trisugandhiyuktaṃ vaisvaryapīnasakaphāruciṣu praśastam || 4
badarīpatrakalkaṃ vā ghṛtabhṛṣṭaṃ sasaindhavam
svaropaghāte kāse ca lehamenaṃ prayojayet || 5
śarkarāmadhumiśrāṇi śṛtāni madhuraiḥ saha
pibetpayāṃsi yasyoccairvadato'bhihataḥ svaraḥ || 6
vyāghrīsvarasavipakvaṃ rāsnāvāṭyālagokṣuravyoṣaiḥ
sarpiḥ svaropaghātaṃ hanyātkāsaṃ ca pañcavidham || 7
śuṣkadravyamupādāya svarasānāmasaṃbhave
udake'ṣṭaguṇe sādhyaṃ grāhyaṃ pādāvaśeṣitam || 8
Adhikāra 14
vānto vacādbhiranile vidhivatpibecca snehoṣṇatoyamadirānyatamena cūrṇam
kṛṣṇāviḍaṅgayavabhasmahareṇubhārṅgīrāsnailahiṅgulavaṇottamanāgarāṇām || 1
pitte guḍāmbumadhurairvamanaṃ praśastaṃ lehaḥ sasaindhavasitāmadhusarpiriṣṭaḥ
nimbāmbuvāmitavataḥ kaphaje'nupānaṃ rājadrumāmbu madhunā saha dīpyakāḍhyam
cūrṇaṃ yaduktamathavā'nilaje tadeva
sarvaiśca sarvakṛtamevamupakrameta || 2
bastiṃ samīraṇe pitte virekaṃ vamanaṃ kaphe
kuryāddhṛdyānukūlāni harṣaṇaṃ ca manoghnaje || 3
icchāvināśabhayajeṣu tu bādhakeṣu
bhāvānbhavāya vitaretkhalu śakyarūpān
artheṣu cātipatiteṣu punarbhavāya paurāṇikaiḥ śrutipathairanumānayettam || 4
sātmyānsvadeśacaritānvividhāṃśca bhakṣyā-
npānāni mūlaphalakhāḍavarāgalehān
sevedrasāṃśca vividhānvividhaiḥ prayogai-
rbhuñjīta cāpi laghurūkṣamanaḥsukhāni || 5
amlikā guḍatoyaṃ ca tvagelāmaricānvitam
abhaktacchandarogeṣu śastaṃ kavaladhāraṇam || 6
kuṣṭhasauvarcalājājīśarkarāmaricaṃ biḍam || 7
dhātryelāpadmakośīrapippalīcandanotpalam
lodhraṃ tejovatī pathyā tryūṣaṇaṃ sayavāgrajam || 8
ārdradāḍimaniryāsaścājājīśarkarāyutam
satailamākṣikāścaite catvāraḥ kavalagrahāḥ
caturo'rocakānhanyurvātādyekajasarvajān || 9
trīṇyūṣaṇāni triphalā rajanīdvayaṃ ca cūrṇīkṛtāni yavaśūkavimiśritāni
kṣaudrānvitāni vitarenmukhadhāvanārthamanyāni tiktakaṭukāni ca bheṣajāni || 10
kāravyajājīmaricaṃ drākṣāvṛkṣāmladāḍimam
sauvarcalaguḍakṣaudraṃ sarvārocakanāśanam || 11
yavānī tittiḍīkaṃ ca nāgaraṃ cāmlavetasam || 12
dāḍimaṃ badaraṃ cāmlaṃ kārṣikāṇyupakalpayet
dhānyasauvarcalājājīvarāṅgaṃ cārdhakārṣikam || 13
pippalīnāṃ śataṃ caikaṃ dve śate maricasya ca
śarkarāyāśca catvāri palānyekatra cūrṇayet || 14
jihvāviśodhanaṃ hṛdyaṃ taccūrṇaṃ bhaktarocanam
hṛtplīhapārśvaśūlaghnaṃ vibandhānāhanāśanam
kāsaśvāsaharaṃ grāhi grahaṇyarśovikāranut || 15
Adhikāra 15
āmāśayotkleśabhavā hi sarvāśchardyo matā laṅghanameva tasmāt
prākkārayenmārutajāṃ vimucya saṃśodhanaṃ vā kaphapittahāri || 1
hanyātkṣīrodakaṃ pītaṃ chardiṃ pavanasaṃbhavām
sasaindhavaṃ pibetsarpirvātacchardinivāraṇam || 2
mudgāmalakayūṣo vā sasarpiṣkaḥ sasaindhavaḥ
yavāgūṃ madhumiśrāṃ vā pañcamūlīkṛtāṃ pibet || 3
pittātmikāyāmanulomanārthaṃ drākṣāvidārīkṣurasaistrivṛtsyāt
kaphāśayasthaṃ tvatimātravṛddhaṃ pittaṃ haretsvādubhirūrdhvameva || 4
śuddhasya kāle madhuśarkarābhyāṃ lājaiśca manthaṃ yadi vā'pi peyām
pradāpayenmudgarasena vā'pi śālyodanaṃ jāṅgalajai rasairvā || 5
candanenākṣamātreṇa saṃyojyā''malakīrasam
pibenmākṣikasaṃyuktaṃ chardistena nivartate || 6
kaṣāyo bhṛṣṭamudgasya salājamadhuśarkaraḥ
chardyatīsāratṛḍdāhajvaraghnaḥ saṃprakāśitaḥ || 7
harītakīnāṃ cūrṇaṃ ca lihyānmākṣikasaṃyutam
adhobhāgīkṛte doṣe chardiḥ kṣipraṃ nivartate || 8
kvāthaḥ parpaṭajaḥ pītaḥ sakṣaudraśchardināśanaḥ
guḍūcītriphalāriṣṭapaṭolīkvathitaṃ jalam
sakṣaudraṃ śarkarāyuktaṃ chardipittāmlanāśanam || 9
kaphātmikāyāṃ vamanaṃ praśastaṃ sapippalīsarṣapanimbatoyaiḥ
piṇḍītakaiḥ saindhavasaṃprayuktairbalāvirodhe kaphaśodhanārtham || 10
viḍaṅgatriphalāviśvacūrṇaṃ madhuyutaṃ pibet
viḍaṅgaplavaśuṇṭhīnāṃ haredvā śleṣmajāṃ vamim || 11
sajāmbavaṃ vā badarasya cūrṇaṃ mustāyutāṃ karkaṭakasya śṛṅgīm
durālabhāṃ vā madhusaṃprayuktāṃ lihyātkaphacchardivinigrahārtham || 12
tarpaṇaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ tisṛṇāmatha bheṣajam || 13
kṛtaṃ guḍūcyā vidhivatkaṣāyaṃ himasaṃjñitam
tisṛṣvapi pibetpathyaṃ mākṣikeṇa samāyutam || 14
śrīphalasya guḍucyā vā kaṣāyo madhusaṃyutaḥ
peyaścharditraye śīto mūrvā vā taṇḍulāmbunā || 15
jambvāmrapallavagavedhukadhānyasevya-
hrīveravāri madhunā pibato'lpamalpam
chardiḥ prayāti śamanaṃ trisugandhiyuktā
līḍhā nihanti madhunā ca durālabhā vā || 16
jātyā rasaḥ kapitthasya pippalīmaricānvitaḥ
kṣaudreṇa yukto leho'yaṃ nihanti cchardimulbaṇām || 17
sauvarcalamajājī ca śarkarā maricāni ca
yukto'yaṃ madhunā lehaḥ śreṣṭhaśchardinibarhaṇaḥ || 18
kolamajjākaṇādhātrīlājāviśvaphalatrikam
śyāmāñjanābdakolāsthi makṣikāviṭsitāyutam || 19
kaṇoṣaṇakapitthāmbutvagelāpatrakaṃ samam
sakṣaudrāḥ pādikā lehāḥ ṣaḍete chardināśanāḥ || 20
kolānalakamajjāno makṣikāviṭsitā madhu
sakṛṣṇātaṇḍulo lehaśchardimāśu niyacchati || 21
lājākapitthamadhumāgadhikoṣaṇānāṃ
kṣaudrābhayātrikaṭudhānyakajīrakāṇām
pathyāmṛtāmaricamākṣikapippalīnāṃ
lehāstrayaḥ sakalavamyarucipraśāntyai || 22
elālavaṅgagajakesarakolamajjā-
lājāpriyaṅgughanacandanapippalīnām
cūrṇāni mākṣikasitāsahitāni līḍhvā
chardiṃ nihanti kaphamārutapittajātām || 23
jambvāmrapallavaśṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ dattvā suśītalaṃ toyam
lājairavacūrṇya pibecchardyatisāre paraṃ siddham || 24
pavanaghnīcirotthāsu prayojyā chardiṣu kriyā || 25
chardiprasaṅgātpavano hyavaśyaṃ dhātukṣayādvṛddhimupaiti tasmāt
cirapravṛttāsvanilāpahāni kāryāṇyupastambhanabṛṃhaṇāni || 26
sarpirguḍaḥ kṣīravimiśritāni kalyāṇakatryūṣaṇajīvanāni
vṛṣyāstathā māṃsarasāḥ salehāśchardiṃ prasaktāṃ praśamaṃ nayanti || 27
vībhatsajāṃ hṛdyatamairdauhṛdīṃ kāṅkṣitaiḥ phalaiḥ
laṅghanairvamanaiścā''māṃ sātmyairvā sātmyakopajām
kṛmihṛdrogavaccāpi sādhayetkṛmijāṃ vamim || 28
padmakāmṛtanimbānāṃ dhānyacandanayoḥ pacet
kalke kkāthe ca haviṣaḥ prasthaṃ chardinivāraṇam
tṛṣṇārucipraśamanaṃ dāhajvaraharaṃ param || 29
Adhikāra 16
tṛṣṇāyāṃ pavanotthāyāṃ saguḍaṃ dadhi śasyate
rasāśca bṛṃhaṇāḥ śītā guḍūcyā rasa eva ca || 1
pañcāṅgikāḥ pañca gaṇā ya uktāsteṣvambu siddhaṃ prathame gaṇe vā
pibetsukhoṣṇaṃ manujo'lpaśaśca tṛṣṇoparodhaṃ na kadā'pi kuryāt
pittotthitāṃ pittaharairvipakvaṃ nihanti toyaṃ paya eva vā'pi || 2
kāśmaryaśarkarāyuktaṃ candanośīrapadmakam
drākṣāmadhukasaṃyuktaṃ pittatarṣe jalaṃ pibet || 3
sasārivādau tṛṇapañcamūlaistathotpalādau madhure gaṇe vā
kuryātkaṣāyāṃstu tathaiva yuktānmadhūkapuṣpādiṣu cāpareṣu || 4
bilvāḍhakīdhātakipañcakoladarbheṣu siddhaṃ kaphajāṃ nihanti
hitaṃ bhaveccharditameva cātra taptena nimbaprasavodakena || 5
sajīrakāṇyārdrakaśṛṅgaberasauvarcalānyardhajalaplutāni
madyāni hṛdyāni ca gandhavanti pītāni sadyaḥ śamayanti tṛṣṇām || 6
kṣatodbhavāṃ rugvinivāraṇena jayedrasānāmasṛjaśca pānaiḥ
kṣayotthitāṃ kṣīrajalaṃ nihanyānmāṃsodakaṃ vā'tha madhūdakaṃ vā || 7
gurvannajāmullikhanairjayettu kṣayādṛte sarvakṛtāṃ ca tṛṣṇām || 8
lājodakaṃ madhuyutaṃ ghṛtaṃ guḍavimiśritam
kāśmaryaśarkarāyuktaṃ pibettṛṣṇārdito naraḥ || 9
āmrajambūkaṣāyaṃ vā pibenmākṣikasaṃyutam
chardiṃ sarvāṃ praṇudati tṛṣṇāṃ caivāpakarṣati || 10
gostanekṣurasakṣīrayaṣṭīmadhumadhūtpalaiḥ
niyataṃ nastataḥ (nāsayā) pītaistṛṣṇā śāmyati dāruṇā || 11
kṣīrekṣurasamārdvīkakṣaudrasīdhuguḍodakaiḥ
vṛkṣāmlāmlaiśca gaṇḍūṣāstāluśoṣapraṇāśanāḥ || 12
vaiśadyaṃ janayatyāsye prarohayati yadvraṇān
dāhatṛṣṇāpraśamanaṃ madhugaṇḍūṣadhāraṇam || 13
vāri śītaṃ madhuyutamākaṇṭhācca pipāsitam
pāyayedvāmayeccāpi tena tṛṣṇā praśāmyati || 14
atirūkṣadurbalānāṃ śamayettṛṣṇāmihā''śu payaḥ
chāgo vā ghṛtabhṛṣṭaḥ śīto madhuro raso hṛdyaḥ || 15
snigdhe'nne bhukte yā tṛṣṇā syāttāṃ guḍāmbunā śamayet || 16
vaṭaśṛṅgasitālodhraṃ dāḍimaṃ madhukaṃ madhu
pibettaṇḍulatoyena ccharditṛṣṇānivāraṇam || 17
tāluśoṣe pibetsarpirghṛtamaṇḍamathāpi vā
mūrchācharditṛṣādāhastrīmadyaśramakarśitāḥ
pibeyuḥ śītalaṃ toyaṃ raktapitte madātyaye || 18
dhānyāmlaṃ mukhavairasyamaladaurgandhyanāśanam
tadevālavaṇaṃ śītaṃ mukhaśoṣaharaṃ param || 19
koladāḍimavṛkṣāmlacukrīkācukrikārasaḥ
pañcāmlako mukhālepaḥ sadyastṛṣṇāṃ niyacchati || 20
vaṭaprarohaṃ madhukuṣṭhamutpalaṃ salājacūrṇaṃ guṭikāṃ prakalpayet
susaṃhitā sā vadanena dhāritā tṛṣṇāṃ pravṛddhāmapi hanti satvaram || 21
odanaṃ raktaśālīnāṃ śītaṃ mākṣikasaṃyutam
bhojayettena śāmyete charditṛṣṇe cirotthite || 22
tṛṣyanpūrvāmayakṣīṇo na labheta jalaṃ yadi
maraṇaṃ dīrgharogaṃ vā prāpnuyāttvaritaṃ naraḥ || 23
sātmyānnapānabhaiṣajyaistṛṣṇāṃ tasya jayetpunaḥ
tasyāṃ jitāyāmanyo'pi vyādhiḥ śakyaścikitsitum || 24
tṛṣito mohamāyāti mohātprāṇānvimuñcati
tasmātsarvāsvavasthāsu na kvacidvāri vāryate || 25
Adhikāra 17
sekāvagāhau maṇayaḥ sahārāḥ śītāḥ pradehā vyajanānilāśca
śītāni pānāni ca gandhavanti sarvāsu mūrchāsvanivāritāni || 1
siddhāni varge madhure payāṃsi sadāḍimā jāṅgalajā rasāśca
tathā yavā lohitaśālayaśca mūrchāsu śastāḥ sasatīnamudgāḥ || 2
yathādoṣaṃ kaṣāyāṇi jvaraghnāni prayojayet
raktajāyāṃ tu mūrchāyāṃ hitaḥ śītakriyāvidhiḥ || 3
madyajāyāṃ vamenmadyaṃ nidrāṃ sevedyathāsukham
viṣajāyāṃ viṣaghnāni bheṣajāni prayojayet || 4
kolamajjoṣaṇośīrakesaraṃ śītavāriṇā
pittamūrchāṃ jayellīḍhā kṛṣṇā vā madhusaṃyutā || 5
mahauṣadhāmṛtā kṣudrā pauṣkaraṃ granthikodbhavam
pibetkaṇāyutaṃ kvāthaṃ mūrchāyāṃ ca madeṣu ca || 6
pibeddurālabhākvāthaṃ saghṛtaṃ bhramaśāntaye
triphalāyāḥ prayogo vā prayogaḥ payaso'pi vā
rasāyanānāṃ kaumbhasya sarpiṣo vā praśasyate || 7
añjanānyavapīḍāśca dhūmaḥ pradhamanāni ca
sūcībhistodanaṃ śastaṃ dāhaḥ pīḍā nakhāntare || 8
luñcanaṃ keśalomnāṃ ca dantairdaśanameva ca
ātmaguptāvagharṣaśca hitastasyāvabodhane || 9
Adhikāra 18
manthaḥ kharjūramṛdvīkāvṛkṣāmlāmlikadāḍimaiḥ
parūṣakaiḥ sāmalakairyukto madyavikāranut || 1
satīnamudgasaṃmiśrāndāḍimāmalakānvitān
drākṣāmalakakharjūraparūṣakarasena vā
kalpayettarpaṇānyūṣānrasāṃśca vividhātmakān || 2
pathyākvāthena vā siddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ dhātrīrasena vā
sarpiḥ kalyāṇakaṃ vā'pi madamūrchāpahaṃ pibet || 3
chardimūrchātisāraṃ ca madaṃ pūgaphalodbhavam
sadyastacchamayetpītamātṛptervāri śītalam || 4
vanyakarīṣaghrāṇājjalapānāllavaṇabhakṣaṇādvā'pi
śāmyati pūgaphalamadaścūrṇarujāśarkarākavalāt || 5
madyaṃ sauvarcalavyoṣayuktaṃ kiṃcijjalānvitam
jīrṇamadye pradātavyaṃ vātapānātyayāpaham || 6
mudgayūṣaḥ sitāyuktaḥ svādurvā paiśito rasaḥ
pittapānātyaye yojyāḥ sarvataśca kriyā himāḥ || 7
pānaroge kaphodbhūte laṅghanaṃ ca yathābalam
dīpanīyauṣadhopetaṃ pibenmadyaṃ samāhitaḥ || 8
sarvaje sarvamevedaṃ prayoktavyaṃ cikitsitam || 9
ābhiḥ kriyābhiḥ siddhābhiḥ śāntiṃ yāti madātyayaḥ || 10
na cenmadyakramaṃ muktvā kṣīramasya prayojayet
laṅghanādyaiḥ kaphe kṣīṇe jāte daurbalyalāghave || 11
ojastulyaguṇaṃ kṣīraṃ viparītaṃ ca madyataḥ
kṣīraprayogaṃ madyaṃ ca krameṇālpālpamācaret || 12
payaḥpunarnavākvāthayaṣṭīkalkaprasādhitam
ghṛtaṃ puṣṭikaraṃ pānānmadyapānahataujasām || 13
sauvarcalamajājyaśca vṛkṣāmlaṃ sāmlavetasam
tvagelāmaricārdhāṃśaṃ śarkarābhāgayojitam || 14
etallaghaṇamaṣṭāṅgamagnisaṃdīpanaṃ param
madātyaye kaphaprāye dadyātsrotoviśodhanam || 15
cavyaṃ sauvarcalaṃ hiṅgu pūrakaṃ viśvadīpyakam
cūrṇaṃ madyena pātavyaṃ pānātyayarujāpaham || 16
jalaplutaścandanabhūṣitāṅgaḥ sragvī sabhaktāṃ piśitopadaṃśām
pibetsurāṃ naiva labheta rogānmanomatighnaṃ ca madaṃ na yāti || 17
drākṣākapitthaphaladāḍimapānakaṃ yattatpānavibhramaharaṃ madhuśarkarāḍhyam || 18
Adhikāra 19
śatadhautaghṛtābhyaktaṃ lihyāttaṃ yavasaktubhiḥ
kolāmalakasaṃyuktairdhānyāmlairapi buddhimān || 1
chādayettasya sarvāṅgamāranālārdravāsasā
lāmajjenātha śuktena candanenānulepayet || 2
candanāmbukaṇasyanditālavṛntopavījite
svapyāddāhārdito jantuḥ kadalīdalasaṃstare || 3
pariṣekāvagāhau ca seveta vyajanāni ca
sasitaṃ śiśiraṃ toyaṃ tṛṣṇādāhopaśāntaye || 4
kṣīraiḥ kṣīrakaṣāyaiśca suśītaiścandanānvitaiḥ
antardāhaṃ praśamayedetaiścānyaiśca śītalaiḥ || 5
kuśādiśāliparṇībhirjīvakādyena sādhitam
tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā dāhaghnaṃ vātapittavināśanam || 6
pittajvaraharaṃ yacca dāhe tatsarvamiṣyate
phalinīrodhrasevyāmbuhemapatrakuṭannaṭam
kālīyakarasopetaṃ dāhe śastaṃ pralepanam || 7
ślakṣṇaśuṣkaghano lepaścandanasyāpi dāhakṛt
tvaggatasyoṣmaṇo rodhācchītakṛttvanyathā'guroḥ || 8
hrīverapadmakośīracandanakṣodavāriṇā
saṃpūrṇāmavagāheta droṇīṃ dāhārdito naraḥ || 9
Adhikāra 20
vātike snehapānaṃ prāgvirekaḥ pittasaṃbhave
kaphaje vamanaṃ kāryaṃ paro bastyādikaḥ kramaḥ || 1
yaccopadekṣyate kiṃcidapasmāracikitsite
unmāde tacca kartavyaṃ sāmānyāddoṣadūṣyayoḥ || 2
brāhmīkūṣmāṇḍīphalaṣaḍgranthāśaṅkhapuṣpikāsvarasāḥ
unmādahṛto dṛṣṭāḥ pṛthagete kuṣṭhamadhumiśrāḥ || 3
daśamūlāmbu saghṛtaṃ yuktaṃ māṃsarasena vā
sasiddhārthakacūrṇaṃ vā kevalaṃ vā'navaṃ ghṛtam || 4
unmādaśāntaye peyo raso vā tālaśākhajaḥ
prayojyaṃ sārṣapaṃ tailaṃ nasyābhyañjanayoḥ sadā || 5
siddhārthako vacā hiṅgu karañjo devadāru ca
mañjiṣṭhā triphalā śvetā kaṭabhītvakkaṭutrikam || 6
samāṃśāni priyaṅguśca śirīṣo rajanīdvayam
bastamūtreṇa piṣṭo'yamagadaḥ pānamañjanam || 7
nasyamālepanaṃ caiva snānamudvartanaṃ tathā
apasmāraviṣonmādakṛtyālakṣmījvarāpahaḥ || 8
bhūtebhyaśca bhayaṃ hanti rājadvāre ca śasyate
sarpiretena siddhaṃ vā sagomūtraṃ tadarthakṛt || 9
tryūṣaṇaṃ hiṅgu lavaṇaṃ vacā kaṭukarohiṇī
śirīṣaṃ naktamālasya bījaṃ śvetāśca sarṣapāḥ || 10
gomūtrapiṣṭairetaistu vartirnetrāñjane hitā
caturthakamapasmāramunmādaṃ ca niyacchati || 11
āśvāsayetsuhṛdvākyairdharmakāryārthasaṃyutaiḥ
brūyādiṣṭavināśaṃ vā darśayedadbhutāni vā || 12
baddhaṃ sarṣapatailāktamuttānaṃ cā''tape nyaset
kapikacchvā'tha vā taptairlohatailajalaiḥ spṛśet || 13
kaśābhistāḍayitvā ca subaddhaṃ vijane gṛhe
rudhyācceto hi vibhrāntaṃ tathā vrajati tatsukham || 14
sarpeṇoddhṛtadaṃṣṭreṇa dāntaiḥ siṃhairgajaiśca tam
trāsayecchastrahastaiśca śatrubhiḥ sūkaraistathā || 15
atha vā rājapuruṣā bahirnītvā susaṃyatam
trāsayeyurvadhairenaṃ tarjayanto nṛpājñayā || 16
dehaduḥkhabhayebhyo'pi paraṃ prāṇabhayaṃ matam
tena tasya śamaṃ yāti sarvato viplutaṃ manaḥ || 17
iṣṭadravyāvināśācca mano yasyopahanyate
tasya tatsadṛśaprāptaiḥ sāntvāśvāsaiḥ śamaṃ nayet || 18
kāmaśokabhayakrodhaharṣerṣyālobhasaṃbhavān
parasparapratidvaṃdvairebhireva śamaṃ nayet || 19
buddhvā doṣaṃ vayaḥ sātmyaṃ deśakālabalābalam
cikitsitamidaṃ kuryādunmāde doṣabhūtaje || 20
devarṣipitṛgandharvairunmattasya ca buddhimān
varjayedañjanādīni tīkṣṇāni krūrakarma ca || 21
sarpiṣpānādirāgantormantrādiśceṣyate vidhiḥ
pūjābalyupahāreṣṭihomamantrāñjanādibhiḥ
jayedāgantumunmādaṃ yathāvadvidhibhirbhiṣak || 22
kalyāṇakaṃ prayuñjīta mahadvā vaitasaṃ ghṛtam
tailaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ nāma mahānārāyaṇaṃ tathā || 23
jaṭilā pūtanā keśī coraṭī markaṭī vacā
trāyamāṇā jayā vīrā corakaḥ kaṭurohiṇī || 24
kāyasthā sūkarī chatrā sāticchatrā palaṃkaṣā || 25
mahāpuruṣadantā ca vayasthā nākulīdvayam
kaṭambharā vṛścikālī sthirā caitaiḥ śṛtaṃ ghṛtam
siddhaṃ caturthakonmādagrahāpasmāranāśanam || 26
mahāpaiśācikaṃ nāma ghṛtametadyathā'mṛtam
medhābuddhismṛtikaraṃ bālānāṃ cāṅgavardhanam || 27
pañcamūlyāvakāśmaryau rāsnairaṇḍatrivṛdbalāḥ
mūrvā śatāvarī ceti kvāthairdvipalikairimaiḥ || 28
kalyāṇakasya cāṅgena vaitasaṃ nāma tadghṛtam
sarvacetovikārāṇāṃ śamanaṃ kīrtitaṃ param || 29
kāryaḥ kaṣāyo dviguṇāṣṭatoyaiḥ pānīyakalyāṇakakalkapādaḥ || 30
hiṅgusauvarcalavyoṣairdvipalāṃśairghṛtāḍhakam
caturguṇe gavāṃ mūtre siddhamunmādanāśanam || 31
Adhikāra 21
vātikaṃ bastibhiḥ prāyaḥ paittaṃ prāyo virecanaiḥ
kaphajaṃ vamanairdhīmānapasmāramupācaret || 1
tatastīkṣṇaṃ prayuñjīta bhiṣaksamyakprabodhanam
sarvataḥ śuddhadehasya syādunmādaharā kriyā || 2
manohvātārkṣyajaṃ caiva śakṛtpārāvatasya ca
añjanaṃ hantyapasmāramunmādaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 3
yaṣṭīhiṅguvacāvakraśirīṣalaśunāmayaiḥ
sājamūtrairapasmāre sonmāde nāvanāñjane || 4
kapitthāñśāradānmudgānmustośīrayavāṃstathā
savyoṣānbastamūtreṇa piṣṭvā vartīḥ prakalpayet || 5
apasmāre tathonmāde sarpadaṣṭe garārdite
viṣapīte jalamṛte caitāḥ syuramṛtopamāḥ || 6
puṣyoddhṛtaṃ śunaḥ pittamapasmāraghnamañjanam
tadeva sarpiṣā yuktaṃ dhūpanaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam || 7
hṛtkampo'kṣirujā yasya svedo hastādiśītatā
daśamūlījalaṃ tasya kalyāṇājyaṃ ca yojayet || 8
yaḥ khādetkṣīrabhaktāśī mākṣikeṇa vacārajaḥ
apasmāraṃ mahāghoraṃ sucirotthaṃ jayeddhruvam || 9
śaṇastrivṛttathairaṇḍo daśamūlaṃ śatāvarī
rāsnā māgadhikā śigruḥ kvāthyaṃ dvipalikaṃ bhavet || 10
vidārī madhukaṃ mede kākolyau dve sitā tathā
kharjūrābhīrumṛdvīkāmuñjātaṃ gokṣurastathā || 11
vaitasasya ghṛtasyāṅgaiḥ paktavyaṃ sarpiruttamam
mahāvaitasasaṃjñaṃ tu sarvāpasmāranāśanam || 12
garonmādapratiśyāyatṛtīyakacaturthakān
pāpālakṣmīṃ jayatyetatsarvagrahanivāraṇam || 13
śvāsakāsaharaṃ caiva śukrārtavaviśodhanam
dravyamāpothitaṃ kvāthyaṃ dattvā ṣoḍaśikaṃ jalam
pādaśeṣaṃ ca kartavyameṣa kvāthavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 14
brāhmīrase vacākuṣṭhaśaṅkhapuṣpībhireva ca
purāṇaṃ sarpirunmādagrahāpasmāranāśanam || 15
kūṣmāṇḍakarase sarpiraṣṭādaśaguṇe pacet
yaṣṭyāhvakalkaṃ tatpānamapasmāraharaṃ param || 16
gośakṛdrasadadhyamlakṣīramūtraiḥ samaṃ ghṛtam
siddhaṃ caturthakonmādagrahāpasmāranāśanam || 17
dvipañcamūlaṃ triphalāṃ rajanyau kaṭukāṃ vacām
saptaparṇamapāmārgaṃ nīlinīṃ kaṭurohiṇīm || 18
śamyākaṃ phalgumūlaṃ ca pauṣkaraṃ sadurālabham
dvipalāni jaladroṇe paktvā pādarase sthite || 19
bhārṅgīpāṭhātrikaṭukaṃ trivṛtāṃ niculāni ca
śreyasīmāḍhakīṃ rāsnāṃ mūrvāṃ bhūnimbacitrakau || 20
sārive dve rohiṣaṃ ca bhūtikaṃ madayantikām
kṣipetpiṣṭvā'kṣamātrāṇi taiḥ prasthaṃ sarpiṣaḥ pacet || 21
gośakṛdrasadadhyamlakṣīramūtraiśca tatsamaiḥ
pañcagavyamidaṃ khyātaṃ mahattadamṛtopamam || 22
apasmāre tathonmāde śvayathāvudareṣu ca
gulmārśaḥpāṇḍurogeṣu kāmalāyāṃ halīmake
alakṣmīgraharakṣoghnaṃ cāturthikavināśanam || 23
Adhikāra 22
abhyaṅgaḥ svedanaṃ bastirnasyaṃ snehavirecanam
snigdhāmlalavaṇaṃ svādu vṛṣyaṃ vātāmayāpaham || 1
paṭolaphalakairyūṣo vṛṣyo vātaharo laghuḥ
vāṭyālakakṛto yūṣaḥ paraṃ vātavināśanaḥ || 2
pañcamūlībalāsiddhaṃ kṣīraṃ vātāmaye hitam
śītoṣṇasnigdharūkṣādyairvātajo yo na śāmyati
vikārastatra vijñeyo duṣṭamevāsti śoṇitam || 3
vājigandhā balāstisro daśamūlī mahauṣadham
dve gṛdhranakhyau rāsnā ca gaṇo'yaṃ vātanāśanaḥ || 4
ānūpamatsyaiśca savesavārairuṣṇaiḥ pradehaḥ pavanāpahaḥ syāt
snehaiścaturbhirdaśamūlasiddhairgandhauṣadhaiścānilahā pradehaḥ || 5
nirasthi piśitaṃ piṣṭaṃ svinnaṃ ghṛtaguḍānvitam
kṛṣṇāmaricasaṃyuktaṃ vesavāra iti smṛtaḥ || 6
kākolyādiḥ savātaghnaḥ sarvāmladravyasaṃyutaḥ
sānūpamāṃsaḥ susvinnaḥ sarvasnehasamanvitaḥ || 7
sukhoṣṇaḥ spaṣṭalavaṇaḥ sālvaṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ
tenopanāhaṃ kurvīta sarvadā vātarogiṇām || 8
kākolyādirgaṇo grāhyo yojyo'ṣṭavargasaṃyutaḥ
vātaghno bhadradārvādirvargo'mlo dāḍimādikaḥ || 9
sarvasnehāścatuḥsnehā lavaṇaṃ saindhavādikam
amlādibhiśca saṃskāryaṃ kākolyāditrayaṃ tribhiḥ || 10
āmāśayagate vāte charditāya yathākramam
deyaḥ ṣaṭcaraṇo yogaḥ saptarātraṃ sukhāmbunā || 11
citrakendrayavāḥ pāṭhākaṭukātiviṣābhayāḥ
mahāvyādhipraśamano yogaḥ ṣaṭcaraṇo mataḥ || 12
karṣo'ṣṭādaśaniṣpāvairdharaṇaṃ tryadhikaistu taiḥ
tena ṣaḍdharaṇo yogaḥ saptarātraṃ prayujyate || 13
pakvāśayagate vāte kāryaṃ snehavirecanam
vastayaḥ śodhanīyāśca prāśāśca lavaṇottarāḥ || 14
kāryo bastigate cāpi vidhirvastiviśodhanaḥ
śrotrādiṣu prakupite kāryaścānilahā kramaḥ || 15
snehābhyaṅgopanāhāṃśca mardanālepanāni ca
tvaṅmāṃsāsṛkśirāprāpte kuryāccāsṛgvimokṣaṇam || 16
snehopanāhāgnikarmabandhanonmardanāni ca
snāyusaṃdhyasthisaṃprāpte kuryādvāte vicakṣaṇaḥ || 17
nigūḍhe'sthigate vāte pāṇimanthena dārite
nāḍīṃ dattvā'sthani bhiṣakcūṣayetpavanaṃ balī || 18
śukraprāpte'nile kāryaṃ śukradoṣacikitsitam || 19
vakṣastrikaskandhagataṃ vāyuṃ manyāgataṃ tathā
vamanaṃ hanti nasyaṃ ca kuśalena prayojitam || 20
māṣabalāśukaśimbākattṛṇarāsnāśvagandhorubūkāṇām
kvātho nasyanipīto rāmaṭhalavaṇānvitaḥ koṣṇaḥ || 21
apaharati pakṣavātaṃ manyāstambhaṃ sakarṇanādarujam
durjayamarditavātaṃ saptāhājjayati cāvaśyam || 22
māṣikaṃ hiṅgusindhūtthe jaraṇādyāstu śāṇikāḥ
māṣātmaguptakairaṇḍaṃ śṛtaṃ vāṭyālakaṃ pibet
hiṅgusaindhavasaṃyuktaṃ pakṣāghātanivāraṇam || 23
palamardhapalaṃ vā'pi rasonasya sukuṭṭitam || 24
hiṅgujīrakasindhūtthaiḥ sauvarcalakaṭutrikaiḥ
cūrṇitairmāṣakonmānairavacūrṇya viloḍitam || 25
yathāgnibhakṣitaṃ prātā rubūkakvāthapānataḥ
dine dine prayoktavyaṃ māsamekaṃ nirantaram || 26
vātarogaṃ nihantyāśu arditaṃ cāpatantrakam
ekāṅgarogiṇe caiva tathā sarvāṅgarogiṇe || 27
ūrustambhe ca gṛdhrasyāṃ kṛmidoṣe viśeṣataḥ
kaṭikukṣyāmayaṃ hanyājjāṭharaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 28
māṣapiṣṭakṛtiṃ jagdhvā navanītena so'rditī
kṣīramāṃsarasairbhuktvā daśamūlījalaṃ pibet || 29
snehābhyaṅgaparo bastinasyadhūmaparāyaṇaḥ
arditaṃ sa jayetsarpiḥ pibeduttarabhaktakam || 30
daśamūlībalāmāṣakvāthaṃ tailājyamiśritam
sāyaṃ bhuktvā pibennasyaṃ viśvācyāmavabāhuke || 31
mūlaṃ balāyāstvatha pāribhadrāttathā''tmaguptāsvarasaṃ pibedvā
nasyaṃ tu yo māṣarasena kuryānmāsādasau vajrasamānabāhuḥ || 32
pañcamūlīkṛtaḥ kvātho daśamūlakṛto'tha vā
rūkṣaḥ svedastathā nasyaṃ manyāstambhe praśasyate || 33
bāhuśoṣe pibedbhuktvā sarpiḥ kalyāṇakaṃ mahat
hṛdayaṃ yadi vā pṛṣṭhamunnataṃ kramaśaḥ saruk
ūrdhvo vāyuryadā kuryāttadā taṃ kubjamādiśet || 34
vātaghnairdaśamūlyā ca navaṃ kubjamupācaret
snaihairmāṃsarasairvā'pi pravṛddhaṃ taṃ vivarjayet || 35
pippalyādirajastūnīpratitūnyoḥ sukhāmbunā
pibedvā snehalavaṇaṃ saghṛtaṃ kṣārahiṅgu vā || 36
ādhmāne laṅghanaṃ pāṇitāpaśca phalavartayaḥ
dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ caiva vastiścaiva viśodhanaḥ || 37
dāruhaimavatīkuṣṭhaiḥ saindhavakṣārahiṅgubhiḥ
amlapiṣṭaiḥ sukhoṣṇaiśca ādhmāna udaraṃ dihet || 38
pratyādhmāne tu vamanaṃ laṅghanaṃ dīpanaṃ tathā || 39
pratyaṣṭhīlāṣṭhīlikayorgulmābhyantaravidradheḥ
kriyā hiṅgvādikaṃ cātra śasyate'tra viśeṣataḥ || 40
viśvācyāṃ khañjapaṅgvośca dāhe harṣe ca pādayoḥ
koṣṭuśīrṣavikāre ca vikāre vātakaṇṭake
śirāṃ yathoktāṃ nirvidhya cikitsā vātarogavat || 41
jihvāstambhe kriyā śreṣṭhā sāmānyoktā tathā'rdite
śirograhe tu kartavyā śirogatamarutkriyā || 42
saharidrā vacā kuṣṭhaṃ pippalī viśvabheṣajam || 43
ajājī cājamodā ca yaṣṭīmadhukasaindhavam
etāni samabhāgāni ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet || 44
taccūrṇaṃ sarpiṣā''loḍya pratyahaṃ bhakṣayennaraḥ
ekaviṃśatirātreṇa bhavecchrutidharo naraḥ || 45
meghadundubhinirghoṣo mattakokilanisvanaḥ
jāḍyagadgadamūkatvaṃ lehaḥ kalyāṇako jayet || 46
gugguluṃ kroṣṭuśīrṣe tu guḍūcītriphalāmbhasā
kṣīreṇairaṇḍatailaṃ vā pibedvā vṛddhadārakam || 47
śyāmāsamālambuṣādyaṃ kāryaṃ vā vātaraktanut
hṛte rakte'nilaharo vidhiḥ kṛtsnaḥ praśasyate || 48
daśamūlībalārāsnāguḍūcīviśvabheṣajam
pibederaṇḍatailena gṛdhrasīkhañjapaṅguṣu || 49
viśodhyairaṇḍabījāni piṣṭvā kṣīre vipācayet
tatpāyasaṃ kaṭīśūle gṛdhrasyāṃ paramauṣadham || 50
pañcamūlīkaṣāyaṃ tu rubutailatrivṛdyutam
gṛdhrasīgulmaśūlaṃ ca pītaṃ sadyo niyacchati || 51
tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā'rdrakamātuluṅgyo rasaṃ sacukraṃ saguḍaṃ pibedvā
kaṭyūrupṛṣṭhatrikaśūlagulmagṛdhrasyudāvartaharaḥ pradiṣṭaḥ || 52
rāsnāyāstu palaṃ caikaṃ karṣānpañca ca gugguloḥ
sarpiṣā vaṭikāṃ kṛtvā khādedvā gṛdhrasīharām || 53
śuṇṭhīgandharvabījābhyāṃ piṣṭābhyāṃ pāyasaṃ kṛtam
bhakṣitaṃ kaṭiśūlaṃ ca gṛdhrasīṃ hantyasaṃśayam || 54
nā''dau bastividhiṃ kuryādyāvadūrdhvaṃ na śudhyati
sneho nirarthakastatra bhasmanīva hutaṃ yathā || 55
gṛdhrasyārtasya jaṅghāyāṃ snehasvede kṛte bhṛśam
padbhyāṃ saṃmarditāyāṃ ca sūkṣmamārgeṇa gṛdhrasīm || 56
avatāryāṅgulau samyakkaniṣṭhāyāṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ
jñātvā samunnataṃ granthiṃ kaṇḍarāyāṃ vyavasthitam || 57
taṃ śastreṇa vidāryā''śu pravālāṅkurasaṃnibham
samuddhṛtyāgninā dagdhvā limpedyaṣṭyāhvacandanaiḥ || 58
vidhyecchirāmindrabasteradhastāccaturaṅgule
yadi nopaśamaṃ gaccheddahetpādakaniṣṭhikām || 59
tailameraṇḍajaṃ vā'pi gomūtreṇa pibennaraḥ
māsamekaṃ prayogo'yaṃ gṛdhrasyūrugrahāpahaḥ || 60
raktāvasecanaṃ kuryādabhīkṣṇaṃ vātakaṇṭake
pibederaṇḍatailaṃ vā dahetsūcībhireva ca || 61
vāyuḥ kaṭyāśritaḥ kruddhaḥ sāmo vā janayedrujam
kaṭigrahaḥ sa evoktaḥ paṅguḥ sakthidvayāśritaḥ || 62
daśamūlakaṣāyeṇa pibedvā nāgarāmbhasā
kaṭiśūleṣu sarveṣu tailameraṇḍasaṃbhavam || 63
pṛthakpalāṃśā triphalā pippalī ca vicūrṇitam
daśamūlāmbunā bhāvyaṃ tvagelārdhapalānvitam || 64
dattvā palāni pañcaiva guggulorvaṭakīkṛtaḥ
eṣa māṃsarasābhyāsādvātarogānaśeṣataḥ || 65
hanti saṃdhyasthimajjasthānvṛkṣamindrāśaniryathā
lehavaddviguṇenāyamāloḍyā''loḍya vā''tape
daśamūlāmbunā śoṣyaḥ saptavārāṃstu gugguluḥ || 66
ābhā'śvagandhā hapuṣā guḍūcī śatāvarī gokṣurakaṃ samaṅgā || 67
śyāmā śaṭhī ghoṣavatī yavānī sanāgarā ceti samaiśca cūrṇaiḥ
tulyaṃ bhavetkauśikamatra madhye deyaṃ tathā sarpirato'rdhabhāgam || 68
ardhākṣamātraṃ ca tataḥ prayogātkṛtvā'nupānaṃ surayā'tha yūṣaiḥ
madyena vā koṣṇajalena vā'pi kṣīreṇa vā māṃsarasena vā'pi || 69
kaṭigrahe gṛdhrasibāhupṛṣṭhe hanugrahe jānuni pādayugme
saṃdhisthite cāsthigate ca vāte majjāgate snāyugate ca koṣṭhe || 70
rogāñjayedvātakaphānuviddhānvāteritānhṛdgrahayonidoṣān
bhagnāsthividdheṣu ca khañjavāte trayodaśāṅgaṃ pravadanti siddhāḥ || 71
daśamūlasya niryūhe jīvanīyaiḥ palonmitaiḥ
kṣīreṇa ca ghṛtaṃ pakvaṃ tarpaṇaṃ pavanārtijit || 72
aśvagandhākaṣāyeṇa kalkaiḥ kṣīraṃ caturguṇam
ghṛtaṃ pakvaṃ tu vātaghnaṃ vṛṣyaṃ māṃsavivardhanam || 73
ājaṃ carmavinirmuktaṃ tyaktaśṛṅgakhurādim
pañcamūlīdvayaṃ samyagjaladroṇe vipācayet || 74

jīvanīyaiḥ sayaṣṭyāhvaiḥ kṣīraṃ caiva śatāvarīm || 75
chāgalādyamidaṃ nāmnā sarvavātavikāranut
ardite karṇaśūle ca bādhirye mūkamimmiṇe || 76
jaḍagadgadapaṅgūnāṃ khañje gṛdhrasikubjayoḥ
apatāne'patantre ca sarpiretatpraśasyate || 77
dvātriṃśacca palānyatra deyāni daśamūlataḥ
ghṛte taile ca yoge ca yaddravyaṃ punarucyate
tajjñātavyamihā''ryeṇa bhāgato dviguṇaṃ bhavet || 78
elāmurāsaralaśailajadārukauntī-
caṇḍāśaṭhīnaladacampakahemapuṣpam
sthauṇeyagandharasapūtidalaṃ mṛṇāla-
śrīvāsakundurunakhāmbuvarāṅgakuṣṭham || 79

rjātyāḥ phalaṃ savikasaṃ sahakuṅkumaṃ ca
spṛkkāturuṣkalaghu lābhatayā vinīya
tailaṃ balākvathanadugdhayutaṃ ca dadhnā || 80
sārdhaṃ pacettu hitametadudāharanti
vātāmayeṣu balavarṇavapuṣprakāśi || 81

sarvavātavikāraghnamevaṃ śaileyapācitam || 82

yavakolakulatthānāṃ kvāthasya payasastathā || 83
aṣṭāvaṣṭau śubhā bhāgāstailādekastadekataḥ
pacedāvāpya madhuraṃ gaṇaṃ saindhavasaṃyutam || 84
tathā'guruṃ sarjarasaṃ saralaṃ devadāru ca
mañjiṣṭhāṃ candanaṃ kuṣṭhamelāṃ kālānusārivām || 85
māṃsīṃ śaileyakaṃ patraṃ tagaraṃ sārivādvayam
śatāvarīmaśvagandhāṃ śatapuṣpāṃ punarnavām || 86
tatsādhusiddhaṃ sauvarṇe rājate mṛnmaye tathā
prakṣipya kalaśe samyaksvanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet || 87
balātailamidaṃ nāmnā sarvavātavikāranut
yathābalamato mātrāṃ sūtikāyai pradāpayet || 88

vātakṣīṇe marmahate mathite'bhihate tathā || 89

hikkāṃ śvāsamadhīmanthaṃ gulmaṃ kāsaṃ sudustaram
ṣaṇmāsānupayuñjīta antravṛddhimapohati || 91
pratyagradhātuḥ puruṣo bhavecca sthirayauvanaḥ
etaddhi rājñā kartavyaṃ rājamātrāśca ye narāḥ
sukhinaḥ sukumārāśca dhaninaścāpi ye narāḥ || 92
bilvo'gnimanthaḥ syonākaḥ pāṭalā pāribhadrakaḥ
prasāriṇyaśvagandhā ca bṛhatī kaṇṭakārikā || 93
balā cātibalā caiva śvadaṃṣṭrā sapunarnavā
eṣāṃ daśapalānbhāgāṃścaturdroṇe'mbhasaḥ pacet || 94
pādaśeṣaṃ parisrāvya tailapātraṃ pradāpayet
śatapuṣpā devadāru māṃsī śaileyakaṃ vacā || 95
candanaṃ tagaraṃ kuṣṭhamelāparṇīcatuṣṭayam
rāsnā turagagandhā ca saindhavaṃ ca punarnavā || 96
eṣāṃ dvipalikānbhāgānpeṣayitvā vinikṣipet
śatāvarīrasaṃ caiva tailatulyaṃ pradāpayet || 97
ājaṃ vā yadi vā gavyaṃ kṣīraṃ dadyāccaturguṇam
pāne bastau tathā'bhyaṅge bhojye nasye ca śasyate || 98
aśvo vā vātasaṃbhagno gajo vā yadi vā naraḥ
paṅgulaḥ pīṭhasarpī ca tailenānena sidhyati || 99
adhobhāge ca ye vātāḥ śiromadhyagatāśca ye
manyāstambhe hanustambhe dantaroge galagrahe || 100
yasya śuṣyati caikāṅgaṃ gatiryasya ca vihvalā
kṣīṇendriyā naṣṭaśukrā jvarakṣīṇāśca ye narāḥ || 101
badhirā lallajihvāśca svalpamedhasa eva ca
alpaprajā ca yā nārī yā ca garbhaṃ na vindati || 102
vātārto vṛṣaṇo yeṣāmantravṛddhiśca dāruṇā
etattailaṃ varaṃ teṣāṃ nāmnā nārāyaṇaṃ smṛtam || 103
śatāvarī cāṃśumatī pṛśniparṇī sahā balā
eraṇḍasya ca mūlāni bṛhatyāḥ pūtikasya ca || 104

gavyājapayasoḥ prasthau dvau dvā'vatra prayojayet

aśvānāṃ vātabhagnānāṃ kuñjarāṇāṃ nṛṇāṃ tathā || 109

śataṃ paktvā'śvagandhāyā jaladroṇeṃ'śaśeṣitam

kalkairmṛṇālaśālūkavisakiñjalkamālatī--
puṣpairhvīveramadhukasārivāpadmakesaraiḥ || 115
medāpunarnavādrākṣāmañjiṣṭhābṛhatīdvayaiḥ

pakvaṃ raktāśrayaṃ vātaṃ raktapittamasṛgdaram

ṣaṇḍhānapi vṛṣānkuryātpānābhyaṅgānuvāsanaiḥ || 118
tailaṃ saṃkucite'bhyaṅgo māṣasaindhavasādhitam

māṣātmaguptātirasorubūka-
rāsnāśatāhvālavaṇaiḥ supiṣṭaiḥ
caturguṇe māṣabalākaṣāye
tailaṃ kṛtaṃ hanti ca pakṣavātam || 120

pādaśeṣe rase tasminkṣīraṃ dadyāccaturguṇam || 121
prasthaṃ ca tilatailasya kalkaṃ dattvā'kṣasaṃmitam

rāsnātmaguptāmadhukaṃ balāvyoṣatrikaṇṭakam
pakṣaghāte'rdite vāte karṇaśūle sudāruṇe || 123
mandaśrutau ca śravaṇe timire ca tridoṣaje
hastakampe śiraḥkampe viśvācyāmavabāhuke || 124
śastaṃ kalāyakhañje ca pānābhyañjanabastibhiḥ
māṣatailamiti khyātamūrdhvajatrugadāpaham || 125
māṣātasīyavakuraṇṭakakaṇṭakārī-
gokaṇṭaṭuṇṭukajaṭākapikacchutoyaiḥ
kārpāsakāsthiśaṇabījakulatthakola-
kvāthena bastapiśitasya rasena vā'pi || 126
śuṇṭhyā samāgadhikayā śatapuṣpayā ca
sairaṇḍamūlasapunarnavayā saraṇyā
rāsnābalāmṛtalatākaṭukairvipakvaṃ
māṣākhyametadavabāhukahāri tailam || 127
ardhāṅgaśoṣamapatānakamāḍhyavāta-
mākṣepakaṃ sabhujakampaśiraḥprakampam
nasyena bastividhinā pariṣecanena

dvipañcamūlīṃ niṣkvāthya tailātṣoḍaśabhirguṇaiḥ
māṣāḍhakaṃ sādhayitvā taṃ niryūhaṃ caturguṇam || 134

avabāhukaviśvācyoḥ khañjaṣaṅgulayorapi || 139

prasthaṃ ca tilatailasya payo dattvā caturguṇam
ātmaguptorubūkaśca śatāhvā lavaṇatrayam || 144

rasonakalkasvarasena pakvaṃ tailaṃ pibedyastvanilāmayārtaḥ
tasyā''śu naśyanti hi vātarogā granthā viśālā iva durgṛhītāḥ || 152
prasāraṇyāstulāmaśvagandhāyā daśamūlataḥ
tulāṃ tulāṃ pṛthagvāridroṇe paktvāṃ'śaśeṣite || 153

samañjiṣṭhāgniyaṣṭyāhvaiḥ palikairjīvanīyakaiḥ

dviguṇaṃ ca payo dattvā kalkāndvipalikāṃstathā

samūlapatrāmutpāṭya śaratkāle prasāraṇīm || 162

paceccaturguṇaṃ toye dravaistailāḍhakaṃ bhiṣak

dadhyāḍhakasamāyuktaṃ pācayenmṛdunā'gninā

tagaraṃ madanaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kesaraṃ mustakaṃ tvacam

suktācchāgarasādathekṣurasataḥ kṣīrācca dattvā''ḍhakaṃ
spṛkkākarkaṭajīvakādyavikasākākolikākacchurā--
sūkṣmailāghanasārakundusaralākāśmīramāṃsīnakhaiḥ

sarvārdhāṅgagataṃ tathā'vayavagaṃ saṃdhyasthimajjāśritaṃ
śleṣmotthānapi paittikāṃśca śamayennānāvidhānāmayān
dhātūnbṛṃhayate sthiraṃ ca kurute puṃsāṃ navaṃ yauvanaṃ
vṛddhasyāpi balaṃ karoti sumahadvandhyāsu garbhapradam || 177
pītvā tailamidaṃ jaratyapi sutaṃ sūte'munā bhūruhāḥ
siktāḥ śoṣamupāgatāśca phalinaḥ snigdhā bhavanti sthirāḥ
bhagnāṅgāḥ sudṛḍhā bhavanti manujā gāvo hayāḥ kuñjarāḥ || 178
yā gandhaṃ ketakīnāṃ vahati parimalaṃ varṇataḥ piñjarābhā
svāde tiktā kaṭurvā laghurapi tulitā marditā cikkaṇā yā
dagdhā no yāti bhūtiṃ cimicimitikarā carmagandhā hutāśe
sā śuddhā śobhanīyā varamṛgatanujā rājayogyā pradiṣṭā || 179
karatalajalamadhye sthāpayitvā mahadbhiḥ
punarapi tadavasthaṃ cintanīyaṃ muhūrtam
yadi bhavati saraktaṃ tajjalaṃ pītavarṇaṃ
na bhavati mṛganābhiḥ kṛtrimo'sau vikāraḥ || 180
dve pale saindhavātpañca śuṇṭhyā granthikacitrakāt
dve dve bhallātakāsthīni viṃśatirdve tathā''ḍhake || 181
āranālātpacetprasthaṃ tailametairapatyadam
gṛdhrasyūrugrahārtasya sarvavātavikāranut || 182
Adhikāra 23
uttānamavagāḍhaṃ ca dvividhaṃ vātaśoṇitam
tvaṅmāṃsāśrayamuttānaṃ gambhīraṃ tvantarāśrayam || 1
piṇḍatailādinā'bhyaṅgaḥ pānaṃ tiktādisarpiṣaḥ
sekalepādyasṛṅmuktiḥ śodhanaṃ cobhayorhitam
gāḍhe viśeṣataḥ sarpiṣpānaṃ śuddhiḥ śirāvyadhaḥ || 2
godhūmacūrṇaṃ chagalīpayaśca sacchāgadugdho rububījakalkaḥ
lepe vidheyaṃ śatadhautasarpiḥ seke payaścā''vikameva śastam || 3
amṛtānāgaradhānyakakarṣatritayena pācanaṃ siddham
jayati saraktaṃ vātaṃ sāmaṃ kuṣṭhānyaśeṣāṇi || 4
vatsādanyudbhavaḥ kvāthaḥ pīto guggulusaṃyutaḥ
samīraṇasamāyuktaṃśoṇitaṃ saṃprasādhayet || 5
rāsnāguḍūcīcaturaṅgulānāmeraṇḍatailena pibetkaṣāyam
krameṇa sarvāṅgajamapyaśeṣaṃ jayedasṛgvātabhavaṃ vikāram || 6
tisro'tha vā pañca guḍena pathyā jagdhvā pibecchinnaruhākaṣāyam
tadvātaraktaṃ śamayatyudīrṇamājānusaṃbhinnamapi hyavaśyam || 7
ghṛtena vātaṃ saguḍā vibandhaṃ pittaṃ sitāḍhyā madhunā kaphaṃ ca
vātāsramugraṃ rubutailamiśrā śuṇṭhyā''mavātaṃ śamayedguḍūcī || 8
amṛtā vātapittaghnī kaphamedoviśoṣiṇī
vātaraktapraśamanī kacchūvīsarpanāśinī || 9
guḍūcyāḥ svarasaṃ kalkaṃ cūrṇaṃ vā kkāthameva vā
prabhūtakālamāsevyaṃ mucyate vātaśoṇitāt || 10
daśamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ sadyaḥ śūlavināśanam
pariṣeko'nilaprāye tadvatkoṣṇena sarpiṣā || 11
paṭolakaṭukābhīrutriphalāmṛtasādhitam
kvāthaṃ pītvā jayejjantuḥ sadāhaṃ vātaśoṇitam || 12
dhātrīmustāharidrāṇāṃ kaṣāyaṃ vā kaphādhike || 13
kokilākhyāmṛtākvāthe pibetkṛṣṇāṃ yathābalam
pathyabhojī trisaptāhānmucyate vātaśoṇitāt || 14
kapharaktapraśamanaṃ kacchūvīsarpanāśanam
vātaraktapraśamanaṃ hṛdyaṃ guḍaghṛtaṃ smṛtam || 15
sarveṣu saguḍāṃ pathyāṃ guḍūcīkvāthameva vā
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā śīlayetsusamāhitaḥ || 16
triphalā nimbamañjiṣṭhā vacā kaṭukarohiṇī
vatsādanī dāruniśā kaṣāyo navakārṣikaḥ || 17
vātaraktaṃ tathā kuṣṭhaṃ pāmānaṃ raktamaṇḍalam
kṛcchraṃ kapālikākuṣṭhaṃ pānādevāpakarṣati || 18
karṣādau tu palaṃ yāvaddadyātṣoḍaśikaṃ jalam
tatastu kuḍavaṃ yāvattoyamaṣṭaguṇaṃ bhavet || 19
lāṅgalyāstvamṛtasamaṃ kandamuddhṛtya yatnataḥ
yojayettriphalaloharajastrikaṭukaiḥ saha || 20
guggulvamṛtavallībhirdrākṣāluṅgarasena vā
triphalāyā rasairyuktā guṭikāḥ kolasaṃmitāḥ
bhakṣayenmadhunā''loḍya śṛṇu kurvanti yatphalam || 21
pādasphoṭaṃ mahāghoraṃ sarvāṅgasphoṭanaṃ ca yat
tatsarvaṃ nāśayatyāśu asādhyaṃ caiva śoṇitam || 22
guḍūcīkvāthakalkābhyāṃ sapayaskaṃ ghṛtaṃ śṛtam
hanti vātaṃ tathā raktaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ jayati dustaram || 23
ekadvitridravairdravyaṃ dravaṃ kuryāccaturguṇam
kṣīraṃ snehasamaṃ dadyātkṣīrāttoyaṃ caturguṇam || 24
śatāvarīkalkagarbhaṃ rasāttasmāccaturguṇam
kṣīratulyaṃ ghṛtaṃ pakvaṃ vātaśoṇitanāśanam || 25
balākaṣāyakalkābhyāṃ tailaṃ kṣīracaturguṇam
daśapākaṃ bhavedetadvātāsṛgvātapittajit || 26
dhanyaṃ puṃsavanaṃ caiva narāṇāṃ śukravardhanam
retoyonivikāraghnametadvātavikāranut || 27
sārivāsarjamañjiṣṭhāyaṣṭīsikthaiḥ payonvitam
tailaṃ pakvaṃ prayoktavyaṃ piṇḍākhyaṃ vātaśoṇite || 28
sārivāsarjayaṣṭyāhvairmadhūcchiṣṭaiḥ payonvitaiḥ
sādhyameraṇḍatailaṃ vā vātaraktarujāpaham || 29
samadhūcchiṣṭamañjiṣṭhaṃ sasarjarasasārivam
piṇḍatailamiti khyātaṃ vātaraktarujāpam || 30
śuddhāṃ pacennāgabalātulāṃ tu jalārmaṇe pādakaṣāyaśiṣṭām
visrāvya tailāḍhakamatra dadyādajāpayastailavimiśritaṃ tu || 31
natasya yaṣṭīmadhukasya kalkaṃ pṛthakpṛthakpañcapalaṃ vipakvam
tadvātaraktaṃ śamayatyudīrṇaṃ bastipradānena hi saptarātrāt || 32
pītaṃ daśāhena karotyarogaṃ tailottamaṃ hyaśvimataṃ pradiṣṭam || 33
varamahiṣalocanodarasaṃnibhavarṇasya gugguloḥ prastham
prakṣipya toyarāśau triphalāṃ ca yathoktaparimāṇām || 34
dvātriṃśacchinnaruhāpalāni deyāni yatnena
vipacettadapramatto darvyā saṃghaṭṭayanmuhuryāvat || 35
ardhakṣapitaṃ toyaṃ jātaṃ jvalanasya saṃparkāt
avatārya vastrapūtaṃ punarapi saṃpādayedayaḥpātre || 36
sāndrībhūte tasminnavatārya himopalaprakhye
triphalācūrṇārdhapalaṃ trikaṭoścūrṇaṃ ṣaḍakṣaparimāṇam || 37
kṛmiripucūrṇārdhapalaṃ karṣaṃ karṣaṃ trivṛddantyoḥ
palamekaṃ tu guḍūcyā dattvā saṃcūrṇya yatnena || 38
upayujya cānupānaṃ yūṣaṃ toyaṃ sugandhisalilena
icchāhāravihārī bheṣajamupayujya sarvakālamidam || 39
tanurodhi vātaśoṇitamekajamatha dvaṃdvajaṃ cirotthaṃ ca
jayati srutaṃ pariśuṣkaṃ sphuṭitaṃ cā''jānu yaccāpi || 40
vraṇakāsakuṣṭhagulmaśvayathūdarapāṇḍurogamehāṃśca
mandāgniṃ ca vibandhaṃ pramehapiṭikāśca nāśayatyāśu || 41
satataṃ niṣevyamāṇaḥ kālavaśāddhanti sarvagadān
abhibhūya jarādoṣaṃ prayāti kaiśorakaṃ rūpam || 42
prasthamekaṃ guḍūcyāśca ardhaprasthaṃ tu gugguloḥ
pratyekaṃ triphalāyāśca tatpramāṇaṃ vinirdiśet || 43
sarvamekatra saṃkṣudya kvāthayennalvaṇe'mbhasi
pādaśeṣaṃ parisrāvya punaragnāvadhiśrayet || 44
tāvatpacetkaṣāyaṃ taṃ yāvatsāndratvamāgatam
dantīvyoṣaviḍaṅgāni guḍūcī triphalātvacaḥ || 45
tataścārdhapalaṃ pūtaṃ gṛhṇīyācca prati prati
karṣaṃ tu trivṛtāyāśca sarvamekatra cūrṇayet || 46
tasminsusiddhaṃ vijñāya kavoṣṇe nikṣipedbudhaḥ
tataścāgnibalaṃ jñātvā tasya mātrāṃ prayojayet || 47
vātaraktaṃ tathā kuṣṭhaṃ gudajānyagnisādanam
duṣṭavraṇaṃ pramehāṃśca āmavātaṃ bhagandaram || 48
nāḍyāḍhyavātaṃ śvayathuṃ sarvānetānvyapohati
aśvibhyāṃ nirmitaḥ pūrvamamṛtādyo hi gugguluḥ || 49
amṛtāyāśca dviprasthaṃ prasthamekaṃ tu gugguloḥ
pratyekaṃ triphalāprasthaṃ varṣābhūprasthameva ca || 50
sarvamekatra saṃkṣudya kvāthayennalvaṇe'mbhasi
punaḥ pacetpādaśeṣaṃ yāvatsāndratvamāgatam || 51
dantīcitrakamūlānāṃ kaṇāviśvaphalatrikam
guḍūcītvagviḍaṅgānāṃ pratyekārdhapalānvitam || 52
trivṛtākarṣamekaṃ tu sarvamekatra cūrṇayet
siddhamuṣṇe kṣipettatra amṛtāgugguloḥ palam || 53
yathāvahnibalaṃ khādedamlapittī viśeṣataḥ
vātaraktaṃ tathā kuṣṭhaṃ gudajānyagnisādanam || 54
duṣṭavraṇapramehāṃśca āmavātaṃ bhagandaram
nāḍyāḍhyavātaśvayathūnhanyātsarvāmayānayam
aśvibhyāṃ nirmito hyeṣa amṛtāgugguluḥ purā || 55
śatāvarī nāgabalā vṛddhadārakamuccaṭā || 56
punarnavā'mṛtā kṛṣṇā vājigandhā trikaṇṭakam
pṛthagdaśa palānyeṣāṃ ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet || 57
tadardhaśarkarāyuktaṃ cūrṇaṃ saṃmardayedbudhaḥ
sthāpayetsudṛḍhe bhāṇḍe madhvardhāḍhakasaṃyutam || 58
ghṛtaprasthena cā''loḍya trisugandhipalena ca
taṃ khādediṣṭaceṣṭānno yathāvahnibalaṃ naraḥ || 59
vātaraktaṃ kṣayaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kāsaṃ pittasamudbhavam
vātapittakaphotthāṃśca rogānanyāṃśca tadvidhān || 60
hatvā karoti puruṣaṃ valīpalitavarjitam
yogasārāmṛto nāma lakṣmīkāntivivardhanaḥ || 61
divāsvapnādi saṃtāpaṃ vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ tathā
kaṭūṣṇagurvabhiṣyandilavaṇāmlāni varjayet || 62
