Adhikāra 24
snehāsṛksrāvavamanaṃ bastikarma virecanam
vivarjayedāḍhyavāte taiśca tasya virodhataḥ || 1
tasmādatra sadā kāryaṃ svedalaṅghanarūkṣaṇam
āmamedaḥkaphādhikyānmārutaṃ parirakṣatā || 2
yatsmātkaphapraśamanaṃ na ca mārutakopanam
tatsarvaṃ sarvadā kāryamūrustambhasya bheṣajam || 3
bhojyāḥ purāṇaśyāmākakodravoddālaśālayaḥ
jāṅgalairaghṛtairmāṃsaiḥ śākaiścālavaṇairhitaiḥ || 4
rūkṣaṇādvātakopaścennidrānāśārtisūcakaḥ
snehasvedakramastatra kāryo vātāmayāpahaḥ || 5
pratārayetpratisroto nadīṃ śītajalāṃ śivām
saraśca vimalaṃ śītaṃ sthiratoyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 6
yathā viśuṣke'sya kaphe śāntimūrugraho vrajet
śarīrabalamagniṃ ca kāryaiṣā rakṣatā kriyā || 7
śilājatuṃ gugguluṃ vā pippalīmatha nāgaram
ūrustambhe pibenmūtrairdaśamūlarasena vā || 8
bhallātakāmṛtāśuṇṭhī dārupathyāpunarnavāḥ
pañcamūlīdvayonmiśrā ūrustambhanibarhaṇāḥ || 9
pippalīpippalīmūlaṃ bhallātakaphalāni ca
kalkaṃ madhuyutaṃ pītvā ūrustambhādvimucyate || 10
triphalācavyakaṭukaṃ granthikaṃ madhunā lihet
ūrustambhavināśāya puraṃ mūtreṇa vā pibet || 11
lihyādvā triphalācūrṇaṃ kṣaudreṇa kaṭukāyutam
sukhāmbunā pibedvā'pi cūrṇaṃ ṣaṭcaraṇaṃ naraḥ || 12
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā mākṣikeṇa guḍena vā
ūrustambhe praśaṃsanti gaṇḍīrāriṣṭameva ca || 13
palābhyāṃ pippalīmūlanāgarādaṣṭakaṭvaraḥ
tailaprasthaḥ samo dadhnā gṛdhrasyūrugrahāpahaḥ
saindhavādyaṃ hitaṃ tailamamṛtādyo hi gugguluḥ || 14
Adhikāra 25
laṅghanaṃ svedanaṃ tiktaṃ dīpanāni kaṭūni ca
virecanaṃ snehapānaṃ bastayaścā''mamārute || 1
śaṭhī śuṇṭhyabhayā cogrā devāhvā'tiviṣā'mṛtā
kaṣāyamāmavātasya pācanaṃ rūkṣabhojanam || 2
śaṭhīviśvauṣadhīkalkaṃ varṣābhūkvāthasaṃyutam
saptarātraṃ pibejjanturāmavātavipācane || 3
daśamūlāmṛtairaṇḍarāsnānāgaradārubhiḥ
kvātho rubūkatailena sāmaṃ hantyanilaṃ gurum || 4
daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa pibedvā nāgarāmbhasā
kukṣibastikaṭīśūle tailameraṇḍasambhavam || 5
rāsnāṃ guḍūcīmairaṇḍaṃ devadāru mahauṣadham
pibetsarvāṅgage vāte sāme saṃdhyasthimajjage || 6
rāsnāmṛtāragvadhadevadārutrikaṇṭakairaṇḍapunarnavānām
kvāthaṃ pibennāgaracūrṇamiśraṃ jaṅghorujānutrikapṛṣṭhaśūlī || 7
śuṇṭhīgokṣurakakvāthaḥ prātaḥ prātarniṣevitaḥ
sāme vāte kaṭīśūle pācano rugvināśanaḥ
yavakṣārasamāyukto mūtrakṛcchravināśanaḥ || 8
mahauṣadhaguḍūcyostu kvāthaṃ pippalisaṃyutam
pibedāme sarukśophe kaṭīśūle viśeṣataḥ || 9
āmavāte kaṇāyuktaṃ daśamūlījalaṃ pibet
khādedvā'pyabhayāviśvaṃ guḍūcīṃ nāgareṇa vā || 10
eraṇḍatailayuktāṃ harītakīṃ bhakṣayennaro vidhivat
āmānilārtiyukto gṛdhrasivṛddhyardito nityam || 11
karṣaṃ nāgaracūrṇasya kāñjikena pibetsadā
āmavātapraśamanaṃ kaphavātaharaṃ param || 12
pañcakolakacūrṇaṃ ca pibeduṣṇena vāriṇā
mandāgniśūlagulmāmakaphārocakanāśanam || 13
amṛtānāgaragokṣurumuṇḍitikāvaruṇakaiḥ kṛtaṃ cūrṇam
mastvāranālapītamāmānilanāśanaṃ khyātam || 14
maṇimanthasya bhāgau dvau yavānyāstadvadeva ca
bhāgāstrayo'jamodāyā nāgarādbhāgapañcakam || 15
daśa dvau ca harītakyāḥ ślakṣṇacūrṇīkṛtāḥ śubhāḥ
mastvāranālatakreṇa sarpiṣoṣṇodakena vā || 16
pītaṃ jayatyāmavātaṃ gulmahṛdbastijāngadān
plīhānaṃ granthiśūlādīnarśāṃsyānāhameva ca || 17
vibandhaṃ jāṭharānrogāṃstathā vai hastapādajān
vātānulomanamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ smṛtam || 18
alambuṣāṃ gokṣurakaṃ guḍūcīṃ vṛddhadārakam || 19
pippalīṃ trivṛtāṃ mustaṃ varuṇaṃ sapunarnavam
triphalāṃ nāgaraṃ caiva sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet || 20
mastvāranālatakreṇa payomāṃsarasena vā
āmavātaṃ nihantyāśu śvayathuṃ saṃdhisaṃsthitam || 21
śatapuṣpā viḍaṅgāni saindhavaṃ maricaṃ samam
cūrṇamuṣṇāmbunā pītamagnisaṃdīpanaṃ param || 22
hiṅgu cavyaṃ viḍaṃ śuṇṭhī kṛṣṇājājī sapauṣkaram
bhāgottaramidaṃ cūrṇaṃ pītaṃ vātāmajidbhavet || 23
citrakaṃ pippalīmūlaṃ yavānīṃ kāravīṃ tathā
viḍaṅgānyajamodāṃ ca jīrakaṃ suradāru ca || 24
cavyailāsaindhavaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ rāsnāgokṣuradhānyakam
triphalāmustakaṃ vyoṣaṃ tvaguśīraṃ yavāgrajam || 25
tālīsapatraṃ patraṃ ca sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet
yāvantyetāni dravyāṇi tāvanmātraṃ tu guggulum || 26
saṃmardya sarpiṣā gāḍhaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍe nidhāpayet
ato mātrāṃ prayuñjīta yathecchāhāravānapi || 27
yogarāja iti khyāto yogo'yamamṛtopamaḥ
āmavātāḍhyavātādīnkṛmiduṣṭavraṇānapi || 28
plīhagulmodarānāhadurnāmāni vināśayet
agniṃ ca kurute dīptaṃ tejovṛddhibalaṃ tathā
vātarogāñjayatyeṣa saṃdhimajjāgatānapi || 29
alambuṣāgokṣurakatriphalānāgarāmṛtāḥ
yathottaraṃ bhāgavṛddhyā śyāmācūrṇaṃ tu tatsamam || 30
pibenmastusurātakrakāñjikoṣṇodakena vā
pītaṃ jayatyāmavātaṃ saśophaṃ vātaśoṇitam || 31
trikajānūrusaṃdhisthaṃ jvarārocakanāśanam
alambuṣādyamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ rogānīkavināśanam || 32
harītakyakṣadhātrībhiḥ prasiddhā triphalā kramāt
pratyekaṃ tena cāmuṣmādbhāgavṛddhiryathottaram || 33
pathyāviśvayavānībhistulyābhiścūrṇitaṃ pibet || 34
takreṇoṣṇodakenāpi athavā kāñjikena tu
āmavātaṃ nihantyāśu śothaṃ mandāgnitāmapi || 35
ajamodamaricapippaliviḍaṅgasuradārucitrakaśatāhvāḥ
saindhavapippalimūlaṃ bhāgā navakasya palikāḥ syuḥ || 36
śuṇṭhī daśapalikā syātpalāni tāvanti vṛddhadārasya
pathyāpalāni pañca ca sarvaṃ caikatra kārayeccūrṇam || 37
samaguḍavaṭakānkṛtvā cūrṇaṃ vā'pyuṣṇavāriṇā pibataḥ
naśyantyāmānilajāḥ sarve rogāstu koṣṭhagatāḥ || 38
viśvācīpratitūnītūnīrogāśca gṛdhrasī cogrā
kaṭibastijānugudajaṃ sphuṭanaṃ caivāsthijaṅghayostīvram || 39
śvayathuḥ sarvāṅgasaṃdhiṣu ye cānye cā''mavātasaṃbhūtāḥ
sarve prayānti nāśaṃ tama iva sūryāṃśuvidhvastam || 40
nāgarakvāthakalkābhyāṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
caturguṇena tenātha kevalenodakena vā || 41
vātaśleṣmapraśamanamagnisaṃdīpanaṃ param
nāgaraṃ ghṛtamityuktaṃ kaṭyāmaśūlanāśanam || 42
amṛtāyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa kalkena ca mahauṣadhāt
mṛdvagninā ghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ vātaraktaharaṃ param || 43
āmavātāḍhyavātādīnkṛmikuṣṭhavraṇāni ca
arśāṃsi gulmarogāṃśca nāśayedāśu yojitam || 44
hiṅgu trikaṭukaṃ cavyaṃ maṇimanthaṃ tathaiva ca
kalkānkṛtvā tu palikānghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 45
āranālāḍhakaṃ dattvā tatsarpirjaṭharāpaham
śūlaṃ vibandhamānāhamāmavātaṃ kaṭīgraham
nāśayedgrahaṇīdoṣaṃ mandāgnerdīpanaṃ param || 46
puṣṭyarthaṃ payasā sādhyaṃ dadhnā viṇmūtrasaṃgrahe
dīmanārthaṃ matimatā mastunā ca prakīrtitam || 47
sarpirnāgarakalkena sauvīrakacaturguṇam
siddhamagnikaraṃ śreṣṭhamāmavātaharaṃ param || 48
siddhārthakakhaliprasthaṃ sudhautaṃ nistuṣaṃ jale || 49
maṇḍaprasthaṃ vinikṣipya sthāpayeddivasatrayam
dhānyarāśau tato dadyātsaṃcūrṇya palikāni tu || 50
alambuṣā gokṣurakaṃ śatapuṣpā punarnavā
prasāraṇī varuṇatvakśuṇṭhī madanameva ca || 51
samyakpākaṃ ca vijñāya siddhā taṇḍulamiśritā
bhṛṣṭā sarṣapatailena hiṅgusaindhavasaṃyutā || 52
bhakṣitā lavaṇopetā jayedāmaṃ mahārujam
ekajaṃ dvaṃdvajaṃ sādhyaṃ sāṃnipātikameva ca || 53
kaṭyūruvātamānāhaṃ jānugaṃ trikamārutam
udāvartaharā peyā balavarṇāgnikāriṇī || 54
palaśataṃ rasonasya tilasya kuḍavaṃ tathā
hiṅgutrikaṭukaṃ kṣārau dvau pañca lavaṇāni ca || 55
śatapuṣpā tathā kuṣṭhaṃ pippalī mūtracitrakau
ajamodā yavānī ca dhānyakaṃ cāpi buddhimān || 56
pratyekaṃ ca palaṃ caiṣāṃ ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet
ghṛtabhāṇḍe dṛḍhe caiva sthāpayeddinaṣoḍaśa || 57
prakṣipya tailamānīṃ ca prasthārdhaṃ kāñjikasya ca
khādetkarṣapramāṇaṃ tu toyaṃ madyaṃ pibedanu || 58
āmavāte tathā vāte sarvāṅgaikāṅgasaṃsthite
apasmāre'nale mande kāse śvāse gareṣu ca
sonmāde vātabhagne ca śūle jantuṣu śasyate || 59
rasonasya śataṃ kṣuṇṇaṃ tadardhaṃ nistuṣāttilāt
pātre gavyasya takrasya piṣṭairdravyaiḥ samaṃ kṣipet || 60
dhānyaṃ trikaṭukaṃ cavyaṃ citrakaṃ gajapippalī
ajamodā tvagele ca granthikaṃ ca palāṃśakam || 61
śarkarāyāḥ palānyaṣṭau palārdhaṃ maricasya ca
kuṣṭhājājyośca catvāri madhunaḥ kuḍavaṃ tathā || 62
ārdrakasya ca catvāri sarpiṣo'ṣṭau palāni ca
tilatailasya tāvanti śuklasyāpi ca viṃśatim || 63
siddhārthakasya catvāri rājikāyāstathaiva ca
karṣapramāṇaṃ dātavyaṃ hiṅgu lavaṇapañcakam || 64
ekīkṛtya dṛḍhe kumbhe dhānyamadhye nidhāpayet
dvādaśāhātsamuddhṛtya prātaḥ khādedyathābalam || 65
surā sauvīrakaṃ sīdhuṃ madyaṃ cānu pibennaraḥ
jīrṇe yathepsitaṃ bhojyaṃ dadhipiṣṭakavarjitam || 66
eṣa māsopayogena sarvānvyādhīnniyacchati
aśītiṃ vātajānrogāṃścatvāriṃśacca paittikān || 67
viṃśatiṃ ślaiṣmikāṃścaiva pramehāṃścaiva viṃśatim
udarāṣṭakamarśāṃsi gulmaṃ pañcavidhaṃ tathā || 68
aṣṭādaśavidhaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kṣayarogaharaṃ param
vepathuṃ gulmaśūlaṃ ca stambhaṃ cā''śu vyapohati || 69
cyutasaṃdhyasthibhagnānāṃ saṃdhānakaraṇaṃ bhavet
dṛṣṭivarṇakaraṃ vṛṣyamāyuṣyaṃ balavardhanam || 70
kṣuṣṇaṃ rasonapalaśatamasitatilānāṃ palāni pañcāśat
ghṛtaliptabhājanasthe takreṇa samena tatkuryāt || 1
viṃśatipalāni cukrātsaguḍārdrakamātuluṅgametāvat
tilatailāni ca tāvattāvantyevātra sarpiṣo dadyāt || 2
śatapuṣpāgajapippalidāḍimavṛkṣāmladhātakīmustam
tryūṣaṇakuṣṭhajayāni drākṣā sailā sajīrakadvitayā || 3
suradāru sindhu sabiḍaṃ sauvarcalaromakaṃ ca sāmudram
palasaṃmitāni kṛtvā ślakṣṇacūrṇavaddāpayenmatimān || 4
āloḍya dhānyarāśau saptāhāṃstrīnnyasecca kṛtarakṣaḥ
uddhṛtya tadanu siddhaṃ sutithimuhūrte tathā sunakṣatre || 5
tvakpatrailākesarakarpūralavaṅgajāticūrṇena
surabhīkṛtaṃ ca naraḥ khādeddvipalonmitaṃ piṇḍam || 6
atyarthoṣṇavivarjī tṛṣitaśca pibetsurāṃ yatheṣṭam
amṛtādamṛtākhyo'yaṃ rasonapiṇḍaḥ parāśareṇoktaḥ || 7
hatapatitacyutabhagnasaṃdhivimuktāsthibhagnadehānām
asmātparaṃ narāṇāṃ na hyasti bheṣajaṃ vā'nyat || 8
balavarṇavahnijananaṃ smṛtimedhābuddhivardhanaṃ dhanyam
śamayati sarvavikārāñśataśo'nyairbheṣajairajitān || 9
dadhimatsyaguḍakṣīrapotakīmāṣapiṣṭakam
varjayedāmavātārto māṃsamānūpajaṃ ca yat || 71
abhiṣyandakarā ye ca ye cānye gurupicchilāḥ
varjanīyāḥ prayatnena āmavātārditairnaraiḥ || 72
Adhikāra 26
vamanaṃ laṅghanaṃ svedaḥ pācanaṃ phalavartayaḥ
kṣāraścūrṇāni guṭikāḥ śasyante śūlaśāntaye || 1
āśukārī hi pavanastasmāttaṃ tvarayā jayet
tasya śūlābhipannasya sveda eva sukhāvahaḥ || 2
vātātmakaṃ hantyacireṇa śūlaṃ snehena yuktastu kulatthayūṣaḥ
sasaindhavavyoṣayutaḥ salāvaḥ sahiṅgusauvarcaladāḍimāḍhyaḥ || 3
śūle nirannakoṣṭhe'dbhiruṣṇābhiścūrṇitāḥ pibet
hiṅguprativiṣāvyoṣavacāsauvarcalābhayāḥ || 4
balāpunarnavairaṇḍabṛhatīdvayagokṣuraiḥ
sahiṅgulavaṇaṃ pītaṃ sadyo vātarujaṃ jayet || 5
tumburūṇyabhayāhiṅgu pauṣkaraṃ lavaṇatrayam
pibedyavāmbunā vātaśūlagulmāpatantrakī || 6
viśvameraṇḍajaṃ mūlaṃ kvāthayitvā jalaṃ pibet
hiṅgusauvarcalopetaṃ sadyaḥ śūlanivāraṇam || 7
hiṅgupuṣkaramūlābhyāṃ hiṅgusauvarcalena vā
viśvairaṇḍayavakvāthaḥ pītaḥ śūlanivāraṇaḥ || 8
tadvadrubuyavakvātho hiṅgusauvarcalānvitaḥ
yavānīhiṅgusindhūtthakṣārasauvarcalābhayāḥ
surāmaṇḍena pātavyā vātaśūlanivāraṇaḥ || 9
sauvarcikāmlikājājīmaricairdviguṇottaraiḥ
mātuluṅgarasaiḥ piṣṭā guṭikā'nilaśūlanut || 10
hiṅgvamlakṛṣṇāmalakaṃ yavānī-
kṣārābhayāsaindhavatulyabhāgam
cūrṇaṃ pibedvāruṇimaṇḍamiśraṃ
śūle pravṛddhe'nilaje śivāya || 11
tilaiśca guṭikāṃ kṛtvā bhrāmayejjaṭharopari
guṭikā śamayatyāśu śūlaṃ caivātiduḥsaham || 12
nābhilepājjayecchūlaṃ madanaḥ kāñjikānvitaḥ
jīvantīmūlakalko vā satailaḥ pārśvaśūlahā || 13
guḍaḥ śāliryavāḥ kṣīraṃ sarpiṣpānaṃ virecanam
jāṅgalāni ca māṃsāni bheṣajaṃ pittaśūlinām || 14
paitte tu śūle vamanaṃ payomburasaistathekṣoḥ sapaṭolanimbaiḥ || 15
śītāvagāhāḥ sukhinaḥ pravātā bhāṇḍāni kāṃsyāni jalaplutāni
anyāni śastāni ca śītalāni sacandanāścandrakarāḥ suśītāḥ || 16
kāṃsyarājatatāmrāṇiṃ bhājanāni ca sarvataḥ
paripūrṇāni toyasya śūlasyopari nikṣipet || 17
virecanaṃ pittaharaṃ ca śastaṃ rasāśca śastāḥ śaśalāvakānām
saṃtarpaṇaṃ lājamadhūpapannaṃ yogāḥ suśītā madhusaṃprayuktāḥ || 18
chardyāṃ jvare pittabhave ca śūle ghore'pi dāhe tṛṣite'timātram
yavasya peyāṃ madhunā vimiśrāṃ pibetsuśītāṃ manujaḥ sukhārthī || 19
dhātryā rasaṃ vidāryā vā trāyantīgostanāmbunā
pibetsaśarkaraṃ madyaṃ pittaśūlaniṣūdanam || 20
śatāvarīrasaṃ kṣaudrayuktaṃ prātaḥ pibennaraḥ
dāhaśūlopaśāntyarthaṃ sarvapittāmayāpaham || 21
śatāvarīsayaṣṭyāhvavāṭyālakuśagokṣuraiḥ
śṛtaśītaṃ pibettoyaṃ saguḍakṣaudraśarkaram
pittāsṛgdāhaśūlaghnaṃ sadyodāhajvarāpaham || 22
triphalānimbayaṣṭyāhvakaṭukāragvadhaiḥ śṛtam
pāyayenmadhusaṃmiśraṃ dāhaśūlopaśāntaye || 23
bṛhatyau gokṣurairaṇḍakuśakāśekṣuvālikāḥ
pītāḥ pittabhavaṃ śūlaṃ sadyo hanyuḥ sudāruṇam || 24
pralihyātpittaśūlaghnaṃ dhātrīcūrṇaṃ samākṣikam || 25
śleṣmātmake laṅghanacchardanāni śirovirekaṃ madhusīdhupānam
madhūni godhūmayavānariṣṭānseveta rūkṣānkaṭukāṃśca sarvān || 26
lavaṇatrayasaṃyuktaṃ pañcakolaṃ sarāmaṭham
sukhoṣṇenāmbunā pātuṃ kaphaśūle pradāpayet || 27
bilvamūlamathairaṇḍaṃ citrakaṃ viśvabheṣajam
hiṅgusaindhavasaṃyuktaṃ sadyaḥ śūlaharaṃ param || 28
mātuluṅgaraso vā'pi śigrukvāthastataḥ paraḥ
sakṣāro madhunā pītaḥ pārśvahṛdbastiśūlanut || 29
vidārīdāḍimarasaḥ savyoṣalavaṇānvitaḥ
kṣaudrayukto jayatyāśu śūlaṃ doṣatrayodbhavam || 30
eraṇḍaphalamūlāni bṛhatīdvayagokṣuram
parṇinyaḥ sahadevā ca siṃhapucchīkṣuvālikā || 31
tulyairetaiḥ śṛtaṃ toyaṃ yavakṣārayutaṃ pibet
pṛthagdoṣabhavaṃ śūlaṃ hanyātsarvabhavaṃ tathā || 32
gomūtraśuddhaṃ maṇḍūraṃ triphalācūrṇasaṃyutam
vilihanmadhusarpirbhyāṃ śūlaṃ hanti tridoṣajam || 33
śaṅkhacūrṇaṃ salavaṇaṃ sahiṅgu vyoṣasaṃyutam
uṣṇodakena tatpītaṃ śūlaṃ hanti tridoṣajam || 34
āmaśūle kriyā kāryā kaphaśūlavināśinī
sevyamāmaharaṃ sarvaṃ yadyadagnivivardhanam || 35
eraṇḍabilvabṛhatīdvayamātuluṅga-
pāṣāṇabhittrikaṭamūlakṛtaḥ kaṣāyaḥ
sakṣārahiṅgulavaṇo rubutailamiśraḥ
śroṇyaṃsameḍhrahṛdayastanarukṣu peyaḥ || 36
sahiṅgutumburuvyoṣayavānīcitrakābhayāḥ
sakṣāralavaṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ pibetprātaḥ sukhāmbunā || 37
viṇmūtrānilaśūlaghnaṃ pācanaṃ vahnidīpanam
citrakagranthikairaṇḍaśuṇṭhīdhānyaṃ jale śṛtam || 38
śūlānāhavibandheṣu sahiṅguviḍasaindhavam
dīpyakaṃ saindhavaṃ pathyā nāgaraṃ ca catuḥsamam
cūrṇaṃ śūlaṃ jayatyāśu sannasyāgneśca dīpanam || 39
samākṣikaṃ bṛhatyādiṃ pibetpittānilātmake
vyāmiśraṃ vā vidhiṃ kuryācchūle mārutapittaje || 40
paṭolatriphalāriṣṭakvāthaṃ madhuyutaṃ pibet
pittaśleṣmajvaracchardirdāhaśūlopaśāntaye || 41
pittaje kaphaje vā'pi kriyā yā kathitā pṛthak
ekīkṛtya prayuñjīta tāṃ śūle kaphapittaje || 42
vamanaṃ recanaṃ caiva vīkṣya doṣabalābalam
kartavyaṃ kaphapittoktaṃ yathāyogyaṃ bhiṣagjitam || 43
cūrṇaṃ samaṃ rucakahiṅgumahauṣadhānāṃ
śuṇṭhyambunā kaphasamīraṇasaṃbhavāsu
hṛtpārśvapṛṣṭhajaṭharārtiviṣūcikāsu
peyaṃ tathā yavarasena tu viḍvibandhe || 44
kvāthena cūrṇapānaṃ yattatra kvāthapradhānatā
pravartate na tenātra cūrṇāpekṣī caturdravaḥ || 45
hiṅgunaḥ svalpamānokteḥ samaśabde sahārthatā || 46
viśvorubūkadaśamūlayavāmbhasā ca
dvikṣārahiṅgulavaṇatrayapuṣkarāṇām
cūrṇaṃ pibeddhṛdayapārśvakaṭīgrahāma-
pakvāśayāṃsabhṛśarugjvaragulmaśūlī || 47
hiṅgu sauvarcalaṃ pathyā biḍasaindhavatumburu
pauṣkaraṃ ca pibeccūrṇaṃ daśamūlayavāmbhasā || 48
pārśvahṛtkaṭipṛṣṭhāṃsaśūle tandrāpatānake
śothe śleṣmapraseke ca galaroge ca śasyate || 49
rasonaṃ madyasaṃmiśraṃ pibetprātaḥ prakāṅkṣitaḥ
vātaśleṣmabhavaṃ śūlaṃ nihantuṃ vahnidīpanam || 50
hiṅgu trikaṭukaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ yavakṣāraṃ sasaindhavam
mātuluṅgarasopetaṃ plīhaśūlāpahaṃ rajaḥ || 51
tīkṣṇāyaścūrṇasaṃyuktaṃ triphalācūrṇamuttamam
prayojyaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ sarvaśūlanivāraṇam || 52
mūtrāntaḥpācitāṃ śuṣkāṃ lohacūrṇasamanvitām
saguḍāmabhayāmadyātsarvaśūlapraśāntaye || 53
vāte nirūhāḥ savirecanāśca
kṣīraprayogā madhurāśca pitte
tiktāḥ kaṣāyāḥ kaṭukāstathaiva
vāmyaśca śūle kaphasaṃniviṣṭe || 54
nāgaraṃ pippalī bilvaṃ kāravī cavyacitrakam
hiṅgudāḍimavṛkṣāmlavacākṣārāmlavetasam || 55
varṣābhūḥ kṛṣṇalavaṇamajājī bījapūrakam
dadhi triguṇitaṃ sarpistatsiddhaṃ dādhikaṃ smṛtam || 56
gulmārśaḥplīhahṛtpārśvaśūlayonirujāpaham
doṣasaṃśamanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dādhikaṃ paramaṃ smṛtam || 57
kambalāvṛtagātrasya prāṇāyāmaṃ prakurvataḥ
kaṭutailāktasaktūnāṃ dhūpaḥ śūlāpahaḥ paraḥ || 58
vedanā ca tṛṣā mūrchā ānāho gauravārucī
kāsaḥ śvāsaśca hikkā ca śūlasyopadravāḥ smṛtāḥ || 59
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ madyaṃ lavaṇaṃ kaṭukāni ca
vegarodhaṃ śucaṃ krodhaṃ varjayecchūli vaidalam || 60
Adhikāra 27
laṅghanaṃ vamanaṃ śastaṃ virekaścānuvāsanam
nirūho vājigandhādirmādhutailikabastayaḥ || 1
viḍaṅgataṇḍulavyoṣatrivṛddanti sacitrakam
sarvāṇyetāni cā''hṛtya sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet || 2
guḍena modakānkṛtvā bhakṣayetprātarutthitaḥ
uṣṇodakānupānaṃ ca dadyādagnivivardhanam
jayettridoṣajaṃ śūlaṃ pariṇāmasamudbhavam || 3
nāgaratilaguḍakalkaṃ payasā saṃsādhya yaḥ pumānadyāt
ugraṃ pariṇatiśūlaṃ tasyāpaitīha saptarātreṇa || 4
eraṇḍavahniśambūkavarṣābhūgokṣuraṃ samam
antardagdhvā pibedadbhiruṣṇābhiḥ śūlaśāntaye || 5
śambūkajaṃ bhasma pītaṃ jalenoṣṇena tatkṣaṇāt
paktijaṃ vinihantyetacchūlaṃ viṣṇurivāsurān || 6
śambūkaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ caiva pañcaiva lavaṇāni ca
samāṃśāṃ guṭikāṃ kṛtvā kalambakarasena vā || 7
prātarbhojanakāle vā bhakṣayettu yathābalam
śūlādvimucyate jantuḥ sahasā pariṇāmajāt || 8
yaḥ pibati saptarātraṃ saktūnekānkalāyayūṣeṇa
sa jayati pariṇāmarujaṃ cirajāmapi kimuta nūtanajām || 9
lohacūrṇaṃ varāyuktaṃ vilīḍhaṃ madhusarpiṣā
paktiśūlaṃ ca śamayettanmalaṃ vā prayojitam || 10
kṛṣṇābhayālohacūrṇaṃ lihyātsamadhuśarkaram
pariṇāmabhavaṃ śūlaṃ sadyo hanti sudāruṇam || 11
pathyāloharajaḥ śuṇṭhīcūrṇaṃ mākṣikasarpiṣā
pariṇāmarujaṃ hanti vātapittakaphātmikām || 12
sāmudraṃ saindhavaṃ kṣāro rucakaṃ romakaṃ viḍam || 13
dantī loharajaḥkiṭṭaṃ trivṛtsūraṇakaṃ samam
dadhigomūtrapayasā mandapāvakapācitam || 14
tadyathāgnibalaṃ cūrṇaṃ pibeduṣṇena vāriṇā
jīrṇe jīrṇe ca bhuñjīta māṣādighṛtabhojanam || 15
nābhiśūlaṃ harennūnaṃ gulmaplīhakṛtaṃ ca yat
vidradhyaṣṭhīlikaṃ hanti kaphavātodbhavaṃ tathā || 16
śūlānāmapi sarveṣāmauṣadhaṃ nāstyataḥ param
pariṇāmasamutthasya viśeṣeṇāntakṛnmatam || 17
sapippalīguḍaṃ sarpiḥ pacetkṣīre caturguṇe
vinihantyamlapittaṃ ca śūlaṃ ca pariṇāmajam || 18
kvāthena kalkena ca pippalīnāṃ siddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ mākṣikasaṃprayuktam
kṣīrānupasyaiva nihantyavaśyaṃ śūlaṃ pravṛddhaṃ pariṇāmasaṃjñam || 19
kolāgranthikaśṛṅgaberacapalākṣāraiḥ samaṃ cūrṇitaṃ
maṇḍūraṃ surabhījale'ṣṭaguṇite paktvā'tha sāndrīkṛtam
taṃ khādedaśanādimadhyaviratau prāyeṇa dugdhānnabhu-
gjetuṃ vātakaphāmayānpariṇataṃ śūlaṃ ca śūlāni ca || 20
kolāgranthikasahitairviśvauṣadhamāgadhīyavakṣāraiḥ
prasthamayomalarajasaḥ palāṃśikaiścūrṇitairmiśraiḥ || 21
aṣṭaguṇamūtrayuktaṃ kramapākātpiṇḍatāṃ nayetsarvam
kolapramāṇaguṭikāstisro bhojyādimadhyaviratau ca || 22
rasasarpiryūṣapayomāṃsairaśnannaro nivārayati
annavivartanaśūlaṃ gulmaplīhāgnisādāṃśca || 23
maṇḍūrasya palānyaṣṭau gomūtre'rdhāḍhake pacet
kṣīraprasthaṃ ca tatsiddhaṃ paktiśūlaharaṃ nṛṇām || 24
maṇḍūrasya palānyaṣṭau gomūtre'ṣṭaguṇe pacet
capalānāgarakṣārapippalīmūlapippalīḥ || 25
saṃcūrṇāni kṣipettasminpalāṃśāḥ sāndratāṃ gate
guṭikāḥ kalpayettena paktiśūlanivāraṇīḥ || 26
saṃśodhya cūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā maṇḍūrasya palāṣṭakam
śatāvarīrasasyāṣṭau dadhnastu payasastathā || 27
palānyādāya catvāri tathā gavyasya sarpiṣaḥ
vipacetsarvamekatra yāvatpiṇḍatvamāgatam || 28
siddhaṃ tadbhakṣayenmadhye bhojanasyāgrato'pi vā
vātātmakaṃ pittabhavaṃ śūlaṃ ca pariṇāmajam || 29
nihantyeva hi yogo'yaṃ maṇḍūrasya na saṃśayaḥ
raso gandhaḥ śubhaḥ pāke vartiḥ syādgāḍhamardanāt || 30
viḍaṅgaṃ citrakaṃ cavyaṃ triphalā tryūṣaṇāni ca
navabhāgāni caitāni lohakiṭṭasamāni ca || 31
gomūtraṃ dviguṇaṃ dattvā mūtrārdhikaguḍānvitam
śanairmṛdvagninā paktvā susiddhaṃ piṇḍatāṃ gatam || 32
snigdhabhāṇḍe vinikṣipya bhakṣayetkolamātrayā
prāṅmadhyāntakrameṇaiva bhojanasya prayojitaḥ || 33
yogo'yaṃ śamayatyāśu paktiśūlaṃ sudāruṇam
kāmalāṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca śothaṃ mandāgnitāmapi || 34
arśāṃsi grahaṇīdoṣaṃ kṛmigulmodarāṇi ca
nāśayedamlapittaṃ ca sthaulyaṃ caivāpakarṣati || 35
varjayecchuṣkaśākāni vidāhyamlakaṭūni ca
paktiśūlāntako hyeṣa guḍo maṇḍūrasaṃjñitaḥ
śūlārtānāṃ kṛpāhetostārayā parikīrtitaḥ || 36
akṣāmalakaśivānāṃ svarasaiḥ pakvaṃ sulohajaṃ reṇum
saguḍaṃ yadyupayuṅkte muñcati śulī tridoṣajaṃ śūlam || 37
dhātrīcūrṇasyāṣṭau palāni catvāri lohacūrṇasya || 38
yaṣṭīmadhukarajaśca dvipalaṃ dadyātpaṭe ghṛṣṭam
amṛtākvāthenaitaccūrṇaṃ bhāvyaṃ tu saptāham || 39
caṇḍātape viśuṣkaṃ bhūyaḥ piṣṭvā nave ghaṭe sthāpyam
ghṛtamadhunā saṃyuktaṃ bhaktādau madhyato'nte ca || 40
trīnapi vārānkhādetpathyaṃ doṣānubandhena
bhaktasyā''dau nāśayati doṣānpittānilodbhūtān || 41
madhyennaṃ viṣṭambhaṃ jayati nṛṇāṃ vidahyate nānnam
pānānnakṛtāndoṣānbhaktānte śīlitaṃ jayati || 42
evaṃ jīryati cānne śūlaṃ nṝṇāṃ sukaṣṭamapi
harati ca sahasā yukto yogaścāyaṃ jaratpittam || 43
cakṣuṣyaṃ palitaghnaṃ kaphapittabhavāñjayedrogān
prasādayati ca raktaṃ pāṇḍutvaṃ kāmalāṃ jayati || 44
svinnapīḍitakūṣmāṇḍāttulārdhaṃ bhṛṣṭamājyataḥ
prasthārdhe khaṇḍatulyaṃ tu pacedāmalakīrasāt || 45
prasthe susvinnakūṣmāṇḍarasaprasthe ca ghaṭṭayan
darvyā pākaṃ gate tasmiṃścūrṇīkṛtya vinikṣipet || 46
dve dve pale kaṇājājīśuṇṭhīnāṃ maricasya ca
palaṃ tālīsadhānyākaṃ caturjātakamustakam || 47
karṣapramāṇaṃ pratyekaṃ prasthārdhaṃ mākṣikasya ca
paktiśūlaṃ nihantyetaddoṣatrayakṛtaṃ ca yat || 48
chardyamlapittamūrchāśca śvāsakāsāvarocakam
hṛcchūlaṃ raktapittaṃ ca pṛṣṭhaśūlaṃ ca nāśayet
rasāyanamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ khaṇḍāmalakasaṃjñitam || 49
kalāyacūrṇabhāgau dvau lohacūrṇasya cāparaḥ
kāravellapalāśānāṃ rasenaiva vimarditam || 50
karṣamātrāṃ tataścaikāṃ bhakṣayedguṭikāṃ naraḥ
maṇḍānupānā sā hanti jaratpittaṃ sudāruṇam || 51
lihyādvā traiphalaṃ cūrṇamayaścūrṇasamāyutam
yaṣṭīcūrṇena vā yuktaṃ lihyātkṣaudreṇa tadgade || 52
pittāntaṃ vamanaṃ kṛtvā kaphāntaṃ ca virecanam || 53
annadrave tu tatkāryaṃ jaratpitte yadīritam
āmapakvāśaye śuddhe gacchedannadravaḥ śamam || 54
māṣeṇḍarī satuṣikā svinnā sarpiryutā hitā
godhūmamaṇḍakaṃ tatra sarpiṣā guḍasaṃyutam
sasitaṃ śītadugdhena mṛditaṃ vā hitaṃ matam || 55
annadrave duścikitsyo durvijñeyo mahāgadaḥ
tasmāttasya praśamane paraṃ yatnaṃ samācaret || 56
annadrave jaratpitte vahnirmando bhavedyataḥ
tasmādatrānnapānāni mātrāhīnāni kalpayet || 57
annadrave tu yatproktaṃ jaratpitte'pi taddhitam
iti saṃkṣepataḥ proktamannadravacikitsitam || 58
Adhikāra 28
trivṛtsudhāpatratilādiśākagrāmyaudakānūparasairyavānnam
anyaiśca sṛṣṭānilamūtraviḍbhiradyātprasannāguḍasīdhupāyī || 1
āsthāpanaṃ mārutaje snigdhasvinnasya śasyate
purīṣaje tu kartavyo vidhirānāhikastu yaḥ
kṣīravaitaraṇau bastī yuñjyāttatra cikitsakaḥ || 2
śyāmā dantī dravantī snuṅmahāśyāmā snuhī trivṛt || 3
saptalā śaṅkhinī śvetā rājavṛkṣaḥ satilvakaḥ
kampillakaḥ karañjaśca hemakṣīrītyayaṃ gaṇaḥ || 4
sarpistailarajaḥkvāthakalkeṣvanyatamena tu
udāvartodarānāhaviṣagulmavināśanaḥ || 5
trivṛtkṛṣṇāharītakyo dvicatuṣpañcabhāgikāḥ
guṭikā guḍatulyāstā viḍvibandhagadāpahāḥ || 6
harītakī yavakṣāraḥ pīlunī trivṛtā tathā
ghṛtaiścūrṇamidaṃ peyamudāvartavināśanam || 7
hiṅgukuṣṭhavacāsvarjibiḍaṃ ceti dviruttaram
pītaṃ madyena taccūrṇamudāvartaharaṃ param || 8
khaṇḍapalaṃ trivṛtāsamamupakulyākarṣacūrṇitaṃ ślakṣṇam
prāgbhojane ca samadhu viḍālapadakaṃ lihetprājñaḥ || 9
etadgāḍhapurīṣe pitte ca kaphe ca viniyojyam
svādurnṛpayogyo'yaṃ cūrṇo nārācako nāmnā || 10
hiṅgumākṣikasindhūtthaiḥ paktvā varti suvartitām
ghṛtābhyaktāṃ gude dadyādudāvartavināśinīm || 11
madanaṃ pippalī kuṣṭhaṃ vacā gaurāśca sarṣapāḥ
guḍakṣārasamāyuktā phalavartiḥ praśasyate || 12
āgāradhūmasindhūtthatailayuktāmlamūlakam
kṣuṇṇanirguṇḍipatraṃ vā svinne pāyau kṣipedbudhaḥ || 13
sauvarcalāḍhyāṃ madirāṃ mūtre tvabhihate pibet
elāṃ vā'pyatha madyena kṣīravāri pibedbudhaḥ
dusparśāsvarasaṃ vā'pi kaṣāyaṃ kakubhasya ca || 14
īrvārubījaṃ toyena pibedvā'lavaṇīkṛtam
pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ drākṣārasamathāpi vā || 15
sarvathaivopayuñjīta mūtrakṛcchrāśmarīvidhim || 16
snehaiḥ svedairudāvartaṃ jṛmbhajaṃ samupakramet
aśrumokṣo'śruje kāryaḥ snigdhasvinnasya yatnataḥ || 17
kṣavaje kṣavapatreṇa ghrāṇasthenā''nayetkṣavam
udgāraje kramopetaṃ snaihikaṃ dhūmamācaret || 18
chardyāghātaṃ yathādoṣaṃ nasyasnehādibhirjayet
bastiśuddhikarāvāpaṃ caturguṇajalaṃ payaḥ || 19
āvārināśātkvathitaṃ pītavantaṃ prakāmataḥ
ramayeyuḥ priyā nāryaḥ śukrodāvartinaṃ naram || 20
atrābhyaṅgāvagāhāśca madirāścaraṇāyudhāḥ
śāliḥ payo nirūhāśca hitaṃ maithunameva ca || 21
kṣudvighāte hitaṃ snigdhamuṣṇamalpaṃ ca bhojanam
tṛṣṇāghāte pibetmanthaṃ yavāgūṃ cāpi śītalām || 22
rasenādyātsuviśrāntaḥ śramaśvāsāturo naraḥ
nidrāghāte pibetkṣīraṃ svapyācceṣṭakathā naraḥ || 23
Adhikāra 29
ānāhe'pi prayuñjīta udāvartaharāṃ kriyām
dviruttaraṃ hiṅgu vacā sakuṣṭhā suvarcikā caiva viḍaṅgacūrṇam
sukhāmbunā''nāhaviṣūcikārtihṛdrogagulmordhvasamīraṇaghnam || 1
vacābhayācitrakayāvaśūkānsapippalīkātiviṣānsakuṣṭhān
uṣṇāmbunā''nāhavimūḍhavātānpītvā jayedāśu hitaudanāśī || 2
trivṛddharītakī śyāmāḥ snuhīkṣīreṇa bhāvayet
vaṭikā mūtrapītāstāḥ śreṣṭhāścā''nāhabhedikāḥ || 3
rāḍhadhūmaviḍavyoṣaguḍamūtrairvipācitā
gude'ṅguṣṭhasamā vartirnidheyā''nāhaśūlanut || 4
vartistrikaṭukasaindhavasarṣapagṛhadhūmakuṣṭhamadanaphalaiḥ
madhuni guḍe vā pāyvīritā'ṅguṣṭhapramāṇavartiriyam || 5
dṛṣṭaphalā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ praṇihitā gude ghṛtābhyaktā
ānāhodāvartapraśamanī jaṭharagulmanivāraṇī || 6
mūlakaṃ śuṣkamārdraṃ ca varṣābhūmūlapañcakam || 7
ārevataphalaṃ cāpsu tena paktvā pacedghṛtam
tatpītamātraṃ śamayedudāvartamaśeṣataḥ || 8
Adhikāra 30
laghvannaṃ dīpanaṃ snigdhamuṣṇaṃ vātānulomanam
bṛṃhaṇaṃ ca bhavetsarvaṃ taddhitaṃ sarvagulminām || 1
gulmināmanilaśāntirupāyaiḥ sarvaśo vidhivadācaraṇīyā
mārute'tra vijite'nyamudīrṇaṃ doṣamalpamapi karma nihanyāt || 2
srotasāṃ mārdavaṃ kṛtvā jitvā mārutamulbaṇam
bhittvā vibandhaṃ snigdhasya svedo gulmamapohati || 3
prāgeva vātike gulme susnigdhaṃ sveditaṃ naram
recitaṃ sneharekaiśca nirūhaiḥ sānuvāsanaiḥ
upācaredbhiṣakprājño mātrākālavivekataḥ || 4
sukhoṣṇā jāṅgalarasāḥ susnigdhā vyaktasaindhavāḥ
kaṭutrikasamāyuktā hitāḥ pāneṣu gulminām || 5
kumbhīpiṇḍeṣṭakāsvedānkārayetkuśalo bhiṣak || 6
upanāhāśca kartavyāḥ sukhoṣṇāḥ sālvaṇādayaḥ
sthānāvaseko raktasya bāhumadhye śirāvyadhaḥ
svedo'nulomanaṃ caiva praśastaṃ sarvagulminām || 7
mātuluṅgaraso hiṅgu dāḍimaṃ viḍasaindhavam
surāmaṇḍena pātavyaṃ vātagulmarujāpaham || 8
pibederaṇḍatailaṃ vā vāruṇīmaṇḍamiśritam
tadeva tailaṃ payasā vātagulmī pibennaraḥ || 9
svarjikākuṣṭhasahitaḥ kṣāraḥ ketakijo'pi vā
tailena pītaḥ śamayedgulmaṃ pavanasaṃbhavam || 10
vātagulmapratīkāre prakupyati yadā kaphaḥ
śastamullekhanaṃ tatra cūrṇādyāśca kaphāpahāḥ || 11
yadi kupyati vā pittaṃ virekastatra bheṣajam
doṣaghnairapyaśānte tu gulme śoṇitamokṣaṇam || 12
kākolyādimahātiktavāsādyaiḥ pittagulminam
snehitaṃ sraṃsayetpaścādyojayedbastikarmaṇā || 13
snigdhoṣṇaje pittagulme kampillaṃ madhunā lihet
recanārthī rasaṃ vā'tha drākṣāyāḥ saguḍaṃ pibet || 14
dāhaśūlādisaṃkṣobhasvapnanāśārucijvaraiḥ
vidahyamānaṃ jānīyādgulmaṃ tamupanāhayet || 15
pakvastu pīḍitonnāmī bahistuṅgo'lpavedanaḥ
śyāvo bastinibhastatra vyadhaḥ śodhanaropaṇam || 16
svayamūrdhvamadho vā'pi sa ceddoṣaḥ pravartate
dvādaśāhamupekṣeta rakṣannannairupadravān || 17
paraṃ tu śodhanaṃ sarpiḥ śuddhe samadhu tiktakam || 18
snehopanāhanasvedatīkṣṇasraṃsanabastibhiḥ
yogaiśca vātagulmoktaiḥ śleṣmagulmamupācaret || 19
pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ toyaṃ purāṇaṃ vāruṇīrasam
kaphagulmī pibetkāle jīrṇamārdvīkameva ca || 20
yavānīcūrṇitaṃ takraṃ viḍena lavaṇīkṛtam
pibetsaṃdīpanaṃ vātamūtravarconulomanam
vyāmiśradoṣe vyāmiśra eṣa eva kriyākramaḥ || 21
saṃnipātotthite gulme tridoṣaghno vidhirhitaḥ || 22
vacā'bhayā viḍaṃ śuṇṭhīhiṅgukuṣṭhāgnidīpyakāḥ
dvitriṣaṭcaturekāṣṭasaptapañcāṃśakāḥ kramāt || 23
cūrṇaṃ madyādibhiḥ pītaṃ gulmānāhodarāpaham
śūlārśaḥśvāsakāsaghnaṃ grahaṇīdīpanaṃ param || 24
yavānīhiṅgusindhūtthakṣārasauvarcalābhayāḥ
surāmaṇḍena pātavyā gulmaśūlaniṣūdanāḥ || 25
pūtīkapatragajacirbhaṭacavyavahni-
vyoṣaṃ ca saṃstaracitaṃ lavaṇopadhānam
dagdhvā vicūrṇya dadhimastuyutaṃ prayojyaṃ
gulmodaraśvayathupāṇḍugudodbhaveṣu || 26
hiṅgupuṣkaramūlāni tumburūṇi harītakī
śyāmā biḍaṃ saindhavaṃ ca yavakṣāraṃ mahauṣadham || 27
yavakvāthodakenaitadghṛtabhṛṣṭaṃ tu pāyayet
tenāsya bhidyate gulmaḥ saśūlaḥ saparigrahaḥ || 28
vacā harītakī hiṅgu saindhavaṃ sāmlavetasam
yavakṣāraṃ yavānīṃ ca pibeduṣṇena vāriṇā || 29
etaddhi gulmanicayaṃ saśūlaṃ saparigraham
bhinatti saptarātreṇa vahnivṛddhiṃ karoti ca || 30
pippalīpippalīmūlājājīcitrakasaindhavaiḥ
yuktā pītā surā hanti gulmamāśu sudustaram || 31
nādeyīkuṭajārkaśigrubṛhatīsnugbilvabhallātaka-
vyāghrīkiṃśukapāribhadrakajaṭāpāmārganīpāgnikān
vāsāmuṣkakapāṭalāḥ salavaṇā dagdhvā jale pācitaṃ
hiṅgvādiprativāpametaduditaṃ gulmodarāṣṭhīliṣu || 32
hiṅgūgragandhāviḍaśuṇṭyajājīharītakīpuṣkaramūlakuṣṭham
bhāgottaraṃ cūrṇitametadiṣṭaṃ gulmodarājīrṇaviṣūcikāsu || 33
triphalākāñcanakṣīrīsaptalānīlinīvacāḥ
trāyantīhapuṣātiktātrivṛtsaindhavapippalīḥ || 34
pibedvicūrṇya mūtroṣṇavārimāṃsarasādibhiḥ
sarvagulmodaraplīhakuṣṭhārśaḥśoṣapīḍitaḥ || 35
śatāhvācirabilvatvagdārubhārgīkaṇodbhavaḥ
kalkaḥ pīto haredgulmaṃ tilakvāthena raktajam || 36
tilakvātho guḍavyoṣahiṅgubhārgīyuto bhavet
pānaṃ raktabhave gulme naṣṭe puṣpe ca yoṣitām || 37
pīto dhātrīraso yuktyā kiṃśukakṣārasādhitaḥ
kṣāratryūṣaṇasaṃyuktā madirā cāsragulmanut || 38
viśeṣamaparaṃ cāsya śṛṇu raktaprasādanam
palāśakṣāratoyena siddhaṃ sarpiḥ pibecca sā (?)
uṣṇairvā bhedayedbhinne vidhirāsṛgdaro hitaḥ || 39
hapuṣāvyoṣapṛthvīkācavyacitrakasaindhavaiḥ
sājājīpippalīmūladīpyakairvipacedghṛtam || 40
sakolamūlakarasaṃ sakṣīradadhidāḍimam
tatparaṃ vātagulmaghnaṃ śūlānāhavibandhanut || 41
yonyarśograhaṇīdoṣaśvāsakāsārucijvarān
pārśvahṛdbastiśūlaṃ ca ghṛtametadvyapohati || 42
pañcaprabhṛti yatra syurdravāṇi snehasaṃvidhau
tatra snehasamānyāhurarvāktu syāccaturguṇam || 43
jaladroṇe vipaktavyā viṃśatiḥ pañca cābhayāḥ
dantyāḥ palāni tāvanti citrakasya tathaiva ca || 44
tato'ṣṭabhāgaśeṣeṇa paceddantīsamaṃ guḍam
tāścābhayāstrivṛccūrṇāttailāccāpi catuṣpalam || 45
palamekaṃ kaṇāśuṇṭhyoḥ siddhe lehe'tha śītale
kṣaudrārdhakuḍavaṃ dadyāccāturjātapalaṃ tathā || 46
tato lehapalaṃ līḍhvā jagdhvā caikāṃ harītakīm
snigdho viricyate doṣaḥ prasthamadyādrasaudanam || 47
plīhaśvayathugulmārśohṛtpāṇḍugrahaṇīgadāḥ
śāmyantputkleśaviṣamajvarakuṣṭhānyarocakāḥ || 48
tryūṣaṇatriphalādhānyaviḍaṅgacavyacitrakaiḥ
kalkīkṛtairghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ sakṣīraṃ vātagulmanut || 49
drākṣāmadhukakharjūraṃ vidārīṃ saśatāvarīm
parūṣakāṇi triphalāṃ sādhayetpalasaṃmitām || 50
jalāḍhake pādaśeṣe rasamāmalakasya ca
ghṛtamikṣurasaṃ kṣīramabhayākalkapādikam || 51
sādhayettu ghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ śarkarākṣaudrapādikam
pittagulmaharaṃ sarpiḥ sarvapittāmayāpaham || 52
savyoṣakṣāralavaṇaṃ daśamūlaśṛtaṃ ghṛtam
kaphagulmaṃ haratyāśu sahiṅgubiḍadāḍimam || 53
pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
palikaiḥ savayakṣārairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 54
kṣīraprasthena tatsarpirhanti gulmaṃ kaphātmakam
grahaṇīpāṇḍuhṛdrogaplīhakāsajvarāpaham || 55
dhātrīphalānāṃ svarase ṣaḍaṅgaṃ vipacedghṛtam
śarkarāsaindhavopetaṃ taddhitaṃ sarvagulminām || 56
vṛścīvamurubūkaṃ ca varṣābhūbṛhatīdvayam
citrakaṃ ca jaladroṇe pacetpādāvaśeṣitam || 57
māgadhīcitrakakṣaudralipte kumbhe nidhāpayet
madhunaḥ prasthamāvāpya pathyācūrṇārdhasaṃyutam || 58
tuṣoṣitaṃ daśāhaṃ taṃ jīrṇabhaktaḥ pibennaraḥ
ariṣṭo'yaṃ jayedgulmamavipākaṃ sudustaram || 59
vallūraṃ mūlakaṃ matsyāñśuṣkaśākāni vaidalam
na khādedvālukaṃ gulmī madhurāṇi phalāni ca || 60
Adhikāra 31
vātopasṛṣṭe hṛdaye vāmayetsnigdhamāturam
dvipañcamūlīkvāthena sasnehalavaṇena ca || 1
pippalyelā vacā hiṅgu yavakṣāro'tha saindhavam
sauvarcalamatho śuṇṭhī ajamodāvacūrṇitam || 2
phaladhānyāmlakaulatthadadhimadyāsavādibhiḥ
pāyayecchuddhadehaṃ ca snehenānyatamena vā || 3
nāgaraṃ vā pibeduṣṇaṃ kaṣāyaṃ cāgnivardhanam
kāsaśvāsānilaharaṃ śūlahṛdroganāśanam || 4
śrīparṇīmadhukakṣaudrasitāguḍajalairvamet
pittopasṛṣṭe hṛdaye seveta madhuraiḥ śṛtam
ghṛtaṃ kaṣāyāṃścoddiṣṭānpittajvaravināśanān || 5
śītāḥ pradehāḥ pariṣecanāni tathā vireko hṛdi pittaduṣṭe
drākṣāsitākṣaudraparūṣakaiḥ syācchuddhe ca pittāpahamannapānam || 6
piṣṭvā pibeccāpi sitāṃ jalena yaṣṭyāhvayaṃ tiktakarohiṇīṃ ca || 7
arjunasya tvacā siddhaṃ kṣīraṃ yojyaṃ hṛdāmaye
sitayā pañcamūlyā vā balayā madhukena vā || 8
ghṛtena dugdhena guḍāmbhasā vā pibanti cūrṇaṃ kakubhatvaco ye
hṛdrogajīrṇajvarapittaraktaṃ hatvā bhaveyuścirajīvinaste || 9
vacānimbakaṣāyābhyāṃ vāntaṃ hṛdi kaphotthite
vātahṛdrogahṛccūrṇaṃ pippalyādi ca pāyayet || 10
tridoṣaje laṅghanamāditaḥ syādannaṃ ca sarveṣu hitaṃ vidheyam
hīnātimadhyatvamavekṣya caiva kāryaṃ trayāṇāmapi karma śastam || 11
cūrṇaṃ puṣkarajaṃ lihyānmākṣikeṇa samāyutam
hṛcchūlaśvāsakāsaghnaṃ kṣayahikkānivāraṇam || 12
tailājyaguḍavipakvaṃ cūrṇaṃ godhūmapārthajaṃ vā'pi
pibati payo'nu ca sa bhavejjitasakalahṛdāmayaḥ puruṣaḥ || 13
godhūmakakubhacūrṇaṃ chāgapayogavyasarpiṣā pakvam
madhuśarkarāsametaṃ śamayati hṛdrogamuddhataṃ puṃsām || 14
mūlaṃ nāgabalāyāstu cūrṇaṃ dugdhena pāyayet
hṛdrogakāsaśvāsaghnaṃ kakubhasya ca valkalam || 15
rasāyanaṃ paraṃ balyaṃ vātajinmāsayojitam
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa jīvedvarṣaśataṃ dhruvam || 16
hiṅgūgragandhābiḍaviśvakṛṣṇākuṣṭhābhayācitrakayāvaśūkam
pibetsasauvarcalapauṣkarāḍhyaṃ yavāmbhasā śūlahṛdāmayaghnam || 17
daśamūlakaṣāyaṃ tu lavaṇakṣārayojitam
śvāsakāsaṃ sahṛdrogaṃ gulmaśūlaṃ ca nāśayet || 18
kṛmihṛdrogiṇaṃ snigdhaṃ bhojayetpiśitaudanam
dadhnā ca palalopetaṃ tryahaṃ paścādvirecayet
sugandhibhiḥ salavaṇairyogaiḥ sājājiśarkaraiḥ || 19
viḍaṅgagāḍhaṃ dhānyāmlaṃ pāyayeddhitamuttamam || 20
kṛmije ca pibenmūtraṃ viḍaṅgāmayasaṃyutam
hṛdi sthitāḥ patantyevamadhastātkṛmayo nṛṇām || 21
yavānnaṃ vitareccāsmai saviḍaṅgamataḥ param || 22
mukhyaṃ śatārdhaṃ ca harītakīnāṃ sauvarcalasyāpi paladvayaṃ ca
pakvaṃ ghṛtaṃ vallabhaketi nāmnā hṛcchvāsagulmodaramārutaghnam || 23
śvadaṃṣṭrośīramañjiṣṭhābalākāśmaryakattṛṇam
darbhamūlaṃ pṛthakparṇī palāśarṣabhakau sthiram || 24
palikānsādhayetteṣāṃ rase kṣīracaturguṇe
kalkaiḥ svaguptarṣabhakamedājīvantijīvakaiḥ || 25
śatāvaryardhi(yṛddhi)mṛdvīkāśarkarāśrāviṇībisaiḥ
prasthaḥ siddho ghṛtādvātapittahṛdrogaśūlanut || 26
mūtrakṛcchrapramehārśaḥkāsaśvāsakṣayāpahaḥ
dhanuḥstrīmadyabhārādhvakhinnānāṃ balamāṃsadaḥ || 27
ghṛtaṃ balānāgabalārjunāmbusiddhaṃ sayaṣṭīmadhukalkapādam
hṛdrogaśūlakṣayaraktapittakāsānilāsṛkśamayatyudīrṇam || 28
pārthasya kalkasya rasena siddhaṃ śastaṃ ghṛtaṃ sarvahṛdāmayeṣu || 29
Adhikāra 32
aśmarīśāntividhayo ya uktāḥ prasamīkṣya tān
yathādoṣaṃ prayuñjīta snehādimapi ca kramam || 1
abhyañjanasnehanirūhabastisvedopanāhottarabastisekān
sthirādibhirvātaharaiśca siddhāndadyādrasāṃścānilamūtrakṛcchre || 2
amṛtānāgaraṃ dhātrīvājigandhātrikaṇṭakān
prapibedvātarogārtaḥ śūlavānmūtrakṛcchravān || 3
sekāvagāhāḥ śiśirāḥ pradehā
graiṣmo vidhirbastipayovirekāḥ
drākṣāvidārīkṣurasairghṛtaiśca
kṛcchreṣu pittaprabhaveṣu kāryaḥ || 4
kuśaḥ kāśaḥ śaro darbha ikṣuśceti tṛṇodbhavam
pittakṛcchraharaṃ pañcamūlaṃ bastiviśodhanam
etatsiddhaṃ payaḥ pītaṃ meḍhragaṃ hanti śoṇitam || 5
śatāvarīkāśakuśaśvadaṃṣṭrāvidāriśālīkṣukaserukāṇām
kvāthaṃ suśītaṃ madhuśarkarāktaṃ pibañjayetpaittikamūtrakṛcchram || 6
harītakīgokṣurarājavṛkṣapāṣāṇabhiddhanvayavāsakānām
kvāthaṃ pibenmākṣikasaṃprayuktaṃ kṛcchre sadāhe saruje vibandhe || 7
guḍenā''malakaṃ vṛṣyaṃ śramaghnaṃ tarpaṇaṃ priyam
pittāsṛgdāhaśūlaghnaṃ mūtrakṛcchranivāraṇam || 8
mūtreṇa surayā vā'pi kadalīsvarasena vā
kaphakṛcchravināśāya ślakṣṇaṃ piṣṭvā truṭiṃ pibet || 9
takreṇa piṣṭaṃ śitimārakasya bījaṃ pibetkṛcchravināśanāya
pibettathā taṇḍuladhāvanena pravālacūrṇaṃ kaphamūtrakṛcchre || 10
sarvaṃ tridoṣaprabhave tu vāyoḥ
sthānānupūrvyā prasamīkṣya kāryam
tribhyo'dhike prāgvamanaṃ kaphe syā-
tpitte virekaḥ pavane tu bastiḥ || 11
bṛhatīdhāvanīpāṭhāyaṣṭīmadhukaliṅgakāḥ
pācanīyo bṛhatyādiḥ kṛcchre doṣatrayāpahaḥ || 12
guḍena miśritaṃ kṣīraṃ kaduṣṇaṃ kāmataḥ pibet
mūtrakṛcchreṣu sarveṣu śarkarāvātaroganut || 13
tathā'bhighātaje kuryātsadyo vraṇacitsitam
mūtrakṛcchre sadā cāsya kāryā vātaharī kriyā || 14
svedacūrṇakriyābhyaṅgabastayaḥ syuḥ purīṣaje || 15
kvāthaṃ gokṣurabījasya yavakṣārayutaṃ pibet
mūtrakṛcchraṃ śakṛjjaṃ ca pītaḥ śīghraṃ nivārayet || 16
śarkarāśmarikṛcchre ca śarkarāśmarināśanam
lehyaṃ śukravibandhotthe śilājatu samākṣikam || 17
vṛṣyairbṛṃhitadhātośca vidheyāḥ pramadottamāḥ
elāhiṅguyutaṃ kṣīraṃ sarpirmiśraṃ pibennaraḥ
mūtradoṣaviśuddhyarthaṃ śukradoṣaharaṃ ca tat || 18
trikaṇṭakāragvadhadarbhakāśa-
durālabhāparvatabhedapathyāḥ
nighnanti pītā madhunā'śmarīṃ ca
saṃprāptamṛtyorapi mūtrakṛcchram || 19
elāśmabhedakaśilājatupippalīnāṃ
cūrṇāni taṇḍulajalairlulitāni pītvā
yadvā guḍena sahitānyavalihya caitā-
nyāsannamṛtyurapi jīvati mūtrakṛcchrī || 20
ayorajaḥ sūkṣmapiṣṭaṃ madhunā saha yojitam
mūtrakṛcchraṃ nihantyāśu tribhirlehairna saṃśayaḥ || 21
sitātulyo yavakṣāraḥ sarvakṛcchravināśanaḥ
nidigdhikāraso vā'tha sakṣaudraḥ kṛcchranāśanaḥ || 22
śatāvarīkāśakuśaśvadaṃṣṭrā-
vidārikekṣvāmalakeṣu siddham
sarpiḥ payo vā sitayā vimiśraṃ
kṛcchreṣu pittaprabhaveṣu yojyam || 23
trikaṇṭakairaṇḍakuśādyabhīrukarkārukekṣusvarasena siddham
sarpirguḍārdhāṃśayutaṃ prapeyaṃ kṛcchrāśmarīmūtravighātadoṣe || 24
Adhikāra 33
snehasvedopapannasya hitaṃ snehavirecanam
tataḥ saṃśuddhadehasya hitāścottarabastayaḥ || 1
pāṭalyā yāvaśūkācca pāribhadrāttilādapi
kṣārodakena madirāṃ tvageloṣaṇasaṃyutām
pibedguḍopadaṃśānvā lihyādetānpṛthakpṛthak || 2
triphalākalkasaṃyuktaṃ lavaṇaṃ vā'pi pāyayet
nidigdhikāyāḥ svarasaṃ pibedvā tāntavasrutam || 3
jale kuṅkumakalkaṃ vā sakṣaudramuṣitaṃ niśi
satailaṃ pāṭalābhasma kṣāravadvā parisrutam || 4
kalkamīrvārubījānāmakṣamātraṃ sasaindhavam
dhānyāmlayuktaṃ pītvaiva mūtrāghātādvimucyate || 5
surāṃ sauvarcalavatīṃ mūtrāghātī pibennaraḥ || 6
dāḍimāmbuyutaṃ mukhyamelābījaṃ sanāgaram
pītvā surāṃ salavaṇāṃ mūtrāghātādvimucyate || 7
pibecchilājatu kvāthe gaṇe vīratarādike
rasaṃ durālabhāyā vā kaṣāyaṃ vāsakasya vā || 8
trikaṇṭakairaṇḍaśatāvarībhiḥ siddhaṃ ṣayo vā tṛṇapañcamūle
guḍapragāḍhaṃ saghṛtaṃ payo vā rogeṣu kṛcchrādiṣu śastametat || 9
śṛtaśītapayonnāśī candanaṃ taṇḍulāmbunā
pibetsaśarkaraṃ śreṣṭhamuṣṇavāte saśoṇite || 10
strīṇāmatiprasaṅgena śoṇitaṃ yasya sicyate
maithunoparamaścāsya bṛṃhaṇīyo hito vidhiḥ
tāmracūḍavasātailaṃ hitaṃ cottarabastiṣu || 11
svaguptāphalamṛdvīkākṛṣṇekṣurasitārajaḥ
samāṃśamardhabhāgāni kṣīrakṣaudraghṛtāni ca || 12
sarvaṃ samyagvimathyākṣamātraṃ līḍhvā payaḥ pibet
hanti śukrakṣayotthāṃśca doṣānvandhyāsutapradam || 13
citrakaṃ sārivā caiva balā kālānusārivā || 14
drākṣāviśālāpippalyastathā citraphalā bhavet
tathaiva madhukaṃ dadyāddadyādāmalakāni ca || 15
ghṛtāḍhakaṃ pacedetaiḥ kalkairakṣasamanvitaiḥ
kṣīradroṇe jaladroṇe tatsiddhamavatārayet || 16
śītaṃ parisrutaṃ caiva śarkarāprasthasaṃyutam
tugākṣīryāśca tatsarvaṃ matimānpratimiśrayet || 17
tato mitaṃ pibetkāle yathādoṣaṃ yathābalam
vātaretāḥ pittaretāḥ śleṣmaretāśca yo bhavet || 18
raktaretā granthiretāḥ pibedicchannarogitām
jīvanīyaṃ ca vṛṣyaṃ ca sarpiretanmahāguṇam || 19
prajāhitaṃ ca dhanyaṃ ca sarvarogaharaṃ śivam
sarpiretatprayuñjīta strī garbhaṃ labhate'cirāt || 20
asṛgdoṣaṃ jayeccāpi yonidoṣāṃśca saṃhatān
mūtradoṣeṣu sarveṣu kuryādetaccikitsitam || 21
Adhikāra 34
varuṇasya tvacaṃ śreṣṭhāṃ śuṇṭhīgokṣurasaṃyutām
yavakṣāraguḍaṃ dattvā kvāthayitvā pibeddhitām
aśmarīṃ vātajāṃ hanti cirakālānubandhinīm || 1
vīratarādirityeṣa gaṇo vātavikāranut
aśmarīśarkarāmūtrakṛcchrāghātarujāpahaḥ || 2
śuṇṭhyagnimanthapāṣāṇaśigruvaruṇagokṣuraiḥ || 3
abhayāragvadhaphalaiḥ kvāthaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ
rāmaṭhakṣāralavaṇacūrṇaṃ dattvā pibennaraḥ || 4
aśmarīmūtrakṛcchraghnaṃ dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ param
hanyātkoṣṭhāśritaṃ vātaṃ kaṭyūrugudameḍhragam || 5
pāṣāṇabhedo vasuko vaśiro'śmantakastathā
śatāvarī śvadaṃṣṭrā ca bṛhatī kaṇṭakārikā || 6
kapotavaṅkārtagalakāñcanośīragucchakāḥ
vṛkṣādanī bhallakaśca varuṇaḥ śākajaṃ phalam || 7
yavāḥ kulatthāḥ kolāni katakasya phalāni ca
ūṣakādipratīvāpameṣāṃ kvāthe śṛtaṃ ghṛtam
bhinatti vātasaṃbhūtāmaśmarīṃ kṣiprameva ca || 8
ūṣakādiḥ kaphaṃ hanti gaṇo medoviśoṣaṇaḥ
aśmarīśarkarāmūtraśūlaghnaḥ kaphagulmanut || 9
kṣārānyavāgūṃ peyāṃ ca kaṣāyāṇi payāṃsi ca
bhojanāni ca kurvīta varge'sminvātanāśane || 10
kāśaḥ kuśaḥ śaro guccha itkaṭo moraṭo'śmabhit || 11
darbho vidārī vārāhī śālimūlaṃ trikaṇṭakaḥ
bhallūkaḥ pāṭalī pāṭhā pattaro'tha kuraṇṭikā || 12
punarnavā śirīṣaśca kvathitāstena sādhitam
ghṛtaṃ śilāhvamadhukabījairindīvarasya ca || 13
trapuservārukādīnāṃ bījaiścā''vāpitaṃ śubham
bhinatti pittasaṃbhūtāmaśmarīṃ kṣiprameva ca || 14
kṣārānyavāgūḥ peyāśca kaṣāyāṇi payāṃsi ca
bhojanāni ca kurvīta varge'sminpittanāśane || 15
kuryātkṣārādikaṃ kvāthyaistasminkṣepamavāpakaiḥ
vargatvena yathālābhaṃ paribhāṣā pravartate || 16
śilājatu śilāhvaṃ syātpuṇḍrako gundragucchakau
madhukaḥ kṛtahvasvatvādbījairbījaka ucyate || 17
gaṇe varuṇakādau tu guggulvelāhareṇubhiḥ
kuṣṭhamustāhvamaricacitrakaiḥ sasurāhvayaiḥ || 18
etaiḥ siddhamajāsarpirūṣakādigaṇena ca
bhinatti kaphasaṃbhūtāmaśmarīṃ kṣiprameva ca || 19
kṣārānyavāgūṃ peyāṃ ca kaṣāyāṇi payāṃsi ca
bhojanāni prakurvīta varge'sminkaphanāśane || 20
varuṇādigaṇo hyeṣa kaphamedonivāraṇaḥ
vinihanti śiraḥśūlaṃ gulmābhyantaravidradhīn || 21
śukrāśmaryāṃ tu sāmānyo vidhiraśmarināśanaḥ
yavakṣāraguḍonmiśraṃ pibetpuṣpaphalodbhavam
rasaṃ mūtravibandhaghnaṃ śarkarāśmarināśanam || 22
nāgaravaruṇakagokṣurapāṣāṇabhedakapotavaṅkajaḥ kvāthaḥ
guḍayāvaśūkamiśraḥ pīto hantyaśmarīmugrām || 23
varuṇatvakśilābhedaśuṇṭhīgokṣurakaiḥ kṛtaḥ
kaṣāyaḥ kṣārasaṃyuktaḥ śarkarāṃ ca bhinattyapi || 24
pibedvaruṇamūlatvakkvāthaṃ tatkalkasaṃyutam
kvāthaśca śigrumūlotthaḥ kaduṣṇo'śmaripātanaḥ || 25
pibedvaruṇamūlatvakkaṣāyaṃ guḍasaṃyutam
aśmarīṃ pātayatyāśu bastiśūlavināśanam || 26
elopakulyāmadhukāśmamedakauntīśvadaṃṣṭrāvṛṣakorubūkaiḥ
śṛtaṃ pibedaśmajatu pragāḍhaṃ saśarkare sāśmarimūtrakṛcchre || 27
trikaṇṭakasya bījānāṃ cūrṇaṃ mākṣikasaṃyutam
avikṣīreṇa saptāhaṃ peyamaśmaribhedakam || 28
kulatthasindhūtthaviḍaṅgasāraṃ saśarkaraṃ śītaliyāvaśūkam
bījāni kūṣmāṇḍakagokṣurābhyāṃ ghṛtaṃ pacettadvaruṇasya toye || 29
duḥsādhyasarvāśmarimūtrakṛcchre mūtrābhighātaṃ ca samūtrabandham
etāni sarvāṇi nihanti śīghraṃ prarūḍhavṛkṣāniva vajrapātaḥ || 30
śarādipañcamūlyā vā kaṣāyeṇa pacedghṛtam
prasthaṃ gokṣurakalkena siddhamadyātsaśarkaram
aśmarīmūtrakṛcchraghnaṃ retomārgaviśodhanam || 31
varuṇasya tulāṃ kṣuṇṇāṃ jaladroṇe vipācayet
pādaśeṣaṃ parisrāvya ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 32
varuṇaḥ kadalī bilvaṃ tṛṇajaṃ pañcamūlakam
amṛtāṃ cāśmajaṃ deyaṃ bījaṃ ca trapuṣasya ca || 33
śataparvā tilakṣāraḥ palāśakṣārameva ca
yūthikāyāstu mūlāni kārṣikāṇi samāvapet || 34
akṣamātrāṃ pibejjanturdeśakālādyapekṣayā
jīrṇe cāsminpibetpūrvaṃ guḍaṃ jīrṇaṃ tu mastunā || 35
aśmarīṃ śarkarāṃ caiva mūtrakṛcchraṃ vināśayet
saindhavādyaṃ ca yattailamṛṣibhiḥ parikīrtitam || 36
tattailaṃ dviguṇakṣīraṃ pacedvīratarādinā
kvāthena pūrvakalkena sādhitaṃ tu bhiṣagvaraiḥ || 37
etattailavaraṃ śreṣṭhamaśmarīṇāṃ vināśanam
mūtrāghāte mūtrakṛcchre piccite mathite tathā || 38
bhagne śramābhipanne ca sarvathaiva praśasyate
vardhmādhikāranirdiṣṭaṃ saindhavādyamiheṣyate || 39
Adhikāra 35
śyāmākāḥ kodravoddālagodhūmacaṇakāḍhakī
kulatthāśca hitā bhojye purāṇā mehināṃ sadā || 1
mehināṃ tiktaśākāni jāṅgalā hariṇāṇḍajāḥ
yavānnavikṛtirmudgāḥ śasyante śāliṣaṣṭikāḥ || 2
pārijātajayānimbavahnigāyatri(trī)ṇāṃ pṛthak
pāṭhāyāḥ sāguroḥ pītādvayasya śāradasya ca || 3
jalekṣumadyasikatāśanairlavaṇapiṣṭakāḥ
sāndramehānkramādghnanti aṣṭau kvāthāḥ samākṣikāḥ || 4
dūrvākaserupūtīkakumbhīkaplavaśaivalam
jalena kvathitaṃ pītaṃ śukramehaharaṃ param || 5
triphalāragvadhadrākṣākaṣāyo madhusaṃyutaḥ
pīto nihanti phenākhyaṃ pramehaṃ niyataṃ nṛṇām || 6
rodhrābhayākaṭphalamustakānāṃ viḍaṅgapāṭhārjunadhanvanānām
kadambaśālārjunadīpyakānāṃ viḍaṅgadārvīdhavaśalyakānām
catvāra ete madhunā kaṣāyāḥ kaphaprameheṣu niṣevaṇīyāḥ || 7
rodhrārjunośīrakucandanānāmariṣṭasevyāmalakābhayānām || 8
dhātryarjunāriṣṭakavatsakānāṃ nīlotpalailātiniśārjunānām
catvāra ete vihitāḥ kaṣāyāḥ pittapramehe madhusaṃprayuktāḥ || 9
aśvatthacaturaṅgulanyagrodhādiphalatrayam
saraktasāramañjiṣṭhā kvāthāḥ pañca samākṣikāḥ || 10
nīlahāridraśuktākhyakṣāragaṇḍīrikāhvayān
mehānhanyuḥ kramādete sakṣaudro raktamehajit
kvāthaḥ kharjūrakāśmaryatindukāsthyamṛtākṛtaḥ || 11
chinnāvahnikaṣāyeṇa pāṭhākuṭajarāmaṭham
tiktāṃ kuṣṭhaṃ ca saṃcūrṇya sarpirmehe piberannaḥ || 12
kadarakhadirapūgakvāthaṃ kṣaudrāhvaye pibet
agnimanthakaṣāyaṃ tu vasāmehe prayojayet || 13
pāṭhāśirīṣadusparśamūrvākiṃśukatindukam
kapitthānāṃ bhiṣakkvāthaṃ hastimehe prayojayet || 14
kampillasaptacchadaśālajāni vaibhītarauhītakakauṭajāni
kapitthapuṣpāṇi ca cūrṇitāni kṣaudreṇa lihyātkaphapittamehī || 15
snigdhaṃ vāntaṃ viriktaṃ ca nirūḍhaṃ pāyayedrasam
dhātryāḥ sarvaprameheṣu niśākṣaudrasamanvitam || 16
kaṣāyamatha vā dārutriphalāmustakaiḥ śṛtam
triphalādārudārvyabdakvāthaḥ kṣaudreṇa mehahā || 17
kuṭajāsanadārvyabdaphalatrayabhavo'tha vā
guḍūcyāḥ svarasaḥ peyo madhunā sarvamehanut || 18
śālamuṣkakakampillakalkamakṣasamaṃ pibet
dhātrīrasena sakṣaudraṃ sarvamehaharaṃ param || 19
phalatrikaṃ dāruniśāṃ viśālāṃ
mustaṃ ca niṣkvāthya niśāṃśakalkam
pibetkaṣāyaṃ madhusaṃprayuktaṃ
sarvaprameheṣu samutthiteṣu || 20
madhunā triphalācūrṇamatha vā'śmajatūdbhavam
lohajaṃ vā'bhayotthaṃ vā lihyānmehanivṛttaye || 21
nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthaśyonākāragvadhāsanam
āmraṃ kapitthaṃ jambūṃ ca priyālaṃ kakubhaṃ dhavam || 22
madhūkaṃ madhukaṃ rodhraṃ varaṇaṃ pāribhadrakam
paṭolaṃ meṣaśṛṅgīṃ ca dantīṃ citrakamāḍhakīm || 23
karañjaṃ triphalāśakrabhallātakaphalāni ca
etāni samabhāgāni ślakṣṇacūrṇāni kārayet || 24
nyagrodhādyamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ madhunā saha lehayet
phalatrayarasaṃ cānupibenmūtraṃ viśudhyati || 25
etena viṃśatirmehā mūtrakṛcchrāṇi yāni ca
praśamaṃ yānti yogena piṭikā na prajāyate || 26
trikaṇṭakāśmantakasomavalkairbhallātakaiḥ sātiviṣaiḥ sarodhraiḥ
vacāpaṭolārjunanimbamustairharidrayā padmakadīpyakaiśca || 27
mañjiṣṭhayā cāgurucandanaiśca sarvaiḥ samastaiḥ kaphavātajeṣu
meheṣu tailaṃ vipacedghṛtaṃ tu pitteṣu miśraṃ triṣu lakṣaṇeṣu || 28
trikaṭutriphalācūrṇatulyayuktaṃ tu guggulum || 29
gokṣurakvāthasaṃyuktaṃ guṭikāṃ kārayedbhiṣak
doṣakālabalāpekṣī bhakṣayeccā''nulomikīm || 30
na cātra parihāro'sti karma kuryādyathepsitam
pramehānvātarogāṃśca mūtrāghātodaraṃ jayet || 31
kaphamehaharakvāthasiddhaṃ sarpiḥ kaphe hitam
pittamehaghnaniryūhasiddhaṃ pitte hitaṃ ghṛtam || 32
daśamūlaṃ karañjau dvau devadāru harītakī
varṣābhūrvaruṇo dantī citrakaṃ sapunarnavam || 33
sudhānīpakadambāśca bilvabhallātakāni ca
śaṭhī puṣkaramūlaṃ ca pippalīmūlameva ca || 34
pṛthagdaśapalānetānbhāgāṃstoyārmaṇe pacet
yavakolakulatthānāṃ prasthaṃ prasthaṃ ca dāpayet || 35
tena pādāvaśeṣeṇa ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
niculaṃ triphalā bhārgī rohiṣaṃ gajapippalī || 36
śṛṅgaveraṃ viḍaṅgāni vacā kampillakaṃ tathā
garbheṇānena tatsiddhaṃ pāyayecca yathābalam || 37
etaddha(ddhā)nvantaraṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ sarpiruttamam
kuṣṭhagulmapramehāṃśca śvayathuṃ vātaśoṇitam || 38
plīhodarāṇi cārśāṃsi vidradhīnpiṭakāstathā
apasmāraṃ tathonmādaṃ sarpiretanniyacchati
pāṭhāntare śṛtaṃ toyaṃ kvāthyādaṣṭaguṇaṃ tviha || 39
śālasārāditoyena bhāvitaṃ ca śilājatu
pibettenaiva saṃśuddhakāyaḥ piṣṭaṃ yathābalam || 40
jāṅgalānāṃ rasaiḥ sārdhaṃ tasmiñjīrṇe ca bhojanam
kuryādevaṃ tulāṃ yāvadupayuñjīta mānavaḥ || 41
madhumehaṃ vihāyāsau śarkarāmaśmarīṃ tathā
capurvarṇabalopetaḥ śataṃ jīvatyanāmayaḥ || 42
No verses of the Siddhayoga on this page.
The commentary Kusumāvali was not digitized.

mākṣikaṃ dhātumapyevaṃ yuñjyādasyāpyayaṃ guṇaḥ || 43
śarāvikādyāḥ piṭakāḥ sādhayecchophavadbhiṣak
pakvāścikitsedvraṇavattāsāṃ pāneṣu śasyate
kvātho vanaspatervāstaṃ mūtraṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca śodhanam || 44
elādikena kurvīta tailaṃ ca vraṇaropaṇam || 45
āragvadhādinā kuryātkvāthamudvartanāni ca
śālasārādinā sekaṃ bhojyādi ca kaṇādinā || 46
sauvīrakasurāsuktaṃ tailaṃ kṣīraṃ guḍaṃ ghṛtam
amlekṣurasapiṣṭānnānūpamāṃsāni varjayet || 47
pramehiṇo yadā mūtramanāvilamapicchilam
viśadaṃ tiktakaṭukaṃ tadā''rogyaṃ pracakṣate || 48
Adhikāra 36
purāṇāḥ śālayo mudgāḥ kulatthoddālakodravāḥ
lekhanā bastayaścaiva sevyā medasvinā sadā || 1
cintā krodhastathā dhūmo vyāyāmo raktamokṣaṇam
upavāso'sukhā śayyā sattvaudāryaṃ tamojayaḥ
nidrāprasaṅgamahitaṃ vārayanti samutthitam || 2
vyāyāmanityo jīrṇāśī yavagodhūmabhojanaḥ
saṃtarpaṇakṛtairdoṣairlaulyaṃ muktvā vimucyate || 3
śramacintāvyavāyādhvakṣaudrajāgaraṇapriyaḥ
hantyavaśyamatisthaulyaṃ yavaśyāmākabhojanam || 4
prātarmadhuyutaṃ vāri sevitaṃ sthaulyanāśanam || 5
viḍaṅganāgarakṣārakālaloharajo madhu
yavāmalakacūrṇaṃ tu yogo'tisthaulyanāśanaḥ
uṣṇamannasya maṇḍaṃ vā pibetkṛśatanurbhavet || 6
sacavyajīrakavyoṣahiṅgusauvarcalānalāḥ
madhunā saktavaḥ pītā medoghnā vahnidīpanāḥ || 7
vyoṣaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ śigrūṇi triphalāṃ kaṭurohiṇīm
bṛhatyau dve haridre ca pāṭhāmativiṣāṃ sthirām || 8
hiṅgukembukamūlāni yavānīdhānyacitrakam
sauvarcalamajājīṃ ca hapuṣāṃ ceti cūrṇayet || 9
cūrṇatailaghṛtakṣaudrabhāgāḥ syurmānataḥ samāḥ
saktūnāṃ ṣoḍaśaguṇo bhāgaḥ saṃtarpaṇaṃ pibet || 10
prayogādasya śāmyanti rogāḥ saṃtarpaṇotthitāḥ
pramehā mūḍhavātāśca kuṣṭhānyarśāṃsi kāmalāḥ || 11
plīhapāṇḍvāmayaḥ śothā mūtrakṛcchramarocakaḥ
hṛdrogo rājayakṣmā ca kāsaḥ śvāso galagrahaḥ || 12
kṛmayo grahaṇīdoṣāḥ śvaityaṃ sthaulyamatīva ca
narāṇāṃ dīpyate vahniḥ smṛtirbuddhiśca vardhate || 13
triphalātiviṣāmūrvātrivṛccitrakavāsakaiḥ
nimbāragvadhaṣaḍgranthāsaptaparṇaniśādvayaiḥ || 14
guḍūcīndrasurākṛṣṇākuṣṭhasarṣapanāgaraiḥ
tailamebhiḥ samaiḥ pakvaṃ surasādirasāplutam || 15
pānābhyañjanagaṇḍūṣanasyabastiṣu yojitam
sthūlatālasyakaṇḍvādīñjayetkaphakṛtāngadān || 16
śirīṣalāmajjakahemarodhraistvagdoṣasaṃsvedaharaḥ pragharṣaḥ
patrāmbulohābhayacandanāni śarīradaurgandhyaharaḥ pradehaḥ || 17
vāsādalaraso lepācchaṅkhacūrṇena saṃyutaḥ
bilvapatraraso vā'pi gātradaurgandhyanāśanaḥ || 18
harītakī lodhramariṣṭapatraṃ cūtatvaco dāḍimavalkalaṃ ca
eṣo'ṅgarāgaḥ kathito'ṅganānāṃ jaṅghākaṣāyaśca narādhipānām || 19
gomūtrapiṣṭaṃ vinihanti kuṣṭhaṃ varṇojjvalaṃ gopayasā ca yuktam
kakṣādidaurgandhyaharaṃ payobhiḥ śastaṃ vaśīkṛdrajanīdvayena || 20
vyoṣāgnimustatriphalāviḍaṅgairgugguluṃ samam
khādansarvāñjayedvyādhīnmedaḥśleṣmāmavātajān || 21
Adhikāra 37
raktaśāliryavā mudgā jāṅgalāśca rasā hitāḥ
virekāsthāpanaṃ śastaṃ sarveṣu jaṭhareṣu ca || 1
vātodaraṃ balavataḥ snehasvedairupācaret
snigdhāya sveditāṅgāya dadyātsnehavirecanam || 2
suvirakto naro yastu punarādhmāti taṃ bhiṣak
susnigdhairamlalavaṇairnirūhaiḥ samupācaret || 3
sāmudrasauvarcalasaindhavāni kṣāraṃ yavānāmajamodakaṃ ca
sapippalīcitrakaśṛṅgaveraṃ hiṅgurviḍaṃ ceti samāni dadyāt || 4
etāni cūrṇāni ghṛtaplutāni bhuñjīta pūrvaṃ kavalaṃ praśastam
vātodaraṃ gulmamajīrṇabhuktaṃ vātaprakopaṃ grahaṇīṃ ca duṣṭām
arśāṃsi kuṣṭhāni ca pāṇḍurogaṃ bhagaṃdarāṃśceti nihanti sadyaḥ || 5
vātodarī pibettakraṃ pippalīlavaṇānvitam
śarkarāmaricopetaṃ svādu pittodarī pibet || 6
yavānīsaindhavājājīvyoṣayuktaṃ kaphodarī
saṃnipātodarī takraṃ trikaṭukṣārasaindhavaiḥ || 7
snuhīpayobhāvitānāṃ pippalīnāṃ payośanaḥ
sahasramupayuñjīta śaktito jaṭharāmayī || 8
śilājatūnāṃ mūtrāṇāṃ guggulostraiphalasya ca
snuhīkṣīraprayogaśca śamayatyudarāmayam || 9
snukpayasā paribhāvitataṇḍulacūrṇairvinirmitaḥ pūpaḥ
udaramudāraṃ hiṃsyādyogo'yaṃ saptarātreṇa || 10
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā kalpadṛṣṭaṃ prayojayet
jaṭharāṇāṃ vināśāya nāsti tena samaṃ bhuvi || 11
daśamūladārunāgaracchinnaruhāpunarnavābhayākvāthaḥ
jayati jalodaraśothaślīpadagalagaṇḍavātarogāṃśca || 12
paṭolamūlaṃ rajanīṃ viḍaṅgaṃ triphalātvacam || 13
kampillakaṃ nīlinīṃ ca trivṛtāṃ ceti cūrṇayet
ṣaḍādyānkārṣikānantyāṃstrīṃśca dvitricaturguṇān || 14
kṛtvā cūrṇaṃ tu tāṃ muṣṭiṃ gavāṃ mūtreṇa nā pibet
virikto jāṅgalarasairbhuñjīta mṛdumodanam || 15
maṇḍaṃ peyāṃ ca pītvā vā savyoṣaṃ ṣaḍahaṃ payaḥ
śṛtaṃ pibettataścūrṇaṃ pibedevaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 16
hanti sarvodarāṇyetaccūrṇaṃ jātodakāni ca
kāmalāṃ pāṇḍurogaṃ ca śvayathuṃ cāpakarṣati || 17
yavānī hapuṣā dhānyaṃ triphalā sopakuñcikā
kāravī pippalīmūlamajagandhā śaṭhī vacā || 18
śatāhvā jīrakaṃ vyoṣaṃ svarṇakṣīrī sacitrakā
dvau kṣārau pauṣkaraṃ mūlaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ lavaṇapañcakam || 19
viḍaṅgaṃ ca samāṃśāni dantyā bhāgatrayaṃ tathā
trivṛdviśāle dviguṇe sātalā syāccaturguṇā || 20
eṣa nārāyaṇo nāma cūrṇo rogagaṇāpahaḥ
nainaṃ prāpyātivartante rogā viṣṇumivāsurāḥ || 21
takreṇodaribhiḥ peyo gulmibhirbadarāmbunā
ānāhavāte surayā vātaroge prasannayā || 22
dadhimaṇḍena viddha(ddhā)ṅge dāḍimāmbubhirarśasi
parikarte savṛkṣāmlairuṣṇāmbubhirajīrṇake || 23
bhagaṃdare pāṇḍuroge kāse śvāse galagrahe
hṛdroge grahaṇīdoṣe kuṣṭhe mandānale jvare || 24
daṃṣṭrāviṣe mūlaviṣe sagare kṛtrime viṣe
yathārhasnigdhakoṣṭhena peyametadvirecanam || 25
pippalīpippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaraiḥ
sakṣārairardhapalikairdviḥ prasthaṃ sarpiṣaḥ pacet || 26
kalkairdvipañcamūlasya tulārdhasya rasena tu
dadhimaṇḍāḍhakaṃ dattvā tatsarpirjaṭharāpaham
śvayathuṃ vātaviṣṭambhaṃ gulmārśāṃsi ca nāśayet || 27
caturguṇe jale mūtre dviguṇe citrakātpale
kalke siddhaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ sakṣāraṃ jaṭharī pibet || 28
nāgaraṃ tripalaṃ prasthaṃ ghṛtatailaṃ tathā''ḍhakam || 29
mastunaḥ sādhayitvā ca pibetsarvodarāpaham
kaphamārutasaṃbhūte gulme caitatpraśasyate || 30
arkakṣīrapale dve tu snuhīkṣīrapalāni ṣaṭ
pathyā kampillakaṃ śyāmā śamyākaṃ girikarṇikā || 31
nīlinī trivṛtā dantī śaṅkhinī citrakaṃ tathā
eteṣāṃ palikairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 32
athāsya maline koṣṭhe bindumātrāṃ pradāpayet
yāvato'sya pibedbindūṃstāvadvegādviricyate || 33
kuṣṭhaṃ gulmamudāvartaṃ śvayathuṃ sabhagaṃdaram
śamayatyudarāṇyaṣṭau vṛkṣamindrāśaniryathā || 34
etadbindughṛtaṃ nāmnā yenābhyakto viricyate
mūtrāṇyaṣṭāvudariṇāṃ pāne seke prayojayet || 35
devadrumaṃ śigrumayūrakaṃ ca gomūtrapiṣṭāmathavā'śvagandhām
pītvā''śu hanyādudaraṃ pravṛddhaṃ kṛmīnaśeṣānudaraṃ ca dūṣyam || 36
snukkṣīradantītriphalāviḍaṅgasiṃhītrivṛccitrakakarṣakalkaiḥ
ghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ kuḍavapramāṇaṃ toyena tasyākṣamapārdhakarṣam || 37
pītvoṣṇamambho'nupibedvirikte peyāṃ sukhoṣṇāṃ vitaredvidhijñaḥ
nārācametajjaṭharāmayānāṃ yuktyopayuktaṃ śamanaṃ pradiṣṭam || 38
yavānikācitrakayāvaśūkaṣaḍgranthidantīmagadhodbhavānām
plīhānametadvinihanti cūrṇamuṣṇāmbunā mastusurāsavairvā || 39
palāśakṣāratoyena pippalī bhāvitā śubhā
gulmaplīhārtiśamanī agnidīptikarī smṛtā || 40
viḍaṅgārkāgnisindhūtthasaktūndagdhvā vacānvitān
pibetkṣīreṇa saṃcūrṇya gulmaplīhodarāpahān || 41
kṣāraṃ vā biḍakṛṣṇābhyāṃ pūtikasyāmlaniḥsrutam
plīhayakṛtpraśāntyarthaṃ pibetprātaryathābalam || 42
arkapatraṃ salavaṇamantardhūmaṃ vidāhayet
mastunā vā pibetkṣāraṃ plīharogaṃ vyapohati || 43
pātavyo yuktitaḥ kṣāraḥ kṣīreṇodadhiśuktijaḥ
payasā vā prayoktavyāḥ pippalyaḥ plīhaśāntaye || 44
bhallātakābhayājājīguḍena saha modakaḥ
saptarātrānnihantyeṣa plīhānamapi dāruṇam || 45
saubhāñjanakaniryūhaṃ saindhavāgnikaṇānvitam
palāśakṣārayuktaṃ vā yavakṣāraṃ prayojayet || 46
tilānsalavaṇāṃścaiva ghṛtaṃ ṣaṭpalakaṃ tathā
plīhoddiṣṭāṃ kriyāṃ sarvāṃ yakṛtaḥ saṃprakalpayet || 47
laśunaṃ pippalīmūlamabhayāṃ caiva bhakṣayet
pibedgomūtragaṇḍūṣaṃ plīharogavimuktaye || 48
śarapuṅkhāyāḥ kalkastakreṇa niṣevato yathāgnibalam
yadi na jayetplīhānaṃ śailo'pi tadā jale plavate || 49
śārṅge(rṅgī)ṣṭāniryūhaḥ sasaindhavastintiḍīkasaṃmiśraḥ
plīhavyuparamayogaḥ pakvāmraraso'tha vā samadhuḥ || 50
rohitakābhayākṣodabhāvitaṃ mūtramambu vā
pītaṃ sarvodaraplīhamehārśaḥkṛmigulmanut || 51
dadhnā bhuktavato vāmabāhumadhye śirāṃ bhiṣak
vidhyetplīhavināśāya yakṛnnāśāya dakṣiṇe || 52
plīhānaṃ mardayedgāḍhaṃ duṣṭaraktapravṛttaye || 53
nikumbhakuḍavakvāthaprasthe tatkalkasaṃyutam
sarpiḥ prasthaṃ pacetplīhakāmalāpāṇḍuroganut || 54
citrakasya tulākvāthe ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
āranālaṃ tu dviguṇaṃ dadhimaṇḍaṃ caturguṇam
pañcakolakatālīsakṣārairlavaṇasaṃyutaiḥ || 55
dvijīrakaṃ niśāyugmaṃ maricaṃ tatra dāpayet
plīhagulmodarādhmānapāṇḍurogārucijvarān || 56
bastihṛtpārśvakaṭyūruśūlodāvartapīnasān
hanyātpītaṃ tadarśoghnaṃ śophaghnaṃ vahnidīpanam
balavarṇakaraṃ cāpi bhasmakaṃ ca niyacchati || 57
pippalīkalkasaṃyuktaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīracaturguṇam
pacetplīhāgnisādādiyakṛdrogaharaṃ param || 58
rohitakatvacaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ palānāṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ
koladviprasthasaṃyuktaṃ kaṣāyamupakalpayet || 59
palikaiḥ pañcakolaiśca taiḥ sarvaiścāpi tulyayā
rohitakatvacā piṣṭairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 60
plīhātivṛddhiṃ śamayedetadāśu prayojitam
tathā gulmajvaraśvāsakṛmipāṇḍutvakāmalāḥ || 61
rohitakātpalaśataṃ kṣodayedbadarāḍhakam || 62
sādhayitvā jaladroṇe caturbhāgāvaśeṣitam
ghṛtaprasthaṃ samāvāpya cchāgakṣīraṃ caturguṇam || 63
tasmindadyādimānkalkānsarvāṃstānakṣasaṃmitān
vyoṣaṃ phalatrikaṃ hiṅgu yavānīṃ tumbaruṃ biḍam || 64
ajājīṃ kṛṣṇalavaṇaṃ dāḍimaṃ devadāru ca
punarnavāṃ viśālāṃ ca yavakṣāraṃ sapauṣkaram || 65
viḍaṅgaṃ citrakaṃ caiva hapuṣāṃ cabikāṃ vacām
etairghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ tatsthāpayedbhājane śubhe || 66
pāyayettripalāṃ mātrāṃ vyādherbalamapekṣya ca
rasakenātha yūṣeṇa payasā vā'pi bhojayet || 67
upayukte ghṛte tasminvyādhīnhanyādimānbahūn
yakṛtplīhodaraṃ caiva plīhaśūlaṃ yakṛttathā || 68
kukṣiśūlaṃ hṛdi śūlaṃ pārśvaśūlamarocakam
vibandhaśūlaṃ śamayetpāṇḍurogaṃ sakāmalam || 69
chardyatīsāraśamanaṃ tandrājvaravināśanam
mahārohitakaṃ nāma plīhaghnaṃ śreṣṭhamauṣadham || 70
Adhikāra 38
harītakīnāgaradevadārupunarnavāchinnaruhākaṣāyaḥ
saguggulurmūtrayutaśca peyaḥ śothodarāṇāṃ pravaraḥ prayogaḥ || 1
eraṇḍatailaṃ daśamūlamiśraṃ gomūtrayuktastriphalāraso vā
nihanti vātodaraśothaśūlaṃ kvāthaḥ samūtro daśamūlajaśca || 2
punarnavānimbapaṭolaśuṇṭhītiktāmṛtādārvyabhayākaṣāyaḥ
sarvāṅgaśophodarakāsaśūlaśvāsānvitaṃ pāṇḍugadaṃ nihanti || 3
punarnavāṃ dārvabhayāṃ guḍūcīṃ pibetsamūtrāṃ mahiṣākṣayuktām
tvagdoṣaśophodarapāṇḍurogasthaulyaprasekordhvakaphāmayeṣu || 4
gomūtrayuktaṃ mahiṣīpayo vā kṣīraṃ gavāṃ vā triphalāvimiśram
kṣīrānnabhukkevalameva gavyamūtraṃ pibedvā śvayathūdareṣu || 5
purāṇaṃ māṇakaṃ piṣṭvā dviguṇīkṛtataṇḍulam
sādhitaṃ kṣīratoyābhyāmabhyasetpāyasaṃ tu tat || 6
hanti vātodaraṃ śothaṃ grahaṇīṃ pāṇḍutāmapi
siddho bhiṣagbhirākhyātaḥ prayogo'yaṃ niratyayaḥ || 7
punarnavādārvyamṛtā pāṭhā bilvaṃ śvadaṃṣṭrikā
bṛhatyau dve rajanyau dve pippalyaścitrakaṃ vṛṣam || 8
samabhāgāni saṃcūrṇya gavāṃ mūtreṇa nā pibet
bahuprakāraṃ śothaṃ ca sarvagātravisāriṇam
hanti śothodarāṇyaṣṭau vraṇāṃścaivoddhatānapi || 9
Adhikāra 39
śuṇṭhīpunarnavairaṇḍapañcamūlaśṛtaṃ jalam
vātike śvayathau śastaṃ pānāhāraparigrahe || 1
kṣīrāśinaḥ pittakṛte tu śothe trivṛdguḍūcītriphalākaṣāyam
pibedgavāṃ mūtravimiśritaṃ vā phalatrikāccūrṇamathākṣamātram || 2
punarnavāviśvatrivṛdguḍūcīśamyākapathyāmaradārukalkam
śothe kaphotthe mahiṣākṣamūtrayuktaṃ pibedvā salilaṃ tathaiṣām || 3
kaphe tu kṛṣṇāsikatāpurāṇapiṇyākaśigrutvagumāpralepaḥ
kulatthaśuṇṭhījalamūtrasekaścaṇḍāgurubhyāmanulepanaṃ ca || 4
ajājipāṭhāghanapañcakolavyāghrīrajanyaḥ sukhatoyapītāḥ
śothaṃ pravṛddhaṃ cirajaṃ tridoṣaṃ nighnanti bhūnimbamahauṣadhe ca || 5
pippalyādirajonāmnā carake paṭhyate tvayam
yogo'tra cavikāsthāne grāhyā mātaṅgapīppalī || 6
ārdrakasya rasaḥ pītaḥ purāṇaguḍamiśritaḥ
ajākṣīrāśinaḥ śīghraṃ sarvaśothaharo bhavet || 7
punarnavādāruśuṇṭhīkvāthe mūtre'tha kevale
daśamūlarase vā'pi gugguluḥ śothanāśanaḥ || 8
bilvapatrarasaṃ pāṇḍu soṣaṇaṃ śvayathau trije
viṭsaṅge caiva durnāmni vidadhyātkāmalāsu ca || 9
guḍapippaliśuṇṭhīnāṃ cūrṇaṃ śvayathunāśanam
āmājīrṇapraśamanaṃ śūlaghnaṃ bastiśodhanam || 10
puraṃ mūtreṇa saṃsevyaṃ pippalī vā payonvitā
guḍena vā'bhayā tulyā viśvaṃ vā śotharogiṇām || 11
guḍārdrakaṃ vā guḍanāgaraṃ vā guḍābhayāṃ vā guḍapippalīṃ vā
karṣābhivṛddhyā tripalapramāṇaṃ khādennaraḥ pakṣamathāpi māsam || 12
śothapratiśyāyagalāsyarogā-
nsaśvāsakāsārucipīnasādīn
jīrṇajvarārśograhaṇīvikārā-
nhanyāttathā'nyānkaphavātarogān || 13
sthalapadmamayaṃ kalkaṃ payasā''loḍya pāyayet
plīhāmayaharaṃ caiva sarvāṅgaikāṅgaśothajit || 14
kṣīraṃ śothaharaṃ dāruvarṣābhūnāgaraiḥ śṛtam
peyaṃ vā citrakavyoṣavṛddhadāruprasādhitam || 15
siṃhāsyāmṛtabhaṇṭākīkvāthaṃ pītvā samākṣikam
kṛcchraṃ śothaṃ jayejjantuḥ śvāsaṃ kāsaṃ jvaraṃ vamim || 16
bhūnimbaviśvakalkaṃ jagdhvā peyaḥ punanarvākvāthaḥ
apaharati niyatamāśu śvayathuṃ sarvāṅgagaṃ nṝṇām || 17
triphalāyorajaḥkṣāraiḥ śothajittriphalārasaḥ
yaṣṭīdugdhatilairlepo navanītena saṃyutaḥ
śothamāruṣkaraṃ hanti vṛntaiḥ śāladalasya vā || 18
śothe viṣanimitte tu viṣoktā śasyate kriyā || 19
punarnavācitrakadevadārupañcoṣaṇakṣāraharītakīnām
kalkena pakvaṃ daśamūlatoye ghṛtottamaṃ śothaniṣūdanaṃ ca || 20
rase vipācayetsarpiḥ pañcakolakulatthayoḥ
punarnavāyāḥ kalkena ghṛtaṃ śothavināśanam || 21
sacitrakā dhānyayavānipāṭhāḥ
sadīpyakāstryūṣaṇavetasāmlāḥ
bilvātphalaṃ dāḍimayāvaśūkaṃ
sapippalīmūlamathāpi cavyam || 22
piṣṭvā'kṣamātrāṇi jalāḍhakena
paktvā ghṛtaprasthamathopayojyam
arśāṃsi gulmaṃ śvayathuṃ ca kṛtsnaṃ
nihanti vahniṃ ca karoti dīptam || 23
kṣīraṃ ghaṭe citrakakalkalipte dadhyāgataṃ sādhu vimathya tena
tajjaṃ ghṛtaṃ citrakamūlagarbhaṃ takreṇa siddhaṃ śvayathughnamagtyam
arśotisārānilagulmamehāṃstaddhanti saṃvardhayate ca vahnim || 24
māṇakakvāthakalkābhyāṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
ekajaṃ dvaṃdvajaṃ śothaṃ tridoṣaṃ ca vyapohati || 25
śaileyakuṣṭhāgurudārukauntītvakpadmakailāmbupalāśamustaiḥ
priyaṃguthauṇeyakahemamāṃsītālīsapatraplavadhānyapatraiḥ || 26
śrīveṣṭakadhyāmakapippalībhiḥ spṛkkānakhaiścāpi yathopalābham
vātātmake'bhyaṅgamuśanti tailaṃ siddhaṃ supiṣṭairapi ca pradeham || 27
śuṣkamūlakavarṣābhūdārurāsnāmahauṣadhaiḥ
pakvamabhyañjanāttailaṃ samūlaṃ śothanāśanam || 28
dvipañcamūlasya pacetkaṣāye kaṃse'bhayānāṃ ca śataṃ guḍācca
lehe susiddhe ca vinīya cūrṇaṃ vyoṣaṃ trisaugandhyamupasthite ca || 29
prasthādharmātraṃ madhunaḥ suśīte kiṃcicca cūrṇādapi yāvaśūkāt
ekābhayāṃ prāśya tataśca lehācchuktiṃ nihanti śvayathuṃ pravṛddham || 30
śvāsajvarārocakamehagulmānplīhatridoṣodarapāṇḍurogān
kārśyāmavātāvasṛgamlapittavaivarṇyamūtrānilaśukradoṣān || 31
sāṃnidhyānmadhuno mānaṃ vyoṣādermilitasya ca
kiṃcicca karṣaparyāyaḥ śuktirardhapalaṃ matā || 32
daśamūlīharītakyāstu(tu)lyā kaṃsaharītakī
mānaṃ tenātra tatratyaṃ carake prāha jaijjaṭaḥ || 33
daśamūlakaṣāyasya kaṃse pathyāśataṃ pacet
tulāṃ guḍāddhanavyoṣayavakṣāracatuṣpalam || 34
trijātakaṃ suvarṇāṃśaṃ prasthārdhaṃ madhuno hime || 35
daśamūlaharītakyaḥ śothānhanyuḥ sudurjayān
jvarārocakamehārśaḥkuṣṭhapāṇḍūdarāmayān || 36
punarnavāmṛtādārudaśamūlajalāḍhake
ārdrakasvarasaprasthe guḍasya tu tulāṃ pacet || 37
tatsiddhaṃ vyoṣapatrailātvakcavyaiḥ kārṣikaiḥ pṛthak
cūrṇīkṛtaiḥ kṣipecchīte madhunaḥ kuḍavaṃ lihet || 38
lehaḥ paunarnavo nāma śothaśūlaniṣūdanaḥ
kāsaśvāsāruciharo balavarṇāgnivardhanaḥ || 39
piṣṭānnamamlaṃ lavaṇāni madyaṃ mṛdaṃ divā svapnamajāṅgalaṃ ca
striyo ghṛtaṃ tailapayogurūṇi śophaṃ jighāṃsuḥ parivarjayecca || 40
grāmyājānūpapiśitalavaṇaṃ śuṣkaśākaṃ navānnaṃ
gauḍaṃ piṣṭānnaṃ dadhi ca kṛśaraṃ vijjalaṃ madyamamlam
dhānāvallūraṃ samaśanamatho gurvasātmyaṃ vidāhi
svapnaṃ cārātrau śvayathugadavānvarjayenmaithunaṃ ca || 41
Adhikāra 44
gugguluṃ rubutailaṃ vā gomūtreṇa pibennaraḥ
vātavṛddhiṃ nihantyāśu cirakālānubandhinīm || 1
sakṣīraṃ vā pibettailaṃ māsameraṇḍasaṃbhavam
tailaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ yojyaṃ pānābhyañjanabastiṣu || 2
candanaṃ madhukaṃ padmamuśīraṃ nīlamutpalam
kṣīrapiṣṭaiḥ pradehaḥ syāddāhaśotharujāpahaḥ || 3
pañcavalkalakalkena saghṛtena pralepanam
sarvaṃ pittaharaṃ kāryaṃ raktaje raktamokṣaṇam || 4
śleṣmavṛddhiṃ tūṣṇavīryairmūtrapiṣṭaiḥ pralepayet
pītadārukaṣāyaṃ ca pibenmūtreṇa saṃyutam || 5
svinnaṃ medaḥsamutthaṃ tu lepayetsurasādinā
śirovirekadravyairvā sukhoṣṇairmūtrasaṃyutaiḥ || 6
saṃsvedya mūtraprabhavāṃ vastrapaṭṭena veṣṭayet
sīvanyāḥ pārśvato'dhastādvidhyedvrīhimukhena vai || 7
muṣkakośamagacchantyāmantravṛddhau vicakṣaṇaḥ
vātavṛddhikramaṃ kuryāddāhaṃ tatrāgninā hitam || 8
śaṅkhopari ca karṇānte tyaktvā sīvanimādarāt
vyatyāsādvā śirāṃ vidhyedantravṛddhinivṛttaye || 9
aṅguṣṭhamadhye tvakchittvā dahedaṅgaviparyaye || 10
rāsnāyaṣṭyamṛtairaṇḍabalāgokṣurasādhitaḥ
kvātho'ntravṛddhiṃ hantyāśu rubutailena miśritaḥ || 11
tailameraṇḍajaṃ pītvā balāsiddhaṃ payonvitam
ādhmānaśūlopacitāmantravṛddhiṃ jayennaraḥ || 12
harītakīṃ mūtrasiddhāṃ satailāṃ lavaṇānvitām
prātaḥ prātaśca seveta kaphavātāmayāpahām || 13
gomūtrasiddhāṃ rubutailabhṛṣṭāṃ harītakīṃ saindhavacūrṇayuktām
khādennaraḥ koṣṇajalānupānānnihanti vṛddhiṃ cirajāṃ pravṛddhām || 14
triphalākvāthagomūtraṃ pibetprātaratandritaḥ
kaphavātotthitaṃ hanti śvayathuṃ vṛṣaṇodbhavam || 15
saralāgurukuṣṭhāni devadāru mahauṣadham
mūtrāranālasaṃpiṣṭaṃ śophaghnaṃ kaphavātajit || 16
bhṛṣṭo rubūkatailena kalkaḥ pathyāsamudbhavaḥ
kṛṣṇāsaindhavasaṃyukto vṛddhirogaharaḥ paraḥ || 17
gavyaṃ ghṛtaṃ saindhavasaṃprayuktaṃ śambūkabhāṇḍe nihitaṃ prayatnāt
saptāhamādityakarairvipakvaṃ nihanti kauraṇḍamatipravṛddham || 18
vacāsarṣapakalkena pralepaḥ śothanāśanaḥ || 19
atyabhiṣyandigurvāmasevanānnicayaṃ gataḥ
karoti granthivacchophaṃ doṣo vaṅkṣaṇasaṃdhiṣu
jvaraśūlāṅgasādāḍhyaṃ taṃ vardhmamiti nirdiśet || 20
mūlaṃ bilvakapitthayoraralukasyāgnerbṛhatyordvayoḥ
śyāmāpūtikarañjaśigrukatarorviśvauṣadhāruṣkaram
kṛṣṇāgranthikacavyapañcalavaṇakṣārājamodānvitaṃ
pītaṃ kāñjikakoṣṇatoyamathitaiścūrṇīkṛtaṃ vardhmajit || 21
avikṣīreṇa godhūmakalkaṃ kundurukasya ca
pralepanaṃ sukhoṣṇaṃ syādvardhmaśūlaharaṃ param || 22
mṛtamātre tu vai kāke viśaste tu praveśayet
vardhmaṃ muhūrtaṃ medhāvī tatkṣaṇādarujaṃ bhavet || 23
saindhavaṃ madanaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ śatāhvā niculaṃ vacā
hrīveraṃ madhukaṃ bhārgī devadāru sanāgaram || 24
kaṭphalaṃ pauṣkaraṃ medā cavikā citrakaṃ śaṭhī
viḍaṅgātiviṣe śyāmā reṇukā nīlinī sthirā || 25
bilvājamode kṛṣṇā ca dantī rāsnā ca taiḥ samaiḥ
sādhyameraṇḍajaṃ tailaṃ tailaṃ vā kaphavātanut || 26
vardhmodāvartagulmārśaḥplīhamehāmamārutān
ānāhamaśmarīṃ caiva hanyāttadanuvāsanam
ghṛtaṃ saureśvaraṃ yojyaṃ vardhmavṛddhinivṛttaye || 27
Adhikāra 41
yavamudgapaṭolādi kaṭurūkṣaṃ ca bhojanam
chardi saraktamuktiṃ ca galagaṇḍe prayojayet || 1
niculaṃ śigrubījāni daśamūlamathāpi vā
ālepanaṃ vātagaṇḍe sukhoṣṇaṃ saṃpraśasyate || 2
devadāruviśāle ca kaphagaṇḍe pralepanam
taṇḍulodakapiṣṭena mūlena parilepitaḥ
hastikarṇapalāśasya galagaṇḍaḥ praśāmyati || 3
sarṣapāñśigrubījāni śaṇabījātasīyavān
mūlakasya ca bījāni takreṇāmlena peṣayet || 4
gaṇḍāni granthayaścaiva gaṇḍamālāḥ samutthitāḥ
pralepāttena śāmyanti vilayaṃ yānti vā'cirāt || 5
jīrṇakarkārukarasaṃ biḍasaindhavasaṃyutam
nasyena hanti taruṇaṃ galagaṇḍaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 6
jalakumbhīkajaṃ bhasma pakvaṃ gomūtragālitam
pibetkodravatakrāśī galagaṇḍopaśāntaye || 7
mahiṣīmūtravimiśraṃ lohamalaṃ saṃsthitaṃ ghaṭe māsam
antardhūmavidagdhaṃ lihyānmadhunā'tha galagaṇḍe || 8
sūryāvartarasonābhyāṃ galagaṇḍopanāhanam
sphoṭāsrāvaiḥ śamaṃ yāti galagaṇḍo na saṃśayaḥ || 9
tailaṃ pibeccāmṛtavallinimbahaṃsāhvayāvṛkṣakapippalībhiḥ
siddhaṃ balābhyāṃ ca sadevadāru hitāya nityaṃ galagaṇḍaroge || 10
jihvāyāḥ pārśvato'dhastācchirā dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ || 11
tāsāṃ sthūlaśire kṛṣṇe chindyātte ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ
baḍiśenaiva saṃgṛhya kuśapatreṇa buddhimān || 12
srute rakte vraṇe tasmindadyātsaguḍamārdrakam
bhojanaṃ cānabhiṣyandi yūṣaḥ kaulattha iṣyate || 13
karṇayugmabahiḥsaṃdhimadhyābhyāse sthitaṃ ca yat
uparyupari tacchindyādgalagaṇḍe śirātrayam || 14
viḍaṅgakṣārasindhūgrārāsnāgnivyoṣadārubhiḥ
kaṭutumbīphalarase kaṭutailaṃ vipācitam
cirotthamapi nasyena galagaṇḍaṃ vināśayet || 15
viḍaṅgānalasindhūttharāsnogrākṣāradārubhiḥ || 16
tailaṃ caturguṇe siddhaṃ kaṭutumbīrase'tha tat
gaṇḍamālāharaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ galagaṇḍaharaṃ param || 17
mākṣikāḍhyaḥ sakṛtpītaḥ kvātho varuṇamūlajaḥ
gaṇḍamālāṃ nihantyāśu cirakālānubandhinīm || 18
piṣṭvā jyeṣṭhāmbunā peyāḥ kāñcanāratvacaḥ śubhāḥ
viśvabheṣajasaṃyuktā gaṇḍamālāpahāḥ parāḥ || 19
alambuṣādalodbhūtātsvarasāddve pale pibet
apacyā gaṇḍamālāyāḥ kāmalāyāśca nāśane || 20
vanakārpāsikāmūlaṃ taṇḍulaiḥ saha yojitam
paktvā pupūlikāṃ khādedapacīnāśanāya ca || 21
saubhāñjanaṃ devadāru kāñjikena tu peṣitam
koṣṇaṃ pralepanaṃ hanyādapacīmatidustarām || 22
āragvadhaśiphāṃ kṣipraṃ samyaktaṇḍulavāriṇā
piṣṭvā nasyapralepābhyāṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ samuddharet || 23
gaṇḍamālāmayārtānāṃ nasyakarmaṇi yojayet
nirguṇḍyāstu śiphāṃ samyagvāriṇā paripeṣitām || 24
kośātakīnāṃ svarasena nasyaṃ tumbyāstathā pippalisaṃyutena
tailena vā'riṣṭabhavena kuryādvacopakulye saha mākṣikeṇa || 25
granthiṣvāmeṣu kurvīta bhiṣakśothapratikriyām
pakvānāpīḍya saṃśodhya ropayedvraṇabheṣajaiḥ || 26
hiṃsrā sarohiṇyamṛtā'tha bhārgī śyonākabilvāgurukṛṣṇagandhāḥ
gopittapiṣṭāḥ saha tālapatryā granthau vidheyo'nilaje pralepaḥ || 27
jalaukasaḥ pittakṛte hitāstu kṣīrodakābhyāṃ pariṣecanaṃ ca
kākolivargasya ca śītalāni pibetkaṣāyāṇi saśarkarāṇi || 28
drākṣārasenekṣurasena vā'pi cūrṇaṃ pibedvā'pi harītakīnām
madhūkajambvarjunavetasānāṃ tvagbhiḥ pralepānavacārayedvā || 29
hṛteṣu doṣeṣu yathānupūrvyā granthau bhiṣakśleṣmasamutthite tu
svinnasya vimlāpanameva kuryādaṅguṣṭhaveṇūdṛṣadīsutaistu || 30
vikaṅkatāragvadhakākaṇantīkākādanītāpasavṛkṣamūlaiḥ
ālepayedenamalābubhārgīkarañjakālāmadanaiśca vidvān || 31
dantī citrakamūlatvaksaudhārkapayasī guḍaḥ
bhallātakāsthi kāsīsaṃ lepo bhindyācchilāmapi || 32
granthīnamarmaprabhavānapakvānuddhṛtya cāgniṃ vidadhīta vaidyaḥ
kṣāreṇa caitānpratisārayettu saṃlikhya saṃlikhya yathopadeśam || 33
granthyarbudānāṃ ca yato'viśeṣaḥ pradeśahetvākṛtidoṣadūṣyaiḥ
tataścikitsedbhiṣagarbudāni vidhānavidgranthicikitsitena || 34
vātārbude cāpyupanāhanāni snigdhaiśca māṃsairatha vesavāraiḥ
svedaṃ vidadhyātkuśalastu nāḍyā śṛṅgeṇa raktaṃ bahuśo harecca || 35
svedopanāhā mṛdavastu pathyāḥ pittārbude kāyavirecanaṃ ca
vighṛṣya codumbaraśākagojīpatrairbhṛśaṃ kṣaudrayutaiḥ pralepaḥ
ślakṣṇīkṛtaiḥ sarjarasapriyaṃgupatrāṅgalodhrārjunayaṣṭisāhvaiḥ || 36
lepanaṃ śaṅkhacūrṇena saha mūlakabhasmanā
kaphārbudāpahaṃ kuryādgranthyādiṣu viśeṣataḥ || 37
niṣpāvapiṇyākakulatthakalkairmāṃsapragāḍhairdadhimarditaiśca || 38
lepaṃ vidadhyātkrimayo yathā'tra muñcantyapatyānyatha makṣikā vā
alpāvaśiṣṭaṃ krimibhiḥ prajagdhaṃ likhettato'gniṃ vidadhīta paścāt || 39
yadalpamūlaṃ traputāmrasīsaiḥ saṃveṣṭya patrairatha vā''yasairvā
kṣārāgniśastrāṇyavacārayecca muhurmuhuḥ prāṇamavekṣamāṇaḥ || 40
yadṛcchayā copagatāni pākaṃ pākakrameṇopacaredyathoktam
saśeṣadoṣāṇi hi yo'rbudāni karoti tasyā''śu punarbhavanti
tasmādaśeṣāṇi samuddharettu hanyuḥ saśeṣāṇi yathā viṣāgnī || 41
upodakārasābhyaktāstatpatrapariveṣṭitāḥ
praṇaśyantyacirānnṝṇāṃ piṭakārbudajātayaḥ || 42
upodakā kāñjikatakrapiṣṭā tayopanāho lavaṇena miśraḥ
dṛṣṭo'rbudānāṃ praśamāya kaiściddine dine rātriṣu marmajānām || 43
snuhīgaṇḍīrikāsvedo nāśayedarbudāni ca
lavaṇenātha vā svedaḥ sīsakena tathaiva ca || 44
haridrālodhrapatrāṅgagṛhadhūmamanaḥśilāḥ
madhupragāḍho lepo'yaṃ medorbudaharaḥ paraḥ || 45
etāmeva kriyāṃ kuryādaśeṣāṃ śarkarārbude || 46
sarṣapāriṣṭapatrāṇi dagdhvā bhallātakaiḥ saha
chāgamūtreṇa saṃpiṣṭamapacīghnaṃ pralepanam || 47
aśvatthakāṇḍaṃ niculaṃ gavāṃ dantaṃ ca dāhayet
saptāhamalasaṃyuktaṃ bhasma hantyapacīṃ kṣaṇāt || 48
pārṣṇiṃ prati dvādaśa cāṅgulāni
mitvendrabastiṃ parivarjya samyak
vidārya matsyāṇḍanibhāni vaidyo
niṣkṛṣya jālānyanalaṃ vidadhyāt || 49

maṇibandhopariṣṭādvā kuryādreṣātrayaṃ bhiṣak
aṅgulāntaritaṃ samyagapacīnāṃ praśāntaye || 50
gaṇḍamālāpahaṃ tailaṃ siddhaṃ śākhoṭakatvacā
bimbyaśvamāranirguṇḍīsādhitaṃ vā'pi nāvanam || 51
nirguṇḍīsvarasenātha lāṅgalīmūlakalkitam
tailaṃ nasyānnihantyāśu gaṇḍamālāṃ sudustarām || 52
chucchundaryā vipakvaṃ tu kṣaṇāttailavaraṃ dhruvam
abhyaṅgānnāśayennṝṇāṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ sudāruṇām || 53
candanaṃ sābhayā lākṣā vacā kaṭukarohiṇī
etaistailaṃ śṛtaṃ pītaṃ samūlāmapacīṃ jayet || 54
vyoṣaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ madhukaṃ saindhavaṃ devadāru ca
tailamebhiḥ śṛtaṃ nasyātkṛcchrāmapyapacīṃ haret || 55
Adhikāra 42
laṅghanālepanasvedarecanai raktamokṣaṇaiḥ
prāyaḥ śleṣmaharairuṣṇaiḥ ślīpadaṃ samupācaret || 1
dhattūrairaṇḍanirguṇḍīvarṣābhūśigrusarṣapaiḥ
pralepaḥ ślīpadaṃ hanti cirotthamapi dāruṇam || 2
saṃpiṣṭamāranālena rūyikāmūlavalkalam
pralepācchlīpadaṃ hanti baddhamūlamapi sthiram || 3
piṇḍārakatarusaṃbhavavandākaśiphā jayati sarpiṣā pītā
ślīpadamugraṃ niyataṃ baddhā sūtreṇa jaṅghāyām || 4
hitaścā''lepane nityaṃ citrako devadāru ca
siddhārthaśigrukalko vā sukhoṣṇo mūtrapeṣitaḥ || 5
snehasvedopanāhāṃśca ślīpade'nilaje bhiṣak
kṛtvā gulphopari śirāṃ vidhyecca caturaṅgule || 6
gulphasyādhaḥśirāṃ vidhyecchlīpade pittasaṃbhave
pittaghnīṃ ca kriyāṃ kuryātpittārbudavisarpavat || 7
mañjiṣṭhāṃ madhukaṃ rāsnāṃ sahiṃsrāṃ sapunarnavām
piṣṭvā''ranālairlepo'yaṃ pittaślīpadaśāntaye || 8
śirāṃ suviditāṃ vidhyedaṅguṣṭhe śleṣmaślīpade
madhuyutāni cābhīkṣṇaṃ kaṣāyāṇi pibennaraḥ || 9
pibedvā'pyabhayākalkaṃ mūtreṇānyatamena tu
pibedevaṃ guḍūcīṃ vā nāgaraṃ devadāru vā || 10
pibetsarṣapatailena ślīpadānāṃ nivṛttaye
pūtīkarañjacchadajaṃ rasaṃ vā'pi yathābalam || 11
anenaiva vidhānena putrajīvakajaṃ rasam
prayuñjīta bhiṣakprājñaḥ kālasātmyavibhāgavit || 12
palāśamūlasvarasaṃ pibedvā tailena tulyaṃ sitasarṣapāṇām
mūtreṇa pathyāsuradāruviśvaṃ sagugguluślīpadibhirniṣevyam || 13
kāñjikena pibeccūrṇaṃ vṛddhadārukasaṃbhavam
rajanīṃ guḍasaṃyuktāṃ gomūtreṇa pibennaraḥ
varṣotthaṃ ślīpadaṃ hanti dadrukuṣṭhaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 14
gandharvatailabhṛṣṭāṃ harītakīṃ gojalena yaḥ pibati
ślīpadabandhanamukto bhavatyasau saptarātreṇa || 15
dhānyāmlaṃ tailasaṃyuktaṃ kaphavātavināśanam
dīpanaṃ cā''madoṣaghnametacchlīpadanāśanam || 16
trikaṭutriphalā cavyaṃ dārvīvaruṇagokṣuram || 17
alambuṣāṃ guḍūcīṃ ca samabhāgāni cūrṇayet
sarveṣāṃ cūrṇamāhṛtya vṛddhadāru ca tatsamam || 18
kāñjikena ca tatpeyamakṣamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ
jīrṇe cāparihāraḥ syādbhojanaṃ sārvakāmikam || 19
nāśayecchlīpadaṃ sthaulyamāmavātaṃ ca dāruṇam
kuṣṭhagulmāruciharaṃ vātaśleṣmarujāpaham || 20
pippalītriphalādārunāgaraṃ sapunarnavam
bhāgairdvipalikaireṣāṃ tatsamaṃ vṛddhadārukam || 21
kāñjikena pibeccūrṇaṃ karṣamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ
jīrṇe cāparihāraḥ syādbhojanaṃ sārvakāmikam || 22
ślīpadaṃ vātarogāṃśca hanyātplīhānameva ca
āgniṃ ca kurute ghoraṃ bhasmakaṃ ca niyacchati || 23
kṛṣṇācitrakadantīnāṃ karṣamardhapalaṃ palam
viṃśatiśca harītakyo guḍasya tu paladvayam
madhunā modakaṃ khādecchlīpadaṃ hanti dustaram || 24
surasāgranthikavyoṣaviḍaṅgāni vacā śaṭhī || 25
pāṭhailāhapuṣāśyāmācavyadāruvarāpuraiḥ
sapañcalavaṇakṣāraiḥ kārṣikairvipaceddhaviḥ || 26
prasthaṃ tadaṃśairdhānyāmladaśamūlāmbumastubhiḥ
akṣaṃ pibennaro māsaṃ ślīpadaṃ hanti dustaram || 27
vṛddhyarśograhaṇīrogagalagaṇḍārbudāpacīḥ
ghṛtaṃ saureśvaraṃ nāma ślīpadaṃ kṛmiroganut || 28
viḍaṅgamaricārkeṣu nāgare citrake tathā
bhadradārvelukākhye ca sarveṣu lavaṇeṣu ca
tailaṃ pakvaṃ pibedvā'pi ślīpadānāṃ nivṛttaye || 29
Adhikāra 43
jalaukāpātanaṃ śastaṃ sarvasminneva vidradhau
mṛdurvireko laghvannaṃ svedaḥ pittottaraṃ vinā || 1
vātaghnamūlakalkaistu vasātailaghṛtaplutaiḥ
sukhoṣṇo bahulo lepaḥ prayojyo vātavidradhau || 2
svedopanāhāḥ kartavyāḥ śigrumūlasamanvitāḥ
yavagodhūmamudgaiśca siddhapiṣṭaiḥ pralepayet
vilīyate kṣaṇenaivamapakvaścaiva vidradhiḥ || 3
punarnavādāruviśvadaśamūlābhayāmbhasā
guggulaṃ rubutailaṃ vā pibenmārutavidradhau || 4
paittikaṃ śarkarālājāmadhukaiḥ sārivāyutaiḥ
pradihyātkṣīrapiṣṭairvā payasyośīracandanaiḥ || 5
pibedvā triphalākvāthaṃ trivṛtkalkājyasaṃyutam || 6
pañcavalkalakalkena ghṛtamiśreṇa lepanam
iṣṭakāsikatālohairgośakṛttuṣapāṃśubhiḥ
mūtrapiṣṭaiśca satataṃ svedayecchleṣmavidradhim || 7
daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa sasnehena rasena vā
śothaṃ vraṇaṃ vā koṣṇena saśūlaṃ pariṣecayet || 8
pittavidradhivatsarvāṃ kriyāṃ niravaśeṣataḥ
vidradhyoḥ kuśalaḥ kuryādraktāgantunimittayoḥ || 9
saubhāñjanakaniryūho hiṅgusaindhavasaṃyutaḥ
acirādvidradhiṃ hanti prātaḥ prātarniṣevitaḥ || 10
śigrumūlaṃ jale dhautaṃ jalapiṣṭaṃ pragālayet
tadrasaṃ madhunā pītvā hantyantarvidradhiṃ naraḥ || 11
śvetavarṣābhuvo mūlaṃ mūlaṃ varuṇakasya ca
jalena kvathitaṃ pītamapakvaṃ vidradhiṃ jayet || 12
varuṇādigaṇakvāthamapakve'bhyantarotthite
ūṣakādipratīvāpaṃ pibetsaṃśamanāya vai || 13
apakve caitaduddiṣṭaṃ pakve tu vraṇavatkriyā || 14
śamayati pāṭhāmūlaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ taṇḍulāmbhasā pītam
antarbhūtaṃ vidradhimuddhatamāśveva manujasya || 15
priyaṃgudhātakīlodhrakaṭphalaṃ tiniśatvacam
etaistailaṃ vipaktavyaṃ vidradhau vraṇaropaṇam || 16
Adhikāra 44
ādau vimlāpanaṃ kuryāddvitīyamavasecanam
tṛtīyamupanāhaṃ tu caturthaṃ(rthī) pāṭanakriyām || 1
pañcamaṃ śodhanaṃ kuryātṣaṣṭhaṃ ropaṇamiṣyate
ete kramā vraṇasyoktāḥ saptamaṃ vaikṛtāpaham || 2
mātuluṅgāgnimanthau ca suradāru mahauṣadham
ahiṃsrā caiva rāsnā ca pralepo vātaśothajit || 3
kalkaḥ kāñjikasaṃpiṣṭaḥ snigdhaḥ śākhoṭakatvacaḥ
suparṇa iva nāgānāṃ vātaśothavināśanaḥ || 4
dūrvā ca nalamūlaṃ ca madhukaṃ candanaṃ tathā
śītalāśca gaṇāḥ sarve pralepaḥ pittaśothahā || 5
nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthaplakṣavetasavalkalaiḥ
sasarpiṣkaiḥ pralepaḥ syācchothanirvāpaṇaḥ paraḥ || 6
ajagandhā'śvagandhā ca kālā saralayā saha
ekaiṣikā'jaśṛṅgī ca pralepaḥ śleṣmaśothahā || 7
punarnavādāruśigrudaśamūlamahauṣadhaiḥ
kaphavātakṛte śothe lepaḥ koṣṇo vidhīyate || 8
na rātrau lepanaṃ dadyāddattaṃ ca patitaṃ tathā
na ca paryuṣitaṃ śuṣyamāṇaṃ naivāvadhārayet || 9
śuṣyamāṇamupekṣeta pradehaṃ pīḍanaṃ prati
na cāpi mukhamālimpettena doṣaḥ prasicyate || 10
abhyajya svedayitvā tu veṇunāḍyā tataḥ śanaiḥ
vimlāpanārthaṃ mṛdnīyāttalenāṅguṣṭhakena vā || 11
raktāvasecanaṃ kuryādādāveva vicakṣaṇaḥ
śothe mahati saṃrabdhe vedanāvati vā vraṇe || 12
yo na yāti śamaṃ lepasvedasekāpatarpaṇaiḥ
so'pi nāśaṃ vrajatyāśu śothaḥ śoṇitamokṣaṇāt || 13
ekataśca kriyāḥ sarvā raktamokṣaṇamekataḥ
raktaṃ hi vyamlatāṃ yāti taccennāsti na cāsti ruk || 14
na praśāmyati yaḥ śothaḥ pralepādividhānataḥ
dravyāṇi pācanīyāni dadyāttatropanāhane || 15
satilā sātasībījā dadhyamlā saktupiṇḍikā
sakiṇvakuṣṭhalavaṇā śastā syādupanāhane || 16
śaṇaśigrumūlaphalānyatasītilasarṣapāḥ
saktavaḥ kiṇvamuṣṇāni dravyāṇyetāni pācanam || 17
tailena sarpiṣā vā'pi tābhyāṃ vā saktupiṇḍikā
sukhoṣṇā śothapākārthamupanāhe praśasyate || 18
hastidantaṃ jale ghṛṣṭaṃ bindumātrapralepanāt
atyarthakaṭhine cāpi śothe pācanabhedanam || 19
dravyāṇāṃ picchilānāṃ tu tvaṅmūlāni prapīḍanam
yavagodhūmamāṣāṇāṃ cūrṇāni ca samāsataḥ || 20
cirabilvāgnikau dantī citrako hayamārakaḥ
kapotakaṅkagṛdhrāṇāṃ purīṣāṇi ca dāraṇam || 21
kṣāradravyāṇi vā yāni kṣāro vā dāraṇaṃ param
tataḥ prakṣālanaṃ kvāthaḥ paṭolīnimbapatrajaḥ
aviśuddhe viśuddhe tu nyagrodhāditvagudbhavaḥ || 22
pañcamūlīdvayaṃ vāte nyagrodhādiśca paittike
āragvadhādiko yojyaḥ kaphaje sarvakarmasu || 23
apetapūtimāṃsānāṃ māṃsasthānāmarohatām
kalkaḥ saṃropaṇaḥ kāryastilānāṃ madhukānvitaḥ || 24
nimbapatramadhubhyāṃ tu yuktaḥ saṃśodhanaḥ smṛtaḥ || 25
pūrvābhyāṃ sarpiṣā vā'pi yuktaścāpyuparopaṇaḥ || 26
No verses of the Siddhayoga on this page.
