tilavadyavakalkaṃ tu kecidāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ
śamayedavidagdhaṃ ca vidagdhaṃ vā'pi pācayet
pakvaṃ bhinatti bhinnaṃ ca śodhayedropayeta ca || 27
nimbapatratilaiḥ kalko madhunā vraṇaśodhanaḥ
ropaṇaḥ sarpiṣā yukto yavakalke'pyayaṃ vidhiḥ || 28
nimbapatraghṛtakṣaudradārvīmadhukasaṃyutā
vartistilānāṃ kalko vā śodhayedropayedvraṇam || 29
nimbapatraṃ tilā dantī trivṛtsaindhavamākṣikam
duṣṭavraṇapraśamano lepaḥ śodhanakesarī || 30
tilakalkaḥ salavaṇo dve haridre trivṛddhṛtam
madhukaṃ nimbapatrāṇi lepaḥ syādbraṇaśodhanaḥ || 31
triphalā khadiro dārvī nyagrodhādibalākuśāḥ
nimbakolakapatrāṇi kaṣāyaḥ śodhane hitaḥ || 32
yaṣṭī tilāḥ supiṣṭā vā saghṛtā vraṇaropaṇāḥ
ekaṃ vā sārivāmūlaṃ sarvavraṇaviśodhanam || 33
saptadaladugdhakalkaḥ śamayati duṣṭavraṇaṃ pralepena
madhuyuktā śarapuṅkhā sarvavraṇaropaṇī kathitā || 34
vraṇānviśodhayedvartyā sūkṣmāsyānsaṃdhimarmagān
kṛtayā trivṛtādantīlāṅgalīmadhusaindhavaiḥ || 35
suṣavīpatradhattūrakarṇamoṭakuṭhārakāḥ
pṛthagete pralepena gambhīravraṇaropaṇāḥ || 36
pañcavalkalacūrṇairvā śukticūrṇasamāyutaiḥ
dhātakīlodhracūrṇairvā tathā rohanti te vraṇāḥ || 37
sadāhā vedanāvanto ye vraṇā mārutottarāḥ
teṣāṃ tilānumāṃ caiva bhṛṣṭānpayasi nirvṛtān
tenaiva payasā piṣṭvā kuryādālepanaṃ bhiṣak || 38
pittavidradhivīsarpaśamanaṃ lepanādikam
agnidagdhe vraṇe samyakprayuñjīta cikitsakaḥ || 39
vātābhibhūtānsāsrāvāndhūpayedugravedanān
yavājyabhūrjamadanaśrīveṣṭakasurāhvayaiḥ || 40
śrīvāsaguggulvagurusālaniryāsadhūpitāḥ
kaṭhinatvaṃ vraṇā yānti naśyantyāsrāvavedanāḥ || 41
tilāḥ payaḥ sitā kṣaudraṃ tailaṃ madhukacandanam
lepena śotharugdāharaktaṃ nirvāpayedvaṇān || 42
karañjāriṣṭanirguṇḍīraso hanyādvraṇakrimīn || 43
kalāyavidalīpatraṃ kośāmrāsthi ca pūraṇāt
surasādirasaiḥ seko lepanaṃ laśunena vā || 44
nimbaśamyākajātyarkasaptaparṇāśvamārakāḥ
kṛmighnā mūtrasaṃyuktāḥ sekālepanadhāvanaiḥ || 45
pracchādya māṃsapeśyā vā kṛmīnapaharedvraṇāt
laśunenātha vā dadyāllepanaṃ kṛmināśanam || 46
ye kledapākasrutigandhayuktā vraṇā mahāntaḥ sarujaḥ saśothāḥ
prayānti te guggulumiśritena pītena śāntiṃ triphalārasena || 47
triphalācūrṇasaṃyukto guggulurvaṭakīkṛtaḥ
niryantraṇo vibandhaghno vraṇaśodhanaropaṇaḥ || 48
amṛtāgugguluḥ śasto hitaṃ tailaṃ ca vajrakam || 49
viḍaṅgatriphalāvyoṣacūrṇaṃ guggulunā saha
sarpiṣā vaṭikāṃ kṛtvā khādedvā hitabhojanaḥ
duṣṭavraṇāpacīmehakuṣṭhanāḍīviśodhanaḥ || 50
amṛtāpaṭolamūlavāsatriphalātrikaṭukṛmighnānām
samabhāgānāṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvasamo guggulorbhāgaḥ || 51
prativāsaramekaikāṃ guṭikāṃ khādedathākṣaparimāṇām
jetuṃ vraṇavātāsṛggulmodaraśvayathupāṇḍurogādīn || 52
manaḥśilā samañjiṣṭhā salākṣā rajanīdvayam
pralepaḥ saghṛtakṣaudrastvagviśuddhikaraḥ paraḥ || 53
ayorajaḥ sakāsīsaṃ triphalākusumāni ca
pralepaḥ kurute kārṣṇyaṃ sadya eva navatvaci || 54
kālīyakalatāmrāsthihemakālārasottamaiḥ
lepaḥ sagomayarasaḥ sabarṇakaraṇaḥ paraḥ || 55
Adhikāra 45
sadyaḥkṣataṃ vraṇaṃ vaidyaḥ saśūlaṃ pariṣecayet
yaṣṭīmadhukayuktena kiṃciduṣṇena sarpiṣā || 1
buddhvā''gantuvraṇaṃ vaidyo ghṛtakṣaudrasamanvitam
śītāṃ kriyāṃ prayuñjīta pittaraktoṣmanāśanīm || 2
kāntakrāmukamekaṃ suślakṣṇaṃ gavyasarpiṣā piṣṭam
śamayati niyataṃ lepādvraṇamāgantuṃ na saṃdehaḥ || 3
kṣatoṣmaṇo nigrahārthaṃ tatkālaṃ visṛtasya ca
kaṣāyāḥ śītamadhurāḥ snigdhā lepādayo hitāḥ || 4
āmāśayasthe rudhire vamanaṃ pathyamucyate
pakvāśayasthe deyaṃ ca virecanamasaṃśayam || 5
kvātho vaṃśatvageraṇḍaśvadaṃṣṭrāśmabhidā kṛtaḥ
sahiṅgulavaṇaḥ pītaḥ koṣṭhasthaṃ srāvayedasṛk || 6
yavakolakulatthānāṃ nisnehena rasena ca
bhuñjītānnaṃ yavāgūṃ vā pibetsaindhavasaṃyutām || 7
atyarthamasraṃ sravati prāyaśo yatra vai kṣate
tato raktakṣayādvāyau kupite'tirujākare || 8
snehapānaparīṣekasnigdhalepopanāhanam
snehabastiṃ ca kurvīta vātaghnauṣadhasādhitam || 9
iti sāptāhikaḥ proktaḥ sadyovraṇahito vidhiḥ
saptāhātparataḥ kāryā śārīravraṇavatkriyā || 10
gaurā haridrā mañjiṣṭhā māṃsī madhukameva ca
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ hīveraṃ bhadramustaṃ sacandanam || 11
jātīnimbapaṭolaṃ ca karañjaḥ kaṭurohiṇī
madhūcchiṣṭaṃ madhūkaṃ ca mahāmedā tathaiva ca || 12
pañcavalkalatoyena ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
eṣa gauro mahāvīryaḥ sarvabraṇaviśodhanaḥ || 13
āgantusahajāścaiva cirotthāścaiva ye vraṇāḥ
viṣamāmapi nāḍīṃ ca śodhayecchīghrameva ca || 14
naktamālasya patrāṇi taruṇāni phalāni ca
sumanāyāśca patrāṇi paṭolāriṣṭayostathā || 15
dve haridre madhūcchiṣṭaṃ madhukaṃ tiktarohiṇī
mañjiṣṭhā candanośīramutpalaṃ sārive trivṛt || 16
eteṣāṃ kārṣikairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
duṣṭavraṇapraśamanaṃ tathā nāḍīviśodhanam
sadyaśchinnavraṇānāṃ ca karañjādyamidaṃ ghṛtam || 17
jātīnimbapaṭolapatrakaṭukādārvīniśāsārivā-
mañjiṣṭhābhayasikthatutthamadhukairnaktāhvabījaiḥ samaiḥ
sarpiḥ siddhamanena sūkṣmavadanā marmāśritāḥ srāviṇo
gambhīrāḥ sarujo vraṇāḥ sagatikāḥ śudhyanti rohanti ca || 18
prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhāmadhukośīrapadmakaiḥ
saharidraiḥ kṛtaṃ sarpiḥ sakṣīraṃ vraṇaropaṇam || 19
madhūcchiṣṭaṃ samadhukaṃ lodhraṃ sarjarasaṃ tathā
mañjiṣṭhāṃ candanaṃ mūrvāṃ piṣṭvā sarpirvipācayet
sarveṣāmagnidagdhānāmetadropaṇamiṣyate || 20
antardagdhakuṭhārako dahanajaṃ lepānnihanti vraṇa-
maśvatthasya viśuṣkavalkalakṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ tathā guṇḍanāt
abhyaṅgādvinihanti tailamakhilaṃ gaṇḍūpadaiḥ sādhitaṃ
piṣṭvā śālmalitūlakairjalagatā lepāttathā vālukāḥ || 21
siddhaṃ kaṣāyatailābhyāṃ pāṭalyāḥ kaṭutailakam
dagdhavraṇarujāsrāvadāhavisphoṭanāśanam || 22
candanaṃ vaṭaśuṅgāśca mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ tathā
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ dūrvā ca patta(trā)ṅgaṃ dhātakī tathā || 23
etaistailaṃ vipaktavyaṃ sarpiṣkṣīrasamāyutam
agnidagdhavraṇe śreṣṭhaṃ tatkṣaṇādropaṇaṃ param || 24
kuṭhārakātpalaśataṃ kvāthayennalvaṇe'mbhasi || 25
tataḥ pādāvaśeṣeṇa tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet
kalkaiḥ kuṭhārāpāmārgaproṣṭhikāmakṣikāsu ca || 26
etattailaṃ kuṭhārasya vraṇaśodhanaropaṇam
nāḍīṣu paramo'bhyaṅgo nijāsvāgantukīṣu ca || 27
dūrvāsvarasasiddhaṃ vā tailaṃ kampillakena vā
dārvītvacaśca kalkena pradhānaṃ vraṇaropaṇam || 28
yenaiva vidhinā tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ tenaiva sādhayet
raktapittottaraṃ jñātvā sarpirevāvacārayet || 29
ārūḍhe rūḍhamātre vā vraṇe sarvarasāśinaḥ
ārdre vā bandharahite gātre cābhihate'tha vā || 30
vāto'sramasrutaṃ duṣṭaṃ saṃśoṣya grathitaṃ vraṇam
kuryātsadāhaṃ kaṇḍvāḍhyaṃ vraṇagranthiriti smṛtaḥ
kṣārasūtraṃ prayuñjīta duṣṭavraṇaharaṃ vidhim || 31
Adhikāra 46
ādau bhagnaṃ viditvā tu secayecchītavāriṇā
paṅkenā''lepanaṃ kāryaṃ bandhanaṃ ca kuśānvitam || 1
avanāmitamunnahyedunnataṃ cāvapīḍayet
āñchedatikṣiptamadhogataṃ copari vartayet || 2
ālepanārthaṃ mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ cāmlapeṣitam
śatadhautaghṛtonmiśraṃ śālipiṣṭaṃ ca lepanam
nyagrodhādikaṣāyaṃ tu suśītaṃ pariṣecane || 3
pañcamūlīvipakvaṃ tu kṣīraṃ dadyātsavedane
sukhoṣṇamavacāryaṃ vā cakratailaṃ vijānatā || 4
māṃsaṃ māṃsarasaḥ kṣīraṃ sarpiryūṣaḥ satīnajaḥ
bṛṃhaṇaṃ cānnapānaṃ syāddeyaṃ bhagnāya jānatā || 5
guru śītaṃ mṛdu snigdhaṃ bahulaṃ sthūlapicchilam
prāyo mandaṃ sthiraṃ ślakṣṇaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ dravyamucyate || 6
gṛṣṭikṣīraṃ sasarpiṣkaṃ madhurauṣadhasādhitam
śītalaṃ lākṣayā yuktaṃ prātarbhagnaḥ pibennaraḥ || 7
saghṛtenāsthisaṃhāraṃ lākṣāṃ godhūmamarjunam
saṃdhimukte'sthibhagne ca pibetkṣīreṇa mānavaḥ || 8
rasonamadhulākṣājyasitākalkaṃ samaśnatām
chinnabhinnacyutāsthīnāṃ saṃdhānamacirādbhavet || 9
kṣīraṃ salākṣāmadhukaṃ ca sarpiḥ syājjīvanīyaṃ ca pibetsukhārthī
bhagnaḥ pibettvakpayasā'rjunasya godhūmacūrṇaṃ saghṛtena vā'tha || 10
lākṣāsthisaṃhṛtkakubhāśvagandhā cūrṇīkṛtā nāgabalā puraśca
saṃbhagnamuktāsthirujaṃ nihanyādaṅgāni kuryātkuliśopamāni || 11
ābhāphalatrikavyoṣaiḥ sarvairebhiḥ samāṃśakaiḥ
tulyo guggulurāyojyo bhagnasaṃdhiprasādhakaḥ || 12
savraṇasya tu bhagnasya vraṇaṃ sarpirmadhūttaraiḥ
pratisārya kaṣāyaiśca śeṣaṃ bhagnavadācaret || 13
vātavyādhivinirdiṣṭānsnehānatrāpi yojayet || 14
lavaṇaṃ kaṭukaṃ kṣāramamlaṃ maithunamātapam
vyāyāmaṃ ca na seveta bhagno rūkṣānnameva ca || 15
Adhikāra 47
nāḍīnāṃ gatimanviṣya śastreṇā''pāṭya karmavit
sarvaṃ vraṇakramaṃ kuryācchodhanaṃ ropaṇādikam || 1
nāḍīṃ vātakṛtāṃ sādhu pāṭitāṃ lepayedbhiṣak
pratyakpuṣpīphalayutaistilaiḥ piṣṭaiḥ pralepayet || 2
paittikīṃ tilamañjiṣṭhānāgadantīniśādvayaiḥ
ślaiṣmikīṃ tilayaṣṭyāhvanikumbhāriṣṭasaindhavaiḥ || 3
śalyajāṃ tilamadhvājyairlepayecchinnaśodhitām
āragvadhaniśākālācūrṇājyakṣaudrasaṃyutā
sūtravartirvraṇe yojyā śodhanī gatināśanī || 4
ghoṇṭāphalatvaṅmadanātphalāni pūgasya ca tvaglavaṇaṃ ca mukhyam
sruhyarkadugdhena sahaiṣa kalko vartīkṛto hantyacireṇa nāḍīm || 5
vartīkṛtaṃ mākṣikasaṃprayuktaṃ nāḍīghnamuktaṃ lavaṇottamaṃ vā
duṣṭavraṇe yadvihitaṃ ca tailaṃ tatsevyamānaṃ gatimāśu hanti || 6
jātyarkaśamyākakarañjadantīsindhūtthasauvarcalayāvaśūkaiḥ
vartiḥ kṛtā hantyacireṇa nāḍīṃ snukkṣīrapiṣṭā saha citrakeṇa || 7
kṛśadurbalabhīrūṇāṃ nāḍī marmāśritā ca yā
kṣārasūtreṇa tāṃ chindyānna śastreṇa kadācana || 8
eṣiṇyā gatimanviṣya kṣārasūtrānusāriṇīm || 9
sūcīṃ nidadhyādyatnena tathonnamyā''śu nirharet
sūtrasyāntaṃ samānīya gāḍhaṃ bandhaṃ samācaret || 10
tataḥ kṣārabalaṃ vīkṣya sūtramanyatpraveśayet
kṣārāktaṃ matimānvaidyo yāvanna cchidyate gatiḥ
bhagaṃdare'pyeṣa vidhiḥ kāryo vaidyena jānatā || 11
arbudādiṣu cotkṣipya mūle sūtraṃ nidhāpayet || 12
sūcībhiryavavaktrābhirācitaṃ vā samantataḥ
mūle sūtreṇa badhnīyācchinne copacaredbraṇam || 13
guggulustriphalāvyoṣaiḥ samāṃśairājyayojitaḥ
nāḍīduṣṭavraṇaharo bhagaṃdaravināśanaḥ || 14
svarjikāsindhudantyagnirūyikānalanīlikāḥ
kharamañjaribījeṣu tailaṃ gomūtrapācitam
duṣṭavraṇapraśamanaṃ kaphanāḍīvraṇāpaham || 15
kumbhīkakharjūrakapitthabilvavanaspatīnāṃ ca śalāṭuvargam
kṛtvā kaṣāyaṃ vipacettu tailamāvāpya mustāsaralāpriyaṃgūḥ || 16
saugandhikāmocarasāhipuṣpacūrṇāni dattvā khalu dhātakīṃ ca
etena śalyaprabhavā hi nāḍī rohedvraṇo vai sukhamāśu caiva || 17
bhallātakārkamaricairlavaṇottamena
siddhaṃ viḍaṅgarajanīdvayacitrakaiśca
syānmārkavasya ca rasena nihanti tailaṃ
nāḍīṃ kaphānilakṛtāmapacīṃ vraṇāṃśca || 18
samūlapatrāṃ nirguṇḍīṃ pīḍayitvā rasena tu
tena siddhaṃ samaṃ tailaṃ nāḍīduṣṭavraṇāpaham || 19
hitaṃ pāmāpacīnāṃ ca pānābhyañjananāvanaiḥ
vividheṣu ca sphoṭeṣu tathā duṣṭavraṇeṣu ca || 20
yā dvivraṇīye vihitāstu vartyastāḥ sarvanāḍīṣu bhiṣagvidadhyāt || 21
Adhikāra 48
gudasya śvayathuṃ dṛṣṭvā viśoṣya śodhayettataḥ
raktāvasecanaṃ kāryaṃ yathā pākaṃ na gacchati || 1
vaṭapatreṣṭikāśuṇṭhīguḍūcyaḥ sapunarnavāḥ
supiṣṭvā piṭikāvasthe lepaḥ śasto bhagaṃdare || 2
piḍakānāmapakvānāmapatarpaṇapūrvakam
karma kuryādvirekāntaṃ bhinnānāṃ vakṣyate kriyā || 3
eṣaṇaṃ pāṭanaṃ kṣāravahnidāhādikaṃ kramam
vidhāya vraṇavatkāryaṃ yathādoṣaṃ yathākramam || 4
trivṛttilā nāgadantī mañjiṣṭhā saha sarpiṣā
utsādanaṃ bhavedetatsaindhavakṣaudrasaṃyutam || 5
rasāñjanaṃ haridre dve mañjiṣṭhā nimbapallavāḥ
trivṛttejovatī dantī kalko nāḍīvraṇāpahaḥ || 6
nyagrodhādirgaṇo yastu hitaḥ śodhanaropaṇaḥ
tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā tatpakvaṃ bhagaṃdaravināśanam || 7
kuṣṭhaṃ trivṛttilādantīmāgadhyaḥ saindhavaṃ madhu
rajanī triphalā tutthaṃ hitaṃ vraṇaviśodhanam || 8
snuhyarkadugdhadārvībhirvartiṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ
bhagaṃdaragatiṃ jñātvā pūrayettāṃ prayatnataḥ
eṣā sarvaśarīrasthāṃ nāḍīṃ hanyānna saṃśayaḥ || 9
tilābhayākuṣṭhamariṣṭapatraṃ niśe vacā lodhramagāradhūmaḥ
bhagaṃdare nāḍyupadaṃśayośca duṣṭavraṇe śodhanaropaṇo'yam || 10
triphalārasasaṃyuktaṃ biḍālāsthipralepanam
bhagaṃdaraṃ nihantyāśu duṣṭavraṇaharaṃ param || 11
triphalāpurakṛṣṇānāṃ tripañcaikāṃśayojitā guṭikā
kuṣṭhabhagaṃdaranāḍīduṣṭavraṇaśodhinī kathitā || 12
jambūkamāṃsaṃ bhakṣeta prakārairvyañjanādibhiḥ
ajīrṇavarjī māsena mucyate tu bhagaṃdarāt || 13
pañcatiktaṃ ghṛtaṃ śastaṃ tathā guggulupūrvakam || 14
citrakārkau trivṛtpāṭhe malayūhayamārakau
sudhāṃ vacāṃ lāṅgalakīṃ haritālaṃ suvarcikām || 15
jyotiṣmatīṃ ca saṃkṣuṇṇāṃ tailaṃ dhīro vipācayet
etadviṣyandanaṃ nāma tailaṃ dadyādbhagaṃdare
śodhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ caiva savarṇakaraṇaṃ param || 16
karavīraniśādantīlāṅgalīlavaṇāgnibhiḥ
mātuluṅgārkavatsāhvaiḥ pacettailaṃ bhagaṃdare || 17
vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ yuddhaṃ pṛṣṭhayānaṃ gurūṇi ca
saṃvatsaraṃ parihareduparūḍhavraṇo naraḥ || 18
Adhikāra 49
snigdhasvinnasya teṣvādau dhvajamadhye śirāvyadhaḥ
jalaukaḥpātanaṃ vā syādūrdhvādhaḥśodhanaṃ tathā
pāko rakṣyaḥ prayatnena śiśnakṣayakaro hi saḥ || 1
paṭolanimbatriphalāguḍūcīkvāthaṃ pibedvā khadirāsanābhyām
sagugguluṃ vā triphalāyutaṃ vā sarvopadaṃśāpaharaḥ prayogaḥ || 2
prapauṇḍarīkvaṃ madhukaṃ rāsnā kuṣṭhaṃ punarnavā
saralāgurubhadrākhyairvātajaṃ saṃpralepayet || 3
gairikāñjanamañjiṣṭhāmadhukośīrapadmakaiḥ
sacandanotpalaiḥ snigdhaiḥ paittikaṃ saṃpralepayet || 4
nimbārjunāśvatthakadambaśālajambūvaṭodumbaravetaseṣu
prakṣālanālepaghṛtāni kuryāccūrṇaṃ ca pittāsrabhavopadaṃśe || 5
śālājakarṇāśvakarṇadhavatvagbhiḥ kaphotthitam
surāpiṣṭābhiruṣṇābhiḥ satailābhiḥ pralepayet || 6
triphalāyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa bhṛṅgarājarasena vā
vraṇaprakṣālanaṃ kuryādupadaṃśapraśāntaye || 7
dahetkaṭāhe triphalāṃ sā maṣī madhusaṃyutā
upadaṃśe pralepo'yaṃ sadyo ropayati vraṇam || 8
japājātyaśvamārārkaśamyākānāṃ dalaiḥ pṛthak
kṛtaṃ prakṣālane kvāthaṃ meḍhrapāke prayojayet || 9
karañjanimbārjunaśākajambūvaṭādibhiḥ kalkakaṣāyasiddham
sarpirnihanyādupadaṃśadoṣaṃ sadāhapākaṃ srutirāgayuktam || 10
āgāradhūmo rajanī surākiṭṭaṃ tu taistribhiḥ || 11
bhāgottaraiḥ pacettailaṃ kaṇḍūśotharujāpaham
śodhanaṃ ropaṇaṃ caiva savarṇakaraṇaṃ tathā || 12
arśasāṃ chinnadagdhānāṃ kriyā kāryopadaṃśavat || 13
Adhikāra 50
hitaṃ ca sarpiṣaḥ pānaṃ pathyaṃ cāpi virecanam
hitaḥ śoṇitamokṣaśca yaccāpi laghubhojanam || 1
sarṣapīṃ likhitāṃ sūkṣmaiḥ kaṣāyairavacūrṇayet
tairevābhyañjanaṃ tailaṃ sādhayedvraṇaropaṇam || 2
kriyeyamavamanthe'pi raktaṃ srāvyaṃ tathobhayoḥ
aṣṭhīlāyāṃ hṛte rakte śleṣmagranthivadācaret || 3
kumbhikāyāṃ haredraktaṃ pakvāyāṃ śodhite vraṇe
tindukatriphalālodhrairlepastailaṃ ca ropaṇam || 4
alajyāṃ(?) hṛtaraktāyāmayameva kriyākramaḥ
svedayedgrathitaṃ śaśvannāḍīsvedena buddhimān
sukhoṣṇairupanāhaiśca susnigdhairupanāhayet || 5
uttamākhyāṃ tu piḍikāṃ saṃchedya baḍiśoddhṛtām
kalkacūrṇaiḥ kaṣāyāṇāṃ kṣaudrayuktairupācaret || 6
kramaḥ pittavisarpoktaḥ puṣkarīmūḍhayorhitaḥ || 7
tvakpāke sparśahānyāṃ ca secayenmṛditaṃ punaḥ
balātailena koṣṇena madhuraiścopanāhayet || 8
rasakriyā vidhātavyā likhite śatapotake
pṛthakparṇyādisiddhaṃ ca tailaṃ deyamanantaram || 9
raktavidradhivaccāpi kriyā śoṇitaje'rbude
kaṣāyakalkasarpīṃṣi tailacūrṇarasakriyāḥ
śodhane ropaṇe caiva vīkṣya vīkṣyāvacārayet || 10
arbudaṃ māṃsapākaṃ ca vidradhiṃ tilakālakam
pratyākhyāya prakurvīta bhiṣakteṣāṃ pratikriyām || 11
Adhikāra 51
vātottareṣu sarpirvamanaṃ śleṣmottareṣu kuṣṭheṣu
pittottareṣu mokṣo raktasya virecanaṃ cāgre || 1
pracchannamalpakuṣṭhe mahati ca śastaṃ śirāvyadhanam
bahudoṣaḥ saṃśodhyaḥ kuṣṭhī bahuśo'nurakṣatā prāṇān || 2
doṣe hyatimātrahṛte vāyurhanyācca balamāśu
doṣotkliṣṭe hṛdaye vamanaṃ kuṣṭheṣu cordhvabhāgeṣu || 3
vacāvāsāpaṭolānāṃ nimbasya phalinītvacaḥ
kaṣāyo madhunā pīto vāntikṛnmadanānvitaḥ
virecanaṃ prayoktavyaṃ trivṛddantīphalatrikaiḥ || 4
manaḥśilāle maricāni tailamārkaṃ payaḥ kuṣṭhaharaḥ pradehaḥ
karañjabījaiḍagajaṃ sakuṣṭhaṃ gomūtrapiṣṭaṃ ca paraḥ pradehaḥ || 5
parṇāni piṣṭvā caturaṅgulasya takreṇa parṇānyatha kākamācyāḥ
tailāktagātrasya narasya kuṣṭhānyudvartayedaśvahanacchadaiśca || 6
eḍagajakuṣṭhasaindhavasauvīrakasarṣapaiḥ kṛmighnaiśca
kṛmisidhmadadrumaṇḍalakuṣṭhānāṃ nāśano lepaḥ || 7
koṭhābhihitaṃ kuṣṭhādilepamatrāpi kārayet || 8
āragvadhasya patrāṇi āranālena peṣayet
dadrukiṭibhakuṣṭhāni hanti sidhmānameva ca || 9
sthauṇeyaruṅniśādūrvāsaptatālāḥ pralepataḥ
dhattūrarasapiṣṭāstu kaṇḍūkacchvādināśanāḥ || 10
kāsamardakamūlaṃ tu sauvīreṇa ca peṣitam
dadrukiṭibhakuṣṭhāni jayedetatpralepanāt || 11
bījāni vā mūlakasarṣapāṇāṃ lākṣārajanyau prapunāṭabījam
śrīveṣṭakavyoṣaviḍaṅgakuṣṭhaṃ piṣṭvā tu mūtreṇa vilepanaṃ syāt
dadrūṇi sidhmaṃ kiṭibhaṃ ca pāmāṃ kapālakuṣṭhaṃ viṣamaṃ ca hanyāt || 12
viḍaṅgaiḍagajākuṣṭhaniśāsindhūtthasarṣapaiḥ
dhānyāmlapiṣṭairlepo'yaṃ dadrukuṣṭhavināśanaḥ || 13
dūrvābhayāsaindhavacakramardakuṭherakāḥ kāñjikatakrapiṣṭāḥ
tribhiḥ pralepairapi baddhamūlāṃ dadruṃ ca kaṇḍūṃ ca vināśayanti || 14
tulyo rasaḥ śālatarostuṣeṇa sacakramardo'pyabhayāvimiśraḥ
pānīyabhaktena tadambupiṣṭo lepaḥ kṛto dadrugajendrasiṃhaḥ || 15
yadvarendrīsamudbhūtaṃ tṛṇākhyaṃ ca mahauṣadham
gharṣaṇādbhakṣaṇāttasya kuṣṭhaṃ kṣipraṃ praśāmyati || 16
śikharirasena supiṣṭaṃ mūlakabījaṃ pralepataḥ sidhmam
kṣāreṇa kadalyā vā rajanīmiśreṇa nāśayati || 17
gandhapāṣāṇamiśreṇa yavakṣāreṇa lepitam
sidhma nāśamupaityāśu kaṭutailayutena tu || 18
kāsamardakabījāni mūlakānāṃ tathaiva ca
gandhapāṣāṇamiśrāṇi sidhmānāṃ paramauṣadham || 19
dhātrīrasaḥ sarjarasaḥ sapākyaḥ sauvīrapiṣṭaśca tadāsutaśca
bhavanti sidhmāni yathā na bhūyastathedamudvartanakaṃ karoti || 20
patrakoṣaṇakāsīsatailatāpyamanaḥśilāḥ
saptāhamuṣitāḥ kāṃsye sidhmaśvitravināśanāḥ || 21
dhātryakṣapathyākrimiśatruvahnibhallātakāvalgujalohabhṛṅgaiḥ
bhāgābhivṛddhaistilatailamiśraiḥ sarvāṇi kuṣṭhāni nihanti lehaḥ || 22
śaśāṅkalekhā saviḍaṅgakuṣṭhā sapippalīkā sahutāśamūlā
sāyomalā sāmalakā satailā sarvāṇi kuṣṭhāni nihanti līḍhā || 23
āragvadhaḥ saiḍagajaḥ karañjo vāsā guḍūcī madanaṃ haridre
śtyāhvaḥ surāhvaḥ khadiro dhavaśca nimbo viḍaṅgaṃ karavīrakaṃ ca || 24
granthiśca bhaurjo laśunaḥ śirīṣaḥ sālomaśo guggulukṛṣṇagandhe
phaṇijjako vatsakasaptaparṇau pīlūni kuṣṭhaṃ sumanaḥpravālāḥ || 25
vacā hareṇustrivṛtā nikumbhā bhallātako gairikamañjanaṃ ca
manaḥśilāle gṛhadhūma elā kāsīsalodhrārjunamustasarjāḥ || 26
ityardharūpairvihitāḥ ṣaḍete gopittapītāḥ punareva piṣṭāḥ
siddhāḥ paraṃ sarṣapatailayuktāścūrṇapradehā bhiṣajā prayojyāḥ || 27
kuṣṭhāni kṛcchrāṇi navaṃ kilāsaṃ surendraluptaṃ kiṭibhaṃ sadadru
bhagaṃdarārśāṃsyapacīṃ sapāmāṃ hanyuḥ prayuktā na cirānnarāṇām || 28
kṣāre sudagdhe gajaliṇḍaje ca gajasya mūtreṇa bahusrute ca
droṇapramāṇe daśabhāgayuktaṃ dattvā pacedbījamavalgujasya || 29
etadyadā cikkaṇatāmupaiti tadā susiddhāṃ guṭikāṃ prakuryāt
śvitraṃ pralimpedatha tena ghṛṣṭaṃ tadā vrajedāśu savarṇabhāvam || 30
vāyasyeḍagajākuṣṭhakṛṣṇābhirguṭikā kṛtā
bastamūtreṇa saṃpiṣṭā pralepācchvitranāśanī || 31
pūtīkārkasnuṅnarendradrumāṇāṃ mūtraiḥ piṣṭāḥ pallavāḥ saumanāśca
lepācchvitraṃ ghnanti dadruvraṇāṃśca kuṣṭhānyarśoduṣṭanāḍīvraṇāṃśca || 32
kuḍavo'valgujabījāddharitālacaturthabhāgasaṃmiśraḥ
mūtreṇa gavāṃ piṣṭaḥ savarṇakaraṇaḥ paraḥ śvitre || 33
dhātrīkhadirayoḥ kvāthaṃ pītvā'valgujasaṃyutam
śaṅkhendudhavalaṃ śvitraṃ tūrṇaṃ hanti na saṃśayaḥ || 34
cakrāṅkabījaṃ snukkṣīrabhāvitaṃ mūtrasaṃyutam
ravitaptaṃ sakiṇvaṃ ca lepanaṃ kiṭibhāpaham || 35
unmattakasya bījena māṇakakṣāravāriṇā
kaṭutailaṃ vipaktavyaṃ śīghraṃ hanyādvipādikām || 36
nālikerodare nyastastaṇḍulaḥ pūtitāṃ gataḥ
lepādvipādikāṃ hanti cirakālānubandhinīm || 37
āvalgujaṃ kāsamardaṃ cakramardaṃ niśāyutam || 38
māṇimanthena tulyāṃśaṃ madhukāñjikapeṣitam
kacchūṃ kaṇḍūṃ jayatyugrāṃ siddha eṣa prayogarāṭ || 39
komalasiṃhāsyadalaṃ saniśaṃ surabhījalena saṃpiṣṭam
divasatrayeṇa niyataṃ kṣapayati kacchūṃ vilepanataḥ || 40
haridrākalkasaṃyuktaṃ gomūtrasya paladvayam
pibennaraḥ kāmacārī kacchūpāmāvināśanam || 41
śothapāṇḍvāmayaharī gulmamehakaphāpahā
kacchūpāmāharī caiva pathyā gomūtrasādhitā || 42
pibati sakaṭutailaṃ gandhapāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ
ravikiraṇasutaptaṃ pāmano yaḥ palārdham
tridinatadanuṣiktaḥ kṣīrabhojī ca śīghraṃ
bhavati kanakadīptyā kāmayukto manuṣyaḥ || 43
niśāsudhāragvadhakākamācī-
patraiḥ sadārvīprapunāṭabījaiḥ
sauvīrapiṣṭaiḥ kaṭutailamiśraiḥ
pāmādiṣūdvartanametadiṣṭam || 44
māṃsīcandanaśamyākakarañjāriṣṭasarṣapam
śaṭhīkuṭajadārvyabdaṃ hanti kaṇḍūmayaṃ gaṇaḥ || 45
gharmasevī kaduṣṇena vāriṇā bākucīṃ pibet
kṣīrabhojī trisaptāhātkuṣṭharogātpramucyate || 46
eḍagajātilasarṣapakuṣṭhaṃ māgadhikālavaṇadvayamastupūtikṛtam
divasatrayametaddhanti sadadru vicarcikakuṣṭham || 47
bhallātakadvīpisudhārkamūlaguñjāphalatryūṣaṇaśaṅkhacūrṇam
tutthaṃ sakuṣṭhaṃ lavaṇāni pañca kṣāradvayaṃ lāṅgalikāṃ ca paktvā || 48
snuhyarkadugdhe ghanamāyasasthaṃ śalākayā tadvidadhīta lepam
kuṣṭhe kilāse tilakālakeṣu aśeṣadurnāmasu carmakīle || 49
viṣavaruṇaharidrācitrakāgāradhūma-
manalamaricadūrvāḥ kṣīramarkasnuhībhyām
dahati patitamātrātkuṣṭhajātīraśeṣāḥ
kuliśamiva saroṣācchakrahastādvimuktam || 50
triphalāpaṭolarajanīmañjiṣṭhārohiṇīvacānimbaiḥ
eṣa kaṣāyo'bhyasto nihanti kaphapittajaṃ kuṣṭham || 51
chinnāyāḥ svarasaṃ vā'pi sevamāno yathābalam
jīrṇe ghṛtena bhuñjīta svalpayūṣodakena tu
atipūtiśarīro'pi divyamūrtirbhavennaraḥ || 52
paṭolakhadirāriṣṭatriphalākṛṣṇavetrakam
tiktāśanaḥ pibetkvāthaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kuṣṭhī vyapohati || 53
tilājyatriphalākṣaudravyoṣabhallātaśarkarāḥ
vṛṣyaḥ saptasamo medhyaḥ kuṣṭhahā kāmacāriṇaḥ || 54
viḍaṅgatriphalākṛṣṇācūrṇaṃ līḍhaṃ samākṣikam
hanti kuṣṭhakṛmīnmehānnāḍīvraṇabhagaṃdarān || 55
indrāśanaṃ samādāya praśaste'hani coddhṛtam || 56
taccūrṇaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ lihetkṣīraghṛtāśanaḥ
hatvā ca sarvakuṣṭhāni jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam || 57
yaḥ khādedabhayāriṣṭamariṣṭāmalakāni ca
sa jayetsarvakuṣṭhāni māsādūrdhvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 58
dahyamānāccyutaḥ kumbhe mūlage khadirādrasaḥ
sājyadhātrīrasakṣaudro hanyātkuṣṭhaṃ rasāyanam || 59
puṣpakāle tu puṣpāṇi phalakāle phalāni ca
saṃgṛhya picumandasya tvaṅmūlāni dalāni ca || 60
dviraṃśāni samāhṛtya bhāgikāni prakalpayet
triphalā tryūṣaṇaṃ brāhmī śvadaṃṣṭrāruṣkarāgnikāḥ || 61
viḍaṅgasāravārāhīlohacūrṇāmṛtāḥ samāḥ
niśādvayābalgujakavyādhighātāḥ saśarkarāḥ || 62
kuṣṭhendrayavapāṭhāśca kṛtvā cūrṇaṃ susaṃyutam
khadirāsananimbānāṃ ghanakvāthena bhāvayet || 63
saptadhā pañcanimbaṃ tu mārkavasvarasena tu
snigdhaśuddhatanurdhīmānyojayecca śubhe dine || 64
madhunā tiktahaviṣā khadirāsanavāriṇā
lehyamuṣṇāmbunā vā'pi kolavṛddhyā palaṃ pibet
jīrṇe tu bhojanaṃ kāryaṃ snigdhaṃ laghu hitaṃ ca yat || 65
vicarcikodumbarapuṇḍarīkakapāladadrūkiṭibhālasādi
śatāruvisphoṭavisarpamālāḥ kaphaprakopaṃ trividhaṃ kilāsam
bhagaṃdaraślīpadavātaraktaṃ jahāti nāḍīvraṇaśīrṣarogān || 66
sarvapramehānpradarāṃśca sarvāndaṃṣṭrāviṣaṃ mūlaviṣaṃ nihanti

samopayogādapi ye daśanti sarpādayo yānti vināśamāśu

saṃkṣudya sarpiṣā sārdhaṃ guṭikāṃ kārayedbhiṣak

hantyaṣṭādaśa kuṣṭhāni kṛmīnduṣṭavraṇāni ca
grahaṇyarśovikārāṃśca mukhāmayagalagrahān || 72
gṛdhrasīmasthibhagnaṃ ca gulmaṃ cāpi niyacchati

pralepodvartanasnānapānabhojanakarmaṇā
śīlitaṃ khādiraṃ vāri sarvatvagdoṣanāśanam || 74
nimbaṃ paṭolaṃ dārvīṃ durālabhāṃ tiktarohiṇīṃ triphalām
kuryādardhapalāṃśānparpaṭakaṃ trāyamāṇāṃ ca || 75
salilāḍhakasiddhānāṃ raseṣṭabhāgasthite kṣipetpūte

mustaṃ vatsakabījaṃ kalkīkṛtyārdhakārṣikānbhāgān
navasarpiṣaśca ṣaṭpalametatsiddhaṃ ghṛtaṃ peyam || 77
kuṣṭhajvaragulmārśograhaṇīpāṇḍvāmayaśvayathuhantṛ
pāmāvisarpapiṭikākaṇḍūmadagaṇḍanutsiddham || 78

kuryāddaśapalānbhāgānekaikasya sukuṭṭitān || 79

ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacettena triphalāgarbhasaṃyutam || 80
pañcatiktamidaṃ khyātaṃ sarpiḥ kuṣṭhavināśanam
aśītiṃ vātajānrogāṃścatvāriṃśacca paittikān || 81
viṃśatiṃ ślaiṣmikāṃścaiva pānādevāpakarṣati
duṣṭavraṇakṛmīnarśaḥ pañca kāsāṃśca nāśayet || 82
triphalādviniśāvatsayāsaparpaṭakulakān
trāyantīkaṭukānimbānpratyekaṃ dvipalonmitān || 83
kvāthayitvā jaladroṇe pādaśeṣeṇaṃ tena tu
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacedvyakṣaiḥ pippalīghanacandanaiḥ || 84
trāyantīśakrabhūnimbaistatpītaṃ tiktakaṃ ghṛtam
hanti kuṣṭhajvarārśāṃsi śvayathuṃ grahaṇīgadam
pāṇḍurogaṃ visarpaṃ ca klībānāmapi śasyate || 85
saptacchadaṃ prativiṣāṃ śamyākaṃ tiktarohiṇīṃ pāṭhām || 86
mustamuśīraṃ triphalāṃ paṭolapicumandaparpaṭakam
dhanvayavāsaṃ candanamupakulye padmakaṃ rajanyau ca || 87
ṣaḍgranthāṃ saviśālāṃ śatāvarīṃ sārive cobhe

kalkānkuryānmatimānyaṣṭyāhvaṃ trāyamāṇāṃ ca
kalkastu caturbhāgo jalamaṣṭaguṇaṃ raso'mṛtaphalānām || 89
dviguṇo ghṛtātpradeyastatsarpiḥ pāyayetsiddham

visarpamamlapittaṃ vātāsṛkpāṇḍurogaṃ ca
visphoṭakānsapāmānunmādaṃ kāmalāṃ jvaraṃ kaṇḍūm || 91
hṛdrogagulmapiṭakānasṛgdaraṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ ca
hanyādetatsadyaḥ pītaṃ kāle yathābalaṃ sarpiḥ
yogaśatairapyajitānmahāvikārānmahātiktam || 92
khadirasya tulāḥ pañca śiṃśapāsanayostule
tulārdhāḥ sarva evaite karañjāriṣṭavetasāḥ || 93
parpaṭaḥ kuṭajaścaiva vṛṣaḥ kṛmiharastathā
hvaridre kṛtamālaśca guḍūcī triphalā trivṛt || 94
saptapaṇarśca saṃkuṭya daśadroṇena vāriṇā
aṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ tu kaṣāyamavatārayet || 95
dhātrīrasaṃ ca tulyāṃśaṃ sarpiṣaścā''ḍhakaṃ pacet
mahātiktakakalkaiśca yathoktaiḥ palasaṃmitaiḥ || 96
nihanti sarvakuṣṭhāni pānābhyaṅganiṣevaṇāt
mahākhādiramityetatparaṃ kuṣṭhavikārajit || 97
nimbāmṛtāvṛṣapaṭolanidigdhikānāṃ
bhāgānpṛthagdaśapalānvipaceddhaṭe'pām
aṣṭāṃśaśeṣitarasena sunisrutena
prasthaṃ ghṛtasya vipacetpicubhāgakalkaiḥ || 98
pāṭhāviḍaṅgasuradārugajopakulyā-
dvikṣāranāgaraniśāmiśicavyakuṣṭhaiḥ
tejovatīmaricavatsakadīpyakāgni-
rohiṇyaruṣkaravacākaṇamūlayuktaiḥ || 99
mañjiṣṭhayā'tiviṣayā varayā yavānyā
saṃśuddhaguggulupalairapi pañcasaṃkhyaiḥ
tatsevitaṃ vidhamati prabalaṃ samīraṃ
saṃdhyasthimajjagatamapyatha kuṣṭhamīdṛk || 100
nāḍīvraṇārbudabhagaṃdaragaṇḍamālā-
jatrūrdhvasarvagadagulmagudotthamehān
yakṣmāruciśvasanapīnasakāsaśopha-
hṛtpāṇḍurogagalavidradhivātaraktam || 101
vāsāguḍūcītriphalāpaṭolakarañjanīmbāsanakṛṣṇavetram
tatkvāthakalkena ghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ tadvajrakaṃ kuṣṭhaharaṃ pradiṣṭam || 102
viśīrṇakarṇāṅgulihastapādaḥ kṛmyardito bhinnagalo'pi martyaḥ
paurāṇikīṃ kāntimavāpya jīvedavyāhataṃ barṣaśataṃ ca kuṣṭhī || 103
mañjiṣṭhārugniśācakramardāragvadhapallavaiḥ
tṛṇakasvarase siddhaṃ tailaṃ kuṣṭhaharaṃ kaṭu || 104
caturguṇe tṛṇarase kaṭutailaṃ vipācayet
haridrākuṣṭhamañjiṣṭhāśamyākasarṣapāyutaiḥ || 105
kāsamardāriṣṭapatraiścakramardaiḥ samairbhiṣak
aṣṭādaśānāṃ kuṣṭhānāṃ tailametadvināśanam || 106
saptaparṇakarañjārkamālatīkaravīrajam || 107
mūlaṃ snuhāśirīṣābhyāṃ citrakāsphotayorapi
viṣalāṅgalavajrākhyakāsīsālaṃ manaḥśilām || 108
karañjabījaṃ trikaṭu triphalāṃ rajanīdvayam
siddhārthakaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ ca prapunnāṭaṃ ca saṃharet || 109
mūtrapiṣṭaiḥ pacettailamebhiḥ kuṣṭhavināśanam
abhyaṅgādvajrakaṃ nāma nāḍīduṣṭavraṇāpaham || 110
maricālaśilāhvārkapayośvārijaṭātrivṛt
śakṛdrasaviśālāruṅniśāyugdārucandanaiḥ || 111
kaṭutailaṃ pacetprasthaṃ dvyakṣairviṣapalānvitaiḥ
sagomūtraṃ tadabhyaṅgāddadruśvitravināśanam
sarveṣvapi ca kuṣṭheṣu tailametatpraśasyate || 112
maricaṃ trivṛtā dantī kṣīramārkaṃ śakṛdrasaḥ || 113
devadāru haridre dve māṃsī kuṣṭhaṃ sacandanam
viśālā karavīraṃ ca haritālaṃ manaḥśilā || 114
citrakolāṅgalākhyāṃ ca viḍaṅgaṃ cakramardakam
śirīṣaḥ kuṭajo nimbaḥ saptaparṇaḥ snuhā'mṛtā || 115
śamyāko naktamālābdakhadiraṃ pippalī vacā

āḍhakaṃ kaṭutailasya gomūtraṃ tu caturguṇam
mṛtpātre lohapātre vā śanairmṛdvagninā pacet || 117
paktvā tailavaraṃ hyetanmrakṣayetkauṣṭhikānvraṇān
pāmāvicarcikādadrukaṇḍūvisphoṭakāni ca || 118
valayaḥ palitaṃ chāyā nīlī vyaṅgaṃ tathaiva ca
abhyaṅgena praṇaśyanti saukumāryaṃ ca jāyate || 119

parāmapi jarāṃ prāpya na stanā yānti namratām || 120
balīvardasturaṅgo vā gajo vā vāyupīḍitaḥ
tribhirabhyañjanairbāḍhaṃ bhavenmārutavikramaḥ || 121

karavīraṃ vacā kuṣṭhamāsphotā raktacandanam || 122

eṣāmardhapalā bhāgā viṣasyāpi palaṃ bhavet || 123
caturguṇe gavāṃ mūtre tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet
śvitravisphoṭakiṭibhakīṭalūtāvicarcikāḥ || 124
kaṇḍūkacchūvikārāśca ye vraṇā viṣadūṣitāḥ
viṣatailamidaṃ nāma sarvavraṇaviśodhanam || 125
śvetakaravīraraso gomūtraṃ citrako viḍaṅgaṃ ca
kuṣṭheṣu tailayogaḥ siddho'yaṃ saṃmato bhiṣajām || 126
śvetakaravīramūlaṃ viṣāṃśaṃ sādhitaṃ gavāṃ mūtre
carmadalasidhmapāmāsphoṭakṛmikiṭibhajittailam || 127
sindūrārdhapalaṃ piṣṭvā jīrakasya palaṃ tathā
kaṭutailaṃ pacedābhyāṃ sadyaḥ pāmāharaṃ param
vṛddhavaidyopadeśena pacettailaṃ palāṣṭakam || 128
sindūraṃ candanaṃ māṃsī viḍaṅgaṃ rajanīdvayam
priyaṃgupadmakaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ mañjiṣṭhākhadiraṃ vacā || 129
jātyarkatrivṛtānimbakarañjaṃ viṣameva ca
kṛṣṇavetrakalodhraṃ ca prapunnāṭaṃ ca saṃharet || 130

raktapittotthitānhanti rogānevaṃvidhānbahūn || 132
sindūraguggulurasāñjanasikthatutthaiḥ
kalkīkṛtaiśca kaṭutailamidaṃ vipakvam
kaṇḍūṃ sravatpiṭakikāmatha vā viśuṣkā-
mabhyañjanena sakṛduddharati prasahya || 133
mañjiṣṭhātriphalālākṣāniśāgandhaśilālakaiḥ
cūrṇitaistailamādityapakvaṃ pāmāharaṃ param || 134
svarasena hi dūrvāyāḥ pacettailaṃ caturguṇam
kacchūvicarcikāpāmā abhyaṅgādeva nāśayet || 135
arkapatrarase pakvaṃ rajanīkalkasaṃyutam
kaṭutailaṃ nihantyetatkacchūpāmāvicarcikāḥ || 136
gaṇḍīrikācitrakamārkavārkakuṣṭhadrumatvaglavaṇaiḥ samūtraiḥ
tailaṃ pacenmaṇḍaladadrukuṣṭhaduṣṭavraṇāruḥkiṭibhāpahāri || 137
pakṣātpakṣādvāmanānyabhyupeyānmāsānmāsātsraṃsanaṃ cāpyadhastāt
tryahāttryahānnastataścāvapīḍānmāseṣvasṛṅmokṣayetṣaṭsu ṣaṭsu || 138

nīcaromanakho'śrānto nityamauṣadhatatparaḥ
yoṣinmāṃsasurāvarjī kuṣṭhī kuṣṭhamapohati || 140
Adhikāra 52
abhyajya kaṭutailena sekaścoṣṇāmbubhistataḥ
tatrā''śu vamanaṃ kāryaṃ paṭolāriṣṭavāriṇā || 1
triphalāpurakṛṣṇābhirvirekaścātra śasyate
triphalāṃ kṣaudrasahitāṃ pibedvā navakārṣikam || 2
visarpoktāmṛtādiṃ tu bhiṣagatrāpi yojayet
pippalīvardhamānaṃ vā laśunaṃ vā prayojayet
sitāṃ madhukasaṃyuktāṃ guḍamāmalakaiḥ saha || 3
saguḍaṃ dīpyakaṃ yastu khādetpathyānnabhuṅnaraḥ
tasya naśyati saptāhādudardaḥ sarvadehajaḥ || 4
siddhārtharajanīkalkaiḥ prapunnāṭatilaiḥ saha
kaṭutailena saṃmiśrametadudvartanaṃ hitam || 5
dūrvāniśāyuto lepaḥ kacchūpāmāvināśanaḥ
kṛmidadrūharaścaiva śītapittaharaḥ smṛtaḥ || 6
kuṣṭhoktaṃ ca kramaṃ kuryādamlapittaghnameva ca
udardoktāṃ kriyāṃ vā'pi koṭharoge samāsataḥ
sarpiḥ pītvā mahātiktaṃ kāryaṃ śoṇitamokṣaṇam || 7
nimbasya patrāṇi sadā ghṛtena dhātrīvimiśrāṇyatha vopayuñjyāt
visphoṭakoṭhaṃ kṣataśītapittakaṇḍvasrapittaṃ rakasāṃ ca jahyāt || 8
kṣārasindhūtthatailaiśca gātrābhyaṅgaṃ prayojayet || 9
kuṣṭhaṃ haridre surasaṃ paṭolaṃ nimbāśvagandhe suradāruśigrū
sasarṣapaṃ tumbarudhānyavanyaṃ caṇḍāṃ ca cūrṇāni samāni kuryāt || 10
taistakrayuktaiḥ prathamaṃ śarīraṃ tailāktamudvartayituṃ yatet
tato'sya kaṇḍūpiṭakāḥ sakoṭhāḥ kuṣṭhāni śophāśca śamaṃ vrajanti || 11
tailodvartanayogena yojya elādiko gaṇaḥ || 12
śuṣkamūlakayūṣeṇa kaulatthena rasena vā
bhojanaṃ sarvadā kāryaṃ lāvatittirijena vā || 13
śītalānyannapānāni buddhvā doṣagatiṃ bhiṣak
uṣṇāni vā yathādoṣaṃ śītapitte prayojayet || 14
Adhikāra 53
vamanānantaraṃ tatra virekaṃ mṛdu kārayet
samyagvāntaviriktasya susnigdhasyānuvāsanam
āsthāpanaṃ cirodbhūte deyaṃ doṣādyapekṣayā || 1
kriyāśuddhasya śamanī anubandhādyapekṣayā
doṣasaṃsargajā kāryā bheṣajāhārakalpanā || 2
ūrdhvagaṃ vamanairdhīmānadhogaṃ recanairharet
tiktabhūyiṣṭhamāhāraṃ pānaṃ cāpi prakalpayet || 3
yavagodhūmavikṛtīstīkṣṇasaṃskāravarjitāḥ
yathāsvaṃ lājasaktūnvā sitāmadhuyutānpibet || 4
nistuṣayavavṛṣadhātrīkvāthastrisugandhamadhuyutaḥ pītaḥ
apanayati cāmlapittaṃ yadi bhuṅkte mudgayūṣeṇa || 5
kaphapittavamīkaṇḍūjvaravisphoṭadāhahā
pācano dīpanaḥ kvāthaḥ śṛṅgaverapaṭolayoḥ || 6
paṭolaṃ nāgaraṃ dhānyaṃ kvāthayitvā jalaṃ pibet
kaṇḍūpāmātiśūlaghnaṃ kaphapittāgnimāndyajit || 7
paṭolanimbāmṛtarohiṇīkṛtaṃ jalaṃ pibetpittakaphocchraye vā
śūlabhramārocakavahnimāndyadāhajvaracchardinivāraṇaṃ hi tat || 8
yavakṛṣṇāpaṭolānāṃ kvāthaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ pibet
nāśayedamlapittaṃ ca aruciṃ ca vamiṃ tathā || 9
vāsāmṛtāparpaṭakanimbabhūnimbamārkavaiḥ
triphalākulakaiḥ kvāthaḥ sakṣaudraścāmlapittahā || 10
phalatrikaṃ paṭolaṃ ca tiktakvāthaḥ sitāyutaḥ
pītaḥ klītakamadhvakto jvaracchardyamlapittahā || 11
vāsānimbapaṭolatriphalāsanayāsayojito jayati
adhikakaphamamlapittaṃ prayojito gugguluḥ kramaśaḥ || 12
chinnākhadirayaṣṭyāhvadārvyambho vā madhudravam || 13
sadrākṣāmabhayāṃ khādetsakṣaudrāṃ saguḍāṃ ca tām
kaṭukāsitā'valehyā paṭolaviśvaṃ ca kṣaudrasaṃyuktam
raktasrutau ca yuktyā khaṇḍaṃ kūṣmāṇḍakaṃ śreṣṭham || 14
paṭolatriphalāriṣṭaśṛtaṃ madhuyutaṃ pibet
pittaśleṣmajvaracchardidāhatvagdoṣaśāntaye || 15
siṃhāsyāmṛtabhaṇṭākīkvāthaṃ pītvā samākṣikam
amlapittaṃ jayejjantuḥ kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ jvaraṃ vamim || 16
vāsāghṛtaṃ tiktaghṛtaṃ ca yuktyā madhūtkaṭāṃ māgadhikāṃ lihedvā
anyadyadevaṃ guṇavacca sarpistadamlapittaṃ śamayatyudīrṇam || 17
ekoṃ'śaḥ pañcanimbānāṃ dviguṇo vṛddhadārakaḥ
sakturdaśaguṇo deyaḥ śarkarāmadhurīkṛtaḥ || 18
śītena vāriṇā pītaṃ śūlaṃ pittakaphodbhavam
nihanti cūrṇaṃ sakṣaudramamlapittaṃ sudustaram || 19
piṣṭvā'jājīṃ sadhānyākāṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
kaphapittāruciharaṃ mandānalavamiṃ jayet || 20
paṭolaśuṇṭhīkalkābhyāṃ kevalaṃ kulakena vā
ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipaktavyaṃ kaphapittaharaṃ param || 21
pippalīkvāthakalkena ghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ madhuplutam
pibennā prātarutthāya amlapittanivṛttaye || 22
drākṣāmṛtāśakrapaṭolapatraiḥ sośīradhātrīghanacandanaiśca || 23
trāyantikāpadmakirātadhānyaiḥ kalkaiḥ pacetsarpirupetamebhiḥ
yuñjīta mātrāṃ saha bhojanena sarvartupāne tu bhiṣagvidadhyāt || 24
vātasthapittaṃ grahaṇīṃ pravṛddhāṃ kāsāgnisādaṃ jvaramamlapittam
sarvaṃ nihanyādghṛtametadāśu samyakprayuktaṃ hyamṛtopamaṃ ca || 25
śatāvarīmūlakalkaṃ ghṛtaprasthaṃ payaḥ samam
pacenmṛdvagninā samyakkṣīraṃ dattvā caturguṇam || 26
nāśayedamlapittaṃ ca vātapittodbhavāngadān
raktapittaṃ tṛṣāṃ mūrchāṃ śvāsaṃ saṃtāpameva ca || 27
amlapitte prayoktavyaḥ kaphapittaharo vidhiḥ
guḍakūṣmāṇḍakaṃ caiva tathā khaṇḍāmalakyapi || 28
guḍakṣīrakaṇāsiddhaṃ sarpiratrāpi yojayet
guḍapippalīpathyābhistulyābhirmodakaḥ kṛtaḥ
pittaśleṣmāpahaḥ prokto mandamagniṃ ca dīpayet || 29
jvalantamiva cā''tmānaṃ manyate yo'mlapittavān
tasya saṃśodhanaṃ pathyaṃ na śāntiḥ śodhanaṃ vinā || 30
acirotthe cirotthe ca vamanaṃ tatra kārayet || 31
Adhikāra 54
virekavamanālepasecanāsṛgvimokṣaṇaiḥ
upācaredyathādoṣaṃ visarpānavidāhibhiḥ || 1
paṭolapicumandābhyāṃ pippalyā madanena ca
visarpe camanaṃ śastaṃ tathaivendrayavaiḥ saha || 2
triphalārasasaṃyuktaṃ sarpistrivṛtayā saha
prayoktavyaṃ virekārthaṃ visarpajvaraśāntaye
rasamāmalakānāṃ vā ghṛtamiśraṃ pradāpayet || 3
tṛṇavarjyaṃ prayoktavyaṃ pañcamūlacatuṣṭayam
pradehasekasarpirbhirvisarpe vātasaṃbhave || 4
kuṣṭhaṃ śatāhvā suradāru mustā vārāhikustumburukṛṣṇagandhāḥ
vāte'rkavaṃśārtagalāśca yojyāḥ sekeṣu lepeṣu tathā ghṛteṣu || 5
prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhāpadmakośīracandanaiḥ
sayaṣṭīndīvaraiḥ pitte kṣīrapiṣṭaiḥ pralepanam || 6
kaśeruśṛṅgāṭakapadmagundrāḥ saśaivalāḥ sotpalakardamāśca
vastrāntarāḥ pittakṛte visarpe lepā vidheyāḥ saghṛtāḥ suśītāḥ || 7
pradehāḥ pariṣekāśca śasyante pañcavalkalaiḥ
padmakośīramadhukacandanairvā praśasyate || 8
paitte tu padminīpaṅkapiṣṭaṃ vā śaṅkhaśaivalam
gundrāmūlaṃ tu śuktirvā gairikaṃ vā ghṛtānvitam || 9
nyagrodhapādā gundrā ca kadalīgarbha eva ca
bisagranthiśca lepaḥ syācchatadhautaghṛtaplutaḥ || 10
hareṇavo masūrāśca mudgāścaiva saśālayaḥ
pṛthakpṛthakpradehāḥ syuḥ sarve vā sarpiṣā saha || 11
drākṣāragvadhakāśmaryatriphalāmaṇḍapīlubhiḥ
trivṛddharītakībhiśca visarpe śodhanaṃ hitam || 12
gāyatrīsaptaparṇābdavāsāragvadhadārubhiḥ
kuṭannaṭairbhavellepo visarpe śleṣmasaṃbhave || 13
ajā'śvagandhā saralā'tha kālā saikeṣikā cāpyatha vā'jaśṛṅgī
gomūtrapiṣṭo vihimaḥ pradeho hanyādvisarpaṃ kaphajaṃ suśīghram || 14
madanaṃ madhukaṃ nimbaṃ vatsakasya phalāni ca
vamanaṃ ca vidhātavyaṃ visarpe kaphasaṃbhave || 15
triphalāpadmakośīrasamaṅgākaravīrajam
nalamūlamanantā ca lepaḥ śleṣmavisarpahā || 16
āragvadhasya patrāṇi tvacaḥ śleṣmātakodbhavāḥ
śirīṣapuṣpaṃ kāmācī hitā lepāvacūrṇitaiḥ || 17
mustāriṣṭapaṭolānāṃ kvāthaḥ sarvavisarpanut
dhātrīpaṭolamudgānāmathavā ghṛtasaṃyutaḥ || 18
amṛtavṛṣapaṭolaṃ nimbakalkairupetaṃ triphalakhadirasāraṃ vyādhighātaṃ ca tulyam
kvathitamidamaśeṣaṃ guggulorbhāgayuktaṃ jayati viṣavisarpaṃ kuṣṭhamaṣṭādaśākhyam || 19
amṛtavṛṣapaṭolaṃ mustakaṃ saptaparṇaṃ khadiramasitavetraṃ nimbapatraṃ haridre
vividhaviṣavisarpānkuṣṭhavisphoṭakaṇḍūrapanayati masūrīṃ śītapittaṃ jvaraṃ ca || 20
Adhikāra 55
paṭolāmṛtabhūnimbavāsakāriṣṭaparpaṭaiḥ
khadirābdayutaiḥ kvātho visphoṭārtijvarāpahaḥ || 1
paṭolatriphalāriṣṭaguḍūcīmustacandanaiḥ
samūrvā rohiṇī pāṭhā rajanī sadurālabhā || 2
kaṣāyaṃ pāyayedetacchleṣmapittajvarāpaham
kaṇḍūtvagdoṣavisphoṭaviṣavīsarpanāśanam || 3
bhūnimbavāsākaṭukāpaṭolaphalatrikācandananimbasiddhaḥ
visarpadāhajvaravaktraśoṣavisphoṭatṛṣṇāvaminutkaṣāyaḥ || 4
sakaphe pittayukte tu triphalāṃ yojayetpuraiḥ
durālabhāṃ parpaṭakaṃ paṭolaṃ kaṭukāṃ tathā
soṣṇaṃ guggulusaṃmiśraṃ pibedvā khadirāṣṭakam || 5
kuṇḍalīpicumandāmbu khadirendrayavāmbu vā
visphoṭānnāśayatyāśu vāyurjaladharāniva || 6
candanaṃ nāgapuṣpaṃ ca taṇḍulīyakasārivā
śirīṣavalkalaṃ jātī lepaḥ syāddāhanāśanaḥ || 7
śukatarunatamāṃsīrajanīpadmaṃ ca tulyāni
piṣṭāni śītatoyena syāllepaḥ sarvavisphoṭe || 8
śirīṣamūlamañjiṣṭhācavyāmalakayaṣṭikāḥ
sajātīpallavakṣaudrā visphoṭe kavalagrahāḥ || 9
śirīṣodumbarau jambūḥ sekālepanayorhitāḥ
śleṣmātakatvaco vā'pi pralepāścotane hitāḥ || 10
śirīṣayaṣṭīnatacandanailāmāṃsīharidrādvayakuṣṭhavālaiḥ
lepo daśāṅgaḥ saghṛtaḥ pradiṣṭo visarpakuṣṭhagrahaśoṣahārī || 11
śirīṣośīranāgāṅkahiṃsrābharlepanāddrutam
visarpaviṣavisphoṭāḥ praśāmyanti na saṃśayaḥ || 12
vṛṣakhadirapaṭolapatranimbatvagamṛtāmalakīkaṣāyakalkaiḥ
ghṛtamabhinavametadāśu pakvaṃ jayati visarpamadāsrakuṣṭhagulmān || 13
paṭolasaptacchadanimbavāsāphalatrikacchinnaruhāvipakvam
tatpañcatiktaṃ ghṛtamāśu hanti tridoṣavisphoṭavisarpakaṇḍūḥ || 14
śākhāsu kupito doṣaḥ śothaṃ kṛtvā visarpavat
bhittvaivaṃ taṃ kṣate tatra soṣmā māṃsaṃ viśoṣya ca || 15
kuryāttantunibhaṃ sūtraṃ tatpiṇḍaistakrasaktujaiḥ
śanaiḥ śanaiḥ kṣatādyāti cchedātkopaṃ samāvahet || 16
tatpātācchophaśāntiḥ syātpunaḥ sthānāntare bhavet
sa snāyuka iti khyātaḥ kriyoktā tu visarpavat || 17
gavyaṃ sarpistryahaṃ pītvā nirguṇḍīsvarasaṃ tryaham
pītvā snāyukamatyugraṃ hantyavaśyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 18
saubhāñjanamūladalaiḥ kāñjikapiṣṭairlavaṇayutairleṣaḥ
hanti snāyukarogaṃ yadvā mocatvaco lepaḥ || 19
Adhikāra 56
sarvāsāṃ vamanaṃ pūrvaṃ paṭolāriṣṭavāsakaiḥ
kaṣāyaiśca vacāvatsayaṣṭyāhvaphalakalkitaiḥ || 1
sakṣaudraṃ pāyayedbrāhmyā rasaṃ vā hailamocikam
vāntasya recanaṃ deyaṃ śamanaṃ cābale nare || 2
suṣavīpatraniryāsaṃ haridrācūrṇasaṃyutam
romāntī jvaravisphoṭamasūrī śāntaye pibet || 3
ubhābhyāṃ hṛtadoṣasya viśudhyanti masūrikāḥ
nirvikārāścālpapūyāḥ pacyante cālpavedanāḥ || 4
kaṇṭākusvādumūlaṃ kvathanavidhikṛtaṃ hiṅgumāṣaikayuktaṃ
pītaṃ bījaṃ jayāyāḥ saghṛtamuṣitavāḥ pītamaṅghriḥ śakaṭyāḥ
ma(pa)thyāmūlaṃ śiphā vā damanakusumajā soṣaṇā vā'tha pūti-
ryogā vāśāmbunaite prathamamaghagade dṛśyamāne prayojyāḥ || 5
ugrājyavaṃśanīlīyavaviṣakārpāsikīkasabrāhmī--
surasamayūrakalākṣādhūpo romāntikādiharaḥ || 6
ādāveva prayuktasya praśāmyanti masūrikāḥ
na gṛhṇanti viṣaṃ kecidyathālābhaśruteriha || 7
caitrāsitabhūtadine raktapatākānvitā snuhī bhavane
dhavalitakalaśanyastā pāparujaṃ dūrato dhatte || 8
tarpaṇaṃ vātajāyāṃ prāglājacūrṇaiḥ saśarkaraiḥ
bhojanaṃ tiktayūṣaistu pratudānāṃ rasena vā || 9
dvipañcamūlaṃ rāsnā ca dārvyuśīraṃ durālabhā
sāmṛtaṃ dhānyakaṃ mustaṃ jayedvātasamutthitām || 10
drākṣākāśmaryakharjūrapaṭolāriṣṭavāsakaiḥ
lājāmalakadusparśaiḥ sitāyuktastu paittike || 11
śirīṣodumbarāśvatthaśelunyagrodhavatsakaiḥ
pralepaḥ saghṛtaḥ śīghraṃ vraṇavīsarpadāhahā || 12
durālabhāṃ parpaṭakaṃ bhūnimbaṃ kaṭurohiṇīm
ślaiṣmikyāṃ pittajāyāṃ vā pāne niṣkvāthya dāpayet || 13
nimbaṃ parpaṭakaṃ pāṭhāṃ paṭolaṃ kaṭurohiṇīm
vāsāṃ durālabhāṃ dhātrīmuśīraṃ candanadvayam || 14
eṣa nimbādikaḥ kvāthaḥ pītaḥ śarkarayā'nvitaḥ
tridoṣāṃ masūrīṃ hanti jvaravīsarpasaṃbhavām
utthitā praviśedyā tu punastāṃ bāhyato nayet || 15
paṭolakuṇḍalīmustavṛṣadhanvayavāsakaiḥ
bhūnimbanimbakaṭukāparpaṭaiśca śṛtaṃ jalam || 16
masūrīṃ śamayedāmāṃ pakvāṃ caiva viśodhayet
nātaḥ parataraṃ kiṃcidvisphoṭajvaraśāntaye || 17
paṭolamūlāruṇataṇḍulīyakaṃ pibeddharidrāmalakalkasaṃyutam
masūrivisphoṭavidāhaśāntaye tathaiva romāntivamijvarāpaham || 18
paṭolamūlāruṇataṇḍulīyakaṃ tathaiva dhātrīkhadireṇa saṃyutam
pibejjalaṃ sukvathitaṃ suśītalaṃ masūrikārogavināśanaṃ param || 19
khadiratriphalāriṣṭapaṭolāmṛtavāsakaiḥ || 20
kvātho'ṣṭakākhyo jayati romāntikamasūrikāḥ
kuṣṭhavīsarpavisphoṭakaṇḍvādīnapi pānataḥ || 21
amṛtādikaṣāyastu jayetpittakaphātmikām || 22
sauvīreṇa tu saṃpiṣṭaṃ mātuluṅgasya kesaram
pralepātpācayatyāśu dāhaṃ cāṃpi niyacchati || 23
pādadāhaṃ ca kurute piṭikā pādasaṃbhavā
tatra sekaṃ praśaṃsanti bahuśastaṇḍulāmbunā || 24
pākakāle tu sarvāstā viśoṣayati mārutaḥ
tasmātsaṃbṛṃhaṇaṃ kāryaṃ na tu pathyaṃ viśoṣaṇam || 25
guḍūcī madhukaṃ drākṣā moraṭaṃ dāḍimaiḥ saha
pākakāle pradātavyaṃ bhaiṣajyaṃ guḍasaṃyutam
tena pākaṃ vrajantyāśu na tu vāyuḥ prakupyati || 26
lihyādvā bādaraṃ cūrṇaṃ pācanārthaṃ guḍena vā
anenā''śu vipacyante vātapittakaphātmikāḥ || 27
śūlādhmānaparītasya kampamānasya vāyunā
dhanvamāṃsarasāḥ śastā īṣatsaindhavasaṃyutāḥ || 28
dāḍimāmlarasairyuktā yūṣāḥ syurarucau hitāḥ
visarpantī savisphoṭā klinnā srāvavatī tathā
daśāṅgalepacūrṇena cūrṇitā śamameti ca || 29
pibedambhastaptaśītaṃ bhāvitaṃ khadirāsanaiḥ
śauce vāri prayuñjīta gāyatrībahuvārajam || 30
jātīpatraṃ samañjiṣṭhaṃ dārvī pūgaphalaṃ śamī || 31
dhātrīphalaṃ samadhukaṃ kvathitaṃ madhusaṃyutam
mukharoge kaṇṭharoge gaṇḍūṣārthaṃ praśasyate || 32
akṣṇoḥ sekaṃ praśaṃsanti gavedhumadhukāmbunā
madhukaṃ triphalā mūrvā dārvītvaṅnīlamutpalam || 33
uśīralodhramañjiṣṭhāḥ pralepāścotane hitāḥ
naśyantyanena dṛgjātā masūryo na bhavanti ca || 34
pañcavalkalacūrṇena kledinīmavacūrṇayet
bhasmanā kecidicchanti kecigdomayareṇunā || 35
kṛmipātabhayāccāpi dhūpayetsaralādibhiḥ
vedanādāhaśāntyarthaṃ srutānāṃ ca viśuddhaye
sagugguluṃ varākvāthaṃ yuñjyādvā khadirāṣṭakam || 36
kṛṣṇābhayārajo lihyānmadhunā kaṇṭhaśuddhaye
tathā'ṣṭāṅgābaleho vā kabalaścā''rdrakādibhiḥ || 37
pañcatiktaṃ prayuñjīta pānābhyañjanabhojanaiḥ
kuryādvraṇavidhānaṃ ca tailādīnvarjayecciram || 38
nimbātimuktakāsphotabimbīvetasavalkalam
śṛtaśītaṃ prayoktavyaṃ srāvaprakṣālane sadā || 39
niśādvayośīraśirīṣamustakaiḥ salodhrabhadraśriyanāgakesaraiḥ
saṃsvedavisphoṭavisarpakuṣṭhadaurgandhyaromāntiharaḥ pradehaḥ || 40
Adhikāra 57
tathā'jagallikāmāmāṃ jalaukobhirupācaret
śuktisaurāṣṭrikākṣārakalkaiścā''lepayenmuhuḥ || 1
śleṣmavidradhikalpena jayeccānuśayāṃ bhiṣak
vivṛtāmindravṛddhāṃ ca gardabhāṃ jālagardabhām
irivellāṃ gandhanāmāṃ jayetpittavisarpavat || 2
madhurauṣadhasiddhena sarpiṣā śamayedvraṇam || 3
raktāvasekairbahubhiḥ svedanairapatarpaṇaiḥ
jayedvidārikāṃ lepaiḥ śigrudevadrumodbhavaiḥ || 4
panasikāṃ kacchapikāmanena vidhinā bhiṣak
sādhayetkaṭhinānanyāñśophāndoṣasamudbhavān || 5
andhrālajīṃ kacchapikāṃ tathā pāṣāṇagardabham
suradāruśilākuṣṭhaiḥ svedayitvā pralepayet || 6
kaphamārutaśothaghno lepaḥ pāṣāṇagardabhe
lepanaṃ pāṇḍuraṅgeṇa śoṇitasya ca mokṣaṇam || 7
śastreṇotkṛtya valmīkaṃ kṣārāgnibhyāṃ prasādhayet
manaḥśilālabhallātasūkṣmailāgurucandanaiḥ || 8
jātīpallavavakraiśca nimbatailaṃ vipācayet
valmīkaṃ nāśayettaddhi bahucchidraṃ bahusravam || 9
pādadārīṣu tu śirāṃ vyadhayettalaśodhanīm
snehasvedopapannau tu pādau cā''lepayenmuhuḥ
madhūcchiṣṭavasāmajjaghṛtakṣīrairvimiśritaiḥ || 10
sarjāhvasindhūdbhavayoścūrṇaṃ madhughṛtānvitam
nirmathya kaṭutailāktaṃ hitaṃ pādapramārjanam || 11
upodakāsarṣapanimbamocākarkārukairvārukabhasmatoye
tailaṃ vipakvaṃ lavaṇāṃśayuktaṃ tatpādadārīṃ vinihanti lepāt || 12
alase'mlaiściraṃ siktau caraṇau parilepayet
paṭolāriṣṭakāsīsatriphalābhirmuhurmuhuḥ || 13
karañjabījaṃ rajanī kāsīsaṃ madhukaṃ madhu
rocanā haritālaṃ ca lepo'yamalase hitaḥ || 14
lākṣābhayārasālepaḥ kāryaṃ vā raktamokṣaṇam || 15
bṛhatīrasasiddhena tailenābhyajya buddhimān
śilārocanakāsīsacūrṇairvā pratisārayet || 16
dahetkadaramuddhṛtya tailena dahanena vā
cipyamuṣṇāmbunā svinnamuddhṛtyābhyañjanaṃ vraṇe
dattvā sārjarasaṃ cūrṇaṃ baddhvā vraṇavadācaret || 17
svarasena haridrāyāḥ pātre kṛṣṇāyase'bhayām
ghṛṣṭvā tajjena kalkena limpeccipyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 18
nimbodakena vamanaṃ padminīkaṇṭake hitam || 19
nimbodakakṛtaṃ sarpiḥ sakṣaudraṃ pānamiṣyate
nimbāragvadhakalkairvā muhurudvartanaṃ hitam || 20
ahipūtanake dhātryāḥ pūrvaṃ stanyaṃ viśodhayet
triphalākhadirakvāthairvraṇānāṃ dhāvanaṃ hitam || 21
karañjatriphalātiktaiḥ sarpiḥ siddhaṃ śiśorhitam
rasāñjanaṃ viśeṣeṇa pānālepanayorhitam || 22
gudabhraṃśe gudaṃ snehairabhyajyā''śu praveśayet
praviṣṭaṃ svedayeccāpi baddhaṃ goḥphaṇayā bhṛśam || 23
komalaṃ padminīpatraṃ yaḥ khādeccharkarāyutam
etanniścitya nirdiṣṭaṃ na tasya gudanirgamaḥ || 24
vṛkṣāmlānalacāṅgerīviśvapāṭhāyavāgrajam
takreṇa śīlayetpāyubhraṃśārto'naladīpanam || 25
gudaṃ ca gavyavasayā mrakṣayedaviśaṅkitaḥ
duṣpraveśo gudabhraṃśo viśatyāśu na saṃśayaḥ || 26
mūṣikāṇāṃ vasābhirvā gude samyakpralepanam
susvinnamūṣikāmāṃsenāthavā svedayedgudam || 27
cāṅgerīkoladadhyamlanāgarakṣārasaṃyutam
ghṛtamutkvathitaṃ peyaṃ gudabhraṃśarujāpaham || 28
kṣīre mahatpañcamūlaṃ mūṣikāmantravarjitām
paktvā tasminpacettailaṃ vātaghnauṣadhasaṃyutam
gudabhraṃśamidaṃ tailaṃ pānābhyaṅgātprasādhayet || 29
svedopanāhau parivartikāyāṃ kṛtvā samabhyajya ghṛtena paścāt
praveśayeccarma śanaiḥ praviṣṭe māṃsaiḥ sukhoṣṇairupanāhayecca || 30
snehasvedaistathaivaināṃ cikitsedavapāṭikām || 31
carmakīlāñjatumaṇīnmaśakāṃstilakālakān
utkṛtya śastreṇa dahetkṣārāgnibhyāmaśeṣataḥ || 32
yauvanapiṭakānyacchanīlikāvyaṅgaśarkarāḥ
śirāvedhaiḥ pralepaiśca jayedabhyañjanaistathā || 33
lodhradhānyavacālepastāruṇyapiṭikāpahaḥ
tadvadgorocanāyuktaṃ maricaṃ mukhalepanāt || 34
siddhārthakavacālodhrasaindhavaiśca pralepanam
vamanaṃ ca nihantyāśu piṭakānyauvanodbhavān || 35
vyaṅgeṣu cārjunatvagvā mañjiṣṭhā vā samākṣikā
lepaḥ sanavanīto vā śvetāśvakhurajā maṣī || 36
raktacandanamañjiṣṭhākuṣṭhalodhrapriyaṃgavaḥ
vaṭāṅkurā masūrāśca vyaṅgaghnā mukhakāntidāḥ || 37
vyaṅgānāṃ lepanaṃ śastaṃ rudhireṇa śaśasya vā
kevalānpayasā piṣṭvā tīkṣṇāñśālmalikaṇṭakān
āliptaṃ tryahametena bhavetpadmopamaṃ mukham || 38
masūraiḥ sarpiṣā piṣṭairliptamāsyaṃ payonvitaiḥ
saptarātreṇa bhavati puṇḍarīkadalaprabham || 39
mātuluṅgajaṭā sarpiḥ śilā gośakṛto rasaḥ
mukhakāntikaro lepaḥ piṭikātilakālajit || 40
kālīyakotpalāmayadadhisarabadarāsthimadhyaphalinībhiḥ
liptaṃ bhavati hi vadanaṃ śaśiprabhaṃ saptarātreṇa || 41
rakṣoghnaśarvarīdvayamañjiṣṭhāgairikājyabastapayaḥ
siddhena liptamānanamudyaccharadindubimbavadbhāti || 42
pariṇatadadhiśarapuṅkhaiḥ kuvalayadalakuṣṭhacandanośīraiḥ
mukhakamalakāntikarairbhṛkuṭītilakālakāñjayati || 43
haridrādvayayaṣṭyāhvakālīyakakucandanaiḥ
prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhāpadmapadmakakuṅkumaiḥ || 44
kapitthatindukaplakṣavaṭapatraiḥ payonvitaiḥ
lepayetkalkitairebhistailaṃ vā'bhyañjanaṃ pacet || 45
piplavaṃ nīlikāṃ vyaṅgaṃ tilakānmukhadūṣakān
nityasevī jayetkṣipraṃ mukhaṃ kuryānmanoharam || 46
madhukasya kaṣāyeṇa tailasya kuḍavaṃ pacet
kalkaiḥ priyaṃgumañjiṣṭhācandanotpalakesaraiḥ || 47
kanakaṃ nāmatastailaṃ mukhakāntikaraṃ param
abhīlunīlikāvyaṅgaśodhanaṃ paramārcitam || 48
mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ lākṣā mātuluṅgaṃ sayaṣṭikam
karṣapramāṇairetaistu tailasya kuḍavaṃ tathā || 49
ājaṃ payastu dviguṇaṃ śanairmṛdvagninā pacet
nīlikāpiṭikāvyaṅgānabhyaṅgādeva nāśayet || 50
mukhaṃ prasannopacitaṃ nīlīkarkaśavarjitam
saptarātraprayogeṇa bhavetkanakasaṃnibham || 51
kuṅkumaṃ candanaṃ lākṣā mañjiṣṭhā madhuyaṣṭikā
kālīyakamuśīraṃ ca padmakaṃ nīlamutpalam || 52
nyagrodhapādāḥ plakṣasya śuṅgāḥ padmasya kesaram
dvipañcamūlasahitaiḥ kaṣāyaiḥ pālikaiḥ pṛthak || 53
jalāḍhakaṃ vipaktavyaṃ pādaśeṣamathoddharet
mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ lākṣā patta(trā)ṅgaṃ madhuyaṣṭikā || 54
karṣapramāṇairetaistu tailasya kuḍavaṃ pacet
ajākṣīraṃ taddviguṇaṃ śanairmṛdvagninā pacet || 55
samyakpakvaṃ paraṃ hyetanmukhavarṇaprasādanam
nīlikāpiṭikāvyaṅgānabhyaṅgādeva nāśayet || 56
saptarātreṇa vadanaṃ bhavetkamalasaṃnibham
kuṅkumādyamidaṃ tailaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vaidehanirmitam || 57
arūṃṣikāyāṃ rudhire'vasikte śirāvyadhenātha jalaukasā vā
nimbāmbusikte śirasi pralepo deyo'śvavarcorasasaindhavābhyām || 58
purāṇamatha piṇyākaṃ purīṣaṃ kukkuṭasya ca
mūtrapiṣṭaḥ pralepo'yaṃ śīghraṃ hanyādarūṃṣikām || 59
haridrādvayabhūnimbatriphalāriṣṭacandanaiḥ
etattailamarūṃṣīṇāṃ siddhamabhyañjane hitam || 60
dāruṇe tu śirāṃ vidhyetsnigdhasvinnāṃ lalāṭajām
avapīḍaśirobastīnabhyaṅgāṃścāvacārayet || 61
kodravāṇāṃ tṛṇakṣārapānīyaṃ paridhāvane || 62
priyālabījamadhukakuṣṭhamiśraiḥ sasaindhavaiḥ
kāryo dāruṇake mūrdhni pralepo madhusaṃyutaḥ || 63
mālatīkaravīrāgninaktamālairvipācitam
tailamabhyañjane śastamindraluptāpahaṃ param
idaṃ hi tvaritaṃ hanti dāruṇaṃ niyataṃ nṛṇām || 64
snuhīpayaḥ payo'rkasya mārkavo lāṅgalīviṣam
mūtramājaṃ sagomūtraṃ raktikā sendravāruṇī || 65
siddhārthatīkṣṇatailaṃ ca garbhaṃ dattvā vipācayet
vahninā mṛdunā pakvaṃ tailaṃ khālityanāśanam || 66
kūrmapṛṣṭhasamānā'pi rūhā yā romataskarī
digdhā sā'nena jāyeta ṛkṣaśāvīva lomaśā || 67
triphalāyorajomāṃsīmārkavotpalasārivaiḥ
sasaindhavaiḥ pacettailamabhyaṅgo'rūṃṣikāṃ jayet || 68
citrakaṃ dantimūlaṃ ca kośātakisamanvitam
kalkaṃ piṣṭvā pacettailaṃ keśaśatruvināśanam || 69
guñjāphalaiḥ śṛtaṃ tailaṃ bhṛṅgarājarasena tu
kaṇḍūdāruṇahṛtkaṣṭakapālavyādhināśanam || 70
bhṛṅgarajastriphalotpalasārilohapurīṣasamanvitakāri
tailamidaṃ paca dāruṇahāri kuñcitakeśaghanasthirakāri || 71
prapauṇḍarīkamadhukapippalīcandanotpalaiḥ
kārṣikaistailakuḍavaistaddvirāmalakīrasaḥ || 72
sādhyaḥ sa pratimarśaḥ syātsarvaśīrṣagadāpahaḥ || 73
indralupte śirāṃ viddhvā śilākāsīsatutthakaiḥ
lepayetparitaḥ kalkaistailaṃ cābhyañjane hitam
kuṭannaṭaśikhījātīkarañjakaravīrakaiḥ || 74
avagāḍhapadaṃ vā'pi prasthayitvā punaḥ punaḥ
guñjāmūlaiściraṃ limpetkeśabhūmiṃ samantataḥ || 75
indraluptāpaho lepo madhunā bṛhatīrasaḥ || 76
bṛhatīphalarasapiṣṭaṃ guñjāphalamūlamindraluptasya
kanakavighṛṣṭasya sato dātavyaṃ prasthitasya sadā || 77
madhukendīvaramūrvātilājyagokṣīrabhṛṅgalepena
acirādbhavanti keśā ghanadṛḍhamūlāyatā ṛjavaḥ || 78
vaṭāvarohakeśinyoścūrṇenā''dityapācitam
guḍūcīsvarase tailamabhyaṅgātkeśarohaṇam || 79
candanaṃ madhukaṃ mūrvā triphalā nīlamutpalam
kāntā vaṭāvarohaśca guḍūcī bisameva ca || 80
lohacūrṇaṃ tathā keśī sārive dve tathaiva ca
mārkavasvarasenaiva tailaṃ mṛdvagninā pacet || 81
śirasyutpatitāḥ keśā jāyante ghanakuñcitāḥ
dṛḍhamūlāśca snigdhāśca tathā bhramarasaṃnibhāḥ
nasyenākālapalitaṃ nihanyāttailamuttamam || 82
tailaṃ sayaṣṭīmadhukaiḥ kṣīre dhātrīphalaiḥ śṛtam
nasye dattaṃ janayati keśāñśmaśrūṇi vā'pyatha || 83
hastidantamaṣīṃ kṛtvā mukhyaṃ caiva rasāñjanam
romāṇyanena jāyante lepātpāṇitaleṣvapi || 84
triphalā nīlinīpatraṃ lohabhṛṅgarajaḥ samam
avimūtreṇa saṃyuktaṃ kṛṣṇīkaraṇamuttamam || 85
triphalācūrṇasaṃyuktaṃ lohacūrṇaṃ vinikṣipet || 86
īṣatpakve nālikere bhṛṅgarājarasānvite
māsamekaṃ vinikṣipya samyaggarbhāttaduddharet || 87
tataḥ śiro muṇḍayitvā lepaṃ dattvā bhiṣagvaraḥ
saṃveṣṭya kadalīpatrairmocayetsaptame dine || 88
kṣālayettriphalākvāthaiḥ kṣīramāṃsarasāśinaḥ
kapālarañjanaṃ caitatkṛṣṇīkaraṇamuttamam || 89
utpalaṃ payasā sārdhaṃ māsaṃ bhūmau nidhāpayet
keśānāṃ kṛṣṇakaraṇaṃ snehanaṃ ca vidhīyate || 90
lohamalāmalakalkaiḥ sajapāpuṣpairnaraḥ sadāsnāyī
palitānīha na paśyati gaṅgāsnāyīva narakāṇi || 91
nimbasya bījāni vibhāvitāni bhṛṅgasya toyena tathā'sanasya
tailaṃ tu teṣāṃ vinihanti nasyaṃ dugdhānnabhoktuḥ palitaṃ samūlam || 92
nimbasya tailaṃ prakṛtisthameva nasyaṃ vidheyaṃ vidhinā yathāvat
māsena tu kṣīrabhujo narasya jarāgradūtaṃ palitaṃ nihanti || 93
kṣīrātsamārkavarasāddve prasthe madhukātpale
tailasya kuḍavaṃ pakvaṃ tannasyaṃ palitāpaham || 94
kāsīsarocanātutthaharitālarasāñjanaiḥ
amlapiṣṭaiḥ pralepo'yaṃ muṣkakacchvahipūtayoḥ || 95
paṭolapatratriphalārasāñjanavipācitam
pītaṃ ghṛtaṃ nāśayati kṛcchrāmapyahipūtanām || 96
rajanīmārkavamūlaṃ piṣṭaṃ śītena vāriṇā tulyam
hanti visarpaṃ lepādvarāhadaśanāhvayaṃ ghoram || 97
Adhikāra 58
oṣṭhaprakope vātotthe sālvaṇenopanāhanam || 1
mastiṣke caiva nasye ca tailaṃ vātaharaiḥ śṛtam
svedo'bhyaṅgaḥ snehapānaṃ rasapānamapīṣyate || 2
śrīveṣṭakaṃ sarjarasaṃ gugguluṃ suradāru ca
yaṣṭīmadhukacūrṇaṃ ca vidadhyātpratisāraṇe || 3
vedhaṃ śirāṇāṃ vamanaṃ virekaṃ tiktasya pānaṃ rasabhojanaṃ ca
śītānpralepānpariṣecanaṃ ca pittopasṛṣṭeṣvadhareṣu kuryāt || 4
pittaraktābhighātotthaṃ jalaukobhirupācaret
pittavidradhivaccāpi kriyāṃ kuryādaśeṣataḥ || 5
śirovirecanaṃ dhūmaḥ svedaḥ kavaladhāraṇam
hṛte rakte prayoktavyamoṣṭhakope kaphātmake || 6
trikaṭu svarjikākṣāraḥ kṣāraśca yavaśūkajaḥ
kṣaudrayuktaṃ vidhātavyametacca pratisāraṇam || 7
medoje svedite bhinne śodhite jvalano hitaḥ
priyaṃgutriphalālodhraṃ sakṣaudraṃ pratisāraṇam
hitaṃ ca triphalācūrṇaṃ madhuyuktaṃ pralepanam || 8
śītāde hṛtarakte tu toye nāgarasarṣapān
niṣkvāthya triphalāṃ cāpi kuryādgaṇḍūṣadhāraṇam
priyaṃgavaśca mustaṃ ca triphalā ca pralepanam || 9
kuṣṭhaṃ dārvī lodhramabdaṃ samaṅgā pāṭhā tiktā tejinī pītikā ca
cūrṇaṃ śastaṃ gharṣaṇena dvijānāṃ raktāsrāvaṃ hanti kaṇḍūrujaṃ ca || 10
caladdantasthitikaraṃ kāryaṃ bakulacarvaṇam || 11
dantapuppuṭake kāryaṃ taruṇe raktamokṣaṇam
sapañcalavaṇakṣāraḥ sakṣaudraḥ pratisāraṇam || 12
dantānāṃ todaharṣau ca jāyete vātatastayoḥ
uṣṇatailājyavātaghnaniryūhāḥ kavalagrahāḥ || 13
mākṣikaṃ pippalīsarpirmiśritaṃ dhārayenmukhe
dantaśūlaharaṃ proktaṃ pradhānamidamauṣadham || 14
visrāvite dantaveṣṭe vraṇaṃ tu pratisārayet
lodhrapatta(trā)ṅgamadhukalākṣācūrṇairmadhūttaraiḥ || 15
gaṇḍūṣe kṣīriṇo yojyāḥ sakṣaudraghṛtaśarkarāḥ
sauṣire hṛtarakte tu lodhramustarasāñjanaiḥ
sakṣaudraiḥ śasyate lepo gaṇḍūṣe kṣīriṇo hitāḥ || 16
kriyāṃ paridare kuryācchītādoktāṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 17
saṃśodhyobhayataḥ kāyaṃ śiraścopakuśe tataḥ
kākodumbarikāgojīpatrairvisrāvayedbhiṣak
kṣaudrayuktaiśca lavaṇaiḥ savyoṣaiḥ pratisārayet || 18
pippalyaḥ sarṣapāḥ śvetā nāgaraṃ naiculaṃ phalam
sukhodakena saṃsṛjya kavalaṃ tasya yojayet || 19
śastreṇa dantavaidarbhe dantamūlāni śodhayet
tataḥ kṣāraṃ prayuñjīta kriyāḥ sarvāśca śītalāḥ || 20
uddhṛtyādhikadantaṃ tu tato'gnimavacārayet
kṛmidantakavaccātra vidhiḥ kāryo vijānatā || 21
chittvā'dhimāṃsaṃ sakṣaudrairetaiścūrṇairupācaret
pāṭhāvacātejavatīsvarjikāyāvaśūkajaiḥ || 22
kṣaudradvitīyāḥ pippalyaḥ kavalaścātra kīrtitaḥ
paṭolanimbatriphalākaṣāyaścātra dhāvane || 23
śirovirekaśca hito dhūmo vairecanaśca yaḥ
nāḍīvraṇaharaṃ karma dantanāḍīṣu kārayet || 24
yaṃ dantamabhi jāyeta nāḍī dantaṃ tamuddharet
chittvā māṃsaṃ tu śastreṇa yadi noparijo bhavet || 25
śodhayitvā daheccāpi kṣāreṇa jvalanena vā || 26
gatirhinasti hanvasthi daśane samupekṣite
tasmātsamūlaṃ daśanamuddharedbhagnamasthi ca || 27
uddhṛte tūttare dante śoṇitaṃ saṃprasicyate
raktātiyogātpūrvoktā ghorā rogā bhavanti ca
calamapyuttaraṃ dantamato nāpaharedbhiṣak || 28
kaṣāyaṃ jātimadanakaṭukasvādukaṇṭakaiḥ || 29
lodhrakhadiramañjiṣṭhāyaṣṭyāhvaiścāpi yatkṛtam
tailaṃ saṃśodhanaṃ taddhi hanti dantagatāṃ gatim || 30
kaṣāyaṃ parataḥ kṛtvā dṛṣṭvā lodhrādi kalkitam
kaṇṭakī madano yojyaḥ svādukaṇṭā vikaṅkataḥ || 31
sukhoṣṇāḥ snehakavalāḥ sarpiṣastraivṛtasya vā
niryūhāścānilaghnānāṃ dantaharṣapramardanāḥ
snaihikaśca hito dhūmo nasyaṃ snaihikameva ca || 32
ahiṃsandantamūlāni śarkarāmuddharedbhiṣak
lākṣācūrṇairmadhuyutaistatastaṃ pratisārayet || 33
dantaharṣakriyāṃ cāpi kuryānniravaśeṣataḥ
kapālikā kṛcchrasādhyā tatrāpyeṣā kriyā hitā || 34
jayedvisrāvaṇaiḥ svinnamacalaṃ kṛmidantakam || 35
tathā'vapīḍairvātaghnaiḥ snehagaṇḍūṣadhāraṇaiḥ
bhadradārvādivarṣābhūlapaiḥ snigdhaiśca bhojanaiḥ || 36
hiṅgu soṣṇaṃ tu matimānkṛmidante pradāpayet || 37
bṛhatībhūmikadambakapañcāṅgulakaṇṭakārikākvāthaḥ
gaṇḍūṣastailayutaḥ kṛmidantakavedanopaśamaḥ || 38
calamuddhṛtya vā sthānaṃ dahettu suṣirasya vā || 39
tato vidārīyaṣṭyāhvaśṛṅgāṭakakaserubhiḥ
tailaṃ daśaguṇakṣīraṃ siddhaṃ nasye tu pūjitam || 40
hanumokṣe samuddiṣṭāṃ kuryādarditavatkriyām
phalānyamlāni śītāmbu rūkṣānnaṃ dantadhāvanam
tathā'tikaṭhinānbhakṣyāndantarogī vivarjayet || 41
oṣṭhaprakope'nilaje yaduktaṃ prākcikitsitam
kaṇṭakeṣvanilottheṣu tatkāryaṃ bhiṣajā khalu || 42
pittajeṣu nighṛṣṭeṣu niḥsrute duṣṭaśoṇite
pratisāraṇagaṇḍūṣanasyaṃ ca madhuraṃ hitam || 43
kaṇṭakeṣu kaphottheṣu likhiteṣvasṛjaḥ kṣaye
pippalyādirmadhuyutaḥ kāryaṃ tu pratisāraṇam || 44
gṛhṇīyātkavalaṃ vā'pi gaurasarṣapasaindhavaiḥ
paṭolanimbavārtākakṣārayūṣaiśca bhojayet || 45
upajihvāṃ tu saṃlikhya kṣāreṇa pratisārayet
śirovirekagaṇḍūṣadhūmaiścaināmupācaret || 46
vyoṣakṣārābhayāvahnicūrṇametatpragharṣaṇam
upajihvopaśāntyarthamebhistailaṃ vipācayet || 47
tālumūle kaphāsṛgbhyāṃ jāyate galaśuṇḍikā
chittvā tāṃ vyoṣasindhūtthogrākṣaudraiḥ pratisārayet || 48
galaśuṇḍī kṣayaṃ yāti vajrīkṣīreṇa lepanāt
kuṣṭhāruṇāvacāsindhukṛṣṇāpāṭhāplavairapi
sakṣaudrairbhiṣajā kāryaṃ galaśuṇḍyāṃ pragharṣaṇam || 49
vacāmativiṣāṃ pāṭhāṃ rāsnāṃ kaṭukarohiṇīm
niṣkvāthya picumandaṃ ca kevalaṃ tatra yojayet || 50
kṣārasiddheṣu mudgeṣu yūṣaścāpyaśane hitaḥ
tuṇḍikeryatdhruṣe(?) kūrme saṃghāte tālupuppuṭe
eṣa eva vidhiḥ kāryo viśeṣaḥ śastrakarmaṇi || 51
tālupāke tu kartavyaṃ vidhānaṃ pittanāśanam
snehasvedau tāluśoṣe vidhiścānilanāśanaḥ || 52
sādhyānāṃ rohiṇīnāṃ tu hitaṃ śoṇitamokṣaṇam
chardanaṃ dhūmapānaṃ ca gaṇḍūṣā nasyakarma ca || 53
vātikīṃ tu gate rakte lavaṇaiḥ pratisārayet || 54
sukhoṣṇāṃstailakavalāndhārayeccāpyabhīkṣṇaśaḥ
patta(trā)ṅgaśarkarākṣaudraiḥ paittikīṃ pratisārayet || 55
drākṣāparūṣakakvātho hitaśca kavalagrahaḥ
āgāradhūmakaṭukaiḥ kaphajāṃ pratisārayet || 56
śvetāviḍaṅgadantīṣu tailaṃ siddhaṃ sasaindhavam
nasyakarmaṇi dātavyaṃ kavalaśca kaphocchraye || 57
pittavatsādhayedvaidyo rohiṇīṃ raktasaṃbhavām
visrāvya kaṇṭhaśālūkaṃ sādhayettuṇḍikerivat || 58
ekakālaṃ yavānnaṃ ca bhuñjīta snigdhamalpaśaḥ
upajihvakavaccāpi sādhayedadhijihvikām || 59
unnamya jihvāmākṛṣya baḍiśenādhijihvikām
chedayenmaṇḍalāgreṇa tīkṣṇoṣṇairgharṣaṇādibhiḥ
ekavṛndaṃ tu visrāvya vidhiśodhanamācaret || 60
gilāyuścāpi yo vyādhistaṃ ca śastreṇa sādhayet
amarmasthaṃ supakvaṃ ca bhedayedgalavidradhim || 61
kaṇṭharogeṣvasṛṅmokṣastīkṣṇaṃ nasyādikarma ca
kvāthapānaṃ tu dārvītvaṅnimbatārkṣyakaliṅgajam || 62
harītakīkaṣāyo vā peyo mākṣikasaṃyutaḥ || 63
kaṭukātiviṣāpāṭhādārumustakaliṅgakāḥ
gomūtrakvathitāḥ peyāḥ kaṇṭharogavināśanāḥ || 64
gṛhadhūmo yavakṣāraḥ pāṭhā vyoṣaṃ rasāñjanam
tejohvā triphalā lohaṃ citrakaśceti cūrṇitam || 65
sakṣaudraṃ dhārayedetadgalarogavināśanam
kālakaṃ nāma cūrṇaṃ tu dantāsyagalaroganut || 66
manaḥśilā yavakṣāro haritālaṃ sasaindhavam
dārvītvakveti taccūrṇaṃ mākṣikeṇa samāyutam || 67
mūrchitaṃ ghṛtamaṇḍena kaṇṭharogeṣu dhārayet
mukharogeṣu ca śreṣṭhaṃ pītikaṃ nāma kīrtitam || 68
yavāgrajaṃ tejavatīṃ sapāṭhāṃ rasāñjanaṃ dāruniśāṃ sakuṣṭhām
kṣaudreṇa kuryādguṭikāṃ mukhena tāṃ dhārayetsarvagalāmayeṣu || 69
daśamūlaṃ pibeduṣṇaṃ yūṣaṃ mudgakulatthayoḥ
kṣīrekṣurasagomūtradadhimastvamlakāñjikaiḥ
vidadhyātkavalānvīkṣya doṣaṃ tailaghṛtairapi || 70
pañcakolakatālīsapatrailāmaricatvacaḥ || 71
palāśamuṣkakakṣārayavakṣārāśca cūrṇitāḥ
guḍe purāṇe kvathite dviguṇe guṭikāḥ kṛtāḥ || 72
karkandhumātrāḥ saptāhaṃ sthitā muṣkakabhasmani
kaṇṭharogeṣu sarveṣu dhāryāḥ syuramṛtopamāḥ || 73
vātātsarvasaraṃ cūrṇairlāvaṇaiḥ pratisārayet
tailaṃ vātaharaiḥ siddhaṃ hitaṃ kavalanasyayoḥ || 74
pittātmake sarvasare śuddhakāyasya dehinaḥ
sarvaḥ pittaharaḥ kāryo vidhirmadhuraśītalaḥ || 75
pratisāraṇagaṇḍūṣāndhūmaṃ saṃśodhanāni ca
kaphātmake sarvasare kriyāṃ kuryātkaphāpahām || 76
mukhapāke śirāvedhaḥ śiraḥkāyavirecanam
kāryaṃ ca bahudhā nityaṃ jātīpatrasya carvaṇam || 77
jātīpatrāmṛtādrākṣāvāsādārvīphalatrikaiḥ
kvāthaḥ kṣaudrayutaḥ pīto gaṇḍūṣo mukhapākanut || 78
paṭolanimbajambvāmramālatīnavapallavāḥ
pañcapallavakaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kaṣāyo mukhadhāvane || 79
pañcavalkakaṣāyo vā triphalākvātha eva vā
mukhapākeṣu sakṣaudraḥ prayojyo mukhadhāvane || 80
svarasaḥ kvathito dārvyā ghanībhūto rasakriyā
sakṣaudrā mukharogāsṛgdoṣanāḍīvraṇāpahā || 81
saptacchadośīrapaṭolamustaharītakītiktakarohiṇībhiḥ
yaṣṭyāhvarājadrumacandanaiśca kvāthaṃ pibetpākaharaṃ mukhasya || 82
paṭolaśuṇṭhītriphalāviśālā-
trāyantitiktādviniśāmṛtānām
pītaḥ kaṣāyo madhunā nihanti
mukhe sthitaścā''syagadānaśeṣān || 83
kvathitāstriphalāpāṭhāmṛdvīkājātipallavāḥ
niṣevyā bhakṣaṇīyā vā triphalā mukhapākahā || 84
tilā nīlotpalaṃ sarpiḥ śarkarā kṣīrameva ca
sakṣaudro dagdhavaktrasya gaṇḍūṣo dāhapākanut || 85
tulāṃ dhṛtāṃ nīlasahācarasya droṇe'mbhasaḥ saṃśrapayedyathāvat
pūtvā caturbhāgarase tu tailaṃ pacecchanairardhapalaprayuktaiḥ || 86
kalkairanantākhadirerimedajambvāmrayaṣṭīmadhukotpalānām
tattailamāśveva dhṛtaṃ mukhena sthairyaṃ dvijānāṃ vidadhāti sadyaḥ || 87
irimedatvakpalaśatamabhinavamāpothya khaṇḍaśaḥ kṛtvā
toyāḍhakaiścaturbhirniṣkvāthya caturthaśeṣeṇa || 88
kvāthena tena matimāṃstailasyārdhāḍhakaṃ śanairvipacet
kalkairakṣasamāṃśairmañjiṣṭhālodhramadhukānām || 89
irimedakhadirakaṭphalalākṣānyagrodhamustasūkṣmailā--
karpūrāgarupadmakalavaṅgakaṅkolajātīnām || 90
phalapatta(trā)ṅgagairikavarāṅgagajakusumadhātakīnāṃ ca
siddhaṃ bhiṣagvidadhyādidaṃ mukhottheṣu rogeṣu || 91
pariśīrṇadantavidradhisauṣiraśītādadantaharṣeṣu
kṛmidantadaraṇacalitaprakṛṣṭamāṃsāvaśīrṇeṣu
mukhadaurgandhye kāryaṃ prāgukteṣvāmayeṣu tailamidam || 92
tailaṃ lākṣārasaṃ kṣīraṃ pṛthakprasthasamaṃ pacet || 93
caturguṇerimakvāthe dravyaiśca palasaṃmitaiḥ
lodhrakaṭphalamañjiṣṭhāpadmakesarapadmakaiḥ || 94
candanotpalayaṣṭyāhvaistailaṃ gaṇḍūṣadhāraṇam
dālanaṃ dantacālaṃ ca dantamokṣaṃ kapālikām || 95
śītādaṃ pūtivaktraṃ ca aruciṃ virasāsyatām
hanyādāsyagatānetānkuryāddantānapi sthirān || 96
bakulasya phalaṃ lodhraṃ vajrivallīkuraṇṭakam
caturaṅgulababbūlavājikarṇerimāsanam || 97
eṣāṃ kalkakaṣāyābhyāṃ tailaṃ pakvaṃ mukhe dhṛtam
sthairyaṃ karoti calatāṃ dantānāṃ nāvanena tu || 98
khadirasya tulāṃ samyagjaladroṇe vipācayet
śeṣāṣṭabhāge tatraiva prativāpaṃ pradāpayet || 99
jātīkarpūrapūgāni kaṅkolasya phalāni ca
ityeṣā guṭikā kāryā mukhasaubhāgyavardhinī
dantoṣṭhamukharogeṣu jihvātālvāmayeṣu ca || 100
gāyatrīsāratulayā(ye) rimavalkalānāṃ
sārdhaṃ tulāyugulamambu ghaṭaiścaturbhiḥ
niṣkvāthya pādamavaśeṣya suvastrapūtaṃ
bhūyaḥ pacedatha śanairmṛdupāvakena || 101
tasminghanatvamupagacchati cūrṇameṣāṃ
ślakṣṇaṃ kṣipecca kavalagrahabhāgikānām || 102
elāmṛṇālasitacandanacandanāmbu
śyāmātamālavikasāghanalohayaṣṭī--
lajjāphalatrayarasāñjanadhātakībha-
śrīpuṣpagairikakaṭaṃkaṭikaṭphalānām || 103
padmākalodhravaṭarohayavāsakānāṃ
māṃsīniśāsurabhivalkalasaṃyutānām
kaṅkolajātiphalakośalavaṅgakāni
cūrṇīkṛtāni vidadhīta palāṃśakāni || 104
śīte'vatārya ghanasāracatuṣpalaṃ tu
kṣiptvā kalāyasadṛśīrguṭikāḥ prakuryāt
śuṣkā mukhe vinihitā vinivārayanti
rogāngaloṣṭharasanādvijatālujātān || 105
kuryurmukhe surabhitāṃ paṭutāṃ ruciṃ ca
sthairyaṃ paraṃ daśanagaṃ rasanālaghutvam || 106
Adhikāra 59
kapitthamātuluṅgāmlaśṛṅgaverarasaiḥ śubhaiḥ
sukhoṣṇaiḥ pūrayetkarṇaṃ karṇaśūlopaśāntaye || 1
śṛṅgaverarasaḥ kṣaudraṃ saindhavaṃ tailameva ca
kaduṣṇaṃ karṇayordeyametadvā vedanāpaham || 2
laśunārdrakaśigrūṇāṃ muraṅgyā mūlakasya ca
kadalyāḥ svarasaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kaduṣṇaḥ karṇapūraṇe
samudraphenacūrṇena yuktyā vā'pyavacūrṇayet || 3
ārdrakasūryāvartakasaubhāñjanamūlamūlakasvarasāḥ
madhusaindhavatailayutāḥ pṛthaguṣṇāḥ karṇaśūlaharāḥ || 4
saubhāñjanasya niryāsastilatailena saṃyutaḥ
vyaktoṣṇaḥ pūraṇaḥ karṇe karṇaśūlopaśāntaye || 5
aṣṭānāmapi mūtrāṇāṃ mūtreṇānyatamena vā
koṣṇena pūrayetkarṇaṃ karṇaśūlopaśāntaye || 6
aśvatthapatrakhallaṃ vā vidhāya bahupatrakam
tailāktamaṅgāracitaṃ nidadhyācchravaṇopari || 7
yattailaṃ cyavate tasmātkhallādaṅgāratāpitāt
tatprāptaṃ śravaṇasrotaḥ sadyo gṛhṇāti vedanām || 8
mahataḥ pañcamūlasya kāṇḍānyaṣṭāṅgulāni ca
kṣaumeṇā''veṣṭya saṃsicya tailenā''dīpayettataḥ || 9
yattailaṃ cyavate tebhyaḥ sukhoṣṇaṃ tatprayojayet
jñeyaṃ taddīpikātailaṃ karṇaśūlanivāraṇam || 10
arkasya patraṃ pariṇāmapītamājyena liptaṃ śikhinā'bhitaptam
āpīḍya toyaṃ śravaṇe niṣiktaṃ nihanti śūlaṃ bahuvedanāṃ ca || 11
tīvraśūlāture karṇe saśabde kledavāhini
bastamūtraṃ kṣipetkoṣṇaṃ saindhavena samanvitam || 12
vaṃśāvalekhasaṃyuktaṃ mūtra vā''jāvike bhiṣak
tailaṃ pacettena karṇaṃ pūrayetkarṇaśūlinaḥ || 13
dhārayetpūraṇaṃ karṇe karṇamūlaṃ vimardayan
rujaḥ syānmārdavaṃ yāvanmātrāśatamavedane || 14
hiṅgutumburuśuṇṭhībhiḥ sādhyaṃ tailaṃ tu sārṣapam
karṇaśūle praṇāde tu pūraṇaṃ hitamucyate || 15
vālamūlakaśuṇṭhīnāṃ kṣāro hiṅgu sanāgaram
śatapuṣpā vacā kuṣṭhaṃ dāruśigrurasāñjanam || 16
sauvarcalaṃ yavakṣāraḥ svarjikodbhidasaindhavam
bhūrjagranthirviḍaṃ mustaṃ madhuśuktaṃ caturguṇam || 17
mātuluṅgarasaścaiva kadalyā rasa eva ca
tailamebhirvipaktavyaṃ karṇaśūlaharaṃ param || 18
bādhiryaṃ karṇanādaśca pūyāsrāvaśca dāruṇaḥ
pūraṇādasya tailasya kṛmayaḥ karṇasaṃśritāḥ || 19
kṣipraṃ vināśamāyānti kṛṣṇātreyasya śāsanāt
kṣāratailamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mukhapākāmayāpaham || 20
chedaṃ madhviti kṛtvaiva śuktakādi caturguṇam
tailātpratyekamanye tu madhuśuktaṃ likhanti ca || 21
yavāmbhomadhusaṃyuktaṃ śṛṅgaveraguḍānvitam
dhānyarāśau trirātrasthaṃ madhuśuktamudāhṛtam || 22
karṇanāde karṇakṣveḍe kaṭutailena pūraṇam
nādabādhiryayoḥ kuryādvātaśūloktamauṣadham || 23
mārgakṣārajalena tatkṛtakalkena sādhitaṃ tilajam
apaharati karṇanādaṃ bādhiryaṃ cāpi pūraṇataḥ || 24
daśamūlīkaṣāyeṇa tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet
etatkarṇe pradātavyaṃ bādhirye paramauṣadham || 25
phalaṃ bilvasya mūtreṇa piṣṭvā tailaṃ vipācayet
sakṣīraṃ tatra vitaredbādhirye karṇapūraṇam || 26
evameva vidhiḥ kāryaḥ praṇāde nasyapūrvakaḥ
guḍanāgaratoyena nasyaṃ syādubhayorapi || 27
cūrṇaṃ pañcakaṣāyāṇāṃ kapittharasasaṃyutam
karṇasrāve praśaṃsanti pūraṇaṃ madhunā saha || 28
tindukānyabhayā lodhraṃ samaṅgā cā''malakyapi
pañcakaṣāyaśabdena grāhyametadbhavediha || 29
sarjatvakcūrṇasaṃyuktaḥ karpāsīphalajo rasaḥ
madhunā saṃyutaḥ sādhuḥ karṇasrāve praśasyate || 30
jambvāmrapatraṃ taruṇaṃ samāṃśaṃ kapitthakārpāsaphalaṃ ca sārdram || 31
kṣuttvā rasaṃ taṃ madhunā vimiśraṃ srāvāpahaṃ taṃ pravadanti tajjñāḥ
ebhiḥ śṛtaṃ nimbakarañjatailaṃ sasārṣapaṃ srāvaharaṃ pradiṣṭam || 32
puṭapākavidhisvinnahastiviḍjātakauṭajaḥ
rasaḥ satailasindhūtthaḥ karṇasrāvaharaḥ paraḥ || 33
śambūkasya tu māṃsena kaṭutailaṃ vipācayet
tasya pūraṇamātreṇa karṇanāḍī praśāmyati || 34
cūrṇena gandhakaśilārajanībhavena
muṣṭyaṃśakena kaṭutailapalāṣṭakaṃ yat
dhattūrapatrarasatulyamidaṃ vipakvaṃ
nāḍīṃ jayeccirabhavāmapi karṇajātām || 35
atha karṇapratīnāhe snehasvedau prayojayet
tato viriktaśirasaḥ kriyāṃ prāptāṃ samācaret || 36
karṇapākasya bhaiṣajyaṃ kuryātkṣatavisarpavat
nāḍīsvedo'tha vamanaṃ dhūmo mūrdhavirecanam
vidhiśca kaphahṛtsarvaḥ karṇakaṇḍūmapohati || 37
kledayitvā tu tailena svedena pravilāyya ca
śodhayetkarṇagūthaṃ tu bhiṣaksamyakśalākayā || 38
nirguṇḍīsvarasastailaṃ sindhurdhūmarajo guḍaḥ
pūraṇātpūtikarṇasya śamano madhusaṃyutaḥ || 39
jātīpatrarase tailaṃ vipakvaṃ pūtikarṇajit || 40
varuṇārkakapitthāmrajambūpallavasādhitam
pūtikarṇāpahaṃ tailaṃ jātīpatraraso'tha vā || 41
sūryāvartakasvarasaṃ sinduvārarasaṃ tathā
lāṅgalīmūlakarasaṃ tryūṣaṇenāvacūrṇitam
pūrayetkṛmikarṇaṃ tu jantūnāṃ nāśanaṃ param || 42
kṛmikarṇavināśārthaṃ kṛmighnaṃ yojayedvidhim || 43
vārtākadhūpaśca hitaḥ sārṣapasneha eva ca || 44
pūraṇaṃ haritālena gavāṃ mūtrayutena ca
dhūpanaṃ karṇadaurgandhye gugguluḥ śreṣṭha ucyate
kṛmighnaṃ haritālena gavāṃ mūtrayutena ca || 45
rājavṛkṣāditoyena surasādijalena vā
karṇaprakṣālanaṃ kuryāccūrṇairetaistu pūraṇam
vidradhau cāpi kurvīta vidradhyuktaṃ ca bheṣajam || 46
ghṛṣṭaṃ rasāñjanaṃ nāryāḥ kṣīreṇa kṣaudrasaṃyutam
praśasyate cirotthe'pi sāsrāve pūtikarṇake || 47
kuṣṭhahiṅguvacādāruśatāhvāviśvasaindhavaiḥ
pūtikarṇāpahaṃ tailaṃ bastamūtreṇa sādhitam || 48
śatāvarīvājigandhāpayasyairaṇḍabījakaiḥ
tailaṃ vipakvaṃ sakṣīraṃ pālīnāṃ puṣṭikṛtparam || 49
kalkena jīvanīyena tailaṃ payasi pācitam
ānūpamāṃsakvāthena pālipoṣaṇavardhanam || 50
Adhikāra 60
pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ syāccitrakaharītakī
sarpirguḍaḥ ṣaḍaṅgaśca yūṣaḥ pīnasaśāntaye || 1
vyoṣacitrakatālīsatintiḍīkāmlavetasam
sacavyājājitulyāṃśamelātvakpatrapādikam || 2
vyoṣādikamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ purāṇaguḍasaṃyutam
pīnasaśvāsakāsaghnaṃ rucisvarakaraṃ param || 3
pāṭhādvirajanīmūrvāpippalījātipallavaiḥ
dantyā ca tailaṃ saṃsiddhaṃ nasyaṃ pīnasaśāntaye || 4
vyāghrīdantīvacāśigrusurasavyoṣasaindhavaiḥ
pācitaṃ nāvanaṃ tailaṃ pūtināsāgadaṃ haret || 5
trikaṭukaviḍaṅgasaindhavabṛhatīphalaśigrusurasadantībhiḥ
tailaṃ gojalasiddhaṃ nasyaṃ syātpūtināsasya || 6
kaliṅgahiṅgumaricaṃ lākṣāsurasakaṭphalam
kuṣṭhogrāśigru jantughnamavapīḍaḥ praśasyate || 7
taireva mūtrasaṃyuktaiḥ kaṭutailaṃ vipācayet
apīnase pūtināse śamanaṃ kīrtitaṃ param || 8
nāsāpāke pittahṛtsaṃvidhānaṃ kāryaṃ sarvaṃ bāhyamābhyantaraṃ ca
hṛtvā raktaṃ kṣīravṛkṣatvacaśca sājyāḥ seke yojanīyāśca lepe || 9
pūyāsraraktapittaghnāḥ kaṣāyā nāvanāni ca
śuṇṭhīkuṣṭhakaṇābilvadrākṣākalkakaṣāyavat
sādhitaṃ tailamājyaṃ vā nasyaṃ kṣavathuroganut || 10
dīpte roge paittikaṃ saṃvidhānaṃ kuryātsarvaṃ svādu yacchītalaṃ ca
nāsānāhe snehapānaṃ pradhānaṃ snigdhā dhūmā mūrdhabastiśca nityam || 11
vātike tu pratiśyāye pibetsarpiryathākramam || 12
pañcabhirlavaṇaiḥ siddhaṃ prathamena gaṇena vā
nasyādiṣu vidhiṃ kṛtsnamavekṣetārditeritam || 13
pittaraktotthayoḥ peyaṃ sarpirmadhurakaiḥ śṛtam
pariṣekānpradehāṃśca kuryādapi ca śītalān || 14
kaphaje sarpiṣā snigdhaṃ tilamāṣavipakvayā
yavāgvā vāmayitvā vā śleṣmaghnaṃ kramamācaret || 15
dārvīṅgudanikumbhaiśca kiṇihyā surasena ca
vartayo'tha pṛthagyojyā dhūmrapāne yathāvidhi || 16
ghṛtatailena saṃyuktaṃ saktudhūmaṃ pibennaraḥ
pratiśyāyaharaṃ proktaṃ kāsahikkānivāraṇam || 17
athavā saghṛtānsaktūnkṛtvā mallakasaṃpuṭe
navapratiśyāyavatāṃ dhūmaṃ vaidyaḥ prayojayet || 18
puṭapakvaṃ jayāpatraṃ sindhutailasamanvitam
pratiśyāyeṣu sarveṣu śīlitaṃ paramauṣadham || 19
śiraso'bhyañjanasvedanasyakaṭvamlabhojanaiḥ
vamanairghṛtapānaiśca tānyathāsvamupācaret || 20
ūṣaṇaṃ guḍasaṃyuktaṃ snigdhadadhyamlabhojanam
navapratiśyāyaharaṃ viśeṣātkaphapācanam || 21
tataḥ pakvaṃ kaphaṃ jñātvā harecchīrṣavirecanaiḥ
pippalyaḥ śigrubījāni viḍaṅgamaricāni ca
avapīḍaḥ praśasto'yaṃ pratiśyāyanivāraṇaḥ || 22
samūtrapiṣṭāścoddiṣṭāḥ kriyāḥ kṛmiṣu yojayet
dhāvanāni kṛmighnāni bheṣajāni ca buddhimān || 23
śeṣāṇāṃ tu vikārāṇāṃ svaṃ svaṃ kāryaṃ cikitsitam || 24
raktakaravīrapuṣpaṃ jātyā'sanamallikāyāśca
etaiḥ samaṃ tu tailaṃ nāsārśonāśanaṃ śreṣṭham || 25
citrakasyā''malakyāśca guḍūcyā daśamūlajam
śataṃ śataṃ rasaṃ dattvā pathyācūrṇāḍhakaṃ guḍāt || 26
śataṃ paceddhanībhūte paladvādaśakaṃ kṣipet
vyoṣatrijātayoḥ kṣārātpalārdhamapare'hani || 27
prasthārdhaṃ madhuno dattvā yathāgni prāśayedatha
vṛddhaye'gneḥ kṣayaṃ kāsaṃ pīnasaṃ dustaraṃ kṛmīn
gulmodāvartadurnāmaśvāsānhanti rasāyanam || 28
gṛhadhūmakaṇādārukṣāranaktāhvasaindhavaiḥ
siddhaṃ śikharibījaiśca tailaṃ nāsārśasāṃ hitam || 29
Adhikāra 61
laṅghanālepanasvedaśirāvyadhavirecanaiḥ
upācaredabhiṣyandānañjanāścotanādibhiḥ || 1
akṣikukṣibhavā rogāḥ pratiśyāyavraṇajvarāḥ
pañcaite pañcarātreṇa rogā naśyanti laṅghanāt || 2
svedaḥ pralepastiktānnaṃ seko dinacatuṣṭayam
laṅghanaṃ cākṣirogāṇāmāmānāṃ pācanāni ṣaṭ || 3
śrīvāsātiviṣālodhraiścūrṇitairalpasaindhavaiḥ
avyakte'kṣigade kāryaṃ plotasthairguṇḍanaṃ bahiḥ || 4
añjanaṃ pūraṇaṃ kvāthapānamāme na śasyate
yadyakṣirogaṃ manyeta sarujaṃ vyaktalakṣaṇam
vedanānigrahārthaṃ tu kuryādāścotanaṃ sadā || 5
āścotanaṃ mārutaje kvātho bilvādibhirhitaḥ
koṣṇaḥ sairaṇḍabṛhatītarkārīmadhuśigrubhiḥ || 6
eraṇḍapallave mūle tvaci cā''jaṃ payaḥ śṛtam
kaṇṭakāryāśca mūleṣu sukhoṣṇaṃ secanaṃ hitam || 7
tataḥ saṃpakvadoṣasya prāptamañjanamācaret || 8
hemante śiśire caiva madhyāhne'ñjanamiṣyate
pūrvāhṇe vā'parāhṇe ca grīṣme śaradi ceṣyate
varṣāsvanabhre nātyuṣṇe vasante ca sadaiva hi || 9
hareṇumātrāṃ kurvīta varti tīkṣṇāñjane bhiṣak
pramāṇaṃ madhyame'dhyardhaṃ dviguṇaṃ ca mṛdau bhavet || 10
viḍaṅgamātraṃ tvavaraṃ madhyaṃ dvāvuttamaṃ trayam
rasakriyāyāṃ vartīnāṃ pramāṇaṃ parikīrtitam || 11
vairecanikacūrṇaṃ tu dviśalākaṃ vidhīyate
mṛdau tu triśalākaṃ syāccatasraḥ snaihike'ñjane || 12
suvarṇarūpyatāmrāyaḥkāṃsyāśmāsthimayāḥ kṛtāḥ || 13
śalākā añjane śastā aṣṭāṅgulamitāḥ śubhāḥ
vaktrayormukulākārāḥ kalāyaparimaṇḍalāḥ || 14
vāme karatale kṛtvā sajalaṃ nikaṣopalam
dakṣiṇenāñjanaṃ gṛhya kareṇa nikaṣecchanaiḥ || 15
tena kalkena matimāñśalākāgraṃ pralipya ca
utthāpya cottaraṃ vartma vāmenāṅguṣṭhaparvaṇā || 16
vartmaśuklāntare cālpaṃ(lpāṃ) śalākāṃ supravartayet
prathamaṃ savyamañjyāttu paścādañjyāttu dakṣiṇam
śalākāṅguṣṭhadeśinyā na cāntarnayanaṃ spṛśet || 17
bṛhatyeraṇḍamūlatvakśigrumūlaṃ sasaindhavam
ajākṣīreṇa piṣṭaṃ syādvartirvātākṣiroganut || 18
haridre madhukaṃ drākṣā devadāru ca peṣayet
ājena payasā śreṣṭhamabhiṣyande tadañjanam || 19
prapauṇḍarīkayaṣṭyāhvaniśāmalakapadmakaiḥ
śītairmadhusamāyuktaiḥ sakaḥ pittākṣiroganut || 20
drākṣāmadhukamañjiṣṭhājīvanīyaiḥ śṛtaṃ payaḥ
prātarāścotanaṃ pathyaṃ śothaśūlākṣirogiṇām || 21
kaphaje laṅghanaṃ svedo nasyaṃ tiktādibhojanam
tīkṣṇaiḥ pradhamanaṃ kuryāttīkṣṇaiścaivopanāhanam || 22
phaṇijjakāsphotakapitthabilvapattūrapīlūsurasārjabhaṅgaiḥ
svedaṃ vidadhyādathavā'nulepaṃ barhiṣṭhaśuṇṭhīsuradārukuṣṭhaiḥ || 23
śuṇṭhīnimbadalaiḥ piṇḍaḥ sukhoṣṇaiḥ svalpasaindhavaiḥ
dhāryaścakṣuṣi saṃkṣepācchophakaṇḍūvyathāpahaḥ || 24
valkalaṃ pārijātasya tailakāñjikasaindhavaiḥ
kaphajātākṣiśūlaghnaṃ tarughnaṃ kuliśaṃ yathā || 25
sasaindhavaṃ lodhramathā''jyabhṛṣṭaṃ sauvīrapiṣṭaṃ sitavastrabaddham
āścotanaṃ tannayanasya kuryāddāhaṃ ca kaṇḍūṃ ca rujaṃ ca hanyāt || 26
lodhraṃ sarpiṣi saṃpakvaṃ khadirājājisarṣapaiḥ
nāgarāriṣṭasindhūtthairyuktaṃ dṛṣadi cūrṇitam || 27
site vāsasi tadbaddhaṃ nyasetsvacchāmlakāñjikaiḥ
tenākṣṇoḥ pūraṇaṃ kāryaṃ kaṇḍūrogāśrugharṣajit || 28
nimbasya patraiḥ parilipya lodhraṃ svedāgninā cūrṇamathāpi kalkam
āścotanaṃ mānuṣadugdhayuktaṃ pittāsravātāpahamagtyamuktam || 29
snigdhoṣṇairvātajaḥ pittaraktajo mṛduśītalaiḥ
tīkṣṇarūkṣoṣṇaviśadaiḥ praṇaśyati kaphātmakaḥ || 30
tīkṣṇoṣṇamṛduśītānāṃ vyatyāsātsāṃnipātikaḥ
tasya kālo divā sarvo yāmaśca prathamo niśi || 31
atha vā''ścotanaṃ yāvaddīyamānaṃ sahennaraḥ
ahorātre tacca sakṛddvistrirvā deyamucyate || 32
lekhane sapta cāṣṭau vā bindavaḥ snaihike daśa
āścotane prayoktavyā dvādaśaiva tu ropaṇe || 33
samyagāścotane yukte vaimalyaṃ netrayorbhavet
vedanopaśamo vyādhinivṛttirnetralāghavam || 34
mithyākṛte vyādhivṛddhirgauravāvilanetratā
atīvā''ścotite netre rāgadoṣaparisravāḥ || 35
tirīṭatriphalāyaṣṭīśarkarābhadramustakaiḥ
piṣṭaiḥ śītāmbunā seko raktābhiṣyandanāśanaḥ || 36
kaserumadhukābhyāṃ ca cūrṇamambarasaṃvṛtam
nyastamapsvāntarikṣāsu hitamāścotanaṃ bhavet || 37
tiktasya sarpiṣaḥ pānaṃ bahuśaśca virecanam || 38
akṣṇorapi samantācca pātanaṃ ca jalaukasām
pittābhiṣyandaśamano vidhiścāpyupapāditaḥ || 39
śigrupatrarasaiḥ sekaḥ sarvanetrarujāpahaḥ
śigrupallavaniryāsaḥ sughṛṣṭastāmrasaṃpuṭe
ghṛtena dhūpito hanti śothagharṣāśruvedanāḥ || 40
pathyāgairikasindhūtthadārvītārkṣyaiḥ samāṃśakaiḥ
jalapiṣṭairbahirlepaḥ sarvanetrāmayāpahaḥ || 41
piṣṭairnimbasya patrairativimalatarairjātisindhūtthamiśrai-
rantargarbhaṃ dadhānā paṭutaraguṭikā piṣṭalodhreṇa bhṛṣṭā
tūlaiḥ sauvīrasāndrairatiśayamṛdubhirveṣṭitā sā samantā-
ccakṣuṣkopopaśāntiṃ ciramuparidṛśo bhrāmyamāṇā karoti || 42
yathāsvamauṣadhaṃ cūrṇaṃ mātrāṃ badarasaṃmitām || 43
viśuddhe mṛduni kṣaume baddhvā cākṣi spṛśetpunaḥ
tena dāhāśrupākāśca rāgaḥ śophaśca śāmyati || 44
bilvapatrarasaḥ pūtaḥ sājyasindhukaṇānvitaḥ
śulbe varāṭikāghṛṣṭo dhūpito gomayāgninā || 45
payasā''loḍitaścākṣṇoḥ pūraṇācchophaśūlanut
abhiṣyande'dhimanthe ca srāve rakte ca śasyate || 46
salavaṇakaṭutailaṃ kāñjikaṃ kāṃsyapātre
ghanamupalasughṛṣṭaṃ dhūpitaṃ gomayāgnau
sapavanakaphakopaṃ chāgadugdhāvasiktaṃ
jayati nayanaśūlaṃ srāvaśophaṃ sarāgam || 47
ayameva vidhiḥ sarvo manthādiṣvapi śasyate
aśāntau sarvathā manthe bhruvorupari dāhayet || 48
jalaukaḥpātanaṃ saṃdhau netrapāke virecanam
śirāvyadhaṃ vā kurvīta sekālepāṃśca śukravat || 49
vibhītakaśivādhātrīpaṭolāriṣṭavāsakaiḥ
kvātho guggulunā peyaḥ śophaśūlākṣipākahā || 50
pillaṃ ca savraṇaṃ śukraṃ rāgādīṃścāpi nāśayet
etairapi ghṛtaṃ pakvaṃ rogāṃstānnāśayedbahūn || 51
āṭarūṣābhayānimbadhātrīmustāṃkṣaṃkūlakaiḥ
raktasrāvaṃ kaphaṃ hanti cakṣuṣyaṃ vāsakādikam || 52
pathyāstisro vibhītakyaḥ ṣaḍdhātryo dvādaśeti ca
prasthārdhasalile kvāthamaṣṭabhāgāvaśeṣitam || 53
pītvā'bhiṣyandamāsrāvaṃ rāgaṃ ca timiraṃ jayet
saṃrambharāgaśūlāśrunāśanaṃ dṛkprasādanam || 54
netre tvabhihate kuryācchītamāścotanādikam
dṛṣṭiprasādajananaṃ vidhimāśu kuryā-
tsnigdhairhimaiśca madhuraiśca tathā prayogaiḥ || 55
svedāgnidhūmabhayaśokarujopatāpai-
rabhyāhatāmapi tathaiva bhiṣakcikitset || 56
āgantudoṣaṃ prasamīkṣya kāryaṃ vaktroṣmaṇā sveditamāditastu
āścotanaṃ strīpayasā ca sadyo yaccāpi pittakṣatajāpahaṃ syāt || 57
sūryoparāgānalavidyutādivilokanenopahatekṣaṇasya
saṃtarpaṇaṃ snigdhahimādi kāryaṃ sāyaṃ niṣevyāstriphalāprayogāḥ || 58
niśābdatriphalādārvīsitāmadhukasaṃyutam
abhighātākṣiśūlaghnaṃ nārīkṣīreṇa pūraṇam
itkaṭāṅkurajastadvatsvaraso netrapūraṇam || 59
ājaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣīrapātraṃ madhukaṃ cotpalāni ca
jīvakarṣabhakau caiva piṣṭvā sarpirvipācayet
sarvanetrābhighāteṣu sarpiretatpraśasyate || 60
prathame paṭale doṣaḥ sādhyaḥ syādabhighātajaḥ
dvitīye paṭale yāpyastṛtīye ca na sidhyati || 61
saindhavaṃ dāru śuṇṭhī ca mātuluṅgaraso ghṛtam
stanyodakārdhaṃ kartavyaṃ śuṣkapāke tadañjanam || 62
vātābhiṣyandavaccānyavāte mārutaparyaye
pūrvabhaktahitaṃ sarpiḥ kṣīraṃ vā'pyatha bhojane || 63
vṛkṣādanyāṃ kapitthe ca pañcamūle mahatyapi
sakṣīraṃ karkaṭarase siddhaṃ cātra ghṛtaṃ pibet || 64
amlādhyuṣitaśāntyarthaṃ kuryāllepāṃśca śītalān || 65
tailvakaṃ traiphalaṃ sarpirjīrṇaṃ vā kevalaṃ pibet
śirāvyadhaṃ vinā kāryaḥ pittasyandaharo vidhiḥ || 66
sarpiḥ kṣaudraṃ cāñjanaṃ syācchirotpātasya bheṣajam
tadvatsaindhavakāsīsaṃ stanyaghṛṣṭaṃ ca pūjitam || 67
śirāharṣe'ñjanaṃ kuryātphāṇitaṃ madhusaṃyutam
madhunā tārkṣyaśailaṃ vā kāsīsaṃ vā samākṣikam
vetasāmlaṃ stanyayuktaṃ phāṇitaṃ tu sasaindhavam || 68
gavyakṣīrotthitaṃ sarpistarpaṇārthaṃ vidhīyate
dṛṣṭiprasādanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ timirasyāpakarṣaṇam || 69
abhiṣyandādhimanthābhyāṃ karṣate'kṣṇi balāvaham
doṣānutsādayatyāśu tathaivāśru niyacchati || 70
uttānaśāyinaḥ kāryaṃ niścalasya yathāsukham || 71
pratisūryāgnirahite nīrajodhūmamārute
gṛhe sukhe nirābādhe vītakrodhabhayasya ca || 72
māṣapiṣṭamayādhāraṃ dṛḍhīkṛtyāparisravam
ardhāṅgulordhvavistāraṃ mūle dvyaṅgulameva ca || 73
pramāṇenākṣikośasya yathā syānnākṣirodhakṛt
pakṣmāṇi yāvanmajjanti romāntādābhruvastathā || 74
tato vastreṇa pītena nīlena haritena vā
patrairvā''cchādya nayane tataḥ paśyedyathāsukham || 75
vācāṃ sahasramātraṃ vā dhārayenmārutāmaye
pittaje raktaje cāṣṭau kaphaje ṣaṭśatāni ca
saṃnipātātmake sapta pañca svasthavidhau tathā || 76
tataścāpāṅgataḥ snehaṃ srāvayitvā'kṣi śodhayet
prāyogikeṇa dhūmena kaphamasya biśodhayet || 77
svasthavṛtte vidhātavyaṃ dvyantaraṃ tarpaṇaṃ bhavet
ahanyahani vātotthe pittarakte dināntaram || 78
tarpaṇaṃ saṃnipātotthe dvyantaraṃ tryantaraṃ kaphe
kaduṣṇaṃ kaphavāyvostu śītalaṃ pittaśoṇite || 79
sukhasvapnāvabodhatvaṃ vaiśadyaṃ varṇapāṭavam || 80
nirvṛtirvyādhisaṃśāntiḥ kriyālāghavameva ca
tarpaṇe tṛptiliṅgāni samyagetāni lakṣayet || 81
vraṇaśukrapraśāntyarthaṃ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ gugguluṃ pibet
katakasya phalaṃ śaṅkhaṃ tindukaṃ rūpyameva ca
kāṃsye nighṛṣṭaṃ stanyena kṣataśukrārtirogajit || 82
candanaṃ gairikaṃ lākṣā mālatīkalikānvitā
vraṇaśukraharā vārtiḥ śoṇitasya prasādanī || 83
śirayā vā haredraktaṃ jalaukābhiśca locanāt || 84
ekaṃ vā puṇḍarīkaṃ ca cchāgakṣīreṇa sevitam
rāgāśruvedanā hanyātkṣatapākātyayājakāḥ || 85
dhātrīphalaṃ nimbakapitthapatraṃ yaṣṭyāhvalodhraṃ khadirastilāśca
kvāthaḥ suśīto nayane niṣiktaḥ sarvaprakāraṃ vinihanti śukram || 86
tutthakaṃ vāriṇā yuktaṃ śukraṃ hantyakṣipūraṇāt
samudraphenadakṣāṇḍatvaksindhūtthaiḥ samākṣikaiḥ
śigrubījayutairvartiḥ śukraghnī śigruvāriṇā || 87
dakṣāṇḍatvakśilāśaṅkhakācacandanagairikaiḥ
tulyairañjanayogo'yaṃ puṣpārmādivilekhanaḥ || 88
śirīṣabījamaricapippalīsaindhavairapi
śukrasya gharṣaṇaṃ kāryamathavā saindhavena ca || 89
gṛhītvā naktamālasya phalacūrṇaṃ subhāvitam || 90
rasena hi palāśasya kusumotthena dhīmatā
vartirnetrāñjane yojyā aśeṣakusumāpahā || 91
saindhavatriphalākṛṣṇākaṭukāśaṅkhanābhayaḥ
satāmrarajaso vartiḥ piṣṭā śukravināśanī || 92
kṣuṇṇapuṃnāgapatreṇa paribhāvitavāriṇā
śyāmākvāthāmbunā vā'tha secanaṃ kusumāpaham || 93
śaṅkhasya bhāgāścatvārastato'rdhena manaḥśilā
manaḥśilārdhaṃ maricaṃ maricārdhena saindhavam || 94
etaccūrṇāñjanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ śukralekhanamuttamam
piccaṭe madhunā yojyamarbude mastunā tathā || 95
tāpyaṃ madhukasāro vā bījaṃ cākṣasya saindhavam
madhunā'ñjanayogāḥ syuścatvāraḥ śukraśāntaye || 96
vaṭakṣīreṇa saṃyuktaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ karpūrajaṃ rajaḥ
kṣipramañjanato hanti śukraṃ cāpi ghanonnatam || 97
tālasya nārikelasya tathaivāruṣkarasya ca
karīrasya ca vaṃśānāṃ kṛtvā kṣāraṃ parisrutam || 98
karabhāsthikṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṣāreṇa paribhāvitam
saptakṛtvo'ṣṭakṛtvo vā sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu kārayet || 99
etacchukreṣvasādhyeṣu kṛṣṇīkaraṇamuttamam
yāni śukrāṇi sādhyāni teṣāṃ paramamañjanam || 100
paṭolaṃ kaṭukāṃ dārvīṃ nisbaṃ vāsāṃ phalatrikam
durālabhāṃ parpaṭakaṃ trāyantīṃ ca palonmitām || 101
prasthamāmalakānāṃ ca kvāthayennalvaṇe'mbhasi
pādaśeṣe rase tasminghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 102
kalkairbhūnimbakuṭajamustayaṣṭyāhvacandanaiḥ
sapippalīkaistatsiddhaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ śukrayorhitam || 103
ghrāṇakarṇākṣivartmatvaṅmukharogavraṇāpaham
kāmalājvaravīsarpagaṇḍamālāharaṃ param || 104
kṛṣṇāviḍaṅgamadhuyaṣṭikasindhujanma-
viśvauṣadhaiḥ payasi siddhamidaṃ chagalyāḥ
tailaṃ nṛṇāṃ timiraśukraśirokṣiśūla-
pākātyayāñjayati nasyavidhau prayuktam || 105
saindhavaṃ vājipādaṃ ca gorocanasamanvitam
selutvagrasasaṃyuktaṃ pūraṇaṃ cājakāpaham || 106
śaśakasya śiraḥkalke śeṣāṅgakvathite jale
ghṛtasya kuḍavaṃ pakvaṃ pūraṇaṃ cājakāpaham || 107
śaśakasya kaṣāyeṇa sarpiṣaḥ kuḍavaṃ pacet
yaṣṭīprapauṇḍarīkasya kalkena payasā samam || 108
chāgalyāḥ pūraṇācchukrakṣatapākātyayājakāḥ
hanti bhrūśaṅkhaśūlaṃ ca dāharāgau viśeṣataḥ || 109
na vinā śoṇitaṃ śukrakṣatapākātyayājakāḥ
bhavanti rudhiraṃ teṣu jalaukobhirato haret || 110
mūlaṃ dṛṣṭivināśasya timiraṃ samudāhṛtam
ṛṣibhistvaritaṃ tasmāttasya kuryāccikitsitam || 111
ekadeśe tu yo doṣo dṛṣṭyāṃ tiṣṭhati piṇḍitaḥ
yatsaṃsthānaḥ sa bhavati tādṛśaṃ tena paśyati
tṛtīye paṭale kācaścaturthe nīlikāhvayam || 112
sādhyamaprāptarāgaṃ syāttimiraṃ ca kriyāvataḥ
yāpyaṃ saṃprāptarāgaṃ syādvarjyaṃ paṭalatāṃ gatam || 113
triphalāghṛtaṃ madhu yavāḥ pādābhyaṅgaḥ śatāvarīmudgāḥ
cakṣuṣyaḥ saṃkṣepādvargaḥ kathito bhiṣagbhirayam || 114
jīvantiśākaṃ suniṣaṇṇakaṃ ca sataṇḍulīyaṃ varavāstukaṃ ca
cillī tathā mūlakapotikā ca dṛṣṭerhitaṃ śākunajāṅgalaṃ ca || 115
paṭolakarkoṭakakāravellavārtākatarkārikarīrajāni
śākāni śigrvārtagalāni caiva hitāni dṛṣṭerghṛtasādhitāni || 116

kācātsaṃjāyate nīlī tadā'ndho jāyate naraḥ || 117
