ghṛtapragāḍhāṃ timire'tha pittaje
samīraje tailayutāṃ kaphātmake
madhupragāḍhāṃ vidadhīta yuktitaḥ || 118

madhunā haviṣā vā'pi samastatimirāntakṛt || 119

sa mucyate netragatairvikārairbhṛtyairyathā kṣīṇadhano manuṣyaḥ || 120
saghṛtaṃ vā varākvāthaṃ śīlayettimirāmayī || 121

jātā rogā vinaśyanti na bhavanti kadācana || 122
jalagaṇḍūṣaiḥ prātarbahuśo'mbhobhiḥ prapūrya mukharandhram
nirdayamukṣannakṣi kṣapayati timirāṇi nā sadyaḥ || 123

acireṇaiva tadvāri timirāṇi vyapohati || 124
raviriva bhūgatatimiraṃ vinihanti nāvanātsarpiḥ
śatavarṣasthitakaṭhinaṃ saptāhādañjanānniyatam || 125
katakasya phalaṃ śaṅkhaṃ saidhavaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ sitā
pheno rasāñjanaṃ kṣaudraṃ viḍaṅgāni manaḥśilā || 126
sarvametatsamaṃ kṛtvā chāgakṣīreṇa peṣayet
timiraṃ paṭalaṃ kācamarma śukraṃ vyapohati
kaṇḍūkledārbudaṃ hanti malaṃ cā''śu sukhāvatī || 127
harītakī vacā kuṣṭhaṃ pippalī maricāni ca || 128
vibhītakasya majjā ca śaṅkhanābhirmanaḥśilā
sarvametatsamaṃ kṛtvā chāgakṣīreṇa peṣayet || 129
nāśayettimiraṃ kaṇḍūpaṭalānyarbudāni ca
adhikāni ca māṃsāni yaśca rātrau na paśyati || 130
api trivārṣikaṃ puṣpaṃ māsenaikena nāśayet
candrodayā nāma vartirnṛṇāṃ dṛṣṭiviśodhanī || 131
divā na tu prayoktavyaṃ netrayostīvramañjanam

yathā hi kanakādīnāṃ maṇīnāṃ vividhātmanām
dhautānāṃ vividhā śuddhistailacelakarādibhiḥ || 134
evaṃ netreṣu martyānāmañjanāścotanādibhiḥ
dṛṣṭirnirākulā bhāti nirmale nabhasīnduvat || 135

nīlotpalaṃ viḍaṅgāni phenaṃ ca saritāṃ pateḥ || 137

aśītistilapuṣpāṇi ṣaṣṭiḥ pippalitaṇḍulāḥ || 139

candanatriphalāpūgapalāśataruśoṇitaiḥ
jalapiṣṭairiyaṃ vartiraśeṣatimirāpahā || 141

kledopadehakaṇḍūghnī vartiḥ śastā kaphāpahā || 143

vibhītakasya majjā tu śaṅkhanābhirmanaḥśilā || 145

saṃgṛhyoparatānalaktakarasenā''mṛjya gaṇḍūpadā-

ahitācārato vā'pi
yathāsvaṃ tānupācaret || 179

viṣamajvaramarmāṇi śukraṃ cā''śu vyapohati || 207

pratisāraṇamekaikaṃ bhinne lagaṇa iṣyate
nimiṣe nāsayā peyaṃ sarpistena ca pūraṇam || 237
svedayitvā bisagranthiṃ chidrāṇyasya nirāśrayam
pakvaṃ bhittvā tu śastreṇa saindhavenāvacūrṇayet || 238
vartmāvalekhaṃ bahuśastadvacchoṇitamokṣaṇam
punaḥ punarvirekaṃ ca pillarogāturo bhajet || 239
pillī snigdho vametpūrvaṃ śirāvyadhasrute'sṛji
śilārasāñjanavyoṣagopittaiścakṣurañjayet || 240
haritālavacādāru surasārasapeṣitam
abhayārasapiṣṭaṃ vā tagaraṃ pillanāśanam || 241
bhāvitaṃ bastamūtreṇa sasnehaṃ devadāru ca || 242
kākamācīphalaikena ghṛtayuktena buddhimān
dhūpayetpillarogārtaṃ patanti kṛmayo'cirāt || 243
rasāñjanaṃ sarjaraso jātīpuṣpaṃ manaḥśilā
samudrapheno lavaṇaṃ gairikaṃ maricāni ca || 244
etatsamāṃśaṃ madhunā piṣṭaṃ praklinnavartmani
añjanaṃ kledakaṇḍūghnaṃ pakṣmaṇāṃ ca prarohaṇam || 245
tāmrapātre guhāmūlaṃ sindhūtthamaricānvitam
āranālena saṃghṛṣṭamañjanaṃ pillanāśanam || 246
haridre triphalā lodhraṃ madhukaṃ raktacandanam
bhṛṅgarājarase piṣṭvā gharṣayellohabhājane || 247
tathā tāmre ca saptāhaṃ kṛtvā vartiṃ rajo'tha vā
piccaṭī dhūmadarśī ca timiropahatekṣaṇaḥ
prātarniśyañjayennityaṃ sarvanetrāmayāpaham || 248
mañjiṣṭhāmadhukotpalodadhikaphatvaksevyagorocanā-
māṃsīcandanaśaṅkhapatragirimṛttālīsapuṣpāñjanaiḥ
sarvaireva samāṃśamañjanamidaṃ śastaṃ sadā cakṣuṣaḥ
kaṇḍūkledamalāśruśoṇitarujāpillārmaśukrāpaham || 249
tutthakasya palaṃ śvetamaricāni ca viṃśatiḥ
triṃśatā kāñjikapalaiḥ piṣṭvā tāmre nidhāpayet || 250
pillānapillānkurute bahuvarṣotthitānapi
tatsekenopadehāśrukaṇḍūśothāṃśca nāśayet || 251
karañjabījaṃ surasaṃ sumanaḥkorakāṇi ca || 252
saṃkṣudya sādhayetkvāthaṃ pūte tatra rasakriyā
añjanaṃ pillabhaiṣajyaṃ pakṣmaṇāṃ ca prarohaṇam || 253
puṣpakāsīsacūrṇaṃ vā surasārasabhāvitam
tāmre daśāhaṃ tatpillapakṣmarogajidañjanāt || 254
yāpyaḥ pakṣmoparodhastu romoddharaṇalakṣaṇaiḥ
vartmanyupacitaṃ lekhyaṃ srāvyamutkliṣṭaśoṇitam || 255
pravṛddhāntarmukhaṃ roma sahiṣṇoruddharecchanaiḥ
saṃdaṃśenoddhareddṛṣṭyālakṣyaromāṇi buddhimān
sūcyagreṇāgnivarṇena romakūpaṃ vinirdahet || 256
pradehāḥ śītalā nasyaṃ kriyā pakṣmāparodhahṛt || 257
rakṣannakṣi dahetpakṣma taptahemaśalākayā
pakṣmakope punarnaivaṃ kadācidromasaṃbhavaḥ || 258
triphalāvyoṣasindhūtthairghṛtaṃ siddhaṃ pibennaraḥ
cakṣuṣyaṃ bhedanaṃ hṛdyaṃ dīpanaṃ kaphanāśanam || 259
Adhikāra 62
vātike śiraso roge snehasvedānsanāvanān
pānānnamupanāhāṃśca kuryādvātāmayāpahān || 1
kuṣṭhameraṇḍamūlaṃ ca lepātkāñjikapeṣitam
śirortiṃ nāśayatyāśu puṣpaṃ vā mucukundajam || 2
pañcamūlīśṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ nasyaṃ dadyācchirogade || 3
āśiro vyāyataṃ carma ṣoḍaśāṅgulamucchritam
tenā''veṣṭya śiro'dhastānmāṣakalkena lepayet
niścalasyopaviṣṭasya tailairuṣṇaiḥ prapūrayet || 4
dhārayedārujaḥ śāntyai yāmaṃ yāmārdhameva ca
śirobastirjayatyeṣa śirorogaṃ marudbhavam || 5
hanumanyākṣikarṇārtimarditaṃ mūrdhakampanam
tailenā''pūrya mūrdhānaṃ pañca mātrāśatāni ca || 6
tiṣṭhecchleṣmaṇi pitte'ṣṭau daśa vāte śirogadī
eṣa eva vidhiḥ kāryastathā karṇākṣipūraṇe || 7
pittātmake śiroroge snigdhaṃ samyagvirecayet
mṛdvīkātriphalekṣūṇāṃ rasaiḥ kṣīrairghṛtairapi || 8
śarkarākṣīrasalilaiḥ śiraśca pratiṣecayet
sarpiṣaḥ śatadhautasya śirasā dhāraṇaṃ hitam
nimajjanaṃ ca śirasaḥ śītale śasyate'mbhasi || 9
kumudotpalapadmānāṃ śītānāṃ candanāmbubhiḥ
sparśāḥ sukhāśca pavanāḥ sevyā dāhārtiśāntaye || 10
candanośīrayaṣṭyāhvabalāvyāghranakhotpalaiḥ
kṣīrapiṣṭaiḥ pradehaḥ syācchṛtairvā pariṣecanam || 11
mṛṇālabisaśālūkacandanotpalakesaraiḥ
snigdhaśītaiḥ śiro dihyāttadvadāmalakotpalaiḥ || 12
yaṣṭyāhvacandanānantākṣīrasiddhaṃ hitaṃ ghṛtam
nāvanaṃ śarkarādrākṣāmadhukairvā'pi pittaje || 13
raktaje pittavatsarvaṃ bhojanālepasecanam
śītoṣṇayośca vyatyāso viśeṣo raktamokṣaṇam || 14
kaphaje laṅghanaṃ svedo rūkṣoṣṇaiḥ pācanātmakaiḥ
tīkṣṇāvapīḍā dhūmāśca tīkṣṇāśca kabalā hitāḥ || 15
acchaṃ ca pāyayetsarpiḥ purāṇaṃ svedayettataḥ
madhūkasāreṇa śiraḥ svinnaṃ cāsya virecayet || 16
pathyāhvaśuṇṭhīmadhukaśatāhvotpalapākalaiḥ
jalapiṣṭaiḥ śirolepaḥ sadyaḥ śūlanivāraṇaḥ || 17
devadāru nataṃ kuṣṭhaṃ naladaṃ viśvabheṣajam
lepaḥ kāñjikasaṃpiṣṭastailayuktaḥ śirortinut || 18
saṃnipātabhave kāryā doṣatrayaharī kriyā
sarpiṣpānaṃ viśeṣeṇa purāṇaṃ vā diśanti hi || 19
trikaṭukapuṣkarabījakarañjarāsnāturaṅgagandhānām
kvāthaḥ śirortijālaṃ nāsāpīto nivārayati || 20
nāgarakalkavimiśraṃ kṣīraṃ nasyena yojitaṃ puṃsām
nānādoṣodbhūtāṃ śirorujaṃ hanti tīvratarām || 21
śatāhvairaṇḍamūlogrāvakravyāghrīphalaiḥ śṛtam
tailaṃ nasyaṃ marucchleṣmatimirordhvagadāpaham || 22
jīvakarṣabhakau drākṣāsitāyaṣṭīdvayotpalaiḥ
tailaṃ nasyaṃ payaḥpakvaṃ vātapittaśirogade || 23
jīvakarṣabhakau drākṣā madhūkaṃ madhukaṃ balā
nīlotpalaṃ candanaṃ ca vidārī śarkarā tathā || 24
tailaprasthaṃ pacedebhiḥ śanaiḥ payasi ṣaḍguṇe
jāṅgalasya tu māṃsasya tulārdhasya rasena tu || 25
siddhametadbhavennasyaṃ tailamardhāvabhedake
bādhiryaṃ karṇaśūlaṃ ca timiraṃ galaśuṇḍikām || 26
vātikaṃ paittikaṃ caiva śirorogaṃ niyacchati
dantaśūlaṃ śiraḥśūlamarditaṃ cāpakarṣati || 27
eraṇḍamūlaṃ tagaraṃ śatāhvā jīvantirāsnā lavaṇottamaṃ ca
bhṛṅgaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ madhuyaṣṭikā ca viśvauṣadhaṃ kṛṣṇatilasya tailam || 28
ājaṃ payastailavimiśritaṃ ca caturguṇe bhṛṅgarase vipakvam
ṣaḍbindavo nāsikayā ca śīghraṃ sarvānnihanyuḥ śiraso vikārān || 29
cyutāṃśca keśāṃścalitāṃśca dantāndurbaddhamūlāṃśca dṛḍhī karoti
suparṇadṛṣṭipratimaṃ ca cakṣurbāhvorbalaṃ cābhyadhikaṃ dadāti || 30
kṣayaje kṣayamāsādya kartavyo bṛṃhaṇo vidhiḥ
pāne nasye ca sarpiḥ syādvātaghnamadhuraiḥ śṛtam || 31
kṛmije vyoṣanaktāhva(ṅka)śigrubījaiśca nāvanam || 32
ajāmūtrayutaṃ nasyaṃ kṛmije kṛmijitparam
nāvanaṃ saguḍaṃ viśvaṃ pippalī vā sasaindhavā
bhujastambhādirogeṣu sarveṣūrdhvagadeṣu ca || 33
sūryāvarte vidhātavyaṃ nasyakarmādibheṣajam
pāyayetsaguḍaṃ sarpirghṛtapūrāṃśca bhakṣayet || 34
sūryāvarte śirāvedho nāvanaṃ kṣīrasarpiṣā || 35
hitaḥ kṣīraghṛtābhyāsastābhyāṃ caiva virecanam
kṣīrapiṣṭaistilaiḥ svedo jīvanīyaiśca śasyate || 36
saśarkaraṃ kuṅkumamājyabhṛṣṭaṃ nasyaṃ vidheyaṃ pavanāsṛgutthe
bhrūśaṅkhakarṇākṣiśirordhaśūle dinābhivṛddhiprabhave ca roge || 37
śirīṣamūlakaphalairavapīḍaṃ ca yojayet
avapīḍo hito vā syādvacāpippalibhiḥ kṛtaḥ || 38
jāṅgalāni ca māṃsāni kārayedupanāhanam
tenāsya śāmyate vyādhiḥ sūryāvartaḥ sudāruṇaḥ || 39
mahauṣadhaṃ sasvarasaṃ vacāpippalibhiḥ kṛtam
avapīḍaṃ pradātavyaṃ sūryāvartaniṣūdanam
eṣa eva vidhiḥ kṛtsnaḥ kāryaścārdhāvabhedake || 40
sārivotpalakuṣṭhāni madhukaṃ cāmlapeṣitam
sarpistailayuto lepaḥ sūryāvartārdhabhedayoḥ || 41
eṣa eva prayoktavyaḥ śiroroge kaphātmake || 42
pibetsaśarkaraṃ kṣīraṃ nīraṃ vā nālikerajam
suśītaṃ vā'pi pānīyaṃ sarpirvā nastatasta(nāsayā ta)yoḥ || 43
anantavāte kartavyaḥ sūryāvartahito vidhiḥ
śirāvedhaśca kartavyo'nantavātapraśāntaye || 44
āhāraśca vidhātavyo vātapittavināśanaḥ
madhumastakasaṃyāvahaviṣpūraiśca yaḥ kramaḥ || 45
sūryāvarte hitaṃ yacca śaṅkhake svedavarjitam
kṣīrasarpiḥ praśaṃsanti nasye pāne ca śaṅkhake || 46
śatāvarīṃ kṛṣṇatilānmadhukaṃ nīlamutpalam
dūrvāṃ punarnavāṃ cāpi lepaṃ sādhvavacārayet || 47
śītatoyāvasekāṃśca kṣīrasekāṃśca śītalān
kalkaiśca kṣīrivṛkṣāṇāṃ śaṅkhakasya pralepanam || 48
krauñcakādambahaṃsānāṃ śarāryāḥ kacchapasya ca
rasaiḥ saṃbṛṃhitasyātha tasya śaṅkhasya saṃdhijāḥ
ūrdhvaṃ tisraḥ śirāḥ prājño bhindyādeva na tāḍayet || 49
yaṣṭīmadhubalārāsnādaśamūlāmbusādhitam
madhuraiśca ghṛtaṃ siddhamūrdhvajatrugadāpaham || 50
daśamūlabalārāsnāmadhukaistriphalaiḥ saha
mayūraṃ pakṣapittāntraśakṛtpādāsyavarjitam || 51
jale paktvā ghṛtaprasthaṃ tasminkṣīrasamaṃ pacet
madhuraiḥ kārṣikaiḥ kalkaiḥ śirorogārditāpaham || 52
karṇanāsākṣijihvāsyagalarogavināśanam
mayūrādyamidaṃ khyātamūrdhvajatrugadāpaham || 53
prapauṇḍarīkamadhukapippalīcandanotpalaiḥ || 54
siddhaṃ dhātrīrase tailaṃ nasyenābhyañjanena vā
sarvānūrdhvagadānhanti palitāni ca śīlitam || 55
Adhikāra 63
dadhnā sauvarcalājājīmadhukaṃ nīlamutpalam
pibetkṣaudrayutaṃ nārī vātāsṛgdarapīḍitā || 1
pibedaiṇeyakaṃ raktaṃ śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam
vāsakasvarasaṃ paitte guḍūcyā rasameva ca || 2
rohītakānmūlakalkaṃ pāṇḍure'sṛgdare pibet
jalenā''malakādbījakalkaṃ vā sasitāmadhu || 3
dhātakyāścākṣamātraṃ vā āmalakyāṃ madhudravam
kākajāndhakamūlaṃ vā mūlaṃ kārṣāsameva vā
pāṇḍupradaraśāntyarthaṃ pibettaṇḍulavāriṇā || 4
aśokavalkalakvāthaśṛtaṃ dugdhaṃ suśītalam
yathābalaṃ pibetprātastīvrāsṛgdaranāśanam || 5
dārvīrasāñjanakirātavṛṣābdatiktā-
bhallātakāravikṛto madhunā kaṣāyaḥ
pīto jayatyatibalaṃ pradaraṃ saśūlaṃ
pītāsitāruṇavilohitanīlaśuklam || 6
rasāñjanaṃ taṇḍulakasya mūlaṃ kṣaudrānvitaṃ taṇḍulatoyapītam
asṛgdaraṃ sarvabhavaṃ nihanti śvāsaṃ ca bhārgī saha nāgareṇa || 7
kuśamūlaṃ samāhṛtya peṣayettaṇḍulāmbunā
etatpītvā tryahaṃ nārī pradarātparimucyate || 8
kṣaudrayuktaṃ phalarasaṃ kāṣṭhodumbarajaṃ pibet
asṛgdaravināśāya saśarkarapayonnabhuk || 9
pradaraṃ hanti balāyā mūlaṃ dugdhena madhuyutaṃ pītam
kuśavāṭyālakamūlaṃ taṇḍulasalilena raktākhyam || 10
śamayati madirāpānaṃ tadubhayamapi raktasaṃjñaśuklākhyau || 11
guḍena badarīcūrṇaṃ mocamājaṃ tathā payaḥ
pītā lākṣā ca saghṛtā pṛthakśoṇitanāśinī || 12
taruṇyā hitasevinyāstadalpopadravaṃ bhiṣak
raktapittavidhānena yathāvatsamupācaret || 13
pāṭhājambvāmrayormadhyaṃ śilābhedaṃ rasāñjanam
ambaṣṭhakīmocarasaṃ samaṅgā padmakesaram || 14
bāhlīkātiviṣe mustaṃ bilvaṃ lodhraṃ sagairikam
kaṭphalaṃ maricaṃ śuṇṭhīmṛdvīkāraktacandanam || 15
kaṭvaṅgavatsakānantādhātakīmadhukārjunam
puṣyeṇoddhṛtya tulyāni sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet || 16
tāni kṣaudreṇa saṃyojya pāyayettaṇḍulāmbunā
arśassu cātisāreṣu raktaṃ yaccopaveśyate || 17
doṣāgantukṛtā ye ca bālānāṃ tāṃśca nāśayet
yonidoṣaṃ rajodoṣaṃ śvetaṃ nīlaṃ ca pītakam || 18
strīṇāṃ śyāvāruṇaṃ yacca tatprasahya nivartayet
cūrṇaṃ puṣyānugaṃ nāma hitamātreyapūjitam || 19
mudgamāṣasya niryūhe rāsnācitrakanāgaraiḥ
siddhaṃ sapippalīviśvaiḥ sarpiḥ śreṣṭhamasṛgdare || 20
śatāvarīrasaprasthaṃ kṣodayitvā'vapīḍayet
ghṛtaprasthasamāyuktaṃ kṣīradviguṇitaṃ bhiṣak || 21
ataḥ kalkānimāndadyātsthūlodumbarasaṃmitān
jīvanīyāni yānyaṣṭau yaṣṭīcandanapadmakaiḥ || 22
śvadaṃṣṭrā cā''tmaguptā ca balā nāgabalā tathā
śāliparṇī pṛṣṭhaparṇī vidārī sārivādvayam || 23
śarkarā ca samā deyā kāśmaryāśca phalāni ca
samyaksiddhaṃ ca vijñāya taddhṛtaṃ cāvacārayet || 24
raktapittavikāreṣu vātapittakṛteṣu ca
vātaraktaṃ kṣayaṃ śvāsaṃ hikkāṃ kāsaṃ ca dustaram || 25
aṅgadāhaṃ śirodāhaṃ raktapittasamudbhavam
asṛgdaraṃ sarvabhavaṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca dāruṇam
etānrogāñśamayati bhāskarastimiraṃ yathā || 26
kumudaṃ padmakośīraṃ godhūmā raktaśālayaḥ || 27
mudgaparṇī payasyā ca kāśmarī madhuyaṣṭikā
balātibalayormūlamutpalaṃ tālamastakam || 28
vidārī śatapu(pa)trī ca śāliparṇī sajīvakā
phalaṃ trapusabījāni pratyagraṃ kadalīphalam || 29
eṣāmardhapalānbhāgāngavyakṣīraṃ caturguṇam
pānīyaṃ dviguṇaṃ dattvā ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 30
pradare raktagulme ca raktapitte halīmake
bahurūpaṃ ca yatpittaṃ kāmalāyāṃ saśoṇite || 31
arocake jvare jīrṇe pāṇḍuroge made bhrame
taruṇī cālpapuṣpā ca yā ca garbhaṃ na vindati || 32
ahanyahani ca strīṇāṃ bhavati prītivardhanam
śītakalyāṇakaṃ nāma paramuktaṃ rasāyanam || 33
hitaścātra viśeṣeṇa leho yaḥ kuṭajāṣṭakaḥ || 34
Adhikāra 64
yonivyāpatsu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ śasyate karma vātajit
bastyabhyaṅgaparīṣekapralepapicudhāraṇam || 1
vacopakuñcikājājīkṛṣṇāvṛṣakasaindhavam
ajamodāyavakṣāracitrakaṃ śarkarānvitam || 2
piṣṭvā prasannayā''loḍya khādettadghṛtabharjitam
yonipārśvārtihṛdrogagulmārśovinivṛttaye || 3
rāsnāśvadaṃṣṭrāvṛṣakaiḥ śṛtaṃ śūlaharaṃ payaḥ
guḍūcītriphalādantīkvāthaiśca pariṣecanam || 4
natavārtākinīkuṣṭhasaindhavāmaradārubhiḥ
tailātprasādhitāddhāryaḥ picuryonau rujāpahaḥ || 5
pittalānāṃ tu yonīnāṃ sekābhyaṅgapicukriyāḥ
śītapittaharāḥ kāryāḥ snehanārthaṃ ghṛtāni ca || 6
yonyāṃ balāsaduṣṭāyāṃ sarvaṃ rūkṣoṣṇamauṣadham || 7
pippalyā maricairmāṣaiḥ śatāhvākuṣṭhasaindhavaiḥ
vartistulyā pradeśinyā dhāryā yoniviśodhanī || 8
puṣyoddhṛtaṃ lakṣmaṇāyā mūlaṃ piṣṭaṃ ca kanyayā
ṛtvante ghṛtadugdhābhyāṃ pītvā''pnotyabalā sutam || 9
kvāthena hayagandhāyāḥ sādhitaṃ saghṛtaṃ payaḥ
ṛtusnātā'balā pītvā garbhaṃ dhatte na saṃśayaḥ || 10
mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ triphalā śarkarā balā
medā payasyā kākolī mūlaṃ caivāśvagandhajam || 11
ajamodā haridre dve hiṅgukaṃ kaṭurohiṇī
utpalaṃ kumudaṃ drākṣākākolyau candanadvayam || 12
eteṣāṃ kārṣikairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet
śatāvarīrasaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtāddeyaṃ caturguṇam || 13
sarpiretannaraḥ pītvā strīṣu nityaṃ vṛṣāyate
putrāñjanayate nārī medhāḍhyānpriyadarśanān || 14
yā ca vā'sthiragarbhā syādyā vā janayate mṛtam
alpāyuṣaṃ ca janayedyā ca kanyāṃ prasūyate || 15
yonidoṣe rajodoṣe garbhasrāve ca śasyate
prajāvardhanamāyuṣyaṃ sarvagrahanivāraṇam || 16
nāmnā phalaghṛtaṃ caitadaśvibhyāṃ parikīrtitam
anuktaṃ lakṣmaṇāmūlaṃ kṣipantyatra cikitsakāḥ || 17
kuṣṭhaṃ mustaṃ haridre dve pippalī kaṭurohiṇī
kākolī kṣīrakākolī viśālā triphalotpalam
medārāsnāviḍaṅgāni devadāru sahiṅgukam || 18
dve sārive śatāhvā ca vacā yaṣṭī priyaṃgukā
ajamodā mahāmedā candanaṃ raktacandanam || 19
jātīpuṣpaṃ tugākṣīrī kaṭphalaṃ sitaśarkarā
etairakṣasamaiḥ kalkairghṛtaprasthaṃ bhiṣaktamaḥ || 20
caturguṇena payasā vipacedgomayāgninā
śubhe'hni puṣyasaṃyukte bhāṇḍe tāmramaye dṛḍhe || 21
kalaśe hemakalyāṇe kṛtakautukamaṅgale
sarpiretannaraḥ pītvā strīṣu nityaṃ vṛṣāyate || 22
yā ca vandhyā bhavennārī yā ca kanyāprajāyinī(nyāṃ prasūyate)
yā ca vā'sthiragarbhā syādyā ca sūtvā punaḥ sthitā || 23
alpāyuṣaṃ ca janayedyā vā janayate mṛtam
tādṛśaṃ janayetputraṃ vedavedāṅgapāragam || 24
rūpalāvaṇyasaṃpannaṃ śatāyuṃ vigatajvaram
nāmnā phalaghṛtaṃ hyetadbhāradvājena nirmitam || 25
siddhārthakaṃ vacāṃ brāhmīṃ śaṅkhapuṣpīṃ viṣāṇikām
payasyāṃ madhukaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ tathā kaṭukarohiṇīm || 26
sārivāṃ triphalāṃ caiva corakaṃ sumanolatām
vṛṣapuṣpaṃ samañjiṣṭhaṃ devadāru mahauṣadham || 27
pippalyau bhṛṅgarājaṃ ca niśāṃ śyāmāṃ suvarcalām
daśamūlamapāmārgamaśvagandhāṃ śatāvarīm || 28
jaladroṇe pacedetānbhāgairdvipalikaiḥ pṛthak
tatkaṣāyaṃ parisrāvya ghṛtasyārdhāḍhakaṃ pacet || 29
yuktyā pradāpayedetadgāyatryā cābhimantritam
dvimāsagarbhiṇī nārī aṣṭamāsānprayojayet || 30
sarvajñaṃ janayetputraṃ sarvāmayavivarjitam
asya prayogātkukṣisthaḥ sphuṭavāgvyāharatyapi || 31
yoniduṣṭāśca yā nāryaḥ śukraduṣṭāśca ye narāḥ
vandhyā ca labhate putraṃ śūraṃ paṇḍitamāninam || 32
jaḍagadgadamūkaṃ ca pānādeva praśāmyati
saptarātraprayogeṇa susvaraṃ kurute naram || 33
māsatrayopayogena kuryācchrutidharaṃ naram
nāgnirdahati tadveśma na vajramupahanti ca
na tatra mriyate bālo yatrā''ste somasaṃjñitam || 34
nīlotpalośīramadhūkayaṣṭīdrākṣāvidārīkuśapañcamūlaiḥ
syājjīvanīyaiśca ghṛtaṃ vipakvaṃ śatāvarīkārasadugdhamiśram || 35
taccharkarāpādayutaṃ praśastamasṛgdare mārutaraktapittaje
kṣīṇe bale retasi ca pranaṣṭe kṛcchre ca pittaprabhave ca gulme || 36
ākhormāṃsaṃ sapadi bahudhā khaṇḍakhaṇḍīkṛtaṃ ya-
ttailaṃ pācyaṃ dravati niyataṃ yāvadetanna samyak
tattailāktaṃ vasanamaniśaṃ yonibhāge dadhānā
hanti vrīḍākarabhagaphalaṃ nātra saṃdehabuddhiḥ || 37
Adhikāra 65
madhukaṃ śākabījaṃ ca payasyā suradāru ca
aśmantakaḥ kṛṣṇatilāstāmravallī śatāvarī || 1
vṛkṣādanī payasyā ca tathaivotpalasārivā
anantā sārivā rāsnā padmā madhukameva ca || 2
bṛhatīdvayakāśmaryakṣīriśuṅgāstvaco ghṛtam
pṛthakparṇī balāśigruśvadaṃṣṭrāmadhuyaṣṭikam || 3
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ bisaṃ drākṣā kaseru madhukaṃ sitā
māseṣu sapta yogāḥ syurardhaślokāstu saptasu
yathākramaṃ prayoktavyā garbhasrāve payoyutāḥ || 4
kapitthabilvabṛhatīpaṭolīkṣunidigdhikāḥ
mūlāni kṣīrasiddhāni dāpayedbhiṣagaṣṭame || 5
navame madhukānantāpayasyāsārivāḥ pibet
payastu daśame śuṇṭyāḥ śṛtaśītaṃ praśasyate || 6
sakṣīrā vā hitā śuṇṭhī madhukaṃ devadāru ca
evamāpyāyate garbhastīvrā ruk copaśāmyati || 7
samadhucchāgadugdhenakulālakarakardamaḥ
avaśyaṃ sthāpayedgarbhaṃ calitaṃ pānayogataḥ || 8
kuśakāśorubūkāṇāṃ mūlairgokṣurakasya ca
śṛtaṃ dugdhaṃ sitāyuktaṃ garbhiṇyāḥ śūlanutparam || 9
kaseruśṛṅgāṭakajīvanīryaiḥ padmotpalairaṇḍaśatāvarībhiḥ
siddhaṃ payaḥ śarkarayā vimiśraṃ saṃsthāpayedgarbhamudīrṇaśūlam || 10
kaseruśṛṅgāṭakapadmakotpalaṃ samudgayaṣṭīmadhukaṃ saśarkaram
saśūlagarbhasrutipīḍitāṅganā payovimiśraṃ payasā'nnabhukpibet || 11
pāṭhāsurasasiṃhāsyamayūrakuṭajaiḥ pṛthak
nābhibastibhagālepātsukhaṃ nārī prasūyate || 12
parūṣakaśiphālepaḥ sthirāmūlakṛto'thavā
nābhibastibhagādyeṣu mūḍhagarbhāpakarṣaṇaḥ || 13
mātuluṅgasya mūlāni madhukaṃ madhusaṃyutam
ghṛtena saha pātavyaṃ sukhaṃ nārī prasūyate || 14
indro'mṛtaṃ ca somaśca citrabhānuśca bhāmini
uccaiḥśravāśca turago mandire nivasantu te || 15
idamamṛtamapāṃ samuddhṛtaṃ vai tava laghu garbhāmimaṃ vimuñcatu stri
tadanalapavanārkavāsavāste saha lavaṇāmbudharairdiśantu śāntim || 16
muktāḥ pāśā vipāśāśca muktāḥ sūryeṇa raśmayaḥ
muktaḥ sarvabhayādgarbha ehyehi mā ciraṃ svāhā || 17
jalaṃ cyāvanamantreṇa saptavārābhimantritam
pītvā prasūyate nārī dṛṣṭvā cobhayatriṃśakam || 18
nāḍyṛtuvasubhiḥ pakṣadigaṣṭādaśabhireva ca
arkabhuvanābdhisahitairubhayatriṃśakamāścaryam || 19
kaṭutumbyahinirmokakṛtavedhanasarṣapaiḥ
kaṭutailānvitairyonidhūpaḥ pātayate'parām || 20
kacaveṣṭitayā'ṅgulyā ghṛṣṭe kaṇṭhe sukhaṃ patatyaparā
mūlena lāṅgalikyāḥ saṃlipte pāṇipāde vā || 21
upakuñcikāṃ pippalīṃ ca madirāṃ lābhataḥ pibet
sauvarcalena saṃyuktāṃ yoniśūlanivāriṇīm || 22
sūtāyā hṛcchirobastiśūlaṃ makkallasaṃjñitam
yavakṣāraṃ pibettatra sarpiṣoṣṇodakena vā || 23
pippalyādigaṇakvāthaṃ pibedvā lavaṇānvitam || 24
pārāvataśakṛtpītaṃ śālitaṇḍulavāriṇā
garbhapātānantarottharaktasrāvanivāraṇam || 25
amṛtānāgarasahacarabhadretkaṭapañcamūlajaladajalam
śṛtaśītaṃ madhuyuktaṃ nivārayati sūtikātaṅkam || 26
sahacarapuṣkaravetasamūlaṃ vaikaṅkatadārukulatthasamam
jalamantrasasaindhavahiṅguyutaṃ sadyo jvarasūtikāśūlaharam || 27
daśamūlīrasaḥ pūtaḥ sadyaḥ sūtīrujāpahaḥ
pañcamūlasya vā kvāthaṃ taptalohena saṃyutam
sūtikāroganāśāya pibedvā taddhitāṃ surām || 28
pippalī pippalīmūlaṃ cavyaṃ śuṇṭhī yavānikā
jīrake dve haridre dve biḍaṃ sauvarcalaṃ tathā || 29
etairevauṣadhaiḥ piṣṭairāranālaṃ vipācayet
āmavātaharaṃ vṛṣyaṃ kaphagnaṃ vahnidīpanam || 30
kāñjikaṃ vajrakaṃ nāma strīṇāṃ balavivardhanam
makkallaśūlaśamanaṃ paraṃ kṣīrābhivardhanam || 31
vanakarpāsikekṣūṇāṃ mūlaṃ sauvīrakeṇa vā || 32
vidārikandaṃ surayā pivedvā stanyavardhanam
haridrādiṃ vacādiṃ vā pibetstanyaviśuddhaye || 33
tatra vātātmake stanye daśamūlījalaṃ pibet
pittaduṣṭe'mṛtābhīrupaṭolīnimbacandanam
dhātrī kumāraśca pibetkvāthayitvā sasārivam || 34
śophaṃ stanotthitamavekṣya bhiṣagvidadhyā-
dyadvidradhāvabhihitaṃ bahudhā vidhānam
āme vidahyati tathaiva gate ca pākaṃ
tasyāḥ stanau satatameva hi nirduhīta || 35
viśālāmūlalepastu hanti pīḍāṃ stanotthitām || 36
Adhikāra 66
kuṣṭhaṃ vacā'bhayā brāhmī kanakaṃ kṣaudrasarpiṣā
varṇāyuṣkāntijananaṃ lehaṃ bālasya dāpayet
stanyābhāve payaśchāgaṃ gavyaṃ vā tadguṇaṃ pibet || 1
mṛtpiṇḍenāgnitaptena kṣīrasiktena soṣmaṇā
svedayeducchritāṃ nābhiṃ śophastenopaśāmyati || 2
nābhipāke niśālodhrapriyaṅgumadhukaiḥ śṛtam
tailamabhyañjane śastamebhirvā'pyavacūrṇitam || 3
haridrādvayayaṣṭyāhvasiṃhīśakrayavaiḥ kṛtaḥ
śiśorjvarātisāraghnaḥ kaṣāyaḥ stanyadoṣajit || 4
ghanakṛṣṇāruṇāśṛṅgīcūrṇaṃ kṣaudreṇa saṃyutam
śiśorjvarātisāraghnaṃ kāsaśvāsavamīharam || 5
dhātakībilvadhānyākalodhrendrayavavālakaiḥ
lehaḥ kṣaudreṇa bālānāṃ jvarātīsāravāntinut || 6
rajanīdārusaralaśreyasībṛhatīdvayam
pṛśniparṇī śatāhvā ca līḍhaṃ mākṣikasarpiṣā || 7
grahaṇīdīpanaṃ hanti mārutārti sakāmalām
jvarātīsārapāṇḍutvaṃ bālānāṃ sarvarogajit || 8
miśikṛṣṇāñjanairlājaśṛṅgīmaricamākṣikaiḥ
lehaḥ śiśorvidhātavyaśchardikāsajvarāpahaḥ || 9
śṛṅgī samustātiviṣāṃ vicūrṇya lehaṃ vidadhyānmadhunā śiśūnām
kāsajvaracchardibhirarditānāṃ samākṣikāṃ vā'tiviṣāmathaikām || 10
āmrāsthilājasindhūtthairlehaḥ kṣaudreṇa cchardinut || 11
pītaṃ pītaṃ vamedyastu stanyaṃ taṃ madhusarpiṣā
dvivārtākīphalarasaṃ pañcakolaṃ ca lehayet || 12
ekadvitrāṇi ghasrāṇi vātapittakaphajvare
kṣīrapasyāhitaṃ sarpiranyayormadhyadehavat || 13
bilvaṃ ca puṣpāṇi ca dhātakīnāṃ jalaṃ salodhraṃ gajapippalī ca
kvāthāvalehau madhunā vimiśrau bāleṣu yojyāvatisāriteṣu || 14
samaṅgādhātakīlodhrasārivābhiḥ śṛtaṃ jalam
durdhare'pi śiśordeyamatisāre samākṣikam || 15
nāgarātiviṣāmustāvālakendrayavaiḥ śṛtam
kumāraṃ pāyayetprātaḥ sarvātīsāranāśanam || 16
mocarasaḥ samaṅgā ca dhātakī padmakesaram
piṣṭairetairyavāgūḥ syādraktātīsāranāśinī || 17
kalkaḥ priyaṅgukolāsthimadhyamustarasāñjanaiḥ
kṣaudralīḍhaḥ kumārasya cchardistṛṣṇātisāranut || 18
lehastailasitākṣaudratilayaṣṭyāhvakalkitaḥ
bālasya rundhyānniyataṃ raktāsrāvaṃ pravāhikām || 19
lājā sayaṣṭimadhukaṃ śarkarā kṣaudrameva ca
taṇḍulodakasaṃyuktaṃ kṣipraṃ hanti pravāhikām || 20
gudapāke tu bālānāṃ pittaghnīṃ kārayetkriyām
rasāñjanaṃ viśeṣeṇa pānābhyañjanayorhitam || 21
kaṇoṣaṇasitākṣaudrasūkṣmailāsaindhavaiḥ kṛtaḥ
mūtragrahe prayoktavyaḥ śiśūnāṃ leha uttamaḥ || 22
ghṛtena sindhuviśvailāhiṅgubhārgīrajo lihan
ānāhaṃ vātikaṃ śūlaṃ jayettoyena vā śiśuḥ || 23
harītakīvacākuṣṭhakalkaṃ mākṣikasaṃyutam
pītvā kumāraḥ stanyena mucyate tālupātanāt || 24
gṛhadhūmaniśākuṣṭharājikendrayavaiḥ śiśoḥ
lepastakreṇa hantyāśu sidhmapāmāvicarcikāḥ || 25
pañcamūlīkaṣāyeṇa saghṛtena payaḥ śṛtam
saśṛṅgaveraṃ saguḍaṃ śītaṃ hikkārditaḥ pibet || 26
drākṣāyāsābhayākṛṣṇācūrṇaṃ sakṣaudrasarpiṣā
līḍhaṃ śvāsaṃ nihantyāśu kāsaṃ ca tamakaṃ tathā || 27
puṣkarātiviṣāśṛṅgīmāgadhīdhanvayāsakaiḥ
cūrṇaṃ tu madhunā līḍhaṃ bālānāṃ pañcakāsajit || 28
bhaiṣajyaṃ pūrvamuddiṣṭaṃ mahatāṃ yajjvarādiṣu
kāryaṃ gadeṣu bālānāṃ teṣu dāhādikaṃ vinā || 29
ta eva doṣā dūṣyāśca jvarādyā vyādhayaśca te
atastadeva bhaiṣajyaṃ mātrā tvasya kanīyasī || 30
Adhikāra 67
sahāmuṇḍitikodīcyakvāthasnānaṃ grahāpaham
saptacchadāmayaniśācandanaiścānulepanam || 1
sarpatvaglaśunaṃ mūrvā sarṣapāriṣṭapallavāḥ
biḍālaviḍajāloma meṣaśṛṅgī vacā madhu
dhūpaḥ śiśorjvaraghno'yamaśeṣagrahanāśanaḥ || 2
baliśāntīṣṭikarmāṇi kāryāṇi grahaśāntaye || 3
mantraścāyaṃ prayoktavyastatrā''dau sārvakārmi(mi)kaḥ
kaṃ ṭaṃ yaṃ śaṃ vainateyāya namaḥ | oṃṃ hvāṃ hvīṃ kṣaḥ || 4
bāladehapramāṇena puṣpamālāṃ tu sarvataḥ
pragṛhya muṣṭikābhaktaṃ balirdeyastu śāntikaḥ
oṃkārī svarṇapakṣī bālakaṃ rakṣa rakṣa svāhā || 5
vacākuṣṭhaṃ tathā brāhmī siddhārthakamathāpi vā
sārivā saindhavaṃ caiva pippalī ghṛtamaṣṭamam || 6
medhyaṃ ghṛtamidaṃ siddhaṃ pātavyaṃ ca dine dine
dṛḍhasmṛtiḥ kṣipramedhāḥ kumāro buddhimānbhavet || 7
na piśācā na rakṣāṃsi na bhūtā na ca mātaraḥ
prabādhante kumārāṇāṃ pibatāmaṣṭamaṅgalam || 8
pādakalke'śvagandhāyāḥ kṣīre daśaguṇe pacet
ghṛtaṃ peyaṃ kumārāṇāṃ puṣṭikṛdbalavardhanam || 9
lākṣārasasamaṃ siddhaṃ tailaṃ mastucaturguṇam
rāsnācandanakuṣṭhābdavājigandhāniśāyugaiḥ || 10
śatāhvādāruyaṣṭyāhvamūrvātiktāhareṇubhiḥ
bālānāṃ jvararakṣoghnamabhyaṅgādbalavarṇakṛt || 11
piṣṭvā karkaṭakaṃ tailaṃ sakṣīraṃ sādhitaṃ tu tat
pādābhyaṅgena bālasya dantadhvaninivāraṇam || 12
Adhikāra 68
mūlaṃ taṇḍulavāriṇā pibati yaḥ pratyaṅgirāsaṃbhavaṃ
niṣpiṣṭaṃ śucibhadrayogadivase tasyāhibhītiḥ kutaḥ
darpādeva phaṇī yadā daśati taṃ mohānvito mūlapaṃ
sthāne tatra tadaiva yāti niyataṃ vaktraṃ yamasyācirāt || 1
mayūraṃ nimbapatrābhyāṃ khādenmeṣagate ravau
abdamekaṃ na bhītiḥ syādviṣārtasya na saṃśayaḥ || 2
daṃśasyopari badhnīyāttatkṣaṇāccaturaṅgule
kṣaumādibhirveṇikayā siddhairmantraiśca mantravit || 3
ambuvatsetubandhena bandhena stabhyate viṣam
na vahanti śirāścāsya marmasaṃdhigatā api
na jāyate viṣodvego bījanāśādivāṅkuraḥ || 4
sattvamālambya daṣṭavyastatkṣaṇādurago ripuḥ
komalaṃ vā yadanyacca peyaṃ śītaṃ ghṛtaṃ gavām || 5
vācyo vā nīlakaṇṭho'haṃ dhyeyā vā gāruḍī tanuḥ
śūnyatādhyānamātreṇa śūnyatāṃ yāti tadviṣam || 6
śleṣmaṇaḥ karṇagūthasya vāmānāmikayā kṛtaḥ
lepo hanyādviṣaṃ ghoraṃ nṛmūtrāsecanaṃ tathā || 7
kulikamūlanasyena kāladaṣṭo'pi jīvati || 8
piṇḍītagarakamūlaṃ puṣyeṇotpāṭya yojitaṃ daṃśe
mṛtamapi daṣṭakapuruṣaṃ cālayatītyatra no citram || 9
śirīṣapatrasvarase saptāhaṃ maricaṃ sitam
bhāvitaṃ sarpadaṣṭānāṃ pāne nasyāñjane hitam || 10
naktamālaphalavyoṣabilvamūlaniśādvayam
saurasaṃ puṣpamārjaṃ ca mūtraṃ bodhanamañjanam || 11
vandhyākarkoṭakīmūlaṃ chāgamūtreṇa bhāvitam
nasyaṃ kāñjikasaṃpiṣṭaṃ viṣopahatacetasaḥ || 12
trivṛdviśalyā madhukaṃ haridre māñjiṣṭhavargo lavaṇaṃ ca sarvam
kaṭutrikaṃ caiva vicūrṇitāni śṛṅge nidadhyānmadhusaṃyutāni || 13
eṣo'gado hantyupayujyamānaḥ pānāñjanābhyañjananasyayogaiḥ
avāryavīryo viṣavegahantā mahāgado nāma mahāprabhāvaḥ || 14
kaṭabhyarjunasaireyaśelukṣīridrumatvacaḥ
kaṣāyakalkacūrṇāḥ syuḥ kīṭalūtāvraṇāpahāḥ || 15
āgāradhūmamañjiṣṭhārajanīlavaṇottamaiḥ
lepo jayatyākhuviṣaṃ karṇikāyāśca pātanam || 16
yaḥ kāsamardamūlaṃ vadane prakṣipya karṇaphūtkāram
manujo dadāti śīghraṃ jayati viṣaṃ vṛścikānāṃ saḥ || 17
rajanīsaindhavakṣaudrasaṃyuktaṃ ghṛtamuttamam
pānaṃ mūlaviṣārtasya digdhaviddhasya ceṣyate || 18
catuṣpādbhirdvipādbhirvā nakhadantakṣataṃ tu yat
śūyate pacyate cāpi sravati jvarayatyapi || 19
somavalko'śvakarṇaśca gojihvā haṃsapādyapi
rajanyau gairikaṃ lepo nakhadantaviṣāpahaḥ || 20
vacāhiṅguviḍaṅgāni saindhavaṃ gajapippalī
pāṭhā prativiṣā vyoṣaṃ kāśyapena vinirmitam
daśāṅgamagadaṃ pītvā sarvakīṭaviṣaṃ jayet || 21
kīṭadaṣṭakriyāḥ sarvāḥ samānāḥ syurjalaukasām || 22
chatrī jharjharapāṇiśca caredrātrau tathā divā
tacchāyāśabdasaṃtrastāḥ praṇaśyantīha pannagāḥ || 23
sotkampaḥ pulakāvṛtaḥ prati muhurvaktraṃ samālokate
dantenādharapallavaṃ daśati cetsītkānvitaṃ kūjati
yastāpaṃ jaḍatāṃ ca yāti nitarāṃ daṣṭaḥ sa utkaṇṭhate
ra(ga)ntuṃ tvasthiśalāmalāmbaravatīṃ raudrāṃ śmaśānasthalīm || 24
viruddhādhyaśanakrodhakṣudbhayoṣṇaṃ ca maithunam
varjayedviṣamukto'pi divāsvapnaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 25
Adhikāra 69
yajjarāvyādhividhvaṃsi bheṣajaṃ tadrasāyanam
pūrve vayasi madhye vā śuddhakāyaḥ samācaret || 1
aviśuddhaśarīrasya yukto rāsāyano vidhiḥ
na bhāti vāsasi kliṣṭe raṅgayoga ivārpitaḥ || 2
sindhūtthaśarkarāśuṇṭhīkaṇāmadhuguḍaiḥ kramāt
varṣādiṣvabhayā sevyā rasāyanaguṇaiṣiṇā || 3
traiphalenā''yasīṃ pātrīṃ kalkenā''lepayennavām
tamāhorātrikaṃ lepaṃ pibetkṣaudrodakāplutam || 4
prabhūtasnehamaśanaṃ jīrṇe tasminprayojayet
ajaro'ruksamābhyāsājjīveccāpi samāḥ śatam || 5
maṇḍūkaparṇyāḥ svarasaḥ prayojyaḥ kṣīreṇa yaṣṭīmadhukasya cūrṇam
raso guḍūcyāstu samūlapuṣpyāḥ kalkaḥ prayojyaḥ khalu śaṅkhapuṣpyāḥ || 6
āyuṣpradānyāmayanāśanāni balāgnivarṇasvaravardhanāni
medhyāni caitāni rasāyanāni medhyā viśeṣeṇa tu śaṅkhapuṣpī || 7
ye māsamekaṃ svarasaṃ pibanti dine dine bhṛṅgarajaḥsamutham
kṣīrāśinaste balavarṇayuktāḥ samāḥ śataṃ jīvitamāpnuvanti || 8
asitatilavimiśrānpallavānbhakṣayedyaḥ
satatamiha payośī bhṛṅgarājasya māsam
bhavati sa cirajīvī vyādhibhirviprayukto
bhramarasadṛśakeśaḥ kāmacārī manuṣyaḥ || 9
pītā'śvagandhā payasā'rdhamāsaṃ ghṛtena tailena sukhāmbunā vā
kṛśasya puṣṭiṃ vapuṣo bibharti bālasya sasyasya yathā'mbuvṛṣṭiḥ || 10
dhātrītilānbhṛṅgarajovimiśrānye bhakṣayeyurmanujāḥ krameṇa
te kṛṣṇakeśā vimalendriyāśca nirvyādhayo'pyāmaraṇādbhaveyuḥ || 11
māsaṃ vacāmapyupasevamānāḥ kṣīreṇa tailena ghṛtena vā'pi
bhavanti rakṣobhiradhṛṣyarūpā medhāvino nirmalamṛṣṭavācaḥ || 12
vṛddhadārukamūlāni sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet
śatāvaryā rasenaiva saptavārāṃśca bhāvayet || 13
akṣamātraṃ tu taccūrṇaṃ sarpiṣā saha yojayet
māsamātropayogena matimāñjāyate naraḥ
medhāvī smṛtimāṃścaiva valīpalitavarjitaḥ || 14
hastikarṇarajaḥ khādetprātarutthāya sarpiṣā
yatheṣṭāhāracāro'pi sahasrāyurbhavennaraḥ || 15
medhāvī balavānkāmī strīśatāni vrajatyasau
madhunā tvaśvavegaḥ syādbaliṣṭhaḥ strīsahasragaḥ || 16
ayaṃ mantraḥ prayoktavyo bhiṣajā cābhimantraṇe
oṃṃ namo vināyakāya amṛtaṃ rakṣa rakṣa mama phalasiddhiṃ
dehi dehi rudravacanena svāhā || 17
punarnavasyārdhapalaṃ navasya piṣṭaṃ pibedyaḥ payasā'rdhamāsam
māsadvayaṃ tattriguṇaṃ samāṃ vā jīrṇo'pi bhūyaḥ sa punarnavaḥ syāt || 18
dhātrīcūrṇasya kaṃsaṃ svarasaparigataṃ kṣaudrasarpiḥ samāṃśaṃ
kṛṣṇāmānīsitāṣṭaprasṛtiyutamidaṃ sthāpitaṃ bhasmarāśau
varṣānte tatsamaśnanbhavati vipalito rūpavarṇaprabhāvai-
rnirvyādhirbuddhimedhāsmṛtivacanabalasthairyasattvairupetaḥ || 19
guḍūcyapāmārgaviḍaṅgaśaṅkhinīvacābhayāśuṇṭhiśatāvarīsamam
ghṛtena līḍhaṃ prakaroti mānavaṃ tribhirdinaiḥ ślokasahasradhāriṇam || 20
brāhmī vacā aśvagandhāpippalyo madhusaṃyutāḥ
āsāṃ prayogātsaptāhātkiṃnaraiḥ saha gīyate || 21
tīvreṇa kuṣṭhena parītadeho yaḥ somarājīṃ niyamena khādet
saṃvatsaraṃ kṛṣṇatiladvitīyāṃ sa somarājīṃ vapuṣā'tiśete || 22
pañca bhallātakāṃśchitvā sādhayedvidhivajjale || 23
kaṣāyaṃ tu pibecchītaṃ ghṛtenāktoṣṭhatālukaḥ
pañcavṛddhyā pibedyāvatsaptatiṃ hvāsayettataḥ || 24
jīrṇe'dyādodanaṃ śītaṃ ghṛtakṣīropasaṃhitam
etadrasāyanaṃ medhyaṃ valīpalitanāśanam || 25
kuṣṭhārśaḥkṛmidoṣaghnaṃ duṣṭaśukraviśodhanam
bhallātakakvāthapāne mantro'yaṃ paṭhyate kvacit || 26
varuṇa tvaṃ hi devānāmamṛtaṃ parikalpase
āyurārogyasiddhyarthamasmākaṃ varado bhava || 27
tailaṃ bhallātakānāṃ tu pibenmāsaṃ yathābalam
sarvopadravanirmukto jīvedvarṣaśataṃ dṛḍhaḥ || 28
kāsaśvāsātisārajvarapiṭakakaṭīkoṭhakuṣṭhapramehā-
nmūtrāghātodarārśaḥśvayathugalaśiraḥkarṇaśaṅkhākṣirogān
ye cānye vātapittakṣatajakaphakṛtā vyādhayaḥ santi janto-
stāṃstānabhyāsayogādapanayati payaḥ pītamante niśāyāḥ || 29
vigataghananiśīthe prātarutthāya nityaṃ
pivati khalu naro yo ghrāṇarandhreṇa vāri
sa bhavati matipūrṇaścakṣuṣā tārkṣyatulyo
valipalitavihīnaḥ sarvarogairvimuktaḥ || 30
ambhasaḥ prasṛtānyaṣṭau ravāvanudite piban
vātapittagadānhanti jīvedvarṣaśataṃ naraḥ || 31
vyaṅgavalīpalitaghnaṃ pīnasavaisvaryakāsaśoṣaghnam
rajanīkṣaye'mbunasyaṃ rasāyanaṃ dṛṣṭijananaṃ ca || 32
tadvattriphalāmbu niśisthaṃ valipalitaharaṃ dṛṣṭidaṃ śreṣṭham
prasṛtidvayaṃ tu pātavyaṃ nāsayā nādhikaṃ matam || 33
na kevalaṃ dīrghamihā''yuraśnute rasāyanaṃ yo vidhivanniṣevate
gatiṃ sa devarṣiniṣevitāṃ śubhāṃ prapadyate brahma tathaiva cākṣaram || 34
Adhikāra 70
taistairbhāvairahṛdyaistu riraṃsormanasi kṣate
dhvajaḥ patatyatho nṝṇāṃ klaibyaṃ samupajāyate || 1
annairamloṣṇalavaṇairatimātropasevitaiḥ
saumyadhātukṣayo dṛṣṭaḥ klaibyaṃ tadaparaṃ smṛtam || 2
ativyavāyaśīlo vā na ca vājīkriyārataḥ
dhvajabhaṅgamavāpnoti sa śukrakṣayahetukam || 3
asādhyaṃ sahajaṃ klaibyaṃ marmacchedāttu yadbhavet
sādhyānāmavaśiṣṭānāṃ kāryo vājīkaro vidhiḥ || 4
pippalīlavaṇopetau bastāṇḍau kṣīrasarpiṣā
sādhitau bhakṣayedyastu sa gacchetpramadāśatam || 5
bastāṇḍasiddhe payasi bhāvitānasakṛttilān
yaḥ khādetsa naro gacchetstrīṇāṃ śatamapūrvavat || 6
cūrṇaṃ vidāryāḥ sukṛtaṃ svarasenaiva bhāvitam
sarpiḥkṣaudrayutaṃ līḍhvā daśa gacchennaro'ṅganāḥ || 7
evamāmalakaṃ cūrṇaṃ svarasenaiva bhāvitam || 8
śarkarāmadhusarpirbhiryuktaṃ līḍhvā payaḥ pibet
etenāśītivarṣo'pi yuveva parihṛṣyati || 9
vidārikandacūrṇaṃ tu śṛtena payasā naraḥ
udumbarasamaṃ khādanvṛddho'pi taruṇāyate || 10
svayaṃguptekṣurakayorbījacūrṇaṃ saśarkaram
dhāroṣṇena naraḥ pītvā payasā na kṣayaṃ vrajet || 11
uccaṭācūrṇamapyevaṃ kṣīreṇottamamucyate
śatāvaryuccaṭācūrṇaṃ peyamevaṃ sukhāmbunā || 12
karṣaṃ madhukacūrṇasya ghṛtakṣaudrasamanvitam
payonupānaṃ yo lihyānnityavegaḥ sa nā bhavet || 13
gokṣurakaḥ kṣurakaḥ śatamūlī vānarī(ri) nāgabalā'tibalā ca
cūrṇamidaṃ payasā niśi peyaṃ yasya gṛhe pramadāśatamasti || 14
trikaṇṭavidārīnistuṣatilabahuputrīrajaścatuṣprastham
bhallātakaprasthayugaṃ tatsaptonaṃ guḍūcyāśca || 15
pañcatriṃśanmadhuno vyoṣasyāṣṭau palāni daśa vahneḥ
taddviguṇā vārāhī saptaguṇā śarkarā dahanāt || 16
madhvardhaṃ ghṛtametatparimṛjya snigdhabhājane nihitam
adyādyathāgni hantuṃ rogānīkaṃ kṣayaṃ kāsam || 17
aśmaryudarabhagaṃdarakuṣṭhavalīpalitapīnasaṃ ṣāṇḍhyam
nṛsiṃhasadṛśavikramamapatyamabhivāñchitaṃ labhate || 18
balavarṇasvarakāntibuddhyutsāhasattvasaṃyuktam
upayujya cūrṇamabdaṃ śuddhatanurnārasiṃhākhyam || 19
varāhamūrdhavatkando vārāhīkandasaṃjñitaḥ
bhiṣajāṃ tadalābhena carmakārāluko mataḥ || 20
ghṛtaṃ śatāvarīgarbhaṃ kṣīre daśaguṇe pacet
śarkarāpippalīkṣaudrayuktaṃ tadvṛṣyamucyate || 21
godhūmāttu palaśataṃ niṣkvāthya salilāḍhake
pādāvaśeṣe pūte ca dravyāṇīmāni dāpayet || 22
godhūmamuñjātaphalaṃ māṣā drākṣā parūṣakam
kākolī kṣīrakākolī jīvantī saśatāvarī || 23
aśvagandhā sakharjūrā madhukaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ sitā
bhallātakaṃ svayaṃguptā samabhāgāni kārayet || 24
ghṛtaprasthaṃ pacedevaṃ kṣīraṃ dattvā caturguṇam
mṛdvagninā'tha siddhe ca dravyāṇyetāni nikṣipet || 25
tvagele pippalī dhānyaṃ karpūraṃ nāgakesaram
yathālābhaṃ vinikṣipya sitākṣaudrapalāṣṭakam || 26
dattvekṣudaṇḍenā''loḍya vidhivadviniyojayet
śālyodanena bhuñjīta pibenmāṃsarasena vā || 27
kevalasya pibedasya palamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ
na tasya liṅgaśaithilyaṃ na ca śukrakṣayo bhavet || 28
balyaṃ paraṃ vātaharaṃ śukrasaṃjananaṃ param
mūtrakṛcchrapraśamanaṃ vṛddhānāmapi śasyate || 29
paladvayaṃ tadaśnīyāddaśarātramatandritaḥ
strīṇāṃ śataṃ ca bhajate pītvā cānupibetpayaḥ
aśvibhyāṃ nirmitaṃ caitadgodhūmājyaṃ rasāyanam || 30
karpūrāgurucocabolanalikālākṣāśaṭhīdhātakī-
puṣpaiḥ saptadalailabālusaralāśaileyamāṃsīpuraiḥ
elākuṅkumarocanādamanakaśrīvāsajātīphalaiḥ
kaṅkolakramukojaṭāmadamurākāntālavaṅgāmayaiḥ || 31
tailośīrahareṇukāmalayajasthauṇeyacaṇḍānakhai-
rjātīkośakulīrapadmakanataiḥ spṛkkānvitaiḥ pālikaiḥ
lākṣāyojanavallilodhrasalile tailaṃ vipācyā''ḍhakaṃ
tenābhyajya tanuṃ jarannapi bhavetstrīṇāṃ paraṃ vallabhaḥ || 32
śukrāḍhyo dyutimānanalpatanayaḥ ṣaṇḍho'pi ratyutsuko
vandhyā garbhavatī bhavedapi tathā vṛddhā'pi sūte sutam
kaṇḍūsvedavicarcikāmayaharaṃ daurgandhyakuṣṭhāpaha-
maśvibhyāṃ parikīrtitaṃ bahuguṇaṃ tailaṃ sugandhaṃ mahat || 33
kūṣmāṇḍakātpalaśataṃ susvinnaṃ niṣkulīkṛtam
prasthaṃ ca ghṛtatailasya tasmiṃstapte pradāpayet || 34
tvakpatradhānyakavyoṣajīrakailādvayānalam
granthikaṃ cavyamātaṅgapippalyaḥ śṛṅgaverakam || 35
śṛṅgāṭakaṃ kaseruṃ ca pralambaṃ tālamastakam
cūrṇīkṛtaṃ palāṃśaṃ tu guḍasya tulayā pacet || 36
śītībhūte palānyaṣṭau madhunaḥ saṃpradāpayet
kaphapittānilaharaṃ mandāgnīnāṃ ca dīpanam || 37
kṛśānāṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vājīkaraṇamuttamam
pramadāsu prasaktānāṃ ye ca syuḥ kṣīṇaretasaḥ || 38
kṣayeṇa ca gṛhītānāṃ parametadbhiṣagjitam
kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ jvaraṃ hikkāṃ hanti cchardimarocakam || 39
guḍakūṣmāṇḍakaṃ khyātamaśvibhyāṃ samudāhṛtam
khaṇḍakūṣmāṇḍavatpātraṃ svinnakūṣmāṇḍakadravaḥ || 40
yatkiṃcinmadhuraṃ snigdhaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ jīvanaṃ guru
harṣaṇaṃ manasaścaiva tatsarvaṃ vṛṣyamucyate || 41
surūpā yauvanasthā yā bhūṣaṇaiśca vibhūṣitā
yā vaśyā śikṣitā yā ca sā strī vṛṣyatamā matā || 42
nāntarvai ṣoḍaśādvarṣātsaptateḥ parato na ca
āyuṣkāmo naraḥ strībhiḥ saṃyogaṃ kartumarhati || 43
bhojanāni vicitrāṇi pānāni vividhāni ca
vācaḥ śrotrānugāminyastvaksukhasparśanāni ca || 44
gandhā manojñā rūpāṇi vicitropavanāni ca
manasaścāpratīghāto vājīkurvanti mānavam || 45
jarayā cintayā śukraṃ vyādhibhiḥ karmakarśanāt
kṣayaṃ gacchatyanaśanātstrīṇāṃ cātiniṣevaṇāt || 46
kṣayādbhaṅgā(yā)davisrambhācchokātstrīdoṣadarśanāt
nārīṇāmarasajñatvādabhighātādasevanāt || 47
bhallātakāsthijalaśūkamathākṣa(bja)patra-
mantarvidahya matimānsaha saindhavena
etadvirūḍhabṛhatīphalatoyapiṣṭa-
mālepanaṃ mahiṣaviḍvimalīkṛtāṅge || 48
sthūlaṃ mahadvaraturaṃgamatulyamāśu
śephaḥ karotyabhimataṃ na hi saṃśayo'sti || 49
kāsīsaturagagandhāsāvaragajapippalīvipakvena
tailena yānti vṛddhiṃ niyataṃ stanakarṇavarāṅgaliṅgāni || 50
Adhikāra 71
yathādoṣaṃ yathāvyādhi yathākālaṃ yathābalam
snehaṃ pakvamapakvaṃ vā pātuṃ yuñjyāccikitsakaḥ || 1
kevalaṃ paittike sarpirvātike lavaṇānvitam
deyaṃ bahukaphe cāpi vyoṣakṣārasamāyutam || 2
snehasātmyaḥ kleśasaho dṛḍhaḥ kāle ca śītale
acchameva pibetsnehamacchapānaṃ ca śobhanam || 3
uṣṇodakānupānaṃ tu snehānāmatha śasyate
ṛte bhallātakasnehāttatra toyaṃ suśītalam || 4
snehapītastu tṛṣṇāyāṃ pibeduṣṇodakaṃ naraḥ
evaṃ cānupaśāmyantyāṃ snehamuṣṇāmbunoddharet || 5
mithyācārādbahutvādvā yasya sneho na jīryati
viṣṭabhya cāpi jīryeta vāriṇoṣṇena vāmayet || 6
tataḥ snehaṃ punardadyāllaghukoṣṭhāya dehine
jīrṇājīrṇaviśaṅkāyāṃ pibeduṣṇodakaṃ naraḥ
tenodgāro bhavecchuddho ruciścānnaṃ bhavetprati || 7
syuḥ pacyamāne tṛḍdāhabhramasādāruciklamāḥ || 8
pariṣicyādbhiruṣṇābhirjīrṇasnehaṃ tato naram
yavāgūṃ pāyayeduṣṇāṃ klinnāmatyalpataṇḍulām || 9
pibettrayahaṃ caturahaṃ pañcāhaṃ ṣaḍahaṃ tathā
saptarātrātparaṃ snehaḥ sātmyībhāvāya kalpate || 10
śuddhyarthaṃ punarāhāre naiśe jīrṇe prayojayet
pibetsaṃśamanīyaṃ vā annakāle prakāṅkṣitaḥ || 11
snehadviṣaḥ kṛśānvṛddhānsukumārāñśiśūnapi
tṛṣṇāluṃ coṣṇakāle ca saha bhaktena pāyayet || 12
bhṛṣṭā māṃsarase snigdhā yavāgūḥ svalpataṇḍulā
sakṣaudrā sevyamānā tu sadyaḥ snehanamucyate || 13
sarpiṣmatīṃ bahutilāṃ yavāgūṃ svalpataṇḍulām
pibetsukhoṣṇāṃ manujaḥ sadyo raukṣyātpramucyate || 14
śarkarāghṛtasaṃsṛṣṭe duhyādgāṃ kalaśe tathā
pāyayedakṣametaddhi sadyaḥ snehanamucyate || 15
pippalyo lavaṇaṃ snehāścatvāro dadhimastakam
pītāmaikadhyametaddhi sadyaḥsnehanamucyate || 16
vivarjayetsnehapānamajīrṇī taruṇajvarī
durbalo'rocakī sthūlo mūrchārtro madapīḍitaḥ || 17
chardyarditastu tṛṣitaḥ śrāntaḥ pānaklamānvitaḥ
dattabastirviriktaśca vānto yaścāpi mānavaḥ
akāle ca prasūtā strī snehapānaṃ vivarjayet || 18
rūkṣaṃ purīṣaṃ grathitaṃ kṛcchrādannaṃ vipacyate || 19
uro vidahyate vāyuḥ koṣṭhādupari dhāvati
durvarṇo durbalaścaiva rūkṣo bhavati mānavaḥ || 20
glāniḥ sadanamaṅgānāmadhastātsnehadarśanam
samyaksnigdhasya liṅgāni snehadveṣastathaiva ca || 21
bhaktadveṣo'sukhāsrāvo gude dāhaḥ pravāhikā
purīṣātipravṛttiśca bhṛśaṃ snigdhasya lakṣaṇam || 22
rūkṣasya snehanaṃ snehairatisnigdhasya rūkṣaṇam
śyāmākakoradūṣānnatakrapiṇyākasaktubhiḥ
snehātpraskandanaṃ jantustrirātroparataḥ pibet || 23
dīptāntarāgniḥ pariśuddhakoṣṭhaḥ pratyagradhāturbalavarṇayuktaḥ
dṛḍhendriyo mandajaraḥ śatāyuḥ snehopasevī puruṣo bhaveddhi || 24
snehameva paraṃ vidyāddurbalānaladīpanam
nālaṃ snehasamiddhasya śamāyānnaṃ sugurvapi || 25
Adhikāra 72
taptaiḥ saikatapāṇikāṃsyavasanaiḥ svedo'tha vā'ṅgārakai-
rlepādvātaharaiḥ sahāmlalavaṇasnehaiḥ sukhoṣṇaistathā
evaṃ taptapayo'mbuvātaśamanakvāthādisekādibhi-
staptaistoyaniṣecanodbhavabṛhadbāṣpaiḥ śilādyaiḥ kramāt || 1
tāpopanāhadravabāṣpapūrvāḥ svedāstato'ntyaprathamau kaphe staḥ
vāyau dvitīyaḥ pavane kaphe ca pittopasṛṣṭe vihitastṛtīyaḥ || 2
yeṣāṃ nasyaṃ vidhātavyaṃ bastiścāpi hi dehinām
śodhanīyāśca ye kecitpūrvaṃ svedyāśca te matāḥ || 3
paścātsvedyā hṛte śalye mūḍhagarbhā'nupadravā
samyakprasūtā kāle ca paścātsvedyaiva jānatā || 4
svedyaḥ pūrvaṃ ca paścācca bhagaṃdaryarśasastathā
aśmaryā cā''turo jantuḥ śeṣāñśastreṣu vakṣyate || 5
snehaklinnā dhātusaṃsthāśca doṣāḥ
svasthānasthā ye ca mārgeṣu līnāḥ
samyaksvedairyojitāste dravatvaṃ
prāptāḥ koṣṭhaṃ śodhanairyāntyaśeṣāḥ || 6
agnerdīptiṃ mārdavaṃ tvakprasādaṃ bhaktaśraddhāṃ srotasāṃ nirmalatvam
kuryātsvedastandrinidre ca hanyātsaṃdhīṃstabdhāṃśceṣṭayedāśu yuktaḥ || 7
sarvānsvedānnivāte tu jīrṇe'nne cāvacārayet
snehābhyaktaśarīrasya śītairācchādya cakṣuṣī
hṛdayaṃ vṛṣaṇau dṛṣṭī svedayenmṛdu vā na vā || 8
śītaśūleṣūparame stambhagauravanigrahe
saṃjāte mārdave svede svedanādviratirmatā || 9
svedāsrāvo vyādhihānirlaghutvaṃ śītārthitvaṃ mārdavaṃ cā''turasya
samyaksvinne lakṣaṇaṃ prāhuretanmithyāsvinne vyatyayenaitadeva || 10
sphoṭotpattī raktapittaprakopo
mado mūrchā bhrāntidāhau klamaśca
svinne'tyarthaṃ saṃdhipīḍā tṛṣā ca
kriyāḥ śītāstatra kuryādvidhijñaḥ || 11
atisvinnamathā''śvāsya śītāmbutrāsanaṃ hitam || 12
snātamuṣṇāmbunā cāpi nivātaṃ ca layaṃ nayet
bhojayeccānabhiṣyandi sarvamācāramādiśet || 13
nānabhyakte nāpi cāsnigdhadehe svedo yojyaḥ svedavidbhiḥ kathaṃcit || 14
dṛṣṭaṃ loke kāṣṭhamasnigdhamāśu yāyādbhaṅgaṃ svedayogairgṛhītam || 15
pāṇḍurmehī raktapittī tṛṣārtaḥ kṣatakṣīṇo durbalo'jīrṇabhuktaḥ
dakodarī garbhiṇī pānapaśca naite svedyā yaśca martyo'tisārī || 16
svedādeṣāṃ yāti deho vināśaṃ no sādhyatvaṃ yānti caiṣāṃ vikārāḥ || 17
Adhikāra 73
vamanaṃ recanaṃ nasyaṃ nirūhaṃ sānuvāsanam
jñeyaṃ pañcavidhaṃ karma vidhānaṃ tasya kathyate || 1
doṣāḥ kadācitkupyanti jitā laṅnapācanaiḥ
jitāḥ saṃśodhanairye tu na teṣāṃ punarudbhavaḥ || 2
kaphaje vamanaṃ śastaṃ recanaṃ pittaje gade
bastirvātagade kāryamāmaje pācanādikam || 3
snigdhasvinnaṃ naraṃ samyagjānumātrāsanasthitam
kaṇṭhameraṇḍanālena spṛśantaṃ vāmayedbhiṣak || 4
kṛṣṇendrayavasindhūttharāḍhakalkayutaṃ pibet
yaṣṭīkaṣāyaṃ sakṣaudraṃ tena sādhu vamatyalam || 5
kṛśarāṃ rāḍhasaṃsiddhāṃ tadbījairvā śṛtaṃ payaḥ
pītvā rāḍhakaṣāyaṃ vā sindhukṛṣṇānvitaṃ vamet || 6
taṇḍulasalilanipiṣṭaṃ yaḥ pītvā vamati naraḥ pūrvāhṇe
phalinīvalkalamuṣṇaṃ harati garaṃ sakaphapittarujam || 7
āṭarūṣaṃ vacāṃ nimbaṃ paṭolaṃ phalinītvacam
kvāthayitvā pibettoyaṃ vāntikṛnmadanānvitam || 8
kvāthyadravyasya kuḍavaṃ śrapayitvā jalāḍhake
caturbhāgāvaśiṣṭaṃ tu vamaneṣvavacārayet || 9
jīmūtakastathekṣvākuḥ kuṭajaḥ kṛtavedhanaḥ
dhāmārgavaśca saṃyojyā vamane rāḍhavatpṛthak || 10
nimbakaṣāyopetaṃ phalinīgadamadanamadhukasindhūtthaiḥ
madhuyutametadvamanaṃ kaphaparipūrṇāśaye śastam || 11
śleṣmalairvividhairannairdoṣānutkleśya yatnataḥ
snigdhasvinnāya vamanaṃ dattaṃ samyakpravartate || 12
kaphaprasekaṃ hṛdayāviśuddhiṃ śītajvarādhmānamathāvipākam
śirogurutvaṃ madhurāsyatāṃ ca kaṇḍūṃ ca durvāmitaliṅgamāhuḥ || 13
śūnyaṃ śirastīvramatīvakampaṃ pārśvorasordhūpanameva cāpi
pittātiyogaṃ ca visaṃjñatāṃ ca hṛtkaṇṭhapīḍāmapi cātivānte || 14
pitte kaphasyānu sukhaṃ pravṛtte śuddheṣu hṛtkaṇṭhaśiraḥsu cāpi
laghau ca dehe kaphasaṃsrave ca sthite suvāntaṃ puruṣaṃ vyavasyet || 15
tato'parāhṇe suviśuddhadehamuṣṇābhiradbhiḥ pariṣiktagātram
kulatthamudgāḍhakijāṅgalānāṃ yūṣai rasairvā'pyupabhojayettam || 16
kaphaprasekasvarabhedanidrātandrāsyadaurgandhyaviṣopasargāḥ
gurutvakaṇḍūgrahaṇīpradoṣā na santi jantorvamanātkadācit || 17
chinne tarau puṣpaphalaprarohā yathā vināśaṃ sahasā vrajanti
tathā hṛte śleṣmaṇi cchardanena tajjā vikārā vilayaṃ vrajanti || 18
na vāmayettaimirikaṃ na gulminaṃ
na cāpi pāṇḍūdararogapīḍitam
sthūlakṣatakṣīṇakṛśātivṛddhā-
narśorditākṣepakapīḍitāṃśca || 19
rūkṣe pramehe taruṇe ca garbhe gacchatyathordhvaṃ rudhire ca tīvre
duṣṭe ca koṣṭhe kṛmibhirmanuṣyaṃ na vāmayedvarcasi vā'tivṛddhe || 20
ete'pyajīrṇavyathitā vāmyā ye ca viṣāturāḥ
atīva colbaṇakaphāste ca syurmadhukāmbunā || 21
avāmyavamanādrogāḥ kṛcchratāṃ yānti dehinaḥ
asādhyatāṃ vā gacchanti naite vāmyāstvataḥ smṛtāḥ || 22
ativāntaṃ ghṛtābhyaktamavagāhya hime jale
upācaretsitākṣaudramiśrairlehaiścikitsakaḥ || 23
vamane'tipravṛtte ca hṛdyaṃ kāryaṃ virecanam
vireke'tipravṛtte tu vamanaṃ yojayedbhiṣak || 24
Adhikāra 74
snigdhasvinnāya vāntāya pradātavyaṃ virecanam
anyathā yojitaṃ hyetadgrahaṇīrogakṛnmatam || 1
pittena syānmṛduḥ koṣṭhaḥ krūro vātakaphāśrayāt
madhyamaḥ samadoṣatvānmātrā yojyā'nurūpataḥ || 2
śarkarākṣaudrasaṃyuktaṃ trivṛccūrṇāvacūrṇitam
recanaṃ sukumārāṇāṃ tvakpatramaricāṃśakam || 3
bhittvā dvidhekṣuṃ parilipya kalkaistribhaṇḍijātaiḥ pariveṣṭya rajjvā
pakvaṃ tu samyakpuṭapākayuktyā khādettu taṃ pittagadī suśītam || 4
pippalīnāgarakṣāraśyāmāstrivṛtayā saha
lehayenmadhunā sārdhaṃ kaphavyādhau virecanam || 5
harītakī viḍaṅgāni saindhavaṃ nāgaraṃ trivṛt
maricāni ca tatsarvaṃ gomūtreṇa virecanam || 6
trivṛcchāṇatrayasamā triphalā tatsamāni ca
kṣārakṛṣṇāviḍaṅgāni taccūrṇaṃ madhusarpiṣā || 7
lihyādguḍena guṭikāṃ kṛtvā vā'pyupakalpayet
kaphavātakṛtāngulmānplīhodarabhagaṃdarān
hantyanyānapi cāpyetannirapāyaṃ virecanam || 8
abhayā pippalīmūlaṃ maricaṃ viśvabheṣajam
tvakpatrapippalīmustaviḍaṅgāmalakāni ca || 9
etāni samabhāgāni dantī ca triguṇā bhavet
trivṛdaṣṭaguṇā deyā ṣaḍguṇā cātra śarkarā || 10
madhunā modakānvaddhvā cākṣamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ
ekaikaṃ bhakṣayetprātaḥ śītaṃ cānu pibejjalam || 11
tāvadviricyate janturyāvaduṣṇaṃ na sevate
pānāhāravihāreṣu bhaveccāpi niyantraṇaḥ || 12
pāṇḍutvakāsaviṣamajvaravahnisādā-
ñjaṅghorupṛṣṭhajaghanodarapārśvaśūlān
durnāmakuṇḍalabhagaṃdaraśophagulmā-
nyakṣmodarabhramavidāhakamūtrakṛcchrān || 13
plīhākṣirogapavanāśmarikuṣṭhamehā-
nhanyādrasāyanamidaṃ bhiṣajā prayuktam
alpavyayaṃ bahuphalaṃ satataṃ prayojya-
māyuṣkaraṃ palitanāśanamaśvidṛṣṭam || 14
pathyāsaindhavakṛṣṇānāṃ kalkamuṣṇāmbunā pibet
virekaḥ sarvadoṣaghnaḥ śreṣṭho nārācasaṃjñitaḥ || 15
viḍaṅgasārāmalakābhayānāṃ palaṃ palaṃ syāttrivṛtātrayaṃ ca
guḍasya ṣaḍdvādaśabhāgayuktā māsena triṃśadguṭikā vidheyāḥ || 16
nivāraṇe yakṣavareṇa sṛṣṭaḥ sa māṇibhadraḥ kila śākyabhikṣave
ayaṃ hi kāsakṣayakuṣṭhanāśano bhagaṃdaraplīhajalodarārśasām || 17
eraṇḍatailaṃ triphalākvāthena triguṇena ca
yuktaṃ pītaṃ payobhirvā na cireṇa viricyate || 18
hṛtkukṣyaśuddhiḥ paridāhakaṇḍūviṇmūtrasaṅgāśca na sadvirikte
mūrchāgudabhraṃśakaphātiyogāḥ śūlodgamaścātiviriktaliṅgam || 19
gateṣu doṣeṣu kaphāntikeṣu nābhyāṃ laghutve manasaśca tuṣṭau
gate'nile cāpyanulomabhāvaṃ samyagviriktaṃ manujaṃ vyavasyet || 20
mandāgnimakṣīṇamasadviriktaṃ na pāyayettaddivase yavāgūm
kṣīṇaṃ tṛṣārtaṃ suvirecitaṃ ca tanvīṃ sukhoṣṇāṃ laghu pāyayettu || 21
buddheḥ prasādaṃ balamindriyāṇāṃ
dhātusthiratvaṃ jvalanasya dīptim
cirācca pākaṃ vayasaḥ karoti
virecanaṃ samyagupāsyamānam || 22
yathaudakānāṃ bhuvi jaṅgamānāṃ setau vidīrṇe dhruva eva nāśaḥ
pitte hate tvevamupadravāṇāṃ pittātmakānāṃ niyato vināśaḥ || 23
kṣīṇakṣatorūkṣitabālavṛddhā dīno'tha śoṣī bhayaśokataptaḥ
śrāntastṛṣārto'parijīrṇabhakto garbhiṇyadho gacchati yasya vā'sṛk || 24
navapratiśyāyaparītadeho navajvarī yā ca navaprasūtā
kaṣāyanityā na virecanīyāḥ snehādibhirye tvanupaskṛtāśca || 25
mandāgnyatisnehitabālavṛddhasthūlakṣatakṣīṇabhayopataptāḥ
śrāntastṛṣārto'parijīrṇabhakto garbhiṇyadho gacchati yasya cāsṛk || 26
navapratiśyāyamadātyayī ca navajvarī yā ca navaprasūtā
śalyārditāścāpyavirecanīyāḥ snehādibhirye tvanupaskṛtāśca
atyarthapittābhiparītadehānvirecayettānapi mandamandam || 27
virecanairyānti narā vināśamajñaprayuktairavirecanīyāḥ || 28
na cātisnigdhakāyasya dadyātsnehavirecanam
doṣāḥ pracyāvitā bhūyo līyante tena vartmasu || 29
virūkṣasnehasātmyaṃ tu bhūyaḥ saṃsnehya recayet
tena doṣā hatāstasya bhavanti snehabandhanāḥ || 30
snehasvedāvakṛtvaiva yastu saṃśodhanaṃ pibet
dāru śuṣkamivā'' nābherdehastasya vidīryate || 31
snehasvedapracalitā rasaiḥ snigdhairudīritāḥ
doṣāḥ koṣṭhagatā jantoḥ sukhaṃ hartuṃ virecanaiḥ || 32
āsthāpya snehitaṃ tīkṣṇai recayeddhīnarecitam || 33
No verses of the Siddhayoga on this page.

padmakośīranāgāhvacandanāni prayojayet
atiyoge virekasya pānālepanasecanaiḥ || 34
sauvīrapiṣṭaḥ sahakārakalko nābhipralepādatisārahantā || 35
Adhikāra 75
bastirvāte ca pitte ca kaphe rakte ca śasyate
saṃsarge saṃnipāte ca bastireva sadā hitaḥ || 1
virecanātsaptarātre gate jātabalāya vai
kṛtāhārāya sāyāhne bastirdeyo'nuvāsanaḥ || 2
kṣāraṃ na cedvaitaraṇaṃ pradāya dvyahe tryahe vā'pyanuvāsanīyaḥ || 3
prasuptaṃ vāmapārśvena kṛtānnamanuvāsayet || 4
utsṛṣṭānilaviṇmūtre nare bastiṃ nidhāpayet
anyathā vihito bastirnaivāntaḥ pratipadyate || 5
bastiṃ savye kare kṛtvā dakṣiṇenāvapīḍayet
ekenaivāvapīḍena na drutaṃ na vilambitam || 6
prasāritaikajaṅghāyāṃ kāryā'nyopari kuñcitā
pradadyātpuṭakaṃ tasminpāyau doṣaviśodhanam || 7
tataḥ praṇihite sneha uttāno vākśataṃ bhavet
prasāritaiḥ sarvagātraistathā vīryaṃ visarpati || 8
ākuñcayecchanaistristriḥ sakthibāhū tataḥ param
tāḍayettalayorenaṃ trīṃstrīnvārāñśanaiḥ śanaiḥ
sphijoścaivaṃ tataḥ śroṇī śayyāṃ trirutkṣipettataḥ || 9
evaṃ praṇihite bastau mandāyāso'pyamandavāk
svāstīrṇe śayane kāmamāsītā''cārike rataḥ || 10
ṣaṭpalī tu bhavejjyeṣṭhā madhyamā tripalī bhavet
kanīyasyardhapalikā tridhā mātrā'nuvāsane || 11
prāgdānamādye dvipalaṃ palārdhavṛddhirdvitīye palamakṣavṛddhiḥ
karṣadvayaṃ syādvasumāṣavṛddhirbastau tṛtīye krama eṣa uktaḥ || 12
sa tu saindhavacūrṇena śatāhvena ca saṃyutaḥ
bhavetsukhoṣṇaśca tathā nireti sahasā sukham || 13
cūrṇamāṣaḥ pale snehe sindhujanmaśatāhvayoḥ
māṣamekaṃ pale snehe sindhujanmaśatāhvayoḥ || 14
yasyānuvāsano dattaḥ sakṛdanvakṣamāvrajet
atyuṣṇo vā'titīkṣṇo vā vāyunā vā prapīḍitaḥ || 15
savāto'dhikamātro vā gurutvādvā'tibheṣajaḥ
tasyānyo'lpataro deyo na hi snihyatyatiṣṭhati || 16
trīnyasya yāmānanuvartate ca sneho naraḥ syātsa viśuddhadehaḥ || 17
ekāntaropayogena dhānyaśuṇṭhījalaṃ pibet
tenāsya dīpyate vahnirbhaktakāṅkṣā ca jāyate || 18
kvāthārdhamātrayā prātardhānyaśuṇṭhījalaṃ pibet
pittottare kaduṣṇāmbhastāvanmātraṃ pibedatha || 19
snehājīrṇaṃ śamayati śleṣmāṇaṃ ca bhinatti tat
mārutasyānukūlatvaṃ kuryāduṣṇodakaṃ nṛṇām || 20
snehabastirvidheyastu nāviśuddhasya dehinaḥ
snehavīryaṃ tathā datte dehaṃ nānu visarpati || 21
aśuddhamapi vātena kevalenāpi pīḍitam
ahorātrasya kāleṣu sarveṣvevānuvāsayet || 22
tīvrāyāṃ ruji jīrṇānnaṃ bhojayitvā'nuvāsayet || 23
rūkṣasya bahuvātasya dvau trīnvā'pyanuvāsanān
dattvā snigdhatanuṃ jñātvā tataḥ paścānnirūhayet || 24
asnigdhamapi vātena kevalenātipīḍitam
snehapragāḍhairmatimānnirūhaiḥ samupācaret || 25
snehabastiṃ nirūhaṃ vā naikamevābhyasecciram
snehātpittakaphotkleśau nirūhātpavanādbhayam || 26
yathocitātpādahīnaṃ bhojayitvā'nuvāsayet || 27
na cābhuktavataḥ snehaḥ praṇidheyaḥ kathaṃcana
śuddhatvācchūnyakoṣṭhasya kṣiprastūrdhvamathotpatet || 28
ekaṃ tathā trīnkaphaje vikāre pittātmake pañca tu sapta vā'pi
vāte navaikādaśa vā punarvā bastīnayugmānkuśalo vidadhyāt || 29
viṣṭabdhānilaviṇmūtraḥ snehahīne'nuvāsane
dāhaklamapravāhārtikaraścātyanuvāsane || 30
sānilaḥ sapurīṣaśca snehaḥ pratyeti yasya vai
ūṣācoṣau vinā śīghraṃ sa samyaganuvāsitaḥ || 31
jīrṇānnamatha sāyāhne snehe pratyāgate punaḥ
laghvannaṃ bhojayetkāmaṃ dīptāgniḥ sa naro yadi || 32
ahorātrādapi snehaḥ pratyāgacchanna duṣyati
kuryādbastiguṇāṃścāpi jīrṇastvalpaguṇo bhavet || 33
yasya nopadravaṃ kuryātsnehavastiraniḥsṛtaḥ
sarvo'lpo vā''vṛto raukṣyādupekṣyaḥ sa vijānatā || 34
anāyāntamahorātrātsnehaṃ sopadravaṃ haret
snehabastāvanāyāte nānyaḥ sneho vidhīyate || 35
aśuddhasya malonmiśraḥ sneho naiti yadā punaḥ
tadā'ṅgasadanādhmānaśūlāḥ śvāsaśca jāyate || 36
pakvāśayagurutvaṃ ca tatra dadyānnirūhaṇam
tīkṣṇaṃ tīkṣṇauṣadhaireva siddhaṃ vā'pyanuvāsanam || 37
śuddhasya dūrānugate snehe snehasya darśanam
mukhe tu sarvendriyāṇāmupalepo'vasādanam
snehagandhamukhaṃ vā'pi kāsaśvāsāvarocakaḥ || 38
gale niṣpīḍya tatrā''śu kampayettaṃ prayatnataḥ
kāryaṃ tasya sutīkṣṇaṃ ca tīkṣṇaṃ cāpi virecanam || 39
nātyucchritaṃ nāpyatinīcapādaṃ sapādapīṭhaṃ śayanaṃ praśastam
pradhānamūrdhāstaraṇopapannaṃ prākśīrṣakaṃ śuklapaṭottarīyam || 1
anuvāsitamabhyaktaṃ svinnadehaṃ nirūhayet
abhuktaṃ pīḍayedbastimatūrṇamavilambitam || 2
jānumaṇḍalamāveṣṭya dattaṃ dakṣiṇapāṇinā
kṛṣṭanetracchaṭāśabdaśataṃ tiṣṭhedavegavān || 3
dvitīyaṃ vā tṛtīyaṃ vā caturthaṃ vā yathārthataḥ
samyaṅnirūḍhaliṅge tu prāpte bastiṃ nivārayet || 4
madhusnehanakalkākhyakaṣāyāvāpataḥ kramāt
trīṇi ṣaḍ dve daśa trīṇi palānyanilarogiṇām || 5
pitte catvāri catvāri dve dvipañcacatuṣṭayam
ṣaṭtrīṇi dve daśa trīṇi kaphe vā'pi nirūhaṇam || 6
sasneha ekaḥ pavane samāṃso dvau svāduśītau payasā tu pitte
trayaḥ samūtrāḥ kaṭukoṣṇarūkṣāḥ kaphe nirūhā na paraṃ vidheyāḥ || 7
eko'pakarṣatyanilaṃ svamārgātpittaṃ dvitīyastu kaphaṃ tṛtīyaḥ || 8
alpālpavego viḍvātahīno hīnanirūhaṇaḥ
mūrchāśūlakaphaprāyo mahāvego'tiśabditaḥ || 9
yasya mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ ca kapho vāyuśca gacchati
krameṇa laghutā caiva sunirūḍhaḥ sa mānavaḥ || 10
sunirūḍhaṃ tato jantuṃ snātaṃ bhuktarasaudanam
yathoktena vidhānena yojayetsnehabastinā || 11
tadahastasya pavanādbhayaṃ balavadiṣyate
rasaudanastena śastastadahaścānuvāsanam || 12
samyaṅnirūḍhaṃ tailāktaṃ jalenoṣṇena secitam
alpasnehaṃ jāṅgalasya rasenordhvaṃ tu bhojitam
yojayedalpamātreṇa tatkṣaṇaṃ snehabastinā || 13
prathamā madhyamā hvasvā mātrā yā yaistu sevitā
mandāgnitvānnirūḍhānāṃ tadardhenānuvāsanam || 14
paścādagnibalaṃ matvā pavanasya ca ceṣṭitam
annopastambhite koṣṭhe snehabastirvidhīyate || 15
viviktatā manastuṣṭiḥ snigdhatā vyādhinigrahaḥ
āsthāpite snehabastau samyagdatte tu lakṣaṇam || 16
anāyāntaṃ muhūrtānte nirūhaṃ śodhanairharet
nirūhaireva matimānkṣāramūtrāmlasaṃyutaiḥ || 17
viguṇānilaviṣṭabdhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhannirūhaṇam
śūlāratijvarāṭopānmaraṇaṃ vā prayacchati || 18
na tu bhuktavate deyamāsthāpanamiti sthitiḥ || 19
āmaṃ taduddharedbhuktaṃ chardiṃ vā janayedbhṛśam
kopayetsarvadoṣānvā tasmāddeyamanāśite || 20
āvasthikaṃ kramaṃ cāpi matvā kāryaṃ nirūhaṇam
male'pakṛṣṭe doṣāṇāṃ balavattvaṃ na vidyate || 21
atiprapīḍito bastiḥ prakramyā''māśayaṃ tataḥ
vāterito nāsikābhyāṃ mukhato vā pravartate
charditdṛllāsamūrchādīnprakuryāddāhameva ca || 22
tatra tūrṇaṃ galāpīḍaṃ kuryāccāpyavadhūnanam
śiraḥkāyavirekau ca tīkṣṇau sekāṃśca śītalān || 23
dattvā''dau saindhavasyākṣaṃ madhunaḥ prasṛtadvayam
vinirmathya tato dadyātsnehasya prasṛtadvayam || 24
ekībhūte tataḥ snehe kalkasya prasṛtaṃ kṣipet
saṃmūrchite kaṣāyaṃ ca pañcaprasṛtasaṃmitam || 25
vitarecca tathā''vāpamante dviprasṛtonmitam

badaryairāvatīśeluśālmalīdhanvanāṅkurāḥ
kṣīrasiddhāḥ suśītāḥ syuḥ sāsrāḥ picchilasaṃjñitāḥ || 1
vārāhamāhiṣaurabhrabaiḍālaiṇeyakaukkuṭam

madanasya phalaṃ caikaṃ yathāyogena dāpayet || 1

na cābhyaṅgo na ca svedaḥ parihāravidhirna ca || 2

etānyanuktānyapi dāpayeta nirūhayoge madanātphalaṃ ca || 6

guḍasya tu pale dve tu sarvamāloḍya yatnataḥ

kṛmyudāvartagulmādīnsadyo hanyānniṣevitaḥ || 3
palaśuktikarṣakuḍavairamlīkāguḍasindhujanmagomūtraiḥ
īṣattailayuto'yaṃ bastiḥ śūlānāhāmavātaharaḥ || 1

atha cedbalavāñjanturabhuktvā'pi tadā kvacit || 2
api hīnaṃ kramaṃ kuryānna tu kuryādatikramam
viśeṣātsukumārāṇāṃ hīna eva kramo hitaḥ || 1

Adhikāra 77

nasyasya snaihikasyātha deyāstvaṣṭau tu bindavaḥ

dvātriṃśadbindavaścāpi śuktirityabhidhīyate
dve śuktī pāṇiśuktiśca jñeyā'tra kuśalairnaraiḥ || 3
śṛṅgāṭakamabhivyāpya sthāpayenna gileddravam

Adhikāra 79
snigdhoṣṇaiḥ snaihiko vāte svāduśītaiḥ prasādanaḥ
pitte kaṭvamlalavaṇairuṣṇaiḥ saṃśodhanaḥ kaphe
kaṣāyatiktamadhuraiḥ kaṭūṣṇai ropaṇo vraṇe || 1

hīne jāḍyakaphotkleśānna rasajñānameva ca || 5

kalko rasakriyā kṣaudraṃ cūrṇaṃ ceti caturvidham || 9

doṣāṇāṃ sāmyamārogyaṃ vaiṣamyaṃ vyādhirucyate

kṣutsaṃbhavati jīrṇeṣu rasadoṣamaleṣu ca
ucito'nucito vā'pi so'nnakālaḥ praśasyate || 20
ardharātre'pi bhuñjānaḥ paramārthaṃ bubhukṣitaḥ
kṣudhī vaidyaparityāgī vyādhibhirnābhibhūyate || 21
ahitāśanasaṃparkātsarvarogodbhavo yataḥ
atastadahitaṃ tyājyaṃ nyāyyaṃ pathyaniṣevaṇam || 22
kalamāḥ śālayo raktā nīvāraḥ ṣaṣṭikāṅgukāḥ || 23
śyāmākakodravoddālasatīnayavamudgakāḥ
masūrāḍhakigodhūmā hariṇaiṇakuraṅgakāḥ || 24
tittiriḥ krakarā lāvāḥ śreṣṭhā vartīravartikāḥ
suniṣaṇṇakavāstūkacillīmaṇḍūkaparṇikāḥ || 25
taṇḍulīyakajīvantyo dāḍimāmalakaṃ madhu
saindhavaṃ gavyamājyaṃ ca pathyavargo'yamuttamaḥ || 26
vyāyāmaśca niśāsvāpo nivātoṣṇajalaṃ tathā
āyuṣyaṃ bhojanaṃ jīrṇe vegānāmavidhāraṇam
brahmacaryamahiṃsā ca sāhasānāṃ ca varjanam || 27
annādaṣṭaguṇaṃ piṣṭaṃ piṣṭādaṣṭaguṇaṃ payaḥ || 28
payaso'ṣṭaguṇaṃ māṃsaṃ māṃsādaṣṭaguṇaṃ ghṛtam
ghṛtādaṣṭaguṇaṃ tailaṃ mardane na tu bhojane || 29
vallīphalairamlakulatthaśākairmatsyaiḥ surāmāṃsatilaiḥ karīraiḥ
piṇyākajambūlavaṇaiśca dadhnā vyastaiḥ samastaiśca payo virodhi || 30
kapiñjalo lāvakatittirī tu godhāmayūrau rubutailakāṣṭhaiḥ
siddhā viruddhāśca tathā kapotāstaileṣu bhṛṣṭā api sarṣapāktāḥ || 31
uṣṇena divyasalilena varāhagodhā-
māṃsena yāti vikṛtiṃ madhu mūlakena
takreṇa coṣṇamapi tulyaghṛtaṃ dhṛtaṃ ca
kāṃsye daśāhamuṣitaṃ ca tathā ghṛtaṃ ca || 32
ghṛtaṃ dhānāśca takreṇa dadhnā madyaṃ ca kukkuṭaiḥ
balākā na hitā madyaiḥ surākṛśarapāyasaiḥ || 33
matsyā upodakā caiva vikārairaikṣavaistathā
sauvīraistilaśaṣkulyaḥ kākamācī guḍena ca
atisnigdhātirūkṣādīnvarjayedātmavānnaraḥ || 34
vyādhimindriyadaurbalyaṃ maraṇaṃ cādhigacchati
viruddharasavīryāṇi bhuñjāno'nātmavānnaraḥ || 35
yatkiṃciddoṣamutkleśya bhuktaṃ kāyānna nirharet
rasādiṣu yathārthaṃ vā tadvikārāya kalpate || 36
viruddhāśanajānrogānpratihanti virecanam
vamanaṃ śamanaṃ cāpi pūrvaṃ vā hitabhojanam || 37
sātmyato'lpatayā vā'pi dīptāgnestaruṇasya ca
snigdhavyāyāmabalināṃ viruddhaṃ vitathaṃ bhavet || 38
śuṣkaṃ māṃsaṃ striyo vṛddhā bālārkastaruṇaṃ dadhi
prabhāte maithunaṃ nidrā sadyaḥ prāṇaharāṇi ṣaṭ || 39
sadyomāṃsaṃ navaṃ cānnaṃ bālā strī kṣīrabhojanam
ghṛtamuṣṇodakaṃ caiva sadyaḥ prāṇakarāṇi ṣaṭ || 40
śīte śītānilasparśasaṃruddho balināṃ balī
paktā bhavati hemante mātrādravyagurukṣamaḥ || 41

sa yadā nendhanaṃ yuktaṃ labhate dehajaṃ tadā
rasaṃ hinastyato vāyuḥ śītaḥ śīte prakupyati || 42
abhyaṅgotsādanaṃ svedaḥ snigdhoṣṇagurubhojanam || 43

āliṅgyāgurudigdhāṅgīṃ svapyādyauvanaśālinīm || 44

vasante nicitaḥ śleṣmā dinakṛdbhābhirīritaḥ
kāyāgniṃ bādhate rogāṃstataḥ prakurute bahūn
tasmādvasante karmāṇi vamanādīni kārayet || 46

vyāyāmodvartanaṃ dhūmaṃ kabalagrahamañjanam
sukhāmbunā śaucavidhiṃ śīlayetkusumāgame
candanāgurudigdhāṅgo yavagodhūmabhojanaḥ || 48
divā śītapṛhe nidrāṃ niśi candrāṃśuśītale
vyajanaiḥ pāṇisaṃsparśaiścandanodakaśītalaiḥ || 49
kānanāni ca śītāni jalāni kusumāni ca
grīṣmakāle niṣeveta maithunādvirato naraḥ || 50

agniṃ saṃrakṣatā sevyā yavagodhūmaśālayaḥ
purāṇā jāṅgalairmāṃsairbhojyā yūṣaiśca saṃskṛtaiḥ || 53
bhūbāṣpaparihārārthaṃ śayīta ca vihāyasi || 54
udamanthaṃ divāsvapnamavaśyāyaṃ nadījalam
vyāyāmamātapaṃ caiva vyavāyaṃ cātra varjayet || 55
varṣāsu saṃcitaṃ pittaṃ prāyaḥ śaradi kupyati
tatrānnapānaṃ madhuraṃ laghu śītaṃ ca tiktakam
pittapraśamanaṃ sevyaṃ recanaṃ raktamokṣaṇam || 56
ikṣavaḥ śālayo mudgāḥ sarombhaḥ kvathitaṃ payaḥ

kaphapittānilāḥ pūrvamadhyānteṣu vyavasthitāḥ
bhuktāhorātravayasāṃ saṃdhiṣvapi kaphānilau || 61
kṛśo rūkṣo'lpakeśaśca calacitto'navasthitaḥ
bahuvāgvyomagaḥ svapne vātaprakṛtiko naraḥ || 62
akālapalito gauraḥ prasvedī kopano budhaḥ
svapneṣu dīptimatprekṣī pittaprakṛtiko naraḥ || 63
sthiracittaḥ subaddhāṅgaḥ suprajaḥ snigdhamūrdhajaḥ
svapne jalāśayālokī śleṣmaprakṛtiko naraḥ
saṃmiśrairlakṣaṇairjñeyā dvitri(tra) doṣānvayā narāḥ || 64
bahūdakanago'nūpaḥ kaphamārutarogavān
jāṅgalo'lpāmbuśākhī ca vātapittagadottaraḥ || 65
saṃsṛṣṭalakṣaṇopeto deśaḥ sādhāraṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 66
samāḥ sādhāraṇe yasmācchītavarṣoṣṇamārutāḥ
samatā caiva doṣāṇāṃ tasmātsādhāraṇo varaḥ || 67
kṣīritiktakaṣāyāṇāṃ dvādaśāṅgulamāyatam
dantakāṣṭhaṃ niṣeveta mukharogapraṇāśanam || 68
navajvarāsyaśoṣādau na sevyaṃ dantarogiṇā
pratipaddarśaṣaṣṭhīṣu navamyāṃ caiva bhārata || 69
dantānāṃ kāṣṭhasaṃyogo dahatyāsaptamaṃ kulam || 70
patraiḥ syādbakulādīnāṃ tatra dantapragharṣaṇam
jihvānirlekhanādrogāstadgatā na bhavanti hi || 71
pittaraktakṛtā rogā na syurvyaṅgādayo mukhe
bhilloṭakakaṣāyeṇa dhātrīkvāthena dhāvanāt || 72
āmalakasya kaṣāyeṇa netre prakṣālayet | ubhayaṃ śītodakena vā || 73
dṛṣṭiparimārjanārthaṃ mukhasya kāntyai tridoṣaśamanāya
sauvīrāñjanamuktaṃ locanayo rañjanaṃ muninā || 74
śobhārthamañjanaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ śalākābhiryathepsitam
pakṣmalaṃ viśadaṃ kāntamamalojjvalamaṇḍalam
netramañjanasaṃyogādbhaveccāmalatārakam || 75
śrānte prarudite caiva madyapīte navajvare
uṣṇaveśmani dāhe ca añjanaṃ naiva śasyate || 76
nirgadaṃ nirmalaṃ cakṣurbhavatyañjanayogataḥ
pratimarśastato deya ūrdhvajatrugadāpahaḥ || 77
mukhaśuddhiśca pūgādikarpūrasumanaḥphalaiḥ || 78
abhyaṅgotsādanasnānagandhamālyādibhūṣitaḥ
pādatracchatradaṇḍaiśca vicarecca yathāsukham || 79
śrotraghrāṇaśiraḥpādatailanityo'tha dhūmapaḥ || 80
pṛṣṭhato'rkaṃ niṣeveta jaṭhareṇa hutāśanam
svāminaṃ sarvabhāvena paralokamamāyayā || 81
rājanharītakīṃ bhakṣa takraṃ salavaṇaṃ piba
bhuṅkṣva vāstukaśākena naśyantu tava tena te || 82
na vegāndhārayennidrāvātaviṇmūtraretasām
jṛmbhābāṣpakṣavodgāraśvāsatṛṣṇāvamikṣudhām || 83
dhāriteṣu prajāyante tanmārgasthānajā gadāḥ || 84
dhāryā vegāstvaśastānāṃ manovākkāyakarmaṇām
na pīḍayedindriyāṇi na caitānyatilālayet || 85
anuyāyātpratipadaṃ sarvadharmeṣu madhyamām
seveta viṣayānkāle tyaktvā tatparatāṃ vaśī || 86
nātijāgaraṇaṃ nidrāṃ sarvabhūtahitaiṣitām
devagobrāhmaṇācāryaguruvṛddhānsadā'rcayet || 87
catuṣpathe namaskartā dātā yaśca priyaṃvadaḥ
kruddhānāmanunetā ca dīnānāmanasūyakaḥ || 88
āśvāsakaśca bhītānāṃ bandhuvatsarvadehinām
rāgadveṣanidānānāṃ hantā dharmaparāyaṇaḥ || 89
nityaṃ hitāhāravihārasevī samīkṣyakārī viṣayeṣvasaktaḥ
dātā samaḥ satyaparaḥ kṣamāvānāptopasevī ca bhavatyarogaḥ
śiraśca pādau ca sadā'rcayedyaḥ striyaṃ śriyaṃ nābhilaṣetparasya || 90
na kuryāccañcalaṃ ceto na kālamatipātayet || 91
praṇayenāpi no vācyaṃ vacanaṃ paratāpi ca
ahiṃsā satataṃ kāryā dhāryā cittasya nityatā || 92
Adhikāra 82
vaidyo vyādhyupasṛṣṭaśca bheṣajaṃ paricārakaḥ
ete pādāścikitsāyāḥ karmasādhanahetavaḥ || 1
tattvādhigataśāstrārtho dṛṣṭakarmā svayaṃkṛtī
laghuhastaḥ śuciḥ śūraḥ sajjopaskarabheṣajaḥ || 2
pratyutpannamatirdhīmānvyavasāyī viśāradaḥ
satyadharmaparo yaśca sa bhiṣakpāda ucyate || 3
āyuṣmānsattvavānsādhyo dravyavānātmavānapi
ucyate vyādhitaḥ pādo vaidyavākyakṛdāstikaḥ || 4
praśastadeśasaṃbhūtaṃ praśaste'hani coddhṛtam
alpamātraṃ mahāvīryaṃ gandhavarṇarasānvitam || 5
doṣaghnamaglānikaramavikāri viparyaye
samīkṣya kāle dattaṃ ca bheṣajaṃ pāda ucyate || 6
snigdho'jugupsurbalavānyukto vyādhitarakṣaṇe
vaidyavākyakṛdaśrāntaḥ pādaḥ paricaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 7
svadeśe nicitā doṣā anyasminkopamāgatāḥ
balavantastathā na syurjalajā vā sthalāhṛtāḥ || 8
ucite vartamānasya nāsti deśakṛtaṃ bhayam
āhārasvapnaceṣṭādau taddeśasya guṇe sati || 9
vikāre'lpe mahatkarma kriyā laghvī mahāgade
dvayametadakauśalyaṃ kauśalyaṃ yuktakarmatā || 10
kriyāyāstu guṇālābhe kriyāmanyāṃ prayojayet
pūrvasyāṃ śāntavegāyāṃ na kriyāsaṃkaro mataḥ || 11
guṇālābhe'pi kartavyā viśrāmāntaritakriyā
saiva saivānyathā tasyāḥ pūrvavatsaṃkarādbhayam || 12
ṣaḍbhiḥ kecidahorātraiḥ kecitsaptabhireva ca
icchanti munayaḥ prāyo rasasya parivartanam || 13
parivṛtyā rasasyaiva śāntavegā kriyā matā || 14
caṇḍaḥ sāhasiko bhīruḥ kṛtaghno vyagra eva ca
sadvaidyanṛpatidveṣṭā taddviṣṭaḥ śokapīḍitaḥ
yādṛcchiko mumūrṣuśca vihīnaḥ karaṇaiśca yaḥ || 15
vairī vaidyavidagdhaśca śraddhāhīnaḥ suśaṅkitaḥ
bhiṣajāmavidheyaśca nopakramyā bhiṣagvaraiḥ || 16
etānupācaredvaidyo bahūndoṣānavāpnuyāt
ebhyo'nye samupakramyā narāḥ sarvairupakramaiḥ || 17
cikitsitaṃ śarīraṃ yo na niṣkrīṇāti durmatiḥ
sa yatkaroti sukṛtaṃ tatsarvaṃ bhiṣagaśnute || 18
naiva kurvīta lobhena cikitsāpaṇyavikrayam
īśvarāṇāṃ vasumatāṃ lipsetārthaṃ tu vṛttaye || 19
guñjābhiḥ saptabhirmāṣaḥ śāṇo māṣacatuṣṭayam
dvau śāṇau vaṭakaḥ kolastau dvau karṣa udumbaraḥ || 20
akṣaṃ pāṇitalaṃ jñeyaṃ suvarṇaṃ kavalagrahaḥ
picurbiḍālapadakaṃ śuktiḥ pāṇitaladvayam || 21
taddvayena palaṃ muṣṭiḥ prakuñco bilvamucyate
dve pale prasṛtaṃ vidyāttaddvayaṃ kuḍavo'ñjaliḥ || 22
mānikā'ṣṭapalaṃ te dve prasthastasmāccaturguṇam
āḍhakaṃ kaṃsapātre ca caturbhirdroṇa ucyate || 23
tatparyāyā ghaṭonmānanalvaṇārmaṇasūrpakāḥ
tulā palaśataṃ tābhirviṃśatyā bhāra ucyate || 24
śuṣkadravyeṣvidaṃ mānaṃ dviguṇaṃ tu dravārdrayoḥ
jñātavyaṃ kuḍavādūrdhvaṃ prasthādiśrutamānataḥ || 25
dvaiguṇyaṃ na tulāmāna iti mānavido viduḥ || 26
goṇī droṇāḍhakaprasthāḥ kuḍavaśca palaṃ picuḥ
śāṇako māṣakaścaiva yathāpūrvaṃ caturguṇāḥ || 27

samyakprayogaṃ sarveṣāṃ siddhirākhyāti karmaṇām
siddhirākhyāti sarvaiśca guṇairyuktaṃ bhiṣaktamam || 28
iti vividhamunīnāṃ vākyamālokya yatnā-
tsvamatiparimitairvikhyātimadbhiḥ prayogaiḥ
grathita iha mayā'yaṃ saṃgraho vṛndanāmnā
sapadi sa hi likhitvā siddhayogaḥ samāptaḥ || 29
